pUrvottaramImAMsAvAdanakSatramAlA
appayyadIkSitaH

Source: Vidhirasayanadigranthapancakam (Srimad Appayya Deekshithendra Granthavali Series 8, Hyderabad 2007), OCR, corrected.
Transliteration: Kyoto-Harvard. [p.N] = page of the printed edition.

zrImahAgaNapataye namaH || zrIH || pUrvottaramImAMsAvAdanakSatramAlA mAsAgnihotravAdaH tantrAntareSvanupapAditamarthajAtaM yatsiddhavadvyavahRtaM dhvanitaM ca bhASye | tasya prasAdhanamiha kriyate nayoktyA bAlapriyeNa mRduvAdakathApathena || zeSIbhavadapi bhASye sumahati satre'gnihotramiva | svAtantryeNa prakaraNamarthapratipAdane samarthamidam || [p.185] kuNDapAyinAmayanAkhye satre zrUyate 'upasadbhizcaritvA mAsamagnihotraM juhoti' iti | tanmAsAgnihotraM naiyamikAgnihotrAdbhidyate na veti jaranmImAMsakamatAnusAriNaH pUrvapakSiNaH zrIkaNThAcAryamatAnusAriNaH siddhAntinazca vipratipattyA saMzaye sati, siddhAntamatAnusAriNo vAdinaH prathamA kakSyAmAsAgnihotraM naiyamikAgnihotrAnna bhidyate, vidhivAkyazrutAgnihotranAmakatvAt | yadvidhivAkyazrutAgnihotranAmakaM na tannaiyamikAgnihotrAdbhidyate, yathA 'yavAgvAgnihotraM juhoti' iti zrutaM yavAgUguNakamagnihotram | atra vaizvAnaravidyAGgabhUte prANAhuti karmaNi 'sa ya idamavidvAnagnihotraM juhoti yathAGgArAnapohya bhasmani juhuyAttAdRk tatsyAt' ityarthavAdazrutAgnihotranAmake vyabhicAravAraNArthaM vidhivAkyazruteti hetuvizeSaNam | tathA ca vidhivAkyazrutatvaviziSTaM yadagnihotranAma tadviSayatvAditi hetorarthaH | tena arthavAdazrutaM yadagnihotranAma tadeva vidhivAkyeSvapi zrutamiti tadupalakSitatvamAdAya vyabhicAratAdavasthyamiti zaGkAyA nAvakAzaH | yadvA agnihotranAma[p.186]samAnAdhikaraNavidhyuddezazrutajuhotiviSayatvAditi hetuH | na ca AdhAnAGge tUSNIhome punarAdhAnAGgasaptavatIhome pravaryAGgadharmazeSahome ca vyabhicAraH, teSAM 'tatastUSNImagnihotraM juhoti' 'sapta te agne samidhaH sapta jihvA ityagnihotraM juhoti' 'etasmAdeva gharmazeSAdupayamanenAgnihotraM juhoti' iti vidhivAkyazrutAgrihotranAmakatvAt, tatsamAnAdhikaraNavidhivAkyazrutajuhotidhAtuviSayatvAcceti vAcyam | teSvapi uktasAdhyAbhyupagamena teSAM pakSasamatvAditi |
atha prativAdinaH dvitIyA kakSyAvipakSabAdhakatarkAbhAvAt aprayojako'yaM hetuH noktasAdhyasAdhanakSamaH ; 'prAyaNIyo'tirAtraH' iti dvAdazAhike prathame'hani zrutasya prAyaNIyanAmnaH gAvAmayanike ca prathame'hani 'vaizvAnaro jyotiSTomaH prAyaNIyametadaharbhavati jyotireva purastAddadhate' iti zravaNena nAmAbhede satyapi karmabhedasyAbhyupagatatayA nAmaikyasya tadaikyAprayojakatvAt | tasmAnmAsAgnihotraM naiyamikAgnihotrAdbhidyate, naiyamikAgnihotrahomavidhyasaMnihitAnekAviSayakasvAbhidheyAnaGganAMmArthasaMkrAntirahitavidhipara juhotiviSayatvAt-yaduktasAdhanavat taduktasAdhyavat, yathA 'saptavatyA pUrNAhutiM juhoti' iti vihitaM pUrNAhutikarma | tatra yavAgUguNakAgnihotrahome naiyamikAgnihotraprakaraNapaThitayavAgUguNavidhiparajuhotiviSaye vyabhicAravAraNArthaM naiyamikAgnihotrahoma vidhyasaMnihateti vizeSaNam | naiyamikAgnihotrahomaH svavidhyasaMnihitasya AhavanIyAkhyaguNavidhiparasya 'yadAhavanIye juhoti' ityanArabhyAdhItavAkyazrutasya juhoteviSayo bhavan na svasmAdbhidyata iti tatra vyabhicAravAraNArtham anekAviSayaketi | AhavanIyavAkyAnugato juhotidhAtuH nAnAprakaraNapaThitAnanArabhyAdhItAMzca sarvAnapi homAn AhavanIyaguNavidhAnArthamanuvadati-ityanekaviSayakatvAt na vyabhicAraH | 'ya evaM vidvAnagnihotraM juhoti yAvadagniSTomenopApnoti tAvadupApnoti' - ityarthavAde zrutasya naiyamikAgnihotrahomavidhyasaMnihitasya homAntarAviSayamya juhoterviSayo bhavati naiyamikAgnihotram ; atastatra punarapi vyabhicAraH syAt; tadvAraNArthaM vidhipareti vizeSaNam | na ca arthavAdasthajuhoterapi vidhau tAtparyamasti, arthavAdAnAM yatkiMcidvidhyekavAkyatAM vinA prAmANyAbhAvAditi zaGkyam ; vidhiparazabdena [p.187] stutidvArAnapekSavidhyarthatvasya vivakSitatvAt | 'amAvAsyAyAM rAtrau svayaM yajamAno yavAgvAgnihotraM juhoti' ityauktarUpajuhotiviSaye naiyamikAgnihotre vyabhicAravAraNAya svAbhidheyAnaGgasaMkrAntirahiteti vidhivizeSaNam | tena bhavati vyabhicAranirAsaH ; tasminvAkye zrutasya vidheragnihotrahomAnaGgAmAvasyArthadadhiprayuktayavAgUsaMkrAntatvAt | evaM sati hetoH svarUpAsiddhiH syAt, mAsAgnihotravidheH juhotyabhidheyAnaGgatatkaraNakAkhyAtArthabhAvanAsaMkrAntisattvAditi | tadvAraNAya nAmArthavizeSaNam | nAmArthatvamiha nAmamAtrasamarpitatvaM vivakSitam | ato juhotyabhidheyo homaH svasya svayamaGgam agnihotranAmArthazca bhavatIti vidhestatrApi saMkrAntisattvAt svarUpAsiddhitAvasthyamiti zaGkAyA nAvakAzaH | tathA ca naiyamikAgnihotrahomavidhyasaMnihitAnekAviSayakasvAbhidheyAnaGganAmamAtrasamarpitArthasaMkrAntirahitavidhAnaviSayaka stutidvArAnapekSopayogavajjuhotidhAtuviSayattvAt - iti hetvarthaniSkarSaH | yadyucyetayavAgUvidhiH nAtaJcanArthaH, kiM tu 'nAsyaitAM rAtriM kumArAH api payaso labhante' ityasya dRSTArthatvalAbhAya sarvasyApi yajamAnagavInAM payaso dArzikadadhyarthatvena viniyoktavyatvAt, agnihotrasya payo'bhAvena dadhitaNDulayavAgvAdiyatkiMcidvikalpitadravyAntaragrahaNe prApte, yavAgUniyamArthaH; 'yavAgvaitAM rAtrimagnihotraM juhoti' iti 'AdiSTaM vA idaM devatAyai havigRhItaM bhavati yatpayaH yaddhi payasA juhuyAdyathAnyasyai devatAyai havigRhItaM tadanyasyai juhuyAdevaM ha taM samadaM kuryAt tasmAdyavAgvaitAM rAtrimagnihotraM juhoti' iti zatapathabrAhmaNadarzanAt | gRhamedhIyeSTau AtaJcanAbhAve'pi 'sarvAsAM dugdhe sAyaM carum' iti sarvasya yajamAnagavInAM payaso gRhamedhIye viniyuktatayA agnihotrasya payo'bhAvenaiva 'yavAgvaitAM rAtrimagnihotraM juhoti' iti zatapathabrAhmaNa eva vidhAnAcca | tathA ca udAhRtayavAgUvidhiviSaye vyabhicAratAdavasthyamiti-tadA, naiyamikAgnihotraprakaraNAnAmnAtavidhigocaratvAditi hetuH kAryaH| AhavanIyAdivAkyeSu vidherguNasaMkrAntatvAnna vyabhicAraH | evamapi azvamedhasaMkalpAnantaraM dIkSaNIyAyAH prAgrAtrau vAgyamaM vidhAya zrute 'vAgyavatasyaitAM rAtrimagnihotraM juhoti' iti prAtaragnihotrasya rAtrAvuSaHkAle apakarSavidhau vyabhicAraH syAt, kAlasya guNasyApi anupAdeyasya vidhAnAyogena kAlamuddizya homasya vidheyatvAditi | tadA naiyamikAgnihotraprakaraNAnAmnAtahomAnuSThApakavidhigocaratvAditi vA, ubhayasiddhanaiyamikAgnihotraviSayavidhibhinnavidhigocaratvAditi vA heturdraSTavyaH | [p.188] apakarSArthavidheH homAnuSThAnArthatvAbhAvAt Adyahetorna vyabhicAraH | dvitIyahetau saMdigdhasiddhivAraNArtham ubhayasiddha iti vizeSaNam | yadvA svAbhidheyAnaGgeti vizeSaNarahitaH pUrvaheturastu | tena viziSTavyatirekaprayuktodbhAvitavyabhicArazaGkAyA nAvakAzaH | prAk tvidaM datvA idaM deyamiti mate, viziSTavyatireko bhinna iti mate ca vaiyarthyaM nAstIti yavAgvAH zatapathabrAhmaNasiddhahomAGgatvapakSaprasaJjanAvakAzalAbhAya tadvizeSaNamuktam | atrApi apakarSavidhau vyabhicAraH samAna iti anupadoktAveva hetU grAhyau ||
atha vAdinaH tRtIyA kakSyA-- yaduktaM madIyo heturaprayojaka iti, tat tAvat duruktam, mAsAgnihotrasya naiyamikAgnihotrAt bhede tadvidhivAkyazrutAgnihotranAmasvArasyabhaGgaprasaGgasya vipakSabAdhakasya sattvAt | tathAhi tasya naiyamikAgnihotrAt bhedAbhyupagame, tasmin agnihotrazabdasya mukhyavRttiriSyate amukhyA vA, Adye'pi agnaye hotraM homadravyam asmin iti pratItayogarUpA, vAkyapratItikalpyA rUDhirUpA vA? nAdyaH, dyadyaH naiyamikAgnihotre 'yadagnaye ca prajApataye ca sAyaM juhoti' iti tatprakaraNAmnAtena devatAvidhinA 'agnijyotiH' iti mantravarNena vA agnidevatyatvamavagatam-evaM mAsAgnihotre kenacidupAyena tasyAnavagatatvAt | na dvitIyaH, 'anyAyyazcAnekArthaH' iti nyAyaviruddhAnekArthatvakalpanasya ayuktatvAt | na ca agnidevatyatvAbhAve'pi prAtaragnihotre prANAhutikarmaNi ca pravRttasya tasya anekArthatvakalpanam avarjanIyamiti vAcyam, 'agnihotraM juhoti' iti vAkyotpannasya ekasyaiva naiyamikAgnihotrahomasya 'sAyaM juhoti' 'prAtarjuhoti' iti tatprakaraNAmnAtavAkyAbhyAM sAyaMprAtaH kAlayorabhyAsasya coditatvena sAyaMhomAbhinne prAtarhome'pi agnidevatyatvasaMbhavAt ; prANAhutiSu ArthavAdikasya agnihotrazabdasya anyadIyanAmna eva sataH darzapUrNamAsayorapAmutseke 'eSa vai darzapUrNamAsayoravabhRthaH' ityatra avabhRthazabdasyeva, prastarAdiSu yajamAnazabdasyeva ca aupacArikatvena tAsu vRttyarthaM zaktikalpanAnapekSaNAt | dvitIyapakSe amukhyA vRttiH lakSaNA gauNI vA ? nAdyaH, tanmukhyArthabhUtanaiyamikAgnihotrasaMbandhAnavagamAt | satramadhye kartavyasya mAsAgnihotrasya 'na dadAti' [p.189] na juhoti' 'na pacati' iti somaprayogamadhye paryudastena tena ekakAlatvasaMbandhasyApyabhAvAt | gauNyapi prasiddhasAdRzyamUlA na saMbhavati, mAsAgnihotre tena saha homAntaravyAvRttasya anatyantabahiraGgasyakasyacitsAdRzyasya aprasiddhatvAt | tasmAt lakSaNAto jaghanyatvena mImAMsakairabhyupagatAsu gauNavRttiSvapi kalpyasAdRzyamUlatvena atikliSTagauNavRttirabhyupagantavyA | na ca naiyamikAgnihotrAbhedAGgIkAreNa mukhyavRttisamarthanasaMbhave tadabhyupagamo yuktaH | prAyaNIyanAmnastu yathA prayanti prArabhante anena - iti vyutpattyA dvAdazAhike prathame'hani vRttiH tathA gAvAmayanike'pi vRttirupapadyate ityatikliSTavRttyantarakalpanAdoSAbhAvAt tasya tasmAdbheda iSyate | tasmin prathamazrutasya jyotiSTomanAmnaH pravRttinimittasyAklRptatvAt atikliSTakalpyasAdRzyamUlakagauNavRttikalpanAdoSaparihArAya 'jyotireva purastAt dadhate' iti jyotiSTomaprAthamyapratipAdakavAkyazeSasAmaJjasyAya ca tamya jyotiSTomarUpa prakRtyatirAtrAt bheda eva iSyate | tasmAt madIyahotoruktasAdhyAnaGgIkAre agnihotranAmasvArasyabhaGgaprasaGga iti vipakSabAdhakasattvAt nAprayojakatvazaGkAvakAzaH | tvadIyahetustu upanyastapratikUlatarkaparAhatatvAt na sAdhyasAdhanakSama iti ||
atha prativAdinaH caturthI kakSyA-- prakaraNAntareNa mAsAgnihotravAkyazrutapradhAnabhUtadhAtvarthe naiyamikAgnihotrahomAdbhinnatayA pratIyamAne, tadupasarjanIbhUtatannAma na tadabhedapratyabhijJAnaM janayituM kSamate | tasmAt naiyamikAgnihotre prathamaM gRhItazaktikena nAmnA tadupasthiteravazyaMbhAve, tasya nAmnaH pradhAnabhUtadhAtvarthe naiyamikAgnihotrAdbhinne vRtteravazyaM vaktavyatvena tatra pravRttinimittasyAklRptatayA mukhyavRttyasaMbhavAt-- 'siMho devadattaH' ityatra pradhAnabhUtavizeSyapadasvArasyAnurodhAt siMhazabdasyeva gauNavRttereva kalpanIyatvena sA tadupasthitiH gauNavRttimukhabhUtasAdRzyapratiyogyupasthititvena upayujyate ityevAGgIkartavyam | tatra yaduktaM sAdhyasAdRzyamUlA gauNyatikliSTeti, tanna, iha siddhasAdRzyamUlakagauNavRttyapekSayA tasyA eva jyAyastvAt | tathA hiAdyapakSe yathAsiddhasAdRzyAnuvAdakatvamAtraM sidhyet, na tu kazcidanuSThAne vizeSo labhyate | dvitIyapakSe tu mAsAgnihotrahomasya anupadiSTetikartavyatAkasya naiyamikAgnihotravatkartavyatApareNa [p.190] nAmnA naiyamikAgnihotradharmAtidezakena AkAGkSitetikartavyatAlAbhAt anuSThAnavizeSo labhyate iti anyadIyanAmno'nyatra pravartanaM sAphalyamaznute| tasmAt sAdhyasAdRzyamUlakagauNaprasaGgasya iSTApAdanarUpatvAt tvadIyahetoraprayojakatvatAdavasthyam, madIyahetozca na pratikUlatarkaparAhatiriti ||
atha vAdinaH paJcamI kakSyA-- aprasiddhArthasya nAmno rAjasUyanAmavat AkhyAtaparatantratve'pi prasiddhArthaM nAma na AkhyAtaparatantram | kiM tu tadeva nAmaparatantram, darzapUrNamAsaphalavAkye tathA darzanAt | tathA hi-'yadAgneyo'STAkapAlo'mAvAsyAyAM ca paurNamAsyAM cAcyuto bhavati' 'agnISomIyakamekAdazakapAlaM pUrNamAse prAyaccham' 'upA~zuyAjamantarA yajati' 'aindraM dadhyamAvAsyAyAm' 'aindraM payo'mAvAsyAyAm' iti paurNamAsyamAvAsyAkAlayuktavidhivihitAnAm AgneyAdInAM SaNNAM yAgAnAm, 'samidho yajati' 'tanUnapAtaM yajati' iti akAlayuktavidhivihitAnAM prayAjAdiyAgAnAM ca saMnidhAvAmnAte 'darzapUrNamAsAbhyAM svargakAmo yajeta' iti vAkye zrutena yajinA phalasaMbandhabodhanArthaM saMnihitayAgAnanuvadatA saMnihitatvAvizeSAt AgneyAdInAmiva prayAjAdInAmapi anuvAde prApte, paurNamAsyamAvAsyAkAlasaMbandhini AgneyAdiyAgaSaTke eva lakSaNayA vartamAnadarzapUrNamAsanAmadheyamanuruddhaya tasminneva yAgaSaTke yajirvartate iti prasiddhArthanAmaparatantratvam AkhyAtasya aGgIkRtam | tadvadihApi aGgIkartuM yuktam | tasmAdiha nAmno mukhyArthagrahaNe bAdhakAbhAvAt abAdhitamukhyArthasvArasyabhaGgaprasaGgo doSaH syAdeveti madIyahetuH nAprayojakaH, tvadIyazca hetuH pratikUlatarkaparAhata iti ||
atha prativAdinaH SaSThI kakSyA-- sarvatra guNabhUtaM nAmaiva AkhyAtaparatantram | na kvacidapi AkhyAtaM nAmaparatantram | nAmnazca AkhyAtapAratantryam AkhyAtAnupasthApyArthAsaMsparzena AkhyAtopasthApite eva arthe vartanam | taddarzapUrNamAsanAmnorapi avikalam ; na hi tadupasthApyAnAM SaNNAM yAgAnAM madhye kazcidapi AkhyAtAnupasthApyo'sti, yena tamarthaM [p.191] svayamupasthApya tatra AkhyAtamapi vartayatIti nAmnaH svAtantryaM bhavet | tasmAt nAmnaH sarvatra AkhyAtapAratantryAnapAyAt ihApi prakaraNabhedaniyamitahomAntaratAtparyakAkhyAtaparatantrasya nAmno mukhyArthagrahaNe bAdhakasattvAt mukhyArthasvArasyabhaGgo na doSa iti tvaddheturaprayojakaH, maddhetuzca na pratikUlatarkaparAhata iti ||
atha vAdinaH saptamI kakSyA-- nAmnaH svayaM kaMcidarthaM dhAtvanupasthApanIyamupasthApya tatra dhAtorvartanaM yathA svAtantryam, tathA dhAtUpasthApanIyAnAM madhye keSucideva svazaktibalena saMkocanamapi svAtantryameva | tadvadiha nAmnaH svazaktibalAt AkhyAtasvArasyAnupasthApanIye'rthe tadvartanarUpamapi svAtantryamastu | na hi AkhyAtaparatantratvenAbhyupagatasya nAmnaH kvacidviSaye svAtantryaM kvacit na-iti ardhajaratIyamAzrayituM yuktam | tadAzrayaNe vA vaiparItyena svAtantryaM kimiti nAbhyupagamyeta ? tasmAt nAmasvArasyabhaGgo na yukta iti maddheturnAprayojakaH, tvaddhetuzca pratikUlatarkaparAhata iti ||
atha prativAdinaH aSTamI kakSyA-- vyAvartakasvabhAvAnAM vizeSaNAnAM vizeSyapade svAnvayAnurodhena saMkucadvRttitvApAdanaM vizeSaNatAsvabhAvasiddham, 'nIlamutpalam' 'aruNayA gavA krINAti' ityAdilaukikavaidikaprayogadRSTaM ca iti na tat nAmnaH pradhAnabhUtAkhyAte tadviruddhAcaraNarUpam| ataH tAvatA nAmnAmAkhyAtapAratantryasyAhAniriti atrApi AkhyAtasvArasyAnurodhena nAmasvArasyabhaGgo na doSaH | ataH tvaddheturaprayojakaH, maddhetuzca na pratikUlatarkaparAhata iti ||
atha vAdino navamI kakSyA-- yathA vyAvartakasvabhAvavizeSaNasya svazaktibalena vizeSyapadasaMkocanamaviruddham, evaM nAmnaH svazaktibalAnIte'rthe AkhyAtasya vartanamapyaviruddhameva | na [p.192] hi naiyamikAgnihotravidhisaMnidhirnAsti ityetAvatA mAsAgnihotravAkyasthamAkhyAtaM tadbhinnatvena homamupasthApayatIti vaktuM zakyam, tadbhinnatve zaktyabhAvAt | kiMtu naiyamikAgnihotrasaMnidhyabhAvAt tadabhinnatvena nopasthApayatIti etadvaktuM zakyam | tathA ca AkhyAtasya bhedAbhedayoraudAsInye sati, AkhyAtasAmAnAdhikaraNyena nAmnA svazaktibalAdAkhyAte naiyamikAgnihotravartitvApAdanamaviruddham | AkhyAtasyApi kAMsyabhojinyAyena nAmAnuvartanaM nyAyyam | ataH nAmasvArasyabhaGgo doSa iti maddheturnAprayojakaH, tvaddhetuzca pratikUlatarkaparAhata iti ||
atha dazamI kakSyA-- yaduktam-ihAkhyAtaM bhedAbhedayorudAsInamiti, tadayuktam, tasya bhedapakSapAtitvAt | tathAhi ihAkhyAtasya naiyamikAgnihotraviSayatve, tasya parAmarzaH tadanuvAdena guNavidhAnArthaH, tasyApi vidhAnArtho vA syAt | nAdyaH, prAptakarmAnuvAdena mAsopasadAnantaryarUpaguNadvayavidhAne vAkyabhedaprasaGgAt | na ca 'teSAM dvAdazopasadaH' iti upasadAm 'mAsamagnihotraM juhoti' iti agnihotrahomasya ca pAThakramaprAptaH kramaH 'upasadbhizcaritvA' ityanena anUdyate, mAsaguNamAtraM vidhIyate-iti na vAkyabhedaprasaGga iti vAcyam, aGgAGgibhAvAdinA ekaprayogAntargatau satyAM hi anuSThAnAyApekSitaH kramaH pAThakrameNa kalpyate | na ca puruSArthasyAgnihotrasya kratvarthAnAmupasadAM ca aGgAGgibhAvAdikamasti | atreva upasadAmagnihotrAGgatvasya agnihotrasyopasadaGgatvasya vA vidhAne, tasya mAsaguNasya ca vidhAne vAkyabhedaprasaGgaH | tasmAdanuvAdapakSasyAyuktatvAt vidhipakSa evAzrayaNIyaH | na ca vihitasya punarvidhAnaM saMbhavati - ityAkhyAtaM vidhiyogyaM homAntaramevopasthApayet | ato nAmnaH tatparatantrasya tadviruddhArthasamarpakatvAyogAt tasya mukhyArthasvArasyabhaGgo na doSa iti aprayojakastvaddhetuH maddhetuzca na pratikUlatarkaparAhata iti ||
atha vAdina ekAdazI kakSyA-- vidhipakSa evAzrIyate | vihitasyApi vidhAnaM satrAGgatvasiddhyarthaM yuktam sati hi satraprakaraNamadhye vidhAne tasya satrAGgatvaM sidhyati yathA darzapUrNa[p.193]mAsaprakaraNe vihitAnAM prayAjAdInAM darzapUrNamAsAGgatvam | dRSTaM ca prAptasyApi punarvidhAnaM prayojanAntarArtham 'citriNIrupadadhAti' 'vajriNIrupadadhAti' ityAdau | tathAhi - 'iSTakAbhiragniM cinute' iti vidhinA iSTakAnAmagnayAdhArasthaNDilavizeSanirvartakatvamavagatam | na ca anupahitAbhiriSTakAbhiH sthaNDilaM nirvartayituM zakyateiti sarvAsAmiSTakAnAmupadhAnamarthataH prAptameva | tathApi 'citriNIrupadadhAti' ityAdividhibhiH kAsAMcidiSTakAnAM pratyakSavacanenopadhAnaM vidhIyate | tat tAsAM madhyamacitiprAptirUpaprayojanAntarArtham | evaM hi zrUyate 'yAM vai kAMcidbrAhmaNavatImiSTakAmabhijAnIyAttAM madhyamAyAM citAvupadadhyAt' iti | atra abhipUrvo jAnAtiH pratyakSe vartate | tathA ca yAmiSTakAM pratyakSabrAhmaNavihitAM pazyet, tAmagneH paJcasu citiSu madhyamacitAveva upadadhyAt, na cityantare iti tasyArthaH | evaM ca 'vajriNIrupadadhAti' ityAdipratyakSabrAhmaNavidhAnena vajriNyAdISTakAnAmaniyamena yasyAM kasyAMciccitau upadheyatvena prAptAnAM madhyamacitAveva upadheyAH iti niyamarUpaprayojanAntarArthaM prAptopadhAnapunarvidhAnam | tadvadiha vihitasyApi naiyamikAgnihotrasya punarvidhAnamaGgatvarUpaprayojanAntarArthaM bhavet | tasmAt nAmAkhyAtayorvirodhAbhAvAt nAmno mukhyArthabhaGgaprasaGgo doSa eveti na maddheturaprayojakaH, tvaddhetuzca pratikUlatarkaparAhata iti ||
atha dvAdazI prativAdinaH kakSyA-- naiyamikAgnihotrasya svatantrasya kratvarthatvalAbhArthaM punarvidhAnamiti na yujyate, ekasya svAtantryapAratantryayoH virodhAt | na hi svAtantryeNa puruSArthasAdhane zaktaH tatsAdhanArthamanyAGgabhAvaM bhajate iti yuktam | tasmAnmAsAgnihotravAkyena vidheyaH satrAGgabhUto homaH karmAntaramiti nAmAkhyAtayorvirodhAt AkhyAtaparatantrasya nAmno mukhyArthatvabhaGgo na doSa iti tvaddheturaprayojakaH, maddhetuzca na pratikUlatarkaparAhata iti || [p.194]
atha vAdinaH trayodazI kakSyA-- kiMcitphalasAdhane svayaM pradhAnabhUtasyApi phalAntarasAdhane anyAGgatAyAM na tAvadarthavirodhaH, phalabhedenobhayopapatteramAtyAdiSu dRSTatvAt | nApi tadubhayabodhakazabdavirodhaH, ekasyaiva vAkyasya aGgatvapradhAnatvobhayabodhakatve hi virodhaH syAt | iha ca naiyamikAgnihotraprakaraNapaThitavAkyena prAdhAnyam satraprakaraNapaThitenAGgatvamiti viniyojakavAkyabhedAt na zabdavirodho'pi | tasmAt nAmAkhyAtayorvirodhAbhAvAt nAmno mukhyArthabhaGgo doSa eveti na maddheturaprayojakaH, tvaddhetuzca pratikUlatarkaparAhata iti ||
atha prativAdinazcaturdazI kakSyA-- yadvaidikaM karma pradhAnam, na tadanyasya karmaNo'GgamityavyabhicaritasahacAradarzanena niyamo'vadhRtaH | na cetthamavadhRtasya niyamasya bAdhanaM yuktam | ataH satrAGgatayA vidheyahomo naiyamikAgnihotrAdbhinna iti nAmAkhyAtayorvirodhAt nAmno mukhyArthabhaGgo na doSa iti tvaddheturaprayojakaH, maddhetuzca na pratikUlatarkaparAhata iti ||
atha vAdinaH paJcadazI kakSyA-- yatpradhAnaM na tatkarmAntarAGgam iti nAyaM niyamo'sti, prastarapraharaNe vyabhicArAt | tathA hi darzapUrNamAsaprakaraNe 'sUktavAkena prastaraM praharati' iti zrUyate prastarapraharaNarUpaM karma | tasya prakaraNAmnAnasiddhaM darzapUrNamAsAGgatvaM tAvannirvivAdam | AyurAdiphalasAdhanatvarUpaM prAdhAnyamapyasti | tatkaraNatvena zrute hi sUktavAkamantre phalAnyAyurAdIni saMkIrtyante | mantrasya ca kriyAkaraNatvaM tadanuSThAnopayogitatsmaraNasaMpAdanadvArA | na ca AyurAdikIrtanasya sAkSAtpraharaNAdikriyAsmaraNahetutvamasti | ataH kIrtyamAnAnAmAyurAdInAM tayA kriyayA saMbandhaH kalpanIyaH | tathA sati hi kriyAsaMbandhikIrtanasya saMbandhismaraNadvArA kriyA[p.195]smArakatvamupapannaM bhavati | saMbandhazca AzAsyamAnAnAmAyurAdInAM kriyayA saha kalpyamAno yogyatayA phalaphalibhAvarUpa eva paryavasyati | ataH zrutakRtsnasUktavAkamantrakaraNakatvAnyathAnupapattisiddham AyurAdiphalasAdhanatvarUpaM prAdhAnyamavazyamabhyupagantavyamiti sustho vyabhicAraH ||
atha prativAdinaH SoDazI kakSyA-- yatpradhAnaM na tatkarmAntarAGgamiti niyame prAdhAnyaM phalasAdhanatvamAtrarUpaM na vivakSitam | kiMtu yatkarma prati aGgatvamiSyate, tatprayogAdbahiranuSThAnena phalasAdhanatvam | na caitat prastarapraharaNe asti | tasya darzapUrNamAsaprayogAdbahiranuSThAne niSphalatvAt | tasmAt nAstyuktavyAptau vyabhicAra iti ||
atha vAdinaH saptadazI kakSyA-- neyaM vyAptirasti, vipakSe bAdhakatarkAbhAvAt | yathA rAjasUyAntargatAyA eva aveSTeH pavitrAdikSatrasya dhRtyantAnekayAgAntarasAhityena svArAjyaphalasAdhanatve'pi 'etayA annAdyakAmaM yAjayet' iti vidhibalena phalAntarasAdhanatvam, evaM satrAGgatayA taddvArA satraphalopayogino'pi agnihotrahomasya satraprayogAdbahiranuSThIyamAnasya svatantraphalasAdhanatvopapatteH | yadi ca 'etayA' iti saMnihitaparAmarzisarvanAmabalAt rAjasUyaprakaraNAmnAtAyA eva aveSTeH svAtantryeNa phalAntarasAdhanatvamapi iSyate; tarhi svatantraphalasAdhanasyaivAgnihotrasya tatparAmarzakAgnihotranAmabalAt satrAGgatvamapi iSyatAm, vizeSAbhAvAditi ||
atha prativAdino'STAdazI kakSyA-- uktavyAptau asiddho vipakSabAdhakatarkAbhAvaH | prakaraNAntare svatantraphalasAdhakatayA vihitasya anyasminprakaraNe karmAntarAGgatvena vidhyaGgIkAre vAkyabhedaprasaGgasya vipakSabAdhakasya jAgarUkatvAt | mAsAgnihotravAkyaM naiyamikAgnihotrasyaiva [p.196] satre vidhAnaM karoti - ityabhyupagame asaMnihitanaiyamikAgnihotrapratyabhijJApanam, pratyabhijJApitasya satre vidhAnaM ca ityubhayamapyatra vacanena saMpAdanIyam | ekaM cAtra vacanam | na caikaM vacanaM vyApAradvayAviSTaM bhavitumarhati | na ca pratyabhijJApanaM padamAtrakRtyam, vidhAnaM vAkyakRtyamiti avAkyabhedaH zaGkanIyaH, padaM hi svazaktyA naiyamikAgnihotraM smArayati param ; na tu dhAtvarthe tadabhedaM pratyabhijJApayati; 'padamabhyadhikAbhAvAtsmArakAnna viziSyate' iti nyAyAt; abhedabodhanaM tu agnihotranAmajuhotisamabhivyAhArAtmakasya vAkyasya kRtyamiti syAdeva vAkyabhedaH | prastarapraharaNasya aGgasya sataH tatprakaraNAmnAtena vAkyena vidhAnamAtraM kriyate, phalasaMbandhabodhanaM tu mantraliGgakRtyamiti na vAkyabhedaprasaGgaH | 'yavAgvAgnihotraM juhoti' ityatrApi 'danA juhoti' ityatreva saMnidhilabhyaM pratyabhijJApanam, na tu nAmadheyalabhyam | 'etayA annAdyakAmaM yAjayet' ityatrApi tathaiva | etena vAkyAntaragatatvena bahiraGgasaMnidhirapi pratyabhijJApakazcet, ekavAkyagatatvenAntaraGga nAma kathaM na pratyabhijJApakam iti zaGkApi nirastA, saMnidheH, pratyabhijJApakatve tatkalpitAvAkyasya zrutavAkyato bhinnatayA tatra vAkyabhedadoSAprasaGgAt | iha nAmnaH pratyabhijJApakatve nAmamAtrasya tatrAsAmarthyena vidhAyakavAkyasyaiva tatrApi vyApArasya vaktavyatayA vAkyabhedasyAvarjanIyatvAditi ||
atha ekonaviMzatitamI vAdinaH kakSyA-- vidhivyApArabhedenaiva vAkyabhedo bhavati, na tu pratyabhijJApanavidhivyApArAbhyAm | anyathA gRhamedhIye 'AjyabhAgau yajati' iti vidhAvapi vAkyabhedaH prasajyate | tathA hi cAturmAsyeSu AmnAyate 'marudbhyo gRhamedhibhyaH sarvAsAM dugdhe sAyaM carum' iti | tatsaMnidhau AmnAyate 'AjyabhAgau yajati' iti | gRhamedhIyeSTau darzapUrNamAsato'tidezAtsaMbhavatprAptikayoH AjyabhAgayoH punarvidhAnaM kriyate, prAkRtAvAjyabhAgAveva yathA syAtAm, prAkRtAni prayAjAdInyaGgAntarANi yathA na syuH-ityetadartham | tatra AjyabhAganAmopasthApitayoH prAkRtayorAjyabhAgayoH pratyabhijJApanaM tayorgRhamedhIye vidhAnaM ca ekasminvAkye abhyupagamyate | tadvadihApi syAt | na ca tena vAkyena vidhAnamAtraM kriyate, na pratyabhijJAnam; 'darzapUrNamAsa[p.197]vatkuryAt' - ityatidezavAkyarUpeNa codakena viparivartamAnayorAjyabhAgayoH vAkyena pratyabhijJApanasya akaraNIyatvAt, iha tu na tathA pratyabhijJApakamanyadastIti vAcyam; yataH codakAt AjyabhAgaprApyanantaram 'AjyabhAgau yajati' iti vAkyaM na pravartate, prAptayorvidhAnasyAnapekSitatvena vAkyasya AjyabhAgavyatiriktAGgAntaraniSedhArthatvena parisaMkhyArUpatvakalpanAprasaGgAt | na ca iSTApattiH, AjyabhAgavidhirUpasvArthatyAgaH, prayAjAdiniSedharUpaparArthaparigrahaH, codakaprAptaprayAjAdibAdhazca - iti prAptaparisaMkhyAyAM doSatrayaprasaGgAt | kiM tu prAgeva codakakalpanAt gRhamedhIyasya itikartavyatArUpavyApArApekSasya pratyakSazrutaklRptopakArAjyabhAgavyApArasaMyojanayA nirAkAGgatApAdanena tadAkAGgAmUlacodakakalpanApratibandhadvArA gRhamedhIyasya prakRtirAhityarUpApUrvatAsiddhyartham idamAjyabhAgavyApAravidhAnaM pravartate | ataH codakakRtaviparivRttyasiddheH prAkRtAjyabhAgapratyabhijJApanamapi 'AjyabhAgau yajati' ityata eva labhyam | ataH tadabhAvena avAkyabhedo na samarthayituM zakyaHiti vipakSabAdhakatarkAbhAvAt noktA vyAptirastIti ||
atha prativAdino viMzatitamI kakSyA-- gRhamedhIyAjyabhAgavAkyena pratyabhijJApanamAtraM kriyate na vidhAnam; vidhAnaM tu prakaraNakalpyavAkyAntarakRtyamiti upapadyate | evameva hi vArtikakRtA prakaraNAntarAdhikaraNe gavAmayanAdigatAni 'jyotiH' 'AyuH' ityAdIni ahaHklRptivacanAni ekAhakraturUpANAmeva somayAgAnAM satraikadezatayA vidhAnArthaM pratyabhijJApakAni, na tu taddharmakarmAntaraparANi - iti svAbhimatasiddhAntaM vyavasthApayatA pratyabhijJApanamAtram 'jyotirgaurAyuH' ityAdivacanakRtyam, satraikadezatayA phale vidhAnaM tu 'yaM kAmaM kAmayate tametenApnoti' iti phalavacanakRtyam iti avAkyabhedaH samarthitaH | uktaM hi vArtike - 'ekena vacanena paryupasthApya apareNa phale vidhIyate' iti | na caivaM mAsAgnihotravAkyena pratyabhijJApanam, prakaraNakalpitavAkyena satrAGgatayA vidhAnamiti zaGkyam mAsazrutyavaiyarthyAya pratyabhijJApakavAkya eva mAsamuddizya homavidhAnasyApi aGgIkartavyatvAt | ataH vAkyabhedaprasaGga iti vipakSabAdhakatarkasattvAt uktavyAptiH sudRDheti || [p.198]
atha vAdina ekaviMzatitamI kakSyA-- gAvAmayanikAhaHklRptivAkyadRSTAntamavalambya gRhamedhIyavAkyena pratyabhijJApanamAtraM kriyate na vidhAnam - ityavAkyabhedasamarthanamayuktam, teSu vAkyeSu vArtikakRtA 'bhidyate bhAvanAmAtraM dhAtvartho nAyajizruteH' iti vidheyabhAvanAbhedasya tadvidhyartham 'jyotiH kAryam' 'gauH kAryaH' ityAdirUpeNa bhAvanAvidhyarthapadAdhyAhArasya ca aGgIkRtatayA teSAM pratyabhijJApanamAtraM kRtyamiti vaktumazakyatvAt | vArtikakRtA tadanantaramudAhRta eva 'vAruNyA niSkAsena tuSaizcAvabhRthaM yanti' iti vAkyAntare tuSaniSkAsazrutyavaiyarthyAya tadubhayaviziSTakarmavidhAnasyApi aGgIkRtatvena tasya saumikAvabhRthapratyabhijJApanamAtraM kRtyamiti vaktumazakyatvAcca | tasmAt jyotirAdidRSTAntena gRhamedhIyavAkyasya pratyabhijJApanamAtraparatvopapAdanena avAkyabhedaH samarthayituM na zakyate | kiMtu taddRSTAntena pratyabhijJApanaM vidhAnaM ca ityubhayamapi gRhamedhIyavAkye aGgIkaraNIyamiti, tatra vidhAnadvayAbhAvenaiva avAkyabhedaH samarthanIyaH | tadvat mAsAgnihotrAdivAkyeSvapi avAkyabhedasamarthanamupapadyate iti vipakSe bAdhakatarkAbhAvAt tvaduktavyAptiH aprayojiketi ||
atha dvAviMzatitamI prativAdinaH kakSyA-- vidheyabhede satyeva vAkyabheda iti paribhASAmAtram; kiMtu arthabhedamAtre vAkyabhedaniyamaH | 'caitro gacchati' 'maitraH pazyati' ityAdiSu avidhipareSvapi vAkyeSu bhedadarzanAt | tatazca mAsAgnihotravAkye pratyabhijJApyavidheyarUpArthadvayAGgIkAre vAkyabhedaprasaGgo na zakyanivAraNa iti dRDho vipakSe bAdhakatarkaH | gRhamedhIyavAkyena tu pratyabhijJApanamAtraM kriyate | vidhAnaM tu prakaraNakalpyavAkyeneti na vAkyabhedaH prasajyate | na hi tatra bhAvanAbhedo'sti, sauryAdiSu codakaprAptayorivagRhamedhIye pratyakSavacanaprAptayorapi AjyabhAgayoH tadbhedasya anabhyupagantavyatvAt| na vA guNazravaNamasti | yattu jyotirAdidRSTAntAsiddheH taddRSTAntena pratyabhijJApanaparatAmupapAdya avAkyabhedasamarthanamazakyamityuktam, tadabhiprAyAnavabodhavilasitam - na hi gAvAmayanikajyotirAdInAmekAhajyotirAdyabhedo vAruNapraghAsikAvabhRthasya [p.199] saumikAvabhRthAbhedazca vArtikakArAbhimata iti tadabhimataddRSTAntApAdanaM kRtam; vArtikakRtaiva TupTIkAyAm 'tathAvabhRthaH somAt' iti sAptamikAdhikaraNe saumikAvabhRthAdvAruNapraghAsikAvabhRthasya bhedam, 'ekAdazinIvat tryanIkA parivRttiH syAt' iti dAzamikAdhikaraNe jyotirAdyekAhakratubhyo gAvAmayanikajyotirAdInAM bhedaM ca prakaraNAntarAdhikaraNanyAyasiddhaM siddhavatkRtya, tebhyaH teSu nAmnA dharmAtidezasya vyavahRtatvAt | abhede svata eva dharmANAM prAptyA atidezAnapekSaNAt | na vA anumAna iva dRSTAntasadbhAvAyAttamabhimatArthasamarthanamiti prakRtArthasiddhau avazyApekSitaddRSTAntopAdAnaM kRtam, dRSTAntAbhAve'pi vidheyaguNabhAvanAzravaNAbhAvenaiva pratyabhijJApanamAtraM gRhamedhIyAdivAkyakRtyamiti upapAdayituM zakyatvAt | kiMtu yatra pratyabhijJApanamAtraM vAkyakRtyamityabhyupagame anupapattirasti, tatrApi tadabhyupagamaH prauDhivAdena kRto dRzyate; saH yatrAnupapattirnAsti gRhamedhIyAdivAkye, tatra pAramArthikasya tadabhyupagamasya jJApakaH ityabhipretya| tasmAnna ko'pi virodha iti ||
atha vAdinaH trayoviMzatitamI kakSyA-- yaduktam - vidhAnabhede satyeva vAkyabhedaH ityeva nAsti niyama iti, tanniyamabhaJjanamanuktopAlambhanam | na hi tathA mayA niyama uktaH | kiMtu yathA vidhAnabhede vAkyabhedo bhavati, naivaM vidhAnapratyabhijJAbhyAM saMbhavati ityetAvaduktam | tatra nAsti kazcana doSaH | na ca tatrApi pratyabhijJApanaM vidhAnamapIti vyApArabhedena vAkyabhedaH zaGkanIyaH, prasiddhAbhedabodhanaM hi pratyabhijJApanamucyate; na tvaprasiddhAbhedabodhanam | tatazca yathA 'raktaH paTo bhavati' ityatra pratyakSAdiprasiddharaktimaguNaviziSTAbhedavati paTe raktapadasamAnAdhikaraNena paTapadena pratyabhijJApyamAne tasmin bhavanakriyAsaMbandhamAtraM vAkyena bodhyate, yathA vA 'agnaye dAtre puroDAzamaSTAkapAlaM nirvapet' ityabhyudayeSTivAkye mantravarNaprasiddhadAtRtvaguNaviziSTAbhedavati agnau dAtRpadasamAnAdhikaraNenAgnipadena pratyabhijJApyamAne tasmin haviH saMbandhamAtraM vAkyena bodhyate, tatra ubhayatrApi pratyabhijJAnAMze'nuvAdarUpatvAt na vAkyabhedaH tathA atrApi naiyamikAgnihotraprakaraNagatotpattivAkyAdiprasiddhAgnihotranAmaviziSTAbhedavati home nAmasamAnAdhikaraNena dhAtunA pratyabhijJApyamAne tasya vidhisaMbandhamAtraM [p.200] vAkyena bodhyate | ato nAtra vAkyabhedazaGkA prasaraM labhate | na ca tathApi gRhamedhIyavAkyadRSTAntena pratyabhijJApanavidhAnAbhyAM na vAkyabhedaH prasajyate iti samarthanamanupapannam, tasya AjyabhAgapratyabhijJApanamAtraparatvopapatteriti vAcyam; vidhyabhAve samIhitasAdhanatvApratItyA samIhitavizeSAkAGgAviraheNa anyonyAkAGgAlakSaNaprakaraNAnutthityA AjyabhAgayoH tanmUlakagRhamedhIyAGgatvakalpanAsiddhiprasaGgAt | ata evAhurAcAryAH- 'na cAvihitamaGgaM bhavati' iti | yattu gAvAmayanikajyotirAdidRSTAntAsiddhivacanam abhiprAyAnavabodhavilasitam iti vacanam, tadeva vArtikagranthAnavabodhavilasitam, vArtike hi 'bhidyate bhAvanAmAtraM dhAtvartho nAyajizruteH' iti gAvAmayanikajyotirAdiSu ekAhajyotirAdibhyo vAruNapraghAsikAvabhRthe saumikAvabhRthAcca bhAvanAmAtrakRta eva bhedaH na dhAtvarthakRtabhedaH ityayameva pakSaH pAramArthikatvenopanyastaH | na tatra prauDhivAdAzrayaNaM yuktam | dazamasaptamAdhikaraNayoratidezavacanaM tu bhAvanAbhedAdupapadyate | apUrvabhAvanayA khalu itikartavyatAnvayaH na dhAtvarthena | evameva mAsAgnihotradarzapUrNamAsAdiSu naiyamikAgnihotradarzapUrNamAsAdibhyo dharmAtidezasamarthanamapi 'uktaM kriyAbhidhAnaM tacchutAvanyatra vidhipradezaH syAt' iti sAptamikAdhikaraNe kRtaM bhAvanAbhedAdeva | na ca atidezavacanasya bhAvanAbhedenopapattAvapi dhAtvarthAbhede agnihotrAdinAmnAM mAsAgnihotrAdiSu gauNatvokteH nopapattiH, nAmnAM dhAtvarthagocaratvAditi zaGkyam; nityAgnihotrAdiSviva mAsAgnihotrAdiSu prAkaraNikadevatAbhidhAnAdibhiH agnihotrAdinAmnAM pravRttinimittAbhivyaktirnAsti, kiMtu naiyamikAgnihotrAdyabhedAnusaMdhAnena taddharmAtidezAnusaMdhAnena ca pravRttinimittasattvamavagantavyamiti asphuTazaktikatvAbhiprAyAyA gauNatvokteH bhASyAdidRSTAyAH gauNatvAt | 'pratyakSAdguNasaMyogAt kriyAbhidhAnaM syAt tadabhAve'prasiddhaM syAt' iti 'uktaM kriyAbhidhAnam' ityadhikaraNaguNasUtreNaiva tathAbhiprAyasya sphuTIkaraNAt | kiMca 'tathAvabhRthaH somAt' iti sAptamikAdhikaraNe varuNapraghAseSu vidhIyamAno'vabhRthaH saumikAvabhRtha eva vivakSitaH, na tu tato'nyaH taddharmadharmakaH iti avabhRthazabdasya prathamAntatvenAvasIyate | vAruNapraghAsike avabhRthanAmakarmaNi saumikAvabhRthAt dharmagrahaNam ityarthavivakSAyAM hi avabhRthazabde paJcamyA bhAvyam | evamapi tatrAdhikaraNe bhASyAdiSu yastasmin avabhRthazabdasya gauNatvavyavahAraH tasyAbhiprAyaH kathaMciditthameva varNanIyaH | nanu gAvA[p.201]mayanikajyotirAdInAM vAruNapraghAsikAvabhRthasya ca prasiddhAbhedo'stu nAma yajyazravaNAt iha mAsAgnihotramAsadarzapUrNamAsAdiSu juhotiyajatizravaNe sati kathamabhedaH syAditi cet na, prakaraNAntaragatasyApyAkhyAtasya dhAtvarthabhedAdAvaudAsInyasya vyavasthApitatvena nAmnA prApyamANasya prasiddhAbhedasya anivAraNAt | 'bhidyate bhAvanAmAtraM dhAtvartho nAyajizruteH' ityasya ayaM bhAvaH yajizravaNe hi prakaraNAntarAt tadarthabhedaH zaGkyetApi, so'pyatra nAsti | ata eva hi prakaraNAntarAdhikaraNavArtika eva 'nAmnA dhAtvarthamAtraM ca saMnidhApyeta zaktitaH | bhAvanA tvanupasthAnAdbhidyamAnA na vAryate sAmAnAdhikaraNyena hi sarvANi nAmadheyAni dhAtvarthAnAM nAmadheyatvenAvasthitAni na bhAvanAnAm | tena bhAvanAnyatvAnanyatvaparIkSAvelAyAM satyapi dhAtvarthaikatve bhAvanA tAvat ananyapramANatvAt AkhyAtapratyayAntareNAnupanIyamAnA na kathaMcit pratyabhijJApyate ityupapannaM 'prayojanAnyatvam' iti zlokatadvyAkhyAnAbhyAM mAsAgnihotrAdiSu bhAvanAbhedamAtram, na dhAtvarthabhedaH, nAmnA tatpratyabhijJAnAditi dhAtvarthabhede'pi bhAvanAbhedamAtreNa prakaraNAntarasya karmabhede prAmANyamupapannam, bhAvanAnyatvAnanyatvaparIkSAyA eva adhyAyaprameyatvAditi, 'prakaraNAntare prayojanAnyatvam' iti sUtramapi bhAvanAbhedaevArthe samaJjasam, prayojanazabdasya pradhAnavAkyArthabhUtabhAvanAparatva eva svArasyAditi ca spaSTamuktam | tasminnadhikaraNe vArtikakArANAM dhAtvarthasyApi bhedamAzritya pakSAntaropanyAsastu bhAvanAbhedadRDhIkaraNArthaH prauDhivAdaH | yadi naiyamikAgnihotrasya vihitasya punarvidhAnamatra na saMbhavati, tadA vidheyaguNavAcyupapadAzravaNena guNasaMkrAntirAhityAt bhAvArthAdhikaraNanyAyataH svArasyena dhAtvarthe niviSTo vidhiH karmAntarameva vidadhyAt, ataH pradhAnabhUtAkhyAtaparatantranAmApi svopasthApitamukhyArthasAdRzyena karmAntara eva varteta iti zaGkyeta | saMbhavati tu vihitasyApi naiyamikAgnihotrasya satrAGgatvasiddhyarthaM punarvidhAnam iti samarthitameva | evaM prakaraNAntarAdhikaraNanyAyasya bhAvanAbhedamAtraniviSTatvamabhipretyaiva zaMkarabhagavatpAdaiH zrIkaNThAcAryaiH vivaraNAcAryaprabhRtibhizca puruSArthasyaiva sautrAmaNibRhaspatisavAde agnicayanavAjapeyAdiprakaraNAntareSu tattatkarmAGgatayA vidhAnaM tatra tatra dRSTAntatayA vyavahRtam | guruNA ca nibandhanaTIkAyAm agnicayanavAjapeyAGgayoH sautrAmaNibRhaspatisavayoH karmAntaratvaM vyavahRtavatA vivaraNaTIkAyAM prasiddhasautrAmaNyAdi[p.202]rUpatvamevoktam | tasmAt mAsAgnihotrAdiSu prakaraNAntarAt karmabhedaH iti mImAMsakavyavahAre karmazabdaM dhAtvarthaikAntikamabhimanyamAnAnAM naiyamikAgnihotrAt mAsAgnihotraM bhinnamiti gatAnugatikaprasiddhimavalambamAnAnAM ca keSAMcit jaranmImAMsakAnAM prakaraNAntarAdhikaraNavArtikAdihRdayAnabhijJAnAM matamayuktameva || iti mAsAgnihotravAdaH ||1||

[p.202] hisidhAtvarthavAdaH || yathA mAsAgnihotramagnihotrapadavAcyam, evaM mImAMsakAnAM prakaraNAntarAdhItaM kratvarthapazusaMjJapanaM hisidhAtuvAcyam; tathApi 'na hiMsyAt' iti niSedhaviSayo na bhavati - iti siddhAntasthitiH || atra vAdinAM bahuvidhAH panthAnaH prAbhAkarAstAvat - rAgaprayuktA hiMsA niSedhaviSayaH | kratvarthasaMjJapane tu na rAgataH pravRttiH | kiMtu vidhitaH iti vyavasthAM kalpayanti | kaumArilanaiyAyikAdayastu rAgaM vinA pravRttyasaMbhavAt neyaM vyavasthA yujyate; kiMtu vidhisaMspRSTasya niSedhaviSayatAyA viruddhatvena AhavanIyAdividhiH padahomamiva hiMsAniSedhaH kratvarthasaMjJapanaM parihRtya pravartate iti vyavasthA varNanIyA - ityAhuH | sAMkhyAstu na kenApi prakAreNa vyavasthA varNanIyA | kratvarthasaMjJapane vidhyadhInasya iSTasAdhanatvasya niSedhAdhInasyAniSTasAdhanatvasya ca samAvezasaMbhavAt | pazuvadhaprayuktasya alpasyAniSTasya vittavyayAyAsaduHkhasyeva abhyupagamena kratusAdhyamahAphalArthatayA pravRtterupapannatvena vidhiniSedhAdhInapravRttinivRttisamAvezadoSasya anavakAzatvAt ityAhuH | rAmAnujamatAnuvartinastu sAMkhyavat kratvarthasaMjJapanasya niSedhaviSayatvamanaGgIkurvANAH tatsaMmatavidhiniSedhavirodhanirAkaraNopAyamapi anAlocayantaH zrUyate hi 'hiraNyazarIra UrdhvaM svargameti' 'na vA u etanmriyase na riSyasi' ti pazuhiMsAyA ahiMsAtvamiti zrIkaNThAcAryasUktApAtapratipannamarthamavalambamAnAH kratvarthasaMjJapanaM hiMsaiva na bhavati ityabhyupagamena tasya niSedhaviSayatvaprasaktiM pariharanti | idameva mataM pUrvapakSatayA [p.203] nivezya zrIkaNThAcAryANAmAzayagataM kaumArilodIritaM siddhAntatayA parigRhya kakSyAvizeSAnuSaGgikasAMkhyaprAbhAkara matanirAkaraNAntarbhAvA vAdakathA prastUyate | tatra kratvarthapazusaMjJapanaM hisidhAtuvAcyam, mAraNatvAt, yanmAraNaM tat hisidhAtuvAcyaM; yathA zatrumAraNam iti prathamA kakSyA || aprayojakamidamanumAnam, krodhAdikRtasya zarIrAvayavadAhacchedAdeH nigrahatve'pi vraNazAntyarthaM kRtasya tasyAnugraharUpatvena anigrahatvavat - kratvarthAnyamAraNasya hisidhAtuvAcyatve'pi 'na vA u etanmriyase na riSyasi devA~ ideSi pathibhiH sugebhiryatra yanti sukRto nApi duSkRtaH tatra tvA devaH savitA dadhAtu' iti zrutisiddhapazusvargaparyavasAyitayA pazvanugraharUpatvena kratvarthasya tasya tadavAcyatvopapatteH | tasmAt kratvarthapazumAraNaM na hisidhAtuvAcyam, kratvarthatvAt, yatkratvarthaM tanna hisidhAtuvAcyam - yathA prokSaNam iti dvitIyA kakSyA || atha tRtIyA-- madIyAnumAnaM tAvannAprayojakam, kratvarthamAraNe'pi hisidhAtupravRttinimittasya satvena tasya tadvAcyatvAbhAvAyogAt | maraNoddezyakamaraNaphalopahitatatkAraNakriyAtvaM hi tasya pravRttinimittam | maraNakAraNatvamAtrasya yogyatAsAdhAraNasya zatrumaraNoddezyakalakSyApatitAyudhakSepAdau maraNaphalakatvamAtrasya kAraNakAraNarUpAnyathAsiddhasAdhAraNasya paGkapatitagavAdimaraNaphalakataTAkanirmANAdau ca atiprasaktatve'pi viziSTasya anatiprasaktatvAt | na ca kratvarthapazumAraNasya hiMsAtve tadanugrahatvAnupapattiH, vraNazAntyarthakRtadAhachedAdeH pravRttinimittAnusAreNa dAhachedAdirUpatve'pyanugrahatvavat - kratvarthapazumAraNasya tadanusAreNa hiMsAtve'pyanugrahatvopapatteH | tasmAnnAprayojakaM madIyamanumAnam | tvadIyamevAnumAnamaprayojakam, klRptapravRttinimittAdhInasya tattaddhAtvarthatvasya kratvarthatve satyapyupapatteriti || [p.204]
atha caturthI kakSyA-- tvadanumAnaM tAvadaprayojakameva, tvaduktamAtrasya hisidhAtupravRttinimittatvAbhyupagame kratvarthapazumAraNasya 'na hiMsyAtsarvA bhUtAni' iti niSedhaviSayatvApattyA tatpravRttinimitte kratvarthapazumAraNavyAvartakavizeSaNasya nivezanIyatayA tAvanmAtrasya tatpravRttinimittatvAsiddheH | na ca 'na hiMsyAt' iti niSedho rAgaprayuktahiMsAviSayatvAt vidhiprayuktaM kratvarthasaMjJapanaM na spRzati, kAmAdhikAre karaNAMze rAgataH pravRttiH, aGgeSu vaidhIti vizeSAbhyupagamAt; tathA ca tadvyAvartakavizeSaNaM hiMsApadapravRttinimitte na nivezanIyamiti vAcyam; tasya vidhiprayuktAnuSThAnatve'pi hiMsAtvAbhyupagame 'na hiMsyAt' iti vAkye tena rUpeNa kroDIkRtasya tasya niSedhyatAyA anivAryatvAt | setikartavyatAkasya phalakaraNatvena phalasiddhimicchatastadarthakaraNe iva aGgeSvapi rAgAvazyaMbhAvena teSvapi rAgata eva pravRttyupapattyA ardhajaratIyakalpanAyogAcca | bhagavatA jaimininA tRtIyAdhyAye 'SaTtriMzatprakramA prAcI caturviMzatiragreNa triMzajjaghanena' iti vihitA saumikI mahAvediH kevalapradhAnArthA, 'iyati zakSyAmahe' iti tadvAkyazeSe etAvati bhUbhAge kartuM zakSyAmaha ityarthake tumunpratyayArthacikIrSAsaMyogAt | na tvaGgapradhAnArthA, aGgAnAmIpsitaphalasAdhanatvAbhAvenAcikIrSitatvAt ityevam 'cikIrSayA ca saMyogAt' iti sUtreNa pUrvapakSaM kRtvA, 'tadyukte tu phalazrutiH tasmAtsarvacikIrSA syAt' iti guNasUtreNa sakalAGgayukte phalazruteH sarvatra pradhAne'GgakalApe ca cikIrSA astIti aGgAnAmapi cikIrSitatvAvizeSAdaGgapradhAnArthA mahAvediriti siddhAntayatA aGgeSvapi rAgata eva pravRtteH sthApitatvAcca | SaSThAdhyAye'pi 'sAmAnyaM taccikIrSA hi' 'tA nityaM taccikIrSA hi' ityAdisUtragataizcikIrSAzabdairaGgeSu rAgataH pravRttervyavahRtatvAcca | nanu aGgeSvicchA nAstIti nocyate, icchAM vinA pravRttyayogAt; kiMtu karaNAMze phalasiddhyarthatvena rAgazabdAbhilapyaphalecchAdhInecchayA pravRttiH; kAmAdhikAreSu prathamaparAmRSTasvargAdiphalakaraNabhAvasya yAgasya vidhizabdAbhilapyaniyogAkhyakAryaviSayatayA anvaye sati viSayAvacchinnatayA tasya liGvAcyasya kAryasya pratItiriti kAryapratIteH prAgeva yAge phalakaraNatvasya pratItatayA tatra phalarAgAdhInecchayA pravRttyaucityAt aGgAnAM prathamaM kAryeNa anvayaH pazcAtkAryadvArA [p.205] phalopakArakatvAvagatiriti teSu prathamAnvitakAryasaMpAdanecchayA pravRttyaucityAcceti cet; maivam, cirAtIte yAge svargakaraNatvaghaTakasya niyogAkhyakAryasya pratIteH prAgyAge svargakaraNatvapratItyasaMbhavAt| matAntareSviva gurumate'pi dvAravyatirekanizcayAbhAvena yogyatAsaMdehamAtrAtkaraNatvAvagatisamarthane, tathaiva sAkSAtkRtyasAdhyasyApi niyogasya kRtisAdhyatAsaMghaTakayAgAditadavacchedakarUpaviSayapratItiM vinApi pratItisaMbhavena yAgAdirUpaviSayAvacchinnatayaiva kAryapratItiriti prakriyAyA anAzrayaNIyatvAt | tadAzrayaNAvazyaMbhAve'pi svarUpeNaiva yAgAderviSayatayA kAryAvacchedakatvasaMbhavena viSayatApratIteH pUrvaM yAge svargakaraNatvaparAmarzasyAnapekSaNAt | svargakAmakRtisAdhyatvaM svargakaraNatvamantareNa na saMbhavatIti cet, mAbhUt, tAvatA svargakaraNatvAvagatimantareNa zabdato niyogasya svargakAmakRtisAdhyatvAvagatau kAnupapattiH | na hi vahnimantareNa dhUmo na saMbhavatItyetAvatA parvate dhUmo'stIti zabdataH tatra dhUmAvagatirvahvayavagamamantareNa na saMbhavati | nanu gurumataprakriyayA ghaTayitumazakyApi karaNAMze rAgataH pravRttiraGgeSu vaidhIti maryAdA kumArilamate syAdeva, tanmate prathamaM yAgasya svargakaraNatvapratItiH tannirvAhArthamapUrvakalpanA kalpitApUrvavAcakazabdAdhyAhAreNa 'yAgena apUrvaM kRtvA svargaM bhAvayet' iti vAkyArthapratItiH, pazcAdapUrvabhAvanetikartavyatArUpeNa aGgAnAmapUrvAnvaya iti svIkArAditi cet, satyam, apUrvArthatayA aGgAnuSThAne'pi aGgacikIrSAyAH phalarAgAdhInatvaM na hIyate | na hi tanmate vidhisiddhireva vidheH phalamiti tadarthamaGgAnuSThAnam | kiMtvapUrvaM vinA svargAdiphalaM na sidhyatIti paraMparayA phalarAgAdhInameva tadanuSThAnam | tasmAtkratvarthapazusaMjJapanasya hiMsAtvAbhyupagame 'na hiMsyAt' iti niSedhaviSayatvamanivAryamiti tadvyAvartakavizeSaNAvazyaMbhAvAt tvadanumAnaM tAvadaprayojakameva | ata eva madanumAnaM nAprayojakam, kratvarthamAraNasya hisidhAtvarthatve tasya niSedhaprasaGgasya vipakSe bAdhakasya sattvAditi || atha paJcamI-- lAghavAnmaduktamAtraM hisidhAtupravRttinimittam | kratvarthamAraNasya hiMsAtve'pi 'na hiMsyAt' iti niSedho na prasajyate | sa hi niSedhaH zIghrapratItika[p.206]puruSArthahiMsAvyAvartanena nirvRtaH, kalpanIyavilambopasthitikakratvarthahiMsAM na spRzati | tathAhi - 'na hiMsyAt' ityatra puruSArtheva hiMsA hisidhAtunAnUdyate, na kratvarthApi; karturAkhyAtavAcyatvapakSe samAnapadazrutyA hiMsAyAstaccheSatvapratIteH, tasya AkhyAtavAcyabhAvanAkSepyatvapakSe'pi tadAkSiptakartRzeSatvapratIteH | nAtra kratorvAcakaM kiMcidasti | yena 'athAto'gnimagniSTomenAnuyajanti' ityanArabhyAdhItavAkye cityAgneryajizabdoktayAgazeSatvavat asyAH kratuzeSatvamavagamyeta | nApi kratorAkSepakaM kiMcidasti, yena parNatAyA juhvAkSiptakratuzeSatvamiva asyAH kratuzeSatvamavagamyeta | na ca prakaraNena kratorviparivRttirasti, yena 'nAnRtaM vadet' iti darzapUrNamAsaprakaraNAmnAtavAkye anUdyamAnasyAnRtavadanasyeva kratvarthatvapratItirbhavet | tasmAdiha hisidhAtunA anuvAdaH zIghrapratItAyAH puruSArthahiMsAyA iti tasyA eva niSedho na kratvarthAyAH | zabdazaktisAmye'pi zIghropasthitazakyasya vAkyArthAnvayo na vilambopasthitikasya - iti hi nyAyamaryAdA | ata eva 'darzapUrNamAsAbhyAM svargakAmo yajeta' ityadhikAravAkyagatapUrNamAsapadena utpattivAkyazrutapaurNamAsIkAlayorAgneyAgnISomIyayAgayoreva jhaTiti pravRttinimittAvagatyA zIghropasthitayoH phalAnvayaH syAt; upAMzuyAjasya tu 'upA MzuyAjamantarA yajati' iti paurNamAsIkAlakartavyAgneyAgnISomIyayAgadvayAntarAle vidhAnAdarthasiddhapaurNamAsIkAlasya jhaTiti pravRttinimittAnavagatyA vilambopasthitikasya phalAnvayo na syAt; zIghropasthitikapaurNamAsIkAlasaMbandhasya yAgadvayasya phalasaMyojanenaiva vAkyasya cAritArthyAt - iti upAMzuyAjasyApi zrutapaurNamAsIkAlatvena jhaTiti pravRttinimittAvagatyA zIghropasthitikatvasiddhyarthameva antarAlavidhisAmarthyalabhye'pi upAMzuyAjasya paurNamAsIkAlasaMbandhe 'tAvabrUtAmagnISomAvAjyasyaiva nAvupAMzu paurNamAsyAM yajan' iti pratyakSavidhirapekSyate iti ||
atha SaSThI kakSyA-- yatra pravRttinimittAvagatau zaighryavilambavizeSaH tatra zIghrAvagatapravRttinimittasyaivArthasya vAkyArthAnvayaH, nAnyasya iti niyamaH | ata eva upAMzuyAje pravRttinimittAvagatizaighryasAmyasiddhyarthaM pratyakSaH kAlavidhirapekSyate | yatra tu [p.207] pravRttinimittAvagatizaighryavilambavizeSAbhAve'pi tulyakAlAvagatikeSu ekapravRttinimittakroDIkRteSvartheSu kratvarthapuruSArthatvAdivizeSAvagatau zaighryavilambavizeSo'sti, tatra tena vizeSeNa kratvarthasya puruSArthasya vA zIghrapratItikasyaiva vAkyArthAnvaya iti nAsti niyamaH. anyathA 'yadAhavanIye juhvati' ityanArabhyAdhItavAkye'pi AkhyAtopAttabhAvanAkSiptakartRzeSatayA puruSArthAnAmeva homAnAmAhavanIyavidhiH, na kratvarthAnAM nAriSThAmanahomAdInAmityApatteH | evaM nyAyasthitau hisidhAtupravRttinimittasya kratvarthasaMjJapanasAdhAraNye tasyApi 'na hiMsyAt' iti niSedhagocaratvamanivAryaM bhavedityataH tadvyAvRttameva hisidhAtupravRttinimittaM nirvaktavyamiti ||
atha saptamI kakSyA-- yatra pravRttinimittAvizeSeNa kratvarthapuruSArthobhayagrahaNe vidhivairUpyamApadyate, tatra kratvarthapuruSArthayorubhayoH anyatarasyaiva vAkyArthAnvayaH nAnyasyetyapi niyama AdartavyaH | ata eva azvadAneSTihiraNyabharaNAdInAM kratvarthapuruSArthobhayavidhAzvadAnAdisaMyogo nAGgIkriyate, kratvarthAzvadAnAdisaMyogenApatataH kratvarthatvasya puruSArthAzvadAnAdisaMyogenApatataH svatantrapuruSArthasAdhanatvasya ca ekena vAkyena bodhayitumazakyatvAt | kiM tu azvadAnAdizrutisAdhAraNye'pi yathAyathaM kasyacitkratvarthasaMyoga eva kasyacitpuruSArthasaMyoga eveti vyavasthA aGgIkriyate | tathA hi 'prajApatirvaruNAyAzvamanayatsa svAM devatAmArchatsa paryadIryata sa etaM vAruNaM catuSkapAlamapazyattanniravapattato vai sa varuNapAzAdamucyata varuNo vA etaM gRhNAti yo'zvaM pratigRhNAti yAvato'zvAnpratigRhNIyAttAvato vAruNAMzcatuSkapAlAnnirvapet' iti vihitAzvadAnanimitteSTiH kratvarthAzvadAnanimittA, uta puruSArthAzvadAnanimitteti saMzaye, pauNDarIkAdiSu 'anvahaM dvAdaza zatAni dadAti azvasahasramuttame' ityAdivAkyazrutakratudakSiNArUpAzvadAnasya vihitatvenAduSTatvAt tasmindoSasaMkIrtanaM nAnveti | suhRdAdibhyaH kriyamANasya puruSArthAzvadAnasya 'na kesariNo dadAti' iti pratiSiddhatvena duSTatvAt tatra doSasaMkIrtanamanveti | atastadvazAllaukikAzvadAnanimittA iyamiSTiH iti 'doSAttviSTilaukike syAcchAstrAddhi vaidike na doSaH syAt' iti sUtreNa pUrvapakSaM kRtvA, laukikaM vaidikaM vA azvadAnaM [p.208] kRtavato varuNagrahazabdoktajalodaravyAdhyupanipAtAdarzanAt doSasaMkIrtanamidam - yathA varuNagrahe sati tadvimocanasAdhanamavazyAnuSTheyam, evamazvadAne kRte sati iyamiSTiH ityevamavazyakartavyatAparaM dRSTAntAbhedopacAravadarthavAdamAtram | ato na tadvazAt laukikAzvadAnanimitteyamiSTiH | kiMtu vaidikyA iSTervedikameva nimittaM buddhisthaM bhavatIti buddhisaMnidhilakSaNasthAnapramANAnusArAt kratvarthatve kratuphalAtirekeNa puruSArthatva iva phalAntaraM na kalpanIyamiti lAghavAnusArAcca na laukikAzvadAnanimittA - ityevam 'arthavAdo vAnupapAtAttasmAdyajJe pratIyeta' iti sUtreNa siddhAntitam | tathA 'tasmAtsuvarNa hiraNyaM bhAryaM durvarNo'sya bhrAtRvyo bhavati' iti vihitaM hiraNyabharaNaM kratvartha puruSArthaM veti saMzaye, kRtyapratyayena hiraNyasya saMskAryatApratIteH kratudakSiNAdirUpasyaiva hiraNyasyAgre viniyogasadbhAvena saMskAryataucityAt klRptakratuphalavyatirekeNa phalAntarasyAkalpanIyatvena lAghavAcca kratvarthatvamityevaM pUrvapakSaM kRtvA kRtyapratyayena phalabhAgitvamAtrapratItAvapi saMskAryatvAnavagateH kAryAntaradarzanenaiva saMskAryatvasyAvagantavyatvAt aprakaraNAdhIte ca kAryAntarAnavagamAt na saMskAryatAdhIbalena kratvarthatA | kiM tu rAtrisatranyAyenArthavAdikaphalavipariNatyA zrutaphalasadbhAvena kratUpakAraphalasyApyakalpanIyatayA puruSArthameveti 'aprakaraNe tu taddharmastato'vizeSAt' iti sUtreNa siddhAntitam | evamAdiSvadhikaraNeSu kratvarthaM puruSArthaM vetyevaM vicAramavatArya, [p.223] guNaguNyekazaktivAdaH || [p.223] kratvarthasaMjJapanasya hisidhAtuvAcyatvanirUpaNaprasaGgena nIlAdiguNavadravyANAM nIlAdizabdAvAcyatvam 'sarvatra prasiddhopadezAt' ityadhikaraNe bhASye brahmaNaH sarvazabdavAcyatvopapAdanArthaM dRSTAntatayA upAttaM vyavasthApayituM nIlAdizabdAH guNamAtravAcakAH guNini matublopena abhedopacAreNa lakSaNayA zaktibhrameNa vA prayujyante iti naiyAyikAdimataM pUrvapakSatayA parigRhya vAdakathA prastUyate-- tatra nIlaghaTaH nIlatvajAtipravRttinimittakatayA nIlazabdavAcyaH, nIlatvajAtyadhikaraNatvAt, yat nIlatvajAtyadhikaraNam, tat nIlatvajAtipravRttinimittakatayA nIlazabdavAcyam yathA nIlarUpam iti prathamA kakSyA || aprayojakamidamanumAnam, anukUlatarkAbhAvAt | nIlaghaTo na nIlatvajAtipravRttinimittakatayA nIlazabdavAcyaH, nIlarUpAnyatvAt, yat nIlarUpAnyat, na tat nIlatvajAtipravRttinimittakatayA nIlazabdavAcyam yathA zuklarUpam nIlazabdavAcyatvAbhAvavyApyanIlarUpAnyatvavAMzca ayam; tasmAt na nIlatvajAtipravRttinimittakatayA nIlazabdavAcyaH iti dvitIyA || zabdapravRttinimittAzrayasya tadvAcyatvAbhAvAnupapatteH vAdyanumAnaM nAprayojakam | pratyanumAnameva aprayojakam iti tRtIyA || guNagatajAtInAM paraMparayA dravyAzritatvAbhyupagame pravRttinimittAzrayatvamAtraM na zabdavAcyatvaprayojakam | kiM tu sAkSAt tadAzrayatvam | anyathA paraMparayA guNazabdapravRttinimittAzrayasya dravyasyApi guNazabdavAcyatve guNavacanebhyo matupo luganuzAsanaM virudhyeta | ato vAdyanumAnam aprayojakam | pratyanumAnaM ca nAprayojakam iti caturthI || [p.224] [p.225] atha paJcamI-- zabdavAcyatAprayojake tatpravRttinimittAzrayatve sAkSAttvavizeSaNAbhAve matublopAnuzAsanavirodho na prasajyate | dvividhA hi guNazabdAH dravyaparyantA guNamAtravizrAntAzca; 'nIlam utpalasya' 'nIlamutpalam' iti dvividhavyavahAradarzanAt | tathA ca yathA dravyavAcinAM nIlAdizabdAnAM guNaparatvavivakSAyAM nailyaM nIlatvaM nIlimA - iti bhAvapratyayaH, tathA teSAM guNavAcinAM dravyaparatvavivakSAyAM matuppratyayo'pi bhavet - dRzyate hi puNyapApazabdAnAm 'ayaM puruSaH puNyaH' 'ayaM pApaH' 'asya puNyam' 'asya pApam' 'puNyaH puNyena karmaNA bhavati pApaH pApena karmaNA' ityAdidvividhavyavahAradarzanAt dharmAdharmamAtravAcakatvena taddharmiparyantatvena ca dvividhAnAM madhye dharmAdharmamAtravAcakAnAM dharmiparyantatvavivakSAyAm 'puNyavAn' 'pApavAn' ityAdimatubantavyavahAraH | tathA atrApi tatprAptau tallopAnuzAsanam | tena guNavizrAntA nIlAdizabdAH matublopena dravyaparyantaM netavyAH; na tu matubantatayA iti sidhyati | ataH sAkSAttvavizeSaNaM gauravAnna nivezanIyam iti nAprayojakaM vAdyanumAnam, pratyanumAnameva aprayojakamiti || atha SaSThI-- guNavAcinAmeva nIlAdizabdAnAM tadvatsu dravyeSu lakSaNayA prayogasaMbhavAt na teSvapi zaktiH kalpanIyA, gauravAt | ato matublopAnuzAsanaM guNavAcinaH zabdA guNiSu lakSaNayA prayoktavyAH, na tu matubantatayA iti lakSaNAniyamArthameva upapAdanIyam | guNaparanIlimAdizabdaniSpattirapi lakSyamANadravyaparanIlAdizabdAt bhAvapratyayena upapAdanIyA | ato matublopAnuzAsanAnurodhena lakSaNIyadravyAtiprasaGgavAraNAya sAkSAttvavizeSaNApekSaNAt vAdyanumAnamaprayojakam, pratyanumAnaM ca na aprayojakamiti || atha saptamI-- guNazabdAnAM guNiSu zakyantarakalpane hi gauravaM bhavet | lAghavAt sAkSAtparaMparAsAdhAraNyena teSAM svapravRttinimittAzrayamAtre zaktiriti lAghavopanItaikadharmAvacchedena guNeSu guNiSu ca ekazaktigrahaNapakSe gauravaprasaktiH | yadyapi asminpakSe zakyavyaktibAhulyalakSaNaM gauravamasti, tathApi prathamapravRttAvacchedakalAghavAnusAreNa pazcAdApatatphalamukhaM vyaktigauravaM na doSaH | tasmAt nIlAdizabdapravRttinimitte sAkSAttvaM na nivezanIyam | nApi matublopAnuzAsanaM lakSaNAniyamArthamiti upapAdanIyam | tasmAdvAdyanumAnaM nAprayojakam, pratyanumAnamevAprayojakamiti || athASTamI-- luganuzAsanaM lakSaNAniyamArtham ityeva upapAdanIyam; sAkSAttvaM ca avazyaM nivezanIyam; anyathA tvaduktarItyA ghaTavato bhUtalasya paraMparayA ghaTatvAzrayatvena tasyApi ghaTazabdavAcyatvApatteH | na ca pravRttinimittaM prati apRthaksiddhAzrayatvaM vivakSitamiti nAtiprasaGga iti vAcyam; apRthaksiddhirhi na tAvat dvayorekasya aparAzrayatvaniyatiH, tattaddhaTavyaktyatyayAnantaramapi anuvartamAnAyA nIlatvajAtestadAzritatvaniyamAbhAvAt | nApi sati dharmiNi tadAzrayatvaniyatiH, ghaTe prathamakSaNAvacchinne nIlarUpAnutpattyA tasminnAmapAkanihite nIlarUpanAzenaitasyAH satyapi ghaTe tadAzritatvaniyamAsiddheH | nApi dharmiNi paraMparAghaTake ca sati dharmAzritatvaniyatiH apRthaksiddhiH, sAkSAnnIlatvajAtyAzraye nIlarUpe paraMparAghaTakAbhAvena tadasaMgrahApatteH | ataH saMbandhAnuvRttiprayojakayAvatsattve tadAzrayatvaniyamaH tadapRthaksiddhiriti vAcyam | tathA sati ghaTatvasya paraMparayA bhUtalasaMbandhe ghaTabhUtalasaMsargo'pi prayojaka iti tatsattve ghaTatvasya bhUtalAzritatvaniyatisadbhAvAt kathamapRthaksiddhivizeSaNena tadvyAvRttiH, kathaM ca ghaTasya rUpAdizabdavAcyatvaprasaktervyAvRttiH | na hi rUpatvAdInAM nIlatvAdInAM ca dravyAtpRthaksiddhisadasattve kenacidvizeSeNa upapAdayituM zakye | ataH sAkSAttvavizeSaNameva nivezanIyam | [p.226] luganuzAsanaM ca lakSaNAniyamArthamityevopapAdanIyam | tasmAdaprayojakaM vAdyanumAnam, pratyanumAnaM ca nAprayojakamiti || atha navamI-- ghaTatvasya bhUtalaM rUpatvasya rUpi ca Azraya eva na bhavati, 'ghaTo bhUtalam' 'rUpaM ghaTaH' ityevamAdivyavahArAdirUpasya AzrayatAgrAhakapramANasya abhAvAt | na hi AzrayAzrayamAtram AzrayadvArA bhavatyAzrayaH | kiM tu yasya tatrApi AzrayatAgrAhakaM pramANamasti, sa eva tadAzrayo bhavati yathA kAraNakAraNamAtraM na kAraNam, kulAlapitrAdestathAtve'pi akAraNatvAt; kiMtu tasyApi yatra kAraNatAgrAhakaM pramANamasti, tadeva yAgAdi svargAdikAraNaM bhavati tathA ihApi yojanIyam | tasmAt na sAkSAttvaM nivezanIyam | nApi luganuzAsanaM lakSaNAniyamArthamityupapAdanIyam | ato nAprayojakaM vAdyanumAnam, pratyanumAnameva aprayojakamiti || atha dazamI-- yadi ghaTabhUtalAdikaM rUpatvaghaTatvAdyAzrayo na bhavati tathAtvagrAhakapramANAbhAvAt, tarhi ghaTAdikaM nIlatvAdyAzrayo'pi na bhavet, vAdyabhimatasya tathAtvagrAhakapramANasya abhAvAdeva | 'nIlo ghaTaH' ityAdivyavahAro'stIti cet; na, sa hi matublopAnuzAsanAnusAreNa matupaH prayoge yAvAnarthaH, tAvantamarthaM dravyaparasya nIlAdizabdasya gamayati | matupprayoge ca dravyasya nIlatvAdyAzrayaguNAzrayataiva tadartho bhavet; na tu nIlatvAdyAzrayatA | ataH yathA 'daNDI puruSaH' iti vyavahAraH puruSasya daNDatvAzrayatve na pramANam, tathA 'nIlo ghaTaH' ityAdivyavahAro'pi ghaTasya nIlatvAdyAzrayatve | ataH kAraNakAraNarItiM parityajya AzrayAzrayamAtram AzrayadvAreNa Azrayo bhavati iti vAdino rItirAstheyA | tAmeva rItimanurudhya prativAdinA atiprasaGgavAraNAya lakSaNAniyamArthaluganuzAsanAnurodhAya ca sAkSAttvaM nivezanIyamityApAditam | ato vAdyanumAnamaprayojakam, pratyanumAnaM ca na aprayojakamiti || athaikAdazI-- [p.227] ghaTAdInAM nIlatvAdyAzrayatve pramANAbhAvo'siddhaH, 'nIlo ghaTaH' ityAdipratItivyavahArasadbhAvAt | na hi 'nIlo guNaH' ityAdipratItivyahArayozca 'nIlo ghaTaH' ityAdipratItivyavahArayozca mAtrayApi vizeSamIkSAmahe | yena guNaviSayau tau nIlatvAdyAzrayagocarau, guNiviSayau tadAzrayAzrayagocarau iti vaiSamyaM kalpyeta | ubhayaviSayayorapi tayorvizeSAbhAvAdeva hi tau pramANIkRtya guNaguNinoH kecidatyantAbhedaM manyante | anye 'ghaTasya nIlo guNaH' ityAdipratItivyavahArAvanurudhya tayorbhedAbhedaM ca samarthayante | tayoratyantabhedavAdinazca kathaM tarhi 'nIlo ghaTaH' ityAdipratItivyavahArau ityAzaGkya matublopAt abhedopacArAdvA iti matublopatulyatvena abhedopacAramapi tadupapAdakamupAdadate | tayoranubhavasAkSikavizeSasadbhAve tu 'nIlo ghaTaH' ityAdipratItivyavahArAlambanamabhedasamarthanaM bhedAbhedasamarthanaM ca atyantanirAlambanaM syAt; abhedopacAravarNanamapi virudhyeta | tasmAnmatublopAnuzAsanaM puNyavAnityAdirItyA matubantaprayogo na kArya ityetadarthamityeva yuktam | naivam 'ghaTo bhUtalam ' 'rUpaM ghaTaH' ityAdipratItivyavahArau staH | yau ghaTabhUtalAdInAM rUpatvaghaTatvAdyAzrayatve pramANIbhavetAm | na ca 'barhiSi havIMSyAsAdayati' iti vacanAnusAreNa sAkSAdbarhirAzritAnAM haviSAM barhirAzrayabhUtA vedirapi Azraya ityatra - yathA 'vedyAM havIMSyAsAdayati' iti vacanam, kevalaM barhiSAM kUpAcchAdanatRNanyAyena havirAdhAratA na saMbhavatIti laukikadRSTizca pramANam tathA iha kiMcitpramANaM dRzyate | ato vAdyanumAnaM nAprayojakam, pratyanumAnamevAprayojakamiti || atha dvAdazI-- rUpatvasya ghaTAzrayatva iva ghaTatvasya bhUtalAzrayatva iva ca nIlatvasya ghaTAzrayatve'pi pramANAbhAvo na asiddhaH guNaviSayayordravyaviSayayozca nIlapratItivyavahArayoH lokataH spaSTaM vizeSAnavagame'pi Adyau nIlatvajAtyAzrayaviSayau dvitIyau tadviziSTavaiziSTyaviSayau iti vizeSasya avazyAbhyupagantavyatvAt | anyathA 'nIlaH sphaTikaH' 'pItaM suvarNam' 'uSNaM salilam' ityAdipratItivyavahAre [p.228] sphaTikasuvarNasalilAnAmapi nIlatvapItatvoSNatvAdijAtyAzrayatApattyA nIlapItoSNAdizabdavAcyatvaprasaGgAt | evaM dravyaviSayayoH nIlAdipratItivyavahArayoH viziSTavaiziSTyaviSayatvamAlocyaiva bhagavatA kAtyAyanena matublopAnuzAsanaM kRtam | atha pramANAbhAve'pi AzrayAzrayamAtram AzrayadvArA Azrayo bhavati iti paribhASAmAtraM kriyate, tadA ghaTabhUtalAdInAM rUpaghaTAdizabdavAcyatvaprasaGgavAraNAya sAkSAttvavizeSaNaM nivezanIyam | matublopAnuzAsanaM ca lakSaNAniyamArthamityupapAdanIyam | tasmAdaprayojakaM vAdyanumAnam, pratyanumAnaM ca nAprayojakamiti || atha trayodazI-- guNaviSayayoriva dravyaviSayayorapi nIlAdipratItivyavahArayoH nIlatvAdyAzrayatAyAM prAmANyamupapadyata eva | 'nIlaH sphaTikaH' iti pratItivyavahArayostu bhrAntitanmUlavyavahArarUpatvAt na tasyAM prAmANyaM prasajyate na hi sphaTikaH saMnihitendIvarAdinIlimagatanIlatvasya sAkSAdAzrayaH | nApi paraMparayA, nIlimnaH tadAzrayasyendIvarasya ca sphaTikAzrayatvAbhAvAt; paraMparAghaTakasya dvArasya Azritatve hi taddvArA tadAzritamapi AzritaM bhavet yathA dvArasya kAraNatva eva tadvArA tatkAraNamapi dvArAnantarabhAvini kAraNaM bhavati | anyathA yAgasya svargakAraNatAnirvAhArtham apUrvamAtraM kalpyeta; na tasya svargakAraNatvamapi | evaM ca sarvathA nIlatvAnAzraye sphaTike bhavantI nIlapratItiH bhrAntirUpeti tasyAH tanmUlavyavahArasya nIlatvAzrayatAyAM prAmANyAbhAvamAtreNa katham 'nIlo ghaTaH' ityAdipratItivyavahArayoH svataH prAptaM prAmANyamapaiti | 'pItaM suvarNam' iti pratItivyavahArau tu pItimagurutvAdhikaraNapArthivAvayavavyatirekeNa tadantargatasya suvarNazabdavAcyasya taijasAMzasya anaGgIkArAt; naimittikadravatvasya jatuvat pArthiva eva upapatteH | na hi drutasuvarNamadhye pArthivabhAgaH tanmadhyanikSiptapASANakhaNDavat adruta evAvatiSThate | yena dravatvAzrayabhUtamanyat kalpyeta | pratyuta 'pItaM drutam' ityevAnubhUyate | kvathyamAnajalarUpAparAvRtteriva suvarNarUpAparAvRtterapi tadIyadravatvena atyantAnalasaMyuktavajrarUpAparAvRtteriva vastusvabhAvena vA upapatteH | suvarNasya pArthivatve'pi atyantAnalasaMyogena tadIyadravatvasya vajrarUpasyeva anu[p.229]vRtterapi vastusvabhAvAdevopapatteH | athavA suvarNaM taijasamevAstu, zuddhasuvarNe dRzyamAnasyAtyantapItimnaH 'yadagne rohitaM rUpaM tejasastadrUpam' iti zrutau tejorUpatvena pratipanne lauhitya eva avAntaravizeSatvopapatteH | zrutiSu tejo'bannarUpatvena varNitebhyo lohitazuklakRSNavarNebhyo'tiriktasya rUpasyAprAmANikatvAt | gurutvamapi karakAvasthe jale kAThinyamiva suvarNAvasthe tejasi anubhavabalAdupapannam | udbhUtarUpasya tejasaH paraprakAzakatvadRSTAvapi atra tadabhAvaH tathaivAnubhavabalAdupapannaH | suvarNagatatejoM'zasya paraprakAzakatvAbhAvo'pi parapakSe pArthivAMzagatapItimnA abhibhavAditi anyatrAdRSTa eva svabhAvavizeSaH kalpanIyaH | na hi parapakSe'pi anyatra ratnadIpAdau upaSTambhakapArthivabhAgarUpasya tejogatabhAsvaratvAbhibhAvakatvaM dRSTam | evam 'uSNaM jalam' iti pratItivyavahArAvapi jalasyoSNatvAzrayatAyAM pramANameva; jalavat svataH dravatvarahitasyApi pArthivasya ghRtalAkSAderdravatvavat svataH zItasyApi jalasya agnisaMyogAnnaimittikasya uSNasparzasyopapatteH tatra tejo'ntarotpattikalpanAyA gauravaparAhatatvAt | na hi ayaHpiNDArUDhavahnivat atitaptatailodbhUtavahnijvAlAvacca taptajalasaMyuktaM kiMcittejo'nubhUyate | tasmAt 'nIlo ghaTaH' iti pratItivyavahArayornIlatvAzrayatAyAM prAmANyasya dRSTAntopanyAsena pratikSeptumazakyatvAt prAguktarItyA vAdyanumAnaM nAprayojakam, pratyanumAnamevAprayojakamiti || atha caturdazI-- nIlasphaTikAdidRSTAntAH prativAdino naiyAyikasya matamanusRtya upanyastAH | tanmate hi 'nIlaH sphaTikaH' iti pratItiH paraMparAsaMbandhena saMnihitopAdhigatanIlaguNavaiziSTyaviSayA pramaiva | tathA 'pItaM suvarNam' iti pratItiH taijase suvarNe tadupaSTambhakapArthivabhAgagatapItaguNavaiziSTyaviSayA | 'uSNaM salilam' iti pratItiH tadantaH praviSTAnudbhUtarUpatejogatoSNaguNavaiziSTyaviSayA | na ca tanmatamaryAdAsiddhadRSTAntopanyAsasya satA asatA vA vAdyupanyastadUSaNena kiMciddhIyate | santi vAdyabhimatA api dRSTAntAH prAk Adizabdena gRhItAH 'nIlaM vastram' 'lohitaM kASTham' ityAdipratItivyavahArAH | na hi vastraM raJjakadravyAvayavAnAM kASTham AzrayAzasya vahnervA nAzraya iti vaktuM zakyam | tasmAt vAdinApi tanmatasiddhadRSTAntAnusAreNa [p.230] dravyaviSayayornIlAdipratItivyavahArayoH nIlatvAdyAzrayatvaviSayatAbhimAnastyaktavya iti || atha paJcadazI-- 'nIlaM vastram' ityAdipratItivyavahArA api nIlatvAdyAzrayatAviSayA eva na; kiMtu bhrAntirUpAH 'anIlameva vastraM raJjakadravyasaMyogAnnIlaM bhAsate' 'alohitameva kASThaM tadupArUDhavahnisaMparkAllohitaM bhAsate' iti viruddhapratItivyavahArayoH tatpratItivyavahAradazAyAmapyanuvRtteH | na caivam 'anIla eva ghaTo nIlatayA bhAsate' iti viruddhapratItivyavahArau staH 'ghaTo nIlaguNo na bhavati' iti pratItivyavahArau tu nIlatvAzrayatAbAdhakau, guNatvAbhAvena cAritArthyAt | tasmAdabAdhitau pratItivyavahArau ghaTasya nIlatvAzrayatAyAM pramANIbhavetAmeva - iti || atha SoDazI-- nIlatvajAtyAzrayatvaviSayatAyAmabAdhitau etau pratItivyavahArau ityetadasiddham, ihApi bAdhakasadbhAvAt | tathAhi matublopAnuzAsanaM tAvat ekaM bAdhakam - pratyayazravaNaprayuktayaugikArthapratItisadbhAve hi tallopAnuzAsanaM sArthakaM bhavet| ata eva 'paJcAlA nAma kecana kSatriyAH teSAM nivAso janapadaH' - iti vivakSAyAm 'so'sya nivAsaH' - ityarthe prAgdIvyatIyaH pratyayo bhavati iti mattvA 'tasya janapade lup' iti yallopAnuzAsanaM jaradvaiyAkaraNaiH kRtam; tanna kartavyam, janapadavizeSe vartamAnena tatsaMjJArUpeNa paJcAlA iti zabdena paJcAlanAmakakSatriyANAM nivAso janapada iti yaugikArthapratItyanudayAt, tato rUDhyA janapadavizeSasyaiva pratItyudayAditi dUSitaM pANininA 'lubyogAprakhyAnAt' iti sUtreNa 'aziSyam' iti pUrvasUtrAdanuvRttimatA | atha aruNAdhikaraNanyAyavirodharUpam anyadbAdhakam aruNAdhikaraNe hi 'aruNayA piGgAkSyaikahAyanyA somaM krINAti' iti zrutigatasya aruNApadasya aruNimaguNaparatvamaGgIkRtya aruNimaguNaikahAyanIdravyobhayasAdhanakakrayabhAvanAvidhiH aGgIkRtaH | sa tu guNavacanAnAM guNamAtrazaktyaGgIkAre saMgacchate | [p.231] aruNAzabdasya piGgAkSyAdizabdasAmAnAdhikaraNyarUpeNa vAkyapramANena dravyaparatvapratItAvapi tasya guNamAtrazaktipakSe dravyaparatvasya lakSaNayA upapAdanIyatvena zrutiviruddhavAkyabAdhanopapatteH | tasya guNisAdhAraNazaktipakSe tu sa na saMgacchate, zrutyaviruddhasya vAkyapramANasya upekSAnarhatvAt | tasmAt 'nIlo ghaTaH' iti pratIteH ghaTagatanIlatvajAtyAzrayatAviSayatve bAdhakasadbhAvena bhrAntitvApatteH tatprAmANyamabhyupagacchatA viziSTavaiziSTyaviSayatvamevAbhyupagantavyam | tasmAddhaTasya rUpatvAzrayatve nIlatvAzrayatve ca pramANAbhAvastulya iti || atha saptadazI-- matublopAnuzAsanasvArasyabhaGgastAvat na bAdhakaH,'nIlo ghaTaH' 'nIlo guNaH' iti pratItyoravizeSAnubhavavirodhena tatsvArasyabhaGgasya ucitatvAt; matublope nIlAdiguNaviziSTadravyapratItiH, anyatra nIlatvAdiviziSTadravyapratItiH iti vyavasthayA ubhayasAmaJjasyasamarthanasaMbhavAcca | aruNAdhikaraNanyAyavirodhastu na prasajyate, guNazabdAnAM guNaguNyubhayavAcakatve'pi aruNAvAkye guNiparigrahe vibhakteH zabdasAdhutvamAtrArthatayA vaiyarthyaM syAt; guNigatakaraNakArakatvasya samAnAdhikaraNapadAntaragatatRtIyAvibhaktilabdhatvAt | na ca guNaparigraheNa tadgatakaraNakArakatvArthakatayA sArthakatve saMbhavati vaiyarthyAGgIkAro yuktaH | na ca dravyaikaparapiGgAkSyaikahAyanIpadagatavibhaktidvayavat ananyagatikatvamasti | aruNApadasya guNArthatvaparigraheNa tatsArthakatvasamarthanasaMbhavAt | ataH prakRtizrutyavirodhe'pi vibhaktizrutivirodhena vAkyabAdhanamucitamAlocya pUrvamImAMsakaiH guNArthaparigrahaH kRtaH | asmAbhistu sAmAnAdhikaraNyasyApi anugrahasaMbhave tadbAdhanAyogAt aruNAzabdasya guNiparyantatvamAzrityaiva vibhaktyarthasya vizeSaNasaMkrAntiH iSyate | tasmAt bAdhakAbhAvena 'nIlo ghaTaH' iti pratIteH 'nIlo guNaH' iti pratIteriva nIlatvAzrayatAyAM prAmANyamupapadyata eva | ato ghaTasya nIlazabdapravRttinimittAzrayatvAt zabdavAcyatvaprayojake pravRttinimittAzrayatve sAkSAttvavizeSaNasya gauravaparAhatatvAcca nIlazabdapravRttinimittAzrayasya ghaTasya nIlazabdavAcyatvam avyAhatamiti vipakSe bAdhakatarkasya apratihatatayA vAdyanumAnameva prayojakam, pratyanumAnamaprayojakamiti siddhaM ghaTasya [p.232] [p.233] nIlazabdavAcyatvam | nIlazabdanyAyena anye'pi rUpavizeSavAcino rasagandhavizeSavAcinazca guNaguNyekazaktimantaH pratyetavyAH || iti guNaguNyekazaktivAdaH ||3||

[p.233] guNaguNyanekazaktivAdaH atha eSAM guNavacanAnAM zabdAnAM guNaguNinobhinnA zaktiriti saiddhAntikaM pakSAntaramAzritya kathA prastUyate nIlaguNAzrayo ghaTo nIlazabdasya vAcyaH, ghaTanIlaguNAnyApratiyogikAbhAvapratiyogitvAt, yo ghaTanIlaguNAnyApratiyogikAbhAvapratiyogI, sa nIlazabdavAcyaH yathA nIlaguNaH iti prathamA kakSyA || aprayojakamidamanumAnam, nIlaguNasya nIlazabdapravRttinimittanIlatvajAtyAzrayatvena nIlazabdavAcyatve'pi ghaTasya tadanAzrayatvena nIlazabdAvAcyatvopapatteH | tasmAt ghaTo na nIlazabdavAcyaH, nIlatvajAtyanAzrayatvAt, yo nIlatvajAtyanAzrayaH, sa na nIlazabdavAcyaH yathA zuklaguNaH | nIlazabdavAcyatvAbhAvavyApyanIlatvajAtyanAzrayatvavAMzca ayam, tasmAt na nIlazabdavAcyaH - iti dvitIyA kakSyA || na aprayojakamidamanumAnam, nIlatvajAtivat nIlaguNasyApi nIlazabdapravRttinimittatvena tadAzrayasya nIlazabdavAcyatAyA avyAhatatvAt | tvadanumAnameva aprayojakam, ghaTasya nIlatvAnAzrayatve'pi nIlazabdapravRttinimittAntarAzrayatvena tadvAcyatvopapatteH iti tRtIyA || tvadanumAnaM aprayojakameva, lAghavAt nIlatvajAtimAtrasya nIlazabdapravRttinimittatvopapattau tayA akroDIkRtAnAM nIlaguNAnAmapi tatpravRttinimittatvakalpanAyogAt | ata eva madanumAnaM na aprayojakam, pravRttinimittAnAzrayasya tadvAcyatvAyogAt - iti caturthI || madanumAnaM tAvat na aprayojakam, 'nIlo guNaH' iti vyavahArAt guNagatanIlatvajAteriva 'nIlo ghaTaH' iti vyavahArAt tadgatanIlaguNasyApi tatpravRttinimittatvakalpanAvazyaMbhAvAt; vyavahAramanurudhya pravRttinimittakalpanaucityAt | ata eva tvadanumAnamaprayojakam, pravRttinimittAzrayasya tadvAcyatvopapatteH iti paJcamI || tvadanumAnaM tAvat aprayojakameva, nIlazabdasya svavAcyanIlaguNasaMbandhAt ghaTe lakSaNayA 'nIlo ghaTaH' iti vyavahAropapattau tatrApi nIlazabdasya zaktyantarakalpane gauravAt | ata eva madanumAnaM na aprayojakam, pravRttinimittAnAzrayasya zabdavAcyatAyA vyAhatatvAt - iti SaSThI || atha saptamI-- yadi nIlAdizabdAnAM dravye lakSaNA syAt, tadA 'varNadRDhAdibhyaH SyaJ ca' iti pANinisUtreNa varNavAcibhyo nIlAdizabdebhyaH 'tasya bhAve' ityarthe vihitau SyaJimanicau pratyayau nIlAdiguNasya bhAve nIlatvAdijAtau bhavetAm | nIlAdidravyasya bhAve nIlAdiguNe tau dRzyete - ghaTasya nailyaM nIlimA iti hi loke nIlAdiguNa eva ucyete | ataH nIlAdizabdAnAM dravyavAcakatvamavazyamabhyupagantavyamiti dravye teSAM zaktikalpanaM na gauraveNa tyaktuM zakyate - iti ||
atha aSTamI kakSyA-- vaiyAkaraNaiH nIlAdizabdAH kevalAH luptamatubantAzca - iti dvividhAH iSyante | tatra AdyAH guNeSu, dvitIyAH tadviziSTadravyeSu ca pravartante | tadiha varNadRDhAdisUtre nIlAdizabdAH kevalAH dharmaviSayA grAhyAH, dvitIyAH dharmiviSayA vA iti saMdehe dharmiparadRDhAdizabdasAhacaryAt dvitIyA eva grAhyA iti ca tairiSyate | evamakalpita[p.234]nityazabdasvarUpaparijJAnArthaM citraturaganyAyena kalpitaprakRtipratyayatallopAdiyuktAnityazabdaniSpAdane pravRttaiH vaiyAkaraNaiH matublopasya vyavahRtatve'pi vastutattvamanurudhya siddhazabdAnAmeva arthanirNaye pravRttaiH nyAyavidbhiH nyAyAnusAreNa zabdArthanirNayaH kAryaH | tadanusAreNa ca matublopasya vaiyAkaraNakalpitatvAt kevalA eva nIlAdizabdAH guNeSu guNiSu ca pravartante ityabhyupagantavyam | tatazca lAghavAt guNavAcinAmeva teSAM guNiSu lakSaNayA pravRttyupapatteH gaGgAdizabdAnAM tIrAdiSviva - teSAM guNiSu zaktyantaraM na kalpanIyam | nanu guNavAcinAmeva nIlAdizabdAnAM guNiSu lakSaNA cet, matublopAnuzAsanaM na kartavyaM syAt, na hi gaGgAdizabdAnAM tIrAdiSu lakSaNayA pravRttyarthaM tIrAdipratyAyakakiMcitpratyayalopAnuzAsanaM kriyate | ataH tadanuzAsanena idamavagamyate pratyayaprayoge yAvAnarthaH tAvadarthakatvamapi nIlAdizabdAnAmastIti vaiyAkaraNAnAmabhiprAyaH iti | matuppratyayaprayoge ca nIlaguNavattvamartho bhavati iti nirvivAdam | ato nIlAdizabdAnAM guNiSu zaktikalpanamAvazyakam | anyathA zabdArthanirNayAbhiyuktavaiyAkaraNakRtamatublopAnuzAsanavaiyarthyaprasaGgAt - iti cet, maivam, yato nIlAdizabdAnAM guNiSu lakSaNAbhyupagame'pi na matublopAnuzAsanavaiyarthyaM prasajyate yathA hi gaGgAzabdasya lakSaNayA tIre kadAcitprayogaH 'gaGgAyAM ghoSaH' iti; kadAcittIravAcakazabdena saha prayogaH 'gaGgAtIre ghoSaH' iti evaM guNazabdAnAM kadAcit lakSaNayA guNiSu prayogaH 'nIlo ghaTaH' 'pIto ghaTaH' ityAdi, kadAciddravyavAcinA matupA saha prayogaH 'nIlavAnghaTaH' 'pItavAnghaTaH' iti evaM dvividhavyavahAraprasaktau lakSaNayA prayoga eva sAdhuH matubantaprayogastu asAdhuH iti jJApanArthatvopapatteH | evaM ca nIlAdizabdAnAM zaktyA guNamAtraparatvaM lakSaNayA guNiparyantatvam iti sthite guNiparyantAnAmeva teSAM varNadRDhAdisUtre grahaNamiti dharmiparadRDhAdizabdasAhacaryAt nailyAdizabdaiH nIlAdiguNAnAmiva tadgatanIlatvAdijAtInAmabhidhAnadarzanAcca avasIyate | na ca zaktAdeva pratyayaniSpattiH, na lAkSaNikAt iti niyamo'sti; dharmavAcakAnAmeva zabdAnAM vRttiviSaye lakSaNayA dharmiparyantatvasya 'nidrAyate' 'karuNAyate' 'ojAyate' ityAdau bahulamupalambhAt | teSu hi anidro nidrAvAnbhavatItyAdyartheSu nidrAdizabdAH nidrAvadAdidharmilakSakAH | evamalohito lohito bhavati lohitAyate iti zabdaniSpattyarthaM pratyayavidhAyake 'lohitAdiDAjbhyaH kyaS' iti sUtre lohitagrahaNamapi [p.235] lakSaNayA dharmiparam, lohitaguNarahitasya ghaTAdeH pAkAdinA lohitaguNavaiziSTyavat alohitaguNasya lohitaguNatAyAH kvacidapi bhAvasya adarzanAt | tasmAt nIlAdizabdAnAM dravyaparatvasya lakSaNayA upapannatvAt aprayojakaM tvadanumAnam, madanumAnaM ca na aprayojakam - iti || atha navamI-- nIlAdizabdAnAM dravyavAcakatvAbhAve dravyaparebhyastebhyaH SyaJAdipratyayavidhireva na saMbhavati | bhAvArthe hi tadvidhAnam; 'tasya bhAvastvatalau' iti bhAvArthAdhikArAt | bhAvazabdena ca yasmAcchabdAt bhAvArthapratyayavidhAnaM kriyate, tatpravRttinimittabhUto dharma ucyate, na tu tadvAcyadharmigatadharmamAtram, ghaTatvazabdena ghaTazabdapravRttinimittajAteriva ghaTagatakriyAguNAdidharmAntarasya anabhidhAnAt | yasya guNasya hi bhAvAddravye zabdanivezaH tadabhidhAne tvatalau ityAdivadadbhiH zabdavidbhirapi tathaivAGgIkRtatvAcca | na ca zaktyA zabdapravRttau nimittasya zakyatAvacchedakasyeva lakSaNayA zabdapravRttau nimittasya mukhyArthasyApi zabdapravRttinimittatvAvizeSAt iha bhAvazabdena grahaNamastviti vAcyam, tathA sati lakSaNayA tIre gaGgAzabdapravRttinimittasya pravAhatvasyApi gaGgAtvazabdena abhidhAnaprasaGgAt | tasmAt nIlAdizabdAnAM guNeSviva dravyeSvapi zaktiraGgIkaraNIyA | ata eva 'varNo varNena' iti varNavAcinAM varNavAcibhiH saha samAnAdhikaraNasamAsavidhAyakasUtreNa guNavAcinA guNivAcinAM ca teSAmavizeSeNa grahaNamiSyate | tata eva hi dravyeSvapi nIlalohita ityAdiprayogAH | teSu zvetalohitAdiguNAnAM bhinnabhinnAvayavAzritatve'pi avayavaguNena samudAyaH tathA nirdizyate iti sAmAnAdhikaraNyaM bhASyAdiSu upapAditam | ata eva nAtizvete nAtirakte guNini guNe ca 'zvetaraktastu pATalaH' iti zvetaraktazabdaprayogaH | tasya ca guNaguNyubhayasAdhAraNyam 'guNe zuklAdayaH puMsi guNiliGgAstu tadvati' ityagrimavacanena sphuTIkRtam | tasmAt nIlAdizabdAnAM dravyeSvapi zakteravazyaMbhAvAt nAprayojakaM madanumAnam, tvadanumAnameva aprayojakam - iti || [p.236] atha dazamI-- nIlAdizabdAnAM dravyeSvapi zaktiriSyate cet, guNeSu tatpravRttinimittAnAM nIlatvAdijAtInAM paraMparAsaMbandhena dravyAzritatvasyApi 'nIlo guNaH' 'nIlo ghaTaH' ityAdivyavahAraikarUpyAnusAreNa kalpayituM zakyatvAt tvadabhimata 'nIlo ghaTaH' ityAdipratItivyavahArayoH nIlAdiguNaviziSTadravyaviSayatvameva na sidhyet | dUre nIlAdiguNAnAM pravRttinimittatvam | ataH tayoH nIlatvAdijAtiviziSTadravyaviSayatvapakSamapahAya nIlAdiguNaviziSTadravyaviSayatvapakSamAzrayatA tIre gaGgAzabdasya prayogapratItyoH tasminpravAhatvavaiziSTyaviSayatvamapahAya pravAhasaMbandhitIratvaviSayatvamicchatA gaGgAzabdasya tIra iva nIlAdizabdAnAM dravyeSu lakSaNaivAbhyupetavyA | evaM nyAyataH sthitau kvacillakSyatAvacchedake'pi prayogAnusAreNa bhAvapratyayo bhaviSyati, prayogamUlatvAdvyAkaraNasya | 'varNo varNena' ityatra tu prayogAnusAreNa dravyalakSakANAmapi nIlAdizabdAnAM grahaNaM bhaviSyati | ataH tvadanumAnamaprayojakam, madanumAnaM nAprayojakamiti || athaikAdazI-- dravyeSu nIlAdizabdapravRttau nIlAdiguNAnAM nimittatvAbhAve teSu nailyanIlimAdizabdaprayogo na syAt, nIlatvAdijAtInAM nimittatve tAsu teSAM prayogazca syAt, bhAvArthapratyayAnAM pravRttinimittavAcitvAt - iti doSasadbhAvAdeva nIlatvAdijAtipravRttinimittatvAprasakteH, tadbhayAt dravyeSu na zaktirvaktavyA | vyAkaraNaM ca nAnyathAkaraNIyam; kiMtu guNeSu nIlimAdizabdasiddhaye guNaviziSTeSu dravyeSu zaktiraGgIkaraNIyA | ato nAprayojakaM madanumAnam, tvadanumAnamaprayojakamiti || atha dvAdazI-- zakyamatra lAghavamUlakaikazaktipakSAnusAreNa nIlimAdizabdaprayogasya nIlatvAdijAtiviSayatAyAM iSTApattyA parihartum, 'ghaTasya nailyam' 'ghaTasya nIlatvam' iti [p.237] pratItivyavahArANAM viSayaikyAnubhavAt | na ca vAcyam 'pUraNaguNasuhitArthasadavyayatavyasamAnAdhikaraNena' iti sUtrabhASye guNavAcinA saha SaSThIsamAsaniSedhasya balAkAyAH zauklyaM kAkasya kArSNyam ityudAhRtam, ataH SyaJAdipratyayAntAnAM guNavAcizabdAnAM zuklatvAdiguNavAcitvameva aGgIkartavyamiti; tasminsUtre guNazabdasya rUpAdipaJcakAsAdhAraNyAbhAvAt 'guNavacanabrAhmaNAdibhyaH karmaNi ca' ityAdisUtreSviva dharmAntarasAdhAraNyAt | ata eva bhASye kaNTakasya taikSNyam ityapi guNavAcinA saha SaSThIsamAsaniSedhasya udAharaNAntaraM darzitam | avazyaM ca nIlatvAdijAtiSvapi tvayA nIlimAdizabdo'bhyupeyaH | anyathA tAsu nIlatvAdizabdo'pi na syAt | bhAvArthatvena tvataloH imanijAdyavizeSAt | tasmAdiSTApattyA parihArasaMbhavAt zaktipakSe 'nIlo ghaTaH' ityAdipratItivyavahArayoH tvadabhimataM nIlaguNaviziSTaviSayatvaM na sidhyediti tatsiddhimanvicchatA lakSaNaivAzrayaNIyA iti aprayojakaM tvadanumAnam, madanumAnaM ca nAprayojakamiti || atha trayodazI-- yadi sAkSAtparaMparAsaMbandhAbhyAM guNagatajAtireva guNaguNinoH nIlAdizabdapravRttau nimittamiti saiva nIlimAdizabdabhAgapi bhavet, tadA 'ghaTasya nIlimA' itivat 'ghaTarUpasya nIlimA' ityapi vyavahAraH prasajyeta | na caivaM sati sA nIlatvazabdabhAgapi na bhavet iti vAcyam, na hi varNadRDhAdisUtreNa tvatalorvidhiH; yena dravyaviSayebhya eva nIlAdizabdebhyastAvapi syAtAm; kiM tu 'tasya bhAvastvatalau' iti sUtreNa | tatra ca 'tasya' iti SaSThIsamarthamAtrAt tvatalau vidhIyamAnau guNavAcibhyo'pi nIlAdizabdebhyo bhavetAmeva | api ca dravyeSvapi nIlAdizabdAnAM yadi nIlatvAdijAtireva pravRttinimittaM bhavet, tadA tebhyo nIlatvAdiviziSTatayaiva dravyANAM pratItiH syAt; na tu nIlatvAdiviziSTaguNaviSayatayA | tathaiva ca tebhyastatpratItirabhyarthyate; matublopAnuzAsanadarzanAt vyavahAramUlabhUtapratyakSAnusArAcca | pratyakSeNa yathAbhUtamartham AptaH pazyati, tathAbhUtArthabubodhayiSayA sa vAkyaM prayuGke | zrotuzca vaktRtAtparyamavagacchataH tato vAkyAttathAbhUtArthaviSaya eva pratyayo bhavatIti vastusthitiH| iha ca nIlaM ghaTamavalokayataH puruSasya nIlaguNaviziSTadravyaviSayiNI [p.238] buddhiH jAyate; na tu nIlaguNagatanIlatvajAtiviziSTadravyabuddhiH; rUparUpiNoH samAnavittivedyayoH prakAraprakAribhAvena bhAnaniyamasya sarvasaMpratipannatvAt | tathA ca pratyakSamUlaH 'nIlo ghaTaH' iti vyavahAraH tajjanyazAbdapratyayazca kathaM nIlaguNaviziSTadravyaviSayau na bhavetAm | na ca 'nIlo ghaTaH' iti pratIteH 'nIlo guNaH' iti pratItyavizeSAnubhavasyApi sAmaJjasyArthaM matublopamanapekSya 'nIlo ghaTaH' iti vyavahAro nIlatvavaiziSTyaviSayaH, tamapekSya tadvyavahAro nIlaguNavaiziSTyaviSayaH iti kalpyatAmiti vAcyam; matublopavArtikAnantaraM pravRttena 'avyatirekAtsiddhamiti ceddRSTo vyatirekaH' iti vArtikena zuklAdizabdairabhidhIyamAnAnAM guNAnAmavyatirekAt guNyavyabhicArAt zuklaM vastram iti guNinA sahaivAbhidhAnAt zuklAdizabdazaktyaiva guNyabhidhAnasiddheH matublopAnuzAsanaM vyarthamiti zaGkAmudbhAvya, vastrasya zuklaH iti guNAbhidhAnasya guNivyabhicAradRSTeH kevalaguNavAcako'pi zuklAdizabdo'stIti tasya dravyaparyantatvavivakSAyAM matupaH prAptau tallopAnuzAsanaM sArthakamiti parihArArthaM pravRttena matublopAnapekSasyApi dravyeSu zuklAdizabdasya guNaviziSTAbhidhAyakatvAvagamAt zabdazaktinirNayAbhiyuktavRddhavyavahAraviruddhasya laukikAnAmitarasArUpyAnubhavasya pramANIkaraNAyogAt | tatpramANIkaraNe luptamatupprayogajanyajJAnAtyantasArUpyAnubhavabalAt tadanyasyApi 'nIlo ghaTaH' iti jJAnasya guNavaiziSTyaviSayatvakalpanasyaiva ucitatvAt | 'nIlo ghaTaH' iti prayogayoreva sArUpyam, na tajjanyajJAnayoriti kalpane 'nIlo guNaH' 'nIlo ghaTaH' iti prayogayoreva nIlatvAMze sArUpyam na tajjanyajJAnayoriti vaktuM zakyatvAt | tasmAt avyatirekavArtikAnurodhAya 'ghaTarUpasya nIlimA' ityAdi vyavahAraprasaGgaparihArAya ca dravyeSu nIlAdizabdAnAM nIlAdiguNA eva pravRttinimittatayA svIkAryAH, na tu nIlatvAdijAtayaH ; ityataH tvaduktadoSaprasaktyabhAvena nIlAdizabdAnAM nIlaguNAdiviziSTeSu dravyeSu zakteravazyAzrayaNIyatvAt siddhaM ghaTAdInAM nIlAdizabdavAcyatvasAdhakaM madanumAnaM nAprayojakam, teSAM tadavAcyatvasAdhakaM tvadanumAnameva aprayojakamiti || iti guNaguNyanekazaktivAdaH ||4||

[p.238] guNaguNyekazaktipakSazreyastvavAdaH || [p.239] evaM nIlAdizabdAnAM guNaguNiviSayaikAnekazaktipakSau darzitau | anayorekazaktipakSa eva zreyAniti nirNayArthaM kathA prastUyate-- tatra nIlo ghaTaH iti ghaTapadasamAnAdhikaraNanIlapadagatA zaktiH nIlaguNaviziSTaviSayA, nIlaguNapravRttinimittaketi yAvat, nIlaguNazaktapadagataghaTaviSayakazaktitvAt, yA nIlaguNazaktapadagatA ghaTaviSayakazaktiH, sA nIlaguNaviziSTaviSayA yathA prakRtanIlaguNaviziSTaparAmarzinaH 'sa ghaTaH' iti vAkye zrutasya sarvanAmnaH zaktiH | hetau nIlaguNazaktatvaM nIlatvajAtyA pravRttinimittena vivakSitam, ataH nIlaguNaghaTobhayaviSayavastupadAdizaktau na vyabhicAraH iti pUrvapakSiNaH prathamA kakSyA || gauravalakSaNapratikUlatarkaparAhatamidamanumAnam, nIlatvajAtyA pravRttinimittena nIlaguNe zaktasya nIlapadasya nIlaguNena pravRttinimittena dravye zaktyantarasvIkAre hi anekArthatvalakSaNaM gauravaM syAt | ataH pakSIkRteyaM zaktiH na nIlaguNaviziSTaviSayA, nIlatvajAtiprakArakabodhAnukUlazaktitvAt, yA nIlatvajAtiprakArakabodhAnukUlA zaktiH, sA na nIlaguNaviziSTaviSayA yathA tadIyanIlaguNaviSayA zaktiH | paramate nIlapadasya guNaviSayA zaktiH pakSIkRtadravyaviSayazaktito bhinnA dRSTAntaH | manmate pakSIkRtaiva zaktiH guNaviSayatvAkAreNa dRSTAntaH | sAdhyasAdhanavattvena saMpratipattirhi dRSTAntatvaprayojakA | na tu sarvathA pakSAdanyatrApi iti dvitIyA kakSyA || anekazaktikalpanAgauravaM na madanumAnabAdhakam, 'nIlo ghaTaH' 'nIlo guNaH' iti prayogadvayanirvAhakatayA tasya prAmANikatvAt | na hi guNaviSayazaktyA guNiviSayasyApi vyavahArasya nirvAho'sti | na ca lakSaNayA tannirvAhaH tvayApi abhyupagamyate | ataH tvadanumAnameva 'nIlo ghaTaH' iti prayogAsaMbhavalakSaNapratikUlatarkaparAhatam - iti tRtIyA kakSyA || [p.240] guNagatanIlatvajAtyaiva pravRttinimittena guNa iva dravye'pi pravRttiH saMbhavati, lAghavena sAkSAt paraMparayA vA pravRttinimittAzrayamAtrasya zabdavAcyatAprayojakatvAt| tasmAt ekayaiva zaktyA prayogadvayasyApi nirvAhAt madanumAnaM na pratikUlatarkabAdhitam, tvadanumAnameva tadbAdhitam iti caturthI kakSyA || evaM sati 'nIlo ghaTaH' ityasya nIlatvajAtiviziSTo ghaTaH ityevArthaH syAt; na tu nIlaguNaviziSTo ghaTaH iti | nIlaguNaviziSTo ghaTaH ityeva ca tadartho varNanIyaH nIlaguNaviziSTapratyakSamUlakasya pratyakSadRSTArthabubodhayiSayA prayuktasya tasya tadekaviSayatvaniyamAt; matublopAnuzAsanena matubantaprayogasamAnArthatvAvagatezca; nIlaguNaviSayanIlimAdizabdagatabhAvapratyayAnurodhAcca; yaH zabdo yaddharmaviziSTe pravartate tato bhAvapratyayaH tasmindharme iti niyamadarzanAt | tasmAdanekazaktikalpanAvazyaMbhAvAt madanumAnaM na gauravalakSaNapratikUlatarkaparAhatam, tvadanumAnameva 'nIlo ghaTaH' iti prayogAsaMbhavarUpatarkaparAhatam iti paJcamI kakSyA || nIlaguNavaiziSTyaviSayapratyakSAvagatArthatAtparyeNa prayuktasya 'nIlo ghaTaH' ityasya nIlaguNavaiziSTyapratipAdakatvaM bhavatu nAma | tAvatA nIlazabdasya nIlaguNaviziSTe zaktirna sidhyati; tvadudAhRtasya matublopAnuzAsanasyaiva tatpratikUlatvAt | nIlazabdamAtrasya nIlaguNavaiziSTyapratipAdanasAmarthye tadanuzAsanavaiyarthyApatteH | kevalaguNavAcakAH ye nIlAdizabdAH tebhyo matupaH prasaktirastIti tallopAnuzAsanaM sArthakamiti cet; na, tebhya eva luptamatuppratyayebhyo guNipratItyupapattau svato guNaviziSTazaktiyuktanIlAdizabdakalpane pramANAbhAvAt | guNavAcinAmeva matublope sati luptamatubarthAvagamArthaM guNaviziSTe'pi zaktiraGgIkaraNIyA iti cet; na; luptapratyayArthaM lupta eva pratyayaH smRto bodhayatIti pakSe taM prakRtirlakSayatIti pakSe ca tatra teSAM zakteranaGgIkaraNIyatvAt | pratyayalopasthale prakRtestadarthe'pi zaktyantarakalpanAbhyupagame tAdRzasthale lAghavAt pratyayArthamAtre prakRteH zaktyantarakalpanena
