ekapratyayopAttasaMkhyAkArakakRtISTasAdhanatvAdirItyA prakRtigatasAmAnyavizeSaH zakttatyupa[p.241]sthApitayoH prakRtyarthaluptapratyayArthayoH saMsargapratIterupapAdayituM zakyatayA gauravaparAhataprakRtyarthaviziSTapratyayArthagocarazaktyantarakalpanAyogAt | tasmAnmadanumAnaM na pratikUlatarkaparAhatam, tvadanumAnameva tadbAdhitam - iti SaSThI || matublopAnuzAsanaM luptamatupprakRtyaMzasyaiva guNaviziSTe zaktirastItyatra jJApakatvena nopanyastam, yena tadanuzAsanaM tatpratikUlaM syAt | kiMtu dravye pravartamAnasya nIlazabdasya nIlaguNaviziSTaparyantapratipAdakatvam aGgIkaraNIyam; na tu nIlatvAzrayamAtrapratipAdakatvam ityatra jJApakatvena | na ca tAvatA neSTasiddhiH matupprakRtyaMzasya guNaviziSTe zaktiM vinA tatpratipAdakatvasya niryUDhatvAditi vAcyam; tena tu prakRtyaMzasya guNaviziSTe zaktyasiddhAvapi matublopaM vinA dravye prayujyamAnasyApi nIlAdizabdasya tattulyanyAyena guNaviziSTaparyantatvasiddhau tataH tasya tatra zaktisiddheH | na ca tathAbhUtanIlazabdAntarasadbhAve pramANAbhAvaH, matublopAnuzAsanavArtikAnantaraM pravRttasya 'avyatirekAtsiddhamiti cenna dRSTo vyatirekaH' iti vArtikasya tatra pramANatvAt | taddhi vArtikam 'nIlo ghaTaH' iti svata eva nIlazabdasya dravyaparyantatvadRSTeH matubanuzAsanam vyartham iti zaGkAmudbhAvya dravyaparyantavat guNamAtravizrAnto'pi asti nIlAdizabdaH, 'ghaTasya nIlo guNaH' ityAdivyavahAradarzanAt; tatazca guNamAtravizrAntasya nIlAdizabdasya dravyaparyantatvavivakSAyAM matubutpattyA tacchravaNaM syAt, tanmAbhUt ityetadarthaM tallopAnuzAsanamiti parihAraM darzayituM pravRttam | tena matublopaM vinA dravye pravartamAno nIlAdizabdo'stIti spaSTameva avasIyate | tasmAt gauravasya prAmANikatvAt madanumAnaM na pratikUlatarkabAdhitam, tvadanumAnameva tadbAdhitam iti saptamI || dravyaparyantanIlAdizabdasadbhAve pramANAbhAvo mayA noktaH; kiMtu guNaviziSTarUpeNa dravyaparyantasya sadbhAve | na ca 'avyatirekAtsiddham' ityAdivArtikaM guNaviziSTarUpeNa dravyaparyantasya tasya sadbhAve pramANam, tasya nIlatvAdijAtiviziSTe tatra zaktisadbhAve prAmANyenApi cAritArthyAt | lAghavena sAkSAtparaMparAsAdhAraNyena pravRttinimittAzrayatvamAtrasya zabdavAcyatvaprayojakatayA nIlatvajAtiviziSTe dravye tasya zaktyavazyaMbhAvena tata etasya vArtikasya upapattyA nIlaguNaviziSTe dravye [p.242] zaktyantarakalpanAyAH aprAmANikatvAcca | avyatirekavArtikasya 'nIlo guNaH' iti guNavat 'nIlo ghaTaH' iti dravyasyApi guNagatanIlatvajAtyAzrayatayA pratIyamAnatvena guNaguNinoH avyatirekAt abhedAt guNa iva dravye'pi matublopaM vinaiva siddham 'nIlo ghaTaH' iti vyavahAraprayojakam - iti zaGkAmudbhAvya 'ghaTasya nIlaH' iti guNaguNinorbhedadRSTeH 'nIlo ghaTaH' iti pratItiH paraMparayA nIlatvAzrayaviSayeti dravye nIlaguNavaiziSTyabubodhayiSAyAM guNaviSayanIlAdizabdAt matupaH prAptau tallopAnuzAsanam - iti parihArArthatvopapatteH | tasmAt tvadanumAnaM pratikUlatarkaparAhatam, madanumAnaM ca na tadbAdhitam - iti aSTamI || dravyasya nIlatvajAtyAzrayatve syAdetadevam; tatraiva nAsti pramANam | na ca AzrayAzrayamAtram AzrayadvArA bhavatyAzrayaH iti niyamo'sti, rUpatvaghaTatvAdikaM prati ghaTabhUtalAdInAmAzrayatvAt | na ca 'nIlo ghaTaH' iti pratItibalAdeva ghaTasya nIlatvAzrayatvamabhyupagantavyam; tasya dravyaviSayaluptamatubantaprayogasamAnArthataucityAt | na hi dravya eva tattulyaprayogasya klRptaM viSayaM vinA viSayAntarakalpanaM yuktam | ataH zaktyantarakalpanAgauravasya prAmANikatvAt madanumAnaM na pratikUlatarkabAdhitam, tvadanumAnameva bAdhitam iti navamI ||
atha dazamI kakSyA-- ghaTasya nIlatvAzrayatve mA bhUt pramANAntaragaveSaNA; 'nIlo ghaTaH' ityavyatirekaprayoga eva tatra pramANam; tatra nIlazabdasya 'nIlo guNaH' iti prayogasAmyena nIlatvavaiziSTyaviSayatvAt | dravyaviSayaluptamatubantaprayogasAmyena nIlaguNavaiziSTyaviSayatvakalpane 'gauro'ham' ityAdibrahmavidanusaMdhAnamUlake prayoge gaurAdizabdAnAM gaurAdiguNa iva tadviziSTe zarIre zarIraviziSTe jIve jIvaviziSTe antaryAmiNi ca zaktiH kalpanIyA ityeva zakticatuSTayakalpanAprasaGgAt | na ca teSAM sAkSAdguNaviziSTa eva zaktiH, anyatra lakSaNeti na uktadoSaprasaGgaH iti vAcyam; tathA sati tatparyavasAnadRSTAntenaiva antaryAmiNi sarvazabdaparyavasAnavyavasthApanAya dRSTAntaM saMpAdayituM pravRttasya asya vAdasya tatpratikUlatvApatteH | na [p.243] ca sAkSAtparaMparAsAdhAraNyena nIlaguNavaiziSTyenaiva antaryAmiparyantaM zaktiriSyata iti vAcyam; tathA sati sAkSAt paraMparayA vA guNagatajAtivaiziSTyenaiva tatparyantaM zaktirupapadyata iti zaktidvayakalpanasyApi tyAgaucityAt | tasmAt anekazaktikalpanasya aprAmANikatvAt tvadanumAnaM pratikUlatarkaparAhatam, madanumAnaM na tadbAdhitamiti || athaikAdazI-- ghaTasya nIlatvajAtyAzrayatvamaklRptaM dharmaM parikalpya 'nIlo ghaTaH' ityasya tadviSayatvakalpanAt klRptasya nIlaguNavaiziSTyasyaiva gurorapi tadviSayatvakalpanaM jyAyaH| zakticatuSTayaM tu na prasajyate, guNe guNaviziSTe ca samarthitenaiva zaktidvayena sarvanirvAhAt | sarvAdhArasya antaryAmiNaH guNasyeva guNagatajAtyAzrayatayA guNazaktyaiva antaryAmiNi arzaAdyajantatayA jIve ca prayoganirvAhAt | 'arzaAdibhyo'c' iti sUtragRhIte arzaAdigaNe paThito varNazabdo varNavizeSavAcI, zuklanIlagaurAdizabdavat tebhya ajvidhAnAt zuklAdiguNavadravyaviziSTapratItinirvAhArtham - iti nyAsakRtA uktatvAt | ataH zaktidvayakalpanasya prAmANikatvAt atiriktazaktikalpanAgauravaprasaGgAcca madanumAnaM na pratikUlatarkaparAhatam, tvadanumAnameva tadbAdhitam iti || atha dvAdazI-- sAkSAt guNagatajAtyAzrayatvam antaryAmiNastAvadasiddham | paraMparayA tadAzrayatvamiSyate cet, paraMparAghaTakayoH dehajIvayorapi tadAzrayatvam anicchatApi svIkartavyamiti dehatulyanyAyatayA ghaTe'pi guNagatajAtyAzrayatvasiddheH tvadApAditAklRptadharmAntarakalpanAgauravasya na prasaktiH; ekazaktipakSa eva ca paryavasyet | athApi guNaviziSTe dehe pRthak zaktirabhyupagamyeta, tathA sati tadviziSTe tadviziSTe ca pRthak pRthak zaktirabhyupetavyA iti zakticatuSTayakalpanAgauravam anivAryam | arzaAdyajantatayA varNito jIve zaktiM vinA nirvAho'pi na yujyate, varNavAcibhyaH [p.244] ajvidhAnasya zuklAdiguNavadravyaviziSTapratItinirvAhArthatvakalpanAyAM pramANAbhAvAt| tasmAt tvadanumAnaM pratikUlatarkabAdhitam, madanumAnaM na tadbAdhitam iti || atha trayodazI-- 'akSaramambarAntadhRteH' iti sUtre paramezvarasya sarvAdhAratvamuktam; tat sAkSAtsarvAdhAratva eva saMbhavati | ataH tasya avazyaM sAkSAdeva nIlatvAdyAdhAratvam aGgIkaraNIyam iti na tatra guNazaktivyatirekeNa zaktyantarakalpanaM prasajyate | na vA guNazaktyaiva ghaTe dehajIvayozca prayoganirvAho bhavati | guNavAcibhyaH ajvidhAnaM guNavadviziSTapratItinirvAhArtham ityavazyaM kalpanIyam, zuklaguNaviziSTaiH prAsAdaiH viziSTe nagare 'zuklaM nagaram' iti prayoganirvAhArthaM taditi nyAsakRtaiva uktatvAt | ataH jIve'pi zaktyantarakalpanaM na prasajyate | tasmAt madanumAnaM na pratikUlatarkaparAhatam, tvadanumAnameva tadbAdhitam - iti || atha caturdazI-- sAkSAdAzraya eva AdhAra iti na niyamaH; tRNavastrAntaritaghaTAdidhArakatvasya puruSabhUtalAdiSu paryavasAnadarzanAt| puruSasya ghaTAdidhAraNaprayuktatklezodayena kUpAcchAdaneSu tRNeSu kUpasyopari prasArite vastre vA ghaTAdinidhAne sati tadanavasthAnataH tRNAdInAM taddhAraNazaktyabhAvanirNayena ca tatparyavasAnAvasAyAt | ataH paramezvarasya sAkSAt nIlatvAzrayatvaM nIlaguNAzrayatvaM vA nAbhyupetavyamiti na guNazaktyA guNizaktyA vA tatra guNavacanaprayoganirvAhaH | varNavAcibhyo vihitasyAco guNavattvamevArthaH na tu guNavadvaiziSTyam | 'zuklaM nagaram' ityAdivyavahArastu madrItyA nagarasya paraMparayA zuklatvajAtyAzrayatayA nirvAhyaH | tvadrItyA guNavati guNavadviziSTe'pi; na tu arzaAdyacA tadvidhAnasya guNavadviSayakatvamapahAya guNavadviziSTaviSayatvakalpanena guNavati prasaktayoH iniThanoH acA bAdhanAlAbhena 'nIlI' 'nIlikaH' ityapi prayogaprasaGgAt | tasmAt guNe guNini ca zaktibhedamabhyupagacchatA guNavadviziSTe jIve tadviziSTe antaryAmiNi ca punarapi zaktidvayakalpanaM Apatet - [p.245] iti anivAryaM gauravam | ataH tvadanumAnaM pratikUlatarkaparAhatam, madanumAnaM na tadbAdhitam - iti || atha paJcadazI-- dhArakamAtrasya sAkSAt AdhAratAniyamAbhAve'pi upAdAnatayA dhArakasya asti tanniyamaH; sarvopAdAnaM ca paramezvaraH ataH tasya nIlatvanIlaguNAdikaM pratyapi sAkSAdAdhAratvasya avazyAzrayaNIyatvAt guNazaktyA guNizaktyA vA tasmin varNavAcizabdaprayogo nirvahatIti na tatra zaktyantarakalpanAgauravaM prasajyate | ajvidhAnaM guNavadviSayameva iti niyamo na abhyupagamyate; kiM tu guNavadviSayaM guNavadviziSTaviSayaM ca ityabhyupagamyate | arzaAdigaNe paThitena ca varNazabdena 'varNo varNena' iti sUtrapaThiteneva guNazaktAnAM guNizaktAnAM ca zuklAdizabdAnAM grahaNAt | evaM sati iniThanorbAdhanam 'zuklaM nagaram' ityAdiprayoganirvAhazca ityubhayamapi aJjasA upapadyate - iti na guNavadviziSTanagare jIve ca zaktyantarakalpanAgauravaM prasajyate | tasmAt jIve paramezvare ca vinaiva zaktyantarakalpanaM guNavacanaprayoganirvAhAt madanumAnaM na pratikUlatarkaparAhatam, tvadanumAnameva tadbAdhitam - iti || atha SoDazI-- yatpariNAmitayA upAdAnam, tasyaivAvasthAvizeSaviziSTapariNAmarUpasya ekadezaM prati sAkSAdAdhAratvaM niyatam yathA antaHkaraNasya svavRttiM prati | na ca paramezvaraH tathA upAdAnam, nirvikAratvAt; kiM tu prakRtyadhikaraNoktanyAyena prapaJcAkArapariNAmicicchaktatyapRthaksiddhAzrayatayA | ataH paramezvarasya upAdAnatve'pi na sAkSAnnIlaguNAdyAzrayatvaM vAcyamiti na tena tatra guNazaktyA vA guNavacanaprayoganirvAhaH | paraMparayA nIlatvajAtyAzrayatvamavalambya tatra guNazaktyA tannirvAhaH kriyate cet, guNinaH prayogo'pi tathaiva nirvahatIti tava guNizaktirapi na sidhyet | ajvidhAnaM matublopavidhAnavat guNavadravyaviSayameva; na tu guNa[p.246]vadravyatadviziSTobhayaviSayam; anyathA matublopavidhAnaM tadubhayaviSayam ajvidhAnaM kevalaguNavadviSayamiti vaiparItyasyApi kalpanApatteH | guNavadviziSTaviSayasyApi kasyacit matvarthIyapratyayasya kalpane gauravam | putrabhAryAdiSu gaurAdimatsu pitRbhartrAdiSu 'gauro'yam' iti vyavahArApatteH; guNavadviziSTaviSayavat tadviziSTaviSayasyApi kalpayituM zakyatayA antaryAmiNyapi guNavacanAnAM zaktyasiddhyApattezca zakyate hi guNidehazaktAdgaurAdizabdAt arzaAdyacA dehaviziSTajIvagrahaNe sati tato matublopena antaryAmiNyapi prayoganirvAhaH kartum | matvarthIyapratyayAntAt punastatsarUpasya pratyayAntarasyAniSTAvapi 'daNDimatI zAlA' ityAdau tadvirUpasya tasya saMpratipannatvAt | sAkSAt paraMparayA vA guNagatajAtyAzrayatvena guNazaktireva paramezvarasAdhAraNI sidhyet iti zaGkA tu prAgeva nirastA | na ca ghaTAdInAM paramparayA vA guNagatajAtyAzrayatvAbhAve'pi paramezvarasya paramparayA tadAzrayatvamastyeva, anyathA tasya taddhArakatvAbhAvaprasaGgAt iti vAcyam; tathA sati paramparAghaTakayordehajIvayorapi tadAzrayatvaklRptatAyA vaktavyatvena tatklRptereva nyAyataulyena ghaTAdiSvapi tatklRptirUpatApattyA tava guNazaktibhinnAyA guNizakterapyasiddhiprasaGgAt | gurutve sati Apatati dravya eva dhArakApekSaNena svadharmyAzrayaNamAtranirvRteSu jAtyAdiSu tadanapekSaNAcca | tasmAt sarvAntaryAmiNaH paramezvarasya sarvazabdavAcyatvamicchatA tvayA matvarthIyapratyayAdisAdhyanirvAhadurAzayamapahAya guNavacanAnAM guNaguNinoriva guNaviziSTe jIve jIvaviziSTe'ntaryAmiNi ca bhinnabhinnA zaktirabhyupetavyA - ityanivArya zakticatuSTayakalpanAgauravamiti tvadanumAnaM pratikUlatarkabAdhitam; madanumAnaM na tadbAdhitam | tasmAt uktagauravaparihArArtham 'nIlo ghaTaH' 'mama deho gauraH' 'gauro'ham' ityAdipratItivyavahArANAM paramparayA guNagatajAtyAzrayatvaviSayatvamabhyupagamya guNazaktireva guNiSu ghaTadehAdiSu dehaviziSTeSu jIveSu jIvaviziSTe'ntaryAmiNi ca prasRtA bhavatIti ekazaktipakSa evAGgIkaraNIyaH | avazyam 'pANDuraham' 'babhruraham' 'zveto'ham' 'dehaH' ityajantatvAdisaMbhAvanArahitapratItivyavahAreSu eSaiva gatirAzrayaNIyA | na hi dehAtmabhramasamayeSveva 'pANDuraham' ityAdivyavahAraH, dehaviviktAtmajJAnasamayeSu tu ajantatvAdisaMbhAvanAspadagaurAdizabdavyavahAramAtramiti tvatkalpitamaryAdAnusAreNa niyantuM zakyate | etena aklRptadharmAntarakalpanAgauravazaGkApi nirastA; 'babhruraham' [p.247] ityAdivyavahAreSu tatkalpanAvazyaMbhAvAt | zakticatuSTayakalpanApekSayA asya gauravasya lAghavarUpatvAcca | evam 'zuklaM nagaram' ityAdivyavahAreSvapi zuklatvAdiparamparAsaMbandhAzrayaNameva gatiH | tatrApi hi 'pANDu nagaram' 'azvetannagaram' 'adahyata nagaram' ityAdivyavahAreSu evaM gatiravazyamAzrayaNIyA | tathaiva 'apuSpyadvanaM' 'adahyata vanam' ityAdivyavahAro dRzyate | yadi tu ekadezasthAnAM vRkSANAM vanazabdArthatvAt teSu sAkSAdeva puSpadahanAdisaMbandho'stItyucyeta, tadA nyAyataulyAt saMkhyAsaMnivezaviziSTA gRhA eva nagarapadArthatAmApadyeranniti teSu sAkSAt zuklAdiguNasaMbandhasattvAt 'zuklaM nagaram' ityAdivyavahAranirvAhArtham ajvidhAnasya guNavadravyaviSayatvakalpanAdurAzA nitAntaM nirAlambanA syAt | 'nagare gRhAH' iti vyavahArAt gRhavizeSANAmAzrayaH pradezo nagaram iti cet, vane vRkSAH iti vyavahArAt vRkSavizeSANAmAzrayaH pradezo vanam iti tulyam | tasmAdvanapuSpitatvavyavahArataulyAdapi nagarazauklyAdivyavahAraH paramparAsaMbandhAvalambanaH | etena 'varNo varNena' iti sUtragateneva arzaAdigaNagatenApi varNazabdena guNazaktAnAM guNizaktAnAM ca zuklAdizabdAnAM grahaNamavarjanIyam ityapi zaGkA nirastA | yathA 'varNadRDhAdibhyaH SyaJca' iti sUtragatena dharmiparadRDhAdizabdasAhacaryAt guNizaktAnAmeva grahaNam, tathA atra pUrvoktabAdhakavazAt 'zvetaraktaH pATalIkusumavarNaH' 'zvetaraktaM pATalIkusumam' iti tadudAharaNavat etadudAharaNasya kasyacit ubhayavidhazuklAdizabdagrahaNApekSasya adarzanAcca atra guNizaktAnAmeva grahaNamityupapatteH | na ca matublopenaiva guNaviziSTapratItyupapatteH ajvidhAnaM guNavadviziSTaviSayameva syAt ityapi zaGkanIyam, tathA sati guNavadviSaye prAptayoH iniThanoH acA bAdhanAbhAvaprasaGgasya bAdhakasya uktatvAt| luptamatubantasya ajantasya ca zuklAdizabdasya rUpAbhede'pi arthAbhede'pi ktavyaktavyanyAyena svaravizeSasiddhyarthaM pratyayavidhAnopapattezca | tasmAdekazaktipakSa eva zreyAniti niravadyaH panthAH || iti ekazaktipakSazreyastvavAdaH ||5 ||

[p.248] guNalakSaNApakSanirAsavAdaH || kecit guNavacanAnAM zabdAnAM guNaviziSTeSu dravyeSu zaktimabhyupagamya tadvizeSaNeSu guNeSu 'gaurnityA' ityAdiprayogagatAnAM gavAdizabdAnAM gotvAdiSviva lakSaNAM vadanti | tanmate zaktiH tattadguNaviziSTe'pi na vizrAmyati; kiMtu tattadguNaviziSTazarIraviziSTajIvaviziSTAntaryAmiNi paryavasyati | tathaiva ca tairiSyate | ata eva te 'svargakAmo yajeta' ityAdiprayogeSu yAgAdyadhikArisamarpakANAM svargakAmAdizabdAnAm antaryAmiNi zaktAvapi kSudraphalAbhilASeNa antaryAmiNa Izvarasya yAgAdipravRttyasaMbhavAt mukhyArthAnupapattyA tadvizeSaNe svargakAmanAvati jIve lakSaNetyAhuH | tamimamantaryAmyekazaktivAdaM nirasituM kathA prastUyate - tatra nIlaguNaH na svAtantryeNa nIlazabdazaktiviSayaH, nIlazabdazaktiviziSTaikadezatvAt, yo nIlazabdazaktiviziSTaikadezaH, na saH svAtantryeNa nIlazabdazaktiviSayaH yathA nIlatvajAtivizeSaH iti prathamA kakSyA || aprayojakamidamanumAnam, sadbhAvavastvAdizabdAnAM svazaktiviziSTaikadezeSu sattvabhAvatvavastutvAdiSvapi zaktivat ihApyupapatteH | tasmAt nIlaguNaH svAtantryeNa nIlazabdazakyaH, nIlaguNatadviziSTAnyApratiyogikabhedapratiyogitvAt, yat nIlaguNatadviziSTAnyApratiyogikabhedapratiyogi, tat svAtantryeNa nIlaguNazakyam yathA nIlaguNaviziSTam - iti dvitIyA || na aprayojakaM madanumAnam, sattAdiSu sadvyavahAradarzanena 'pramANaM zaraNaM vRttau na bhinnAbhinnate yataH' iti nyAyamanusRtya sattAdisthityabhyupagamAt sattAdInAM pravRttinimittavatAM svAtantryeNa sadAdizabdazakyatve'pi nIlaguNAdInAM nIlaguNAdirUpapravRttinimittAnAzrayatayA svAtantryeNa nIlAdizabdazakyatvaM na saMbhavati - ityevaMrUpAnukUlatarkasadbhAvAt guNagatanIlatvAdijAtInAmapi nIlAdizabdapravRttinimittatvakalpane gauravAt | ataH nAprayojakaM madanumAnam; tvadanumAnamevAprayojakam, [p.249] guNinAM pravRttinimittAzrayatayA nIlAdizabdazakyatve'pi guNAnAM tadanAzrayatayA tadazakyatvopapatteH iti tRtIyA || atha caturthI-- tvadanumAnaM tAvadaprayojakameva, nIlAdiguNAnAM pravRttinimittAzrayatayA nIlAdizabdazakyatvopapatteH | na ca tadasiddhiH, 'nIlo guNaH' iti sArvalaukikavyavahAropapattaye nIlatvAdijAtInAmapi tatpravRttinimittatvAbhyupagamAt - iti || atha paJcamI-- nIlatvAdijAtInAmapi tatpravRttinimittatvAbhyupagame gauravaM bhavet | 'nIlo guNaH' ityAdivyavahArastu 'gaurnityA' ityAdivyavahAraH gozabdasya gotva iva nIlAdizabdAnAM guNeSu lakSaNayApi upapadyate - iti || atha SaSThI-- uktagauravaM na doSaH, matublopAnuzAsanazAstraprApitasya prAmANikatvAt | taddhi zAstraM nIlAdiguNaviziSTadravyapratItyartham | tena tatprakRtInAM nIlAdizabdAnAM guNeSu zaktiH sidhyet | rUpavAnityAdimatubantaprakRtInAM rUpAdizabdAnAmiva iti || atha saptamI-- matublopAnuzAsanaM na nIlaguNavaiziSTyapratItyartham; kiM tu guNiviSayANAM teSAM vizeSyaliGgagrAhitvasiddhyarthaM svaravizeSasiddhyarthaM ca 'svaraliGgavizeSArthaM matublopAnuzAsanam | viziSTavAcakatvaM tu tadAyattaM na manmahe' iti hi manmatam | [p.250] tasmAt matublopAnuzAsanAnusAreNa na nIlAdiguNAnAM nIlAdizabdazakyatvasiddhiriti nAprayojakaM madanumAnam, tvadanumAnaM tu aprayojakameva - iti || athASTamI-- svaravizeSasiddhyarthaM matublopAnuzAsanam iti tAvadayuktam, nIlAdizabdeSu matublopAyattasvaravizeSasiddhyudAharaNasya kasyacidadarzanAt | liGgavizeSasiddhyarthamityapi na yuktam, zabdasvAbhAvyAdeva tatsiddheH | dRzyante hi zabdAH svabhAvAdeva kvacidarthe vyavasthitaikaliGgAH anyatra dviliGgAH aparatra triliGgAzca | ekasminkhalvapi arthe kecana zabdAH svabhAvAt dviliGgAH triliGgAzca bhUyAMsaH arthabhedena vyavasthitAH | vyavasthitaliGgatve ekaliGgAnupapattiH | tasmAt 'avyatirekAtsiddhamiti cet' iti matublopAnuzAsanavaiyarthyAzaGkottareNa 'dRSTo vyatirekaH' iti zabdavArtikena nIlo ghaTaH ityasya vyatirekavat ghaTasya nIlo guNaH iti vyatirekasyApi dRSTatvAt nIlAdizabdAnAM guNeSvapi svAtantryeNa zaktisadbhAvAt guNazaktAnAM teSAM dravyaparyantatvavivakSAyAM matupaH prAptau tallopAnuzAsanaM sArthakam ityevamarthakena uktarItimanusRtya na matublopAnuzAsanasya tadeva prayojanaM kalpayituM yuktam - iti || atha navamI-- matublopAnuzAsanaM liGgavizeSAdisiddhyarthamityeva vAcyam, arzaAdigaNe varNazabdapAThenaiva varNavizeSavAcibhyo'jvidhAnasya matvarthIyapratyayAntarabAdhakatayA guNaviziSTabodhanArthaM matupaH prasaktyabhAvAt| tadabAdhakatAyAm 'ata iniThanau' iti sUtrataH iniThanorapi prAptyA nIlI nIlikaH ityapi rUpAntarasiddhiprasaGgAt; matupa iva tayoH lopAnuzAsanAbhAvAt | 'dRSTo vyatirekaH' iti vArtikaM tu mukhyaparihAraparaM na bhavati | ata eva tadanantarapravRttena 'tathA ca liGgavacanasiddhiH' iti vArtikAntareNa matublopAnuzAsanasya liGgavizeSAdisiddhyarthatvameva uktam - iti || atha dazamI-- nIlAdizabdebhyo matupaH prasaktirnAstItyetadasiddham, ajvidhAnasya matvarthIyapratyayAntarabAdhakatve'pi matuppratyayabAdhakatvAbhAvAt | 'prANisthAdAto lajanyatarasyAm' iti sUtragatasya anyatarasyAMgrahaNasya sarvatraiva matvarthIyaprakaraNe matupsamuccayArthatvena anuvRtteriSTatvAt| sarvatrAnuvartamAnasyApi tasya kvacitkvacit matvarthIyAnekapratyayaprAptau tatra ekaniyamanasAmarthyAt anabhidhAnAdvA bAdhanasattve'pi guNavacaneSu tadbAdhanAGgIkAre tallopavidhAyizAstrAvatAravaiphalyaprasaGgAt | na ca liGgAdisiddhimAtraM tatprayojanam; luptasyApi pratyayasyArthavattvAvazyaMbhAvena tanmAtrasya tatphalatvAsaMbhavAt, tasyApyajantatayaiva siddhisaMbhavAcca | na caivaM tarhi tadvaiphalyameva abhyupeyate iti vAcyam; tathApi acpratyayaprakRtInAM guNazakterapratikSepyatayA bhASyakRdanumoditasya tasya vaiphalyAbhyupagamasAhasasya niSphalatvAt | tasmAt nIlAdizabdAnAM niSkRSTeSu guNeSvapi zaktirastIti teSAM dravyaparyantatvavivakSAyAM matupaH prasakteH tadazravaNArthaM tallopAnuzAsanamityevam 'siddho vyatirekaH' iti vArtikoktaM tasya tatphalatvamavazyamabhyupagantavyam | liGgavacanasiddhivArtikaM tu AnuSaGgikaprayojanAbhiprAyam | evaM ca nIlAdizabdAnAM nIlAdiguNa iva tadviziSTe zarIre zarIraviziSTe jIve jIvaviziSTe'ntaryAmiNi ca zaktiH syAditi gauravApAdanamiSTameva, prAmANikatvAt | anyathA sarveSAM zabdAnAmantaryAmiNyeva zaktiH anyatra sarvatra lakSaNA ityabhyupagame brahmamImAMsAdhikaraNeSu anyaviSayazrutiliGgAdimUlakAnAM pUrvapakSANAmanutthAnaprasaGgAt | tasmAt matublopAnuzAsanazAstrAnusAreNa nIlAdizabdAnAM nIlAdiguNeSu zakteH tadanuguNapravRttinimittasya avazyamabhyupagantavyatvAt teSAM zakyaviziSTaikadezatve'pi sattAdinyAyena zakyatvamupapadyate iti aprayojakaM tvadanumAnam, madanumAnameva tu uktarUpAnukUlatarkasadbhAvAt prayojakamiti siddhaM nIlAdiguNeSvapi zaktiriti || iti guNalakSaNApakSanirAsavAdaH ||6||

[p.252] aruNaikahAyanIsAmAnAdhikaraNyavAdaH || evaM guNavacanAnAM zabdAnAM zaktivaicitryAdinA guNiparazabdasAmAnAdhikaraNyena tadvaiyadhikaraNyena ca prayogaH saMbhavatIti sthitau 'aruNayA piGgAkSyaikahAyanyA somaM krINAti' iti vAkyAmnAtam 'aruNayA' iti padam 'piGgAkSyA ekahAyanyA' iti padAbhyAM sAmAnAdhikaraNyena svArthaM pratipAdayati, tadvaiyadhikaraNyena vA iti bhavati saMzayaH || tatra pUrvamImAMsakAH prAhuH eko hAyano'syA astIti yaugiko'pi ekahAyanIzabdaH dhenuzabdavat gosamavetayaugikArthapratipAdakatvAt gaurUpAnyapadArthaparaH | tathA ca 'ekahAyanyA' iti padena ekahAyanaviziSTA gauH somakrayabhAvanAM prati karaNatvena pratipAdyate | piGgAkSIzabdo'pi bahuvrIhisamAsatvAt piGgAkSatvaguNaviziSTe kvacidanyapadArthe vartamAnaH samabhivyAhRtapadAntaropasthApitaM godravyamavalambate | tataH tRtIyAvibhaktiH vizeSaNavibhaktitvAt prayogasAdhutvArthA, vizeSyavibhaktipratipAditagodravyagatakaraNatvAnuvAdinI vA | 'aruNayA' ityatra tRtIyAvibhaktistu na tadgatakaraNatvAnuvAdinI; aruNAzabdasya hi piGgAkSI iti bahuvrIheriva dravyaparatvAvazyaMbhAvAbhAvena upAttadravyatayA guNamAtraparatvAvagamena ca tasyA guNagatakaraNatvapratipAdakatvasaMbhavAt | tatsaMbhave ca kArakavibhakteranuvAdakatvena vaiyarthyakalpanAyogAt | tasmAt 'aruNayA' iti padaM piGgAkSyaikahAyanIpadavaiyadhikaraNyena vAkyArthapratipAdanopayogi | tasya tAbhyAM sAmAnAdhikaraNyapratItistu vibhaktyaikyanibandhanA bhrAntirUpeti | vayaM tu pratItasAmAnAdhikaraNyAbAdhena guNagatakaraNatvapratipAdanasaMbhavAt na tadvAdhanaM yuktamiti 'aruNayA' iti padaM piGgAkSyaikahAyanIpadasAmAnAdhikaraNyenaiva vAkyArthapratipAdanopayogi iti siddhAntamurarIkurmaH | tamimaM siddhAntaM vyavasthApayituM kathA prastUyate 'aruNayA piGgAkSyaikahAyanyA somaM krINAti' iti vAkyAmnAtam 'aruNayA' iti padam 'ekahAyanyA' iti padasAmAnAdhikaraNyena vAkyArthapratipAdakam, yathA 'piGgAkSyA' iti padam - iti prathamA kakSyA || [p.253] guNadravyarUpabhinnArthaparayoH aruNaikahAyanIzabdayoH sAmAnAdhikaraNyAsaMbhavAt bAdhitamidam anumAnam | na ca guNavacanAnAM kevalaguNa iva guNinyapi sAmarthyasadbhAvAt 'vAyavyaM zvetamAlabheta' ityAdivaidikaprayogeSu zvetAdizabdAnAM dravyaparyantatvasya 'naimittike kAryatvAtprakRte syAttadApatteH' iti dazamAdhyAyAdhikaraNe vyavahRtatvAcca aruNAzabdasyApi dravyaparyantatvamiSyatAm, ato nAsti sAmAnAdhikaraNyabAdha iti vAcyam; padAntareNAnupAtte hi dravye guNavacanAnAM dravyaparyantatvam; na tu upAtte'pi; 'paTasya zuklo guNaH' 'etadvai pazUnAM bhUyiSThaM rUpaM yadrohitam' ityAdilaukikavaidikaprayogeSu upAtte dravye teSAM kevalaguNaparatvadarzanena tathaiva vyutpattisiddhatvAt | tathA ca 'zvetamAlabheta' ityAdau padAntareNa dravyavizeSAnupAdAnAt zvetazabdasya guNavadravyaparatve'pi atra ekahAyanIzabdenAvizeSopAdAnAt aruNAzabdasya kevalaguNaparatvaM yuktam | na ca SaSThyantena dravyopAdAnaM kevalaguNaparatve prayojakamiti vAcyam; lAghavena dravyopAdAnamAtrasya etatprayojakatvopapatteH | na ca dravyopAdAnamAtramapi na tatra prayojakam, 'nIlo ghaTaH' ityatra vyabhicArAditi vAcyam; tatra dravyaparapadasAmAnAdhikaraNasya guNaparatvabAdhakatvena klRptasya sattvAt | SaSThyantapadena dravyopAdAnaM guNaparatvaprayojakamiti vadatApi 'ghaTasya nIlo bhAgaH' ityatra vyabhicAravAraNArthaM tasya tadvAdhakatvaklRpteravazyAbhyupagantavyatvAt | na caivaM tarhi 'aruNayA piGgAkSyaikahAyanyA' ityatrApi sAmAnAdhikaraNyaM tadvAdhakaM syAditi vAcyam; tatra kArakavibhakteH sAmAnAdhikaraNyabAdhikAyAH sattvAt | anyathA kArakavibhaktivaiyarthyApatteH | 'ghaTasya nIlo bhAgaH' ityatra 'nIlo ghaTaH' ityatreva na kArakavibhaktirasti | kiMca ubhayatrApi sAmAnAdhikaraNyabAdhe vAkyabhedaH syAt | aruNAvAkye tu na tatprasaktirasti; AruNyena yathoktaguNakena godravyeNa ca viziSTAyAH krayabhAvanAyA vidhAtuM zakyatvAt | kArakavibhaktisattve'pi vAkyabhedaprasaGge tAmanAdRtya sAmAnAdhikaraNyamiSyate yathA 'nIlaM ghaTamAhara' ityAdau | yatra tu kArakavibhaktirasti vAkyabhedo'pi na prasajyate 'nIlena ghaTena jalamAhara' ityAdau tathAbhUtamudAharaNamaruNAvAkyatulyamastu | na ca udAharaNadvaye'pi amUrtasya guNasya kriyAsAdhanatvAsaMbhavo doSaH, sAkSAttadasaMbhave'pi dravyaparicchedadvArA tatsaMbhavAt | na ca tathA sati dravyAntarasyApi taddvAratvaprasaktiH, vAkyArthapratItivelAyAM dravyaguNayoH parasparAnvayaM vinA pratyekaM kriyAsAdhanatayA anvaye'pi [p.254] dravyasya paricchedakaguNAkAGgayA guNasya paricchedyadravyAkAGgayA ca pazcAdArthikaparasparAnvayopapatteH | tasmAtsAmAnAdhikaraNyAsaMbhavAt anumAnamidaM bAdhitameva | pratyanumAnaM ca - aruNAvAkyAmnAtam 'aruNayA' iti padaM na 'ekahAyanyA' iti padasAmAnAdhikaraNyena vAkyArthapratipAdakam yathA 'aruNimnA zobhate pAdaH' iti laukikavAkyagatam aruNimnA iti padam ityevaM dvitIyA kakSyA ||
atha tRtIya kakSyA-- pratIyamAnasAmAnAdhikaraNyAbAdhena vAkyArthanirvAhe saMbhavati na tadvAdhanaM yuktam | na ca kArakavibhaktisAphalyAya tadvAdhanam; aruNAzabdasya guNagatajAtyaiva pravRttinimittena dravyaparyantatvamiti pakSe guNagatajAteH dravye paramparAsaMbandhaghaTakatayA arthAkSipte guNe kArakavibhaktyarthasaMkramopapatteH | 'AgneyamaSTAkapAlaM nirvapet' ityatra AkhyAtArthasya vidheH prakRtyarthaM nirvApamatilaGghaya dravyadevatAsaMbandhAkSipte yAge saMkrAntyabhyupagamAt | tasya guNena pravRttinimittena dravyaparyantatvamiti pakSe vizeSyamatilaGghaya vizeSaNe tatsaMkramopapatteH | 'caturo muSTInnirvapati' iti saMkhyAmuSTirUpakArakadvayaviziSTanirvApavidhau 'caturaH' iti dvitIyoktakArakatvasya tatprAtipadikena vizeSyatayA abhihitaM samabhivyAhRtamuSTidravyarUpaM saMkhyeyamatilaGghaya tadvizeSaNe saMkhyAyAM saMkrAntyabhyupagamAt | aruNAzabdasya arzaAdyajantatayA dravyaparyantatvamiti kalpane vibhaktyarthasya taddhitArthamatilaGghaya tatprakRtyarthe saMkrAntyupapatteH, 'daNDI praiSAnanvAha' ityAdau tathA darzanAt | tasya luptamatubantatvakalpane luptasya matupo'rthaH prakRtyaiva vizeSazaktyA lakSaNayA vA bodhanIyaH iti dravyamapahAya tatsAmAnyazaktyupasthApite guNe vibhaktyarthAnvayopapatteH 'lohitoSNISA lohitavasanA RtvijaH pracaranti' iti zyenayAgavaizeSikAGgavidhAyake vAkye 'lohitavasanAH' iti bahuvrIhisamAsavartipadayoH sAmAnAdhikaraNyanirvAhArthaM lohitazabdasya avazyavaktavyaM dravyaparyantatvaM matublopena nirvAhyamiti tatra prakRteH sAmAnyavizeSavRttibhyAmupasthApyayoH guNadravyayormadhye dravyamapahAya guNe vidhisaMkrAntisaMpratipatteH aruNApadaM guNaparameva; guNaguNinorabhedopacAreNa 'aruNayA ekahAyanyA' iti padayoH sAmAnAdhikaraNyamiti tannirvAhe, Rjunaiva mArgeNa guNe karaNa[p.255]kArakatvAnvayasaMbhavAt | na ca eteSAM pakSANAM madhye vizeSyaM vihAya vizeSaNe vibhaktyarthAnvayaH iti pakSe 'piGgAkSyA' iti pade'pi tathAtvApattirdoSaH ; iSTApatteH | na hi 'aruNayA' iti pada eva kArakavibhaktiH sAphalyaM netavyA, na 'piGgAkSyA' iti pade ityatra vizeSaheturasti | na ca AruNyasyeva paiGgalyaviziSTasya akSNaH krayasAdhanatvaM na saMbhavati | na ca anyadIyasyApi paiGgalyaviziSTasyAkSNaH uddhRtasya krayasAdhanatvaM saMbhavat, 'azvazaphena krINAti' iti vAkyAdazvazaphasyeveti AruNyasyeva tasya niyamena godravyAnvayApekSA na syAditi vAcyam; arthataH svAtantryasaMbhave'pi bahuvrIhisamAsena godravyavizeSaNabhUtasyaiva tasya samarpaNAt | tasmAt sAmAnAdhikaraNyasya atrAsaMbhavAt nAsti madanumAne bAdhazaGkA | tvadanumAnaM tu aprayojakam, 'aruNimnA zobhate' iti laukikavAkye ekahAyanIpadAbhAvAt imanicpratyayasadbhAvAcca tatsAmAnAdhikaraNyapratipAdakatvAbhAve'pi, atra ekahAyanyAdizravaNena tatsAmAnAdhikaraNyavirodhino bhAvapratyayasya abhAvena ca tatsAmAnAdhikaraNyena vAkyArthapratipAdakatvopapatteH iti ||
atha caturthI kakSyA-- aruNAzabdasya guNamAtraparatvamaGgIkRtya Arjavena tatra vibhaktyarthAnvayasaMgamanAya samAnapadazrutinirvAhopapattau vAkyarUpapadAntarasAmAnAdhikaraNyanirvAhAya zrutisvArasyabhaGgo na yuktaH | abhedopacArakalpane'pi upacAra eva doSaH | ato'tra sAmAnAdhikaraNyaM na saMbhavatIti bAdhitameva tvadanumAnam | madanumAnaM ca nAprayojakam, tRtIyArthasya karaNatvasya svArasyena guNasaMgateH aruNAzabdasya guNamAtraparatayA vaiyadhikaraNya eva saMbhavAt - iti || atha paJcamI-- iha sAmAnAdhikaraNyaM na ekavibhaktyantapadadvayasamabhivyAhAramAtranibandhanamiSyate; kiMtu strIliGgAruNAzabdazrutyApyAnItam | svAtantryeNa guNamAtraparatve hi [p.256] 'guNe zuklAdayaH puMsi' ityanuzAsanAt pu~lliGgatA syAt | ataH sAmAnAdhikaraNyamevAbhyupagantavyamiti na bAdhitaM madanumAnam | tvadanumAnaM tu aprayojakameva, anyatra vaiyadhikaraNye'pi atra uktarItyA sAmAnAdhikaraNyopapatteH iti ||
atha SaSThI kakSyA-- guNeSu prayogasAdhUnyeva liGgAni nArthasAdhUni | na hi guNeSu puMstvaM vA mukhyam | ataH strItvamapi tadvadiSyatAm | tathaiva buddhikaruNAdizabdeSu guNavAciSvapi strIliMgaM dRzyate | tasmAdaruNAzrutisvArasyena sAmAnAdhikaraNyaM nopanIyate | kiM tu ekavibhaktyantapadadvayasamabhivyAhAramAtreNeti tasya ekapadazrutisvArasyavirodhina aupacArikatvApAdakasya ca tyAga eva yuktaH | ataH tvadanumAnaM bAdhitam| madanumAnaM ca nAprayojakam, uktayuktyA vaiyadhikaraNyAvazyaMbhAvAt - iti || atha saptamI-- yaduktaM prayogasAdhviha liMgaM nArthasAdhviti, tadayuktam, 'guNe zuklAdayaH puMsi' ityanuzAsanavirodhena prayogasAdhutvasyApyasiddheH | na hi karuNAdizabdasAmAnyataH dRSTam anuzAsanavirodhe prabhavati | tathA sati zuklAdizabdeSu pu~lliMgadarzanena karuNAdizabdeSvapi kadAcit pu~lliMgaprayogApatteH | na ca kArakavibhaktyanvayasvArasyasiddhyarthamihaiva strIliMgaprayogasya sAdhutvaM kalpyatAmiti vAcyam, ubhayorapi maduktarItyA arthasAdhutvasaMbhave kasya cit prayogasAdhutvakalpanAnaucityAt | anyathA liMgamevArthasAdhu, kArakavibhaktiH prayogasAdhuriti vaiparItyasyApi kalpanAprasaGgAt | vizeSaNavibhakteH sAdhutvArthatvasya anyatra bahuzaH klRptatayA guNazabdaH strIliMga eva syAt | aklRptasya prayogasAdhutvasya akalpanIyatvena tasyaivaucityAcca | etena liMgakArakayoH kArakasya prAdhAnyAt kArakAnvayasvArasyamevAnuroddhavyam, na ca liMgasyAprAdhAnye'pi 'striyAm' iti vyAkaraNasUtrabhASyAdyuktarItyA tasya prAtipadikastrIpratyayAnyatarArthatvena prathamazrutatvAt prathamazrutasya aprAdhAnye'pi 'na prathamayajJe pravRJjayAt' ityatra prAthamyavizeSaNasyaiva prAbalyAt liMgamevAnuroddhavyamiti [p.257] vAcyam, liMgasya prAthamikaprAtipadikastrIpratyayAntarArthatve'pi tatprAbalyahetoH prathamapratIterasiddheH ; 'aruNayA' iti tRtIyAprayoge strIpratyayasya TAbAkArasyAzravaNena TApaM strIliGgAsAdhAraNena vibhaktivikAreNonnIya tadanantaraM tena svayaM vAcakena prAtipadikArthadyotakena vA liMgasya pratyetavyatvAt iti nirastam klRptakalpyayoH klRptasya balIyastvAt svAtantryeNa guNapareSu zuklAdizabdeSu kvacidapi strIliMgasya klRptyabhAvAt liMgAnuzAsanavirodhena kalpanasyAyuktatvAcca | api ca aruNApadasya dravyaparyantatvapakSeSveva kArakavibhaktyanvayasvArasyavirodhazaGkApi | na tu guNamAtraparo'ruNAzabdaH. ekahAyanIsAmAnAdhikaraNyaM paramaupacArikAbhedAvalambanamiti pakSe'pi | na ca tasminpakSe sAmAnAdhikaraNyasya aupacArikatvApattireva doSaH ; pratIyamAnasAmAnAdhikaraNyAtyantabAdhanAt tadaupacArikatvakalpanasya jyAyastvAt | kiMcAyamupacAraH sAMpratikalakSaNAnyAyena atraiva na kalpanIyaH; kiMtu nirUDhalakSaNAnyAyena klRpta eva | tathA hi 'striyAm' iti sUtre bhASyakAraiH strItvasya TAbAdyarthatvapakSe tasya svayamastrItvAt ekatvAcca 'kumArI, kumAyauM, kumAryaH' ityAdiSu strIliMgasya dvivacanabahuvacanayozca anupapattimAzaGkaya, samAhitam guNavacanAnAM hi zabdAnAmAzrayato liMgavacanAni bhavanti; tadyathA 'zuklaM vastram, zuklA zATI, zuklaH kambalaH, zuklau kambalau, zuklAH kambalAH' iti, yadravyaM zrito bhavati guNaH, tasya yalliGgaM vacanaM ca, tat guNasyApi bhavati; evamihApi yadi dravyAzritaM bhavati strItvam, tasya yalliMgaM vacanaM ca tat strItvasyApi bhaviSyati iti | tatra hi zuklazabdaH kevalaguNapara eva udAhRtaH | tasya dravyaparyantatve svata eva liMgavacanayorupapannatvena AzrayatastayorasamarthanIyatvAt | atastatra bhinnArthayorguNadravyazabdayoH sAmAnAdhikaraNyamabhedopacArAvalambanamiti spaSTameva iti | etena aruNAzabdasya kevalaguNaparatvapakSe tavApi strIliMgamayuktamityapi zaGkA nirastA; yatra guNavAcinAmaupacArikadravyavAcipadasAmAnAdhikaraNyam, tatra AzrayaliGgAnurodhasya 'zuklA zATI' ityatra klRptatvAt | tasmAt pratIyamAnasAmAnAdhikaraNyAbAdhanena kArakavibhaktyarthasamarthanopapatteH na tadvAdhanaM yuktamityataH madanumAnaM na bAdhitam, tvadanumAnameva tu aprayojakamiti || [p.258] athASTamI-- sAmAnAdhikaraNyAbAdhanena kArakavibhaktisAphalyasamarthanamayuktam, kArakavibhaktyantatve kriyApadAnvayasya sAmAnAdhikaraNye ekahAyanIpadAnvayasya ca vaktavyatvAt 'aruNayA krINAti, aruNayaikahAyanyA' ityevamekasya padasya yugapadubhayAnvayAyogAt | na ca paryAyeNa ubhayAnvayaH; ekasmAdvA vAkyAt kramikArthadvayapratItyasaMbhavAt | na ca vArtikakAraiH aruNimaikahAyanyoH pArSThikAnvayasamarthanAnantaram 'evaM ca vAkyagatamapi sAmAnAdhikaraNyaM samarthitaM bhaviSyati' ityuktatvAt tatra vibhaktizrutyAnItakriyAnvayavirodhamAzaGkaya, 'kriyApekSita evAyaM saMbandhaH kriyate tayoH | na cAtrAnuguNA vRttirdurbalApi nirudhyate' iti paryAyeNa ubhayAnvayaH svIkRta iti zaGkanIyam; aruNAzabdasya prathamaM niSkRSTaguNamAtrazaktyA aruNimamAtraviSayatvena kArakavibhaktyA kriyAnvitasya pazcAdvArtikakArAbhimatayA guNilakSaNayA tasya ekahAyanIvizeSaNatayApi anvayAsaMbhavAt, zabdAnAM yugapadAvRttyA vA arthadvayapratipAdakatvasya vArtikakAraireva nAmadheyapeTikAdiSu bahuSu pradezeSu pratiSiddhatvAt | tasmAt 'evaM ca' ityAdigranthasya aruNimaikahAyanyoH ArthikaparasparAnvayasamarthanAnantaraM pravRttatvAt tatraiva abhyuccayakathanaparaH iti netavyam, ataH kArakavibhaktitayA sAphalyasamarthanapravRttasya tavApi sAmAnAdhikaraNyAsaMbhavAt tvadanumAnaM bAdhitameva | madanumAnaM ca nAprayojakamiti || atha navamI-- kriyAnvayaH sAmAnAdhikaraNyenAviruddhaH bhinnapravRttinimittAnAM zabdAnAmekasminnarthe vRttirhi sAmAnAdhikaraNyam | tatra ekasminniti padoktamarthekyaM tAttvikaM cet mukhyaM sAmAnAdhikaraNyam, aupacArikaM cet amukhyamiti vizeSaH | dvividhamapi sAmAnAdhikaraNyaM prakRte saMbhavati ityupapAditam | tadvividhamapi kriyAnvaye sati asatyapi nApaiti | na hi AdyapakSAnusAreNa vizeSaNe kArakavibhaktyarthasaMkrAntyA dravyaparyantatvamaruNAzabdasya apaiti | na vA dvitIyapakSAnusAreNa tasya AzrayaliMgagrAhitvaM kriyAnvaye apaiti | na ca anapetamapi sAmAnAdhikaraNyaM [p.259] kriyAnvaye vAkyArthapratItyanupayogi jAtamityayaM doSaH | na hi tasya vAkyArthapratItyupayogitvaparyantam asmAbhiH sisAdhayiSitam; kiM tu vAkyArthapratItisamaye sattvamAtram | na ca tadanupayoginA kiM tena sAmAnAdhikaraNyena iti zaGkanIyam, prayojanAbhAvena liMgavibhaktyaikarUpyAvagatasya tasya pratikSepAyogAt aruNimaikahAyanyoH parasparAnvayasya nyAyalabhyatve'pi tatpratiSThAhetutayA saprayojanatvAt | na ca kriyAnvaye sati sAmAnAdhikaraNyasya vAkyArthapratItyanupayogitvamapi, sAmAnAdhikaraNyabalAt dravyAvacchedakatayA pratIyamAnasyaiva guNasya kriyAzeSatvenApyanvayasaMbhavAt | abhyupagantavyazca pUrvamImAMsakairapi tathA anvayaH 'caturo muSTInnirvapati' ityatra | tathA hipAJcamike muSTikapAlAdhikaraNe caturmuSTinirvApa ekaH padArthaH ; na tu ekaikamuSTinirvApazcatvAraH padArthAH | ato naikapUroDAzAsviSTiSu padArthAnusamayena aGgakalApe kartavye sthite sati, pratipuroDAzam ekaikamuSTinA anusamayo na kAryaH ; pratimuSTi nirvApapadArthasya bhinnatvAbhAvAt | kiM tu prAthamikasya puroDAzasya caturmuSTinirvApaM samApya dvitIyasya sa ArambhaNIyaH ityevaM caturmuSTinirvApeNa anusamayaH kAryaH, tAvata ekapadArthatvAt iti vyavasthApitam | atreyaM zaGkA samunmiSati - dAzamike saMkhyAmuSTyadhikaraNe catuH saMkhyAyA muSTidravyazeSatvaM nirAkRtya nirvApakriyAzeSatvaM samarthitam ; tathA ca 'tisra AhutIH' ityatra AhutikriyAnvayinyA tritvasaMkhyayA AhutikriyAbheda iva nirvApakriyAnvayinyA catuHsaMkhyayA nirvApakriyAbhedo'pi syAt; ato muSTinA anusamaya iti pUrvapakSa eva yuktaH iti | tatredamuttaraM vAcyam yuktaM 'tisra AhutIrjuhoti' ityatra saMkhyayA kriyAbhedaH; tatra tritvasaMkhyAyAH sAkSAdAhutikriyAvacchedakatvenAnvayAt; iha catuH saMkhyA na nirvApakriyAvacchedakatvena anveti; tathA sati 'saMkhyAyAH kriyAbhyAvRttigaNane kRtvasuc' 'dvitricaturthyaH suc' iti pANinIyavihitakriyAbhyAvRttigaNanAviSayasujantatayA catuH iti rUpaprasaGgAt | na ca 'tisra AhutIH' ityatra Ahutizabdavat iha 'caturo nirvApAn' iti nirvApazabdaH 'caturo vArAn' iti vArazabdo vA asti | na ca tadadhyAhAro yuktaH; liMgavibhaktyaikarUpyAvagatamuSTisAmAnAdhikaraNyanirvRtasya 'caturaH' ityasya adhyAhAranirapekSatvAt | ata eva muSTyatiriktadravyavAcizabdAntarAdhyAhAreNa liMgavibhaktyupapAdanAzaGkApi nirastA syAt; muSTisAmAnAdhikaraNyAt muSTyavacchedikaiva satI kArakavibhaktibalAt [p.260] kriyAyAM zeSatvamAtreNa anveti; na tu tadavacchedakatvenApi; kiMcidavacchedakasaMkhyAyA taditarAvacchedakatvavirodhAt | avacchedakatvena dravyAnvitAyA api saMkhyAyAH zeSatvena kriyAnvayastu na virudhyate; kayA cidvidhayA kiMcitsaMbandhino'pi vidhAntareNa taditarasaMbandhasaMbhavAt; 'saptadazAratnirvAjapeyasya yUpaH' ityatra aratnyavacchedikAyAH saMkhyAyA viSayatayA vidhyanvayasya saMpratipannatvAcca | ata eva 'saptadaza prAjApatyAnpazUnAlabheta' ityAdiSu tathA anvayo'bhyupagantavyaH| tadyathA - 'pRthaktvanivezitvAtsaMkhyayA karmabhedaH syAt' iti dvaitIyIkAdhikaraNe 'tisra AhutIrjuhoti' ityetat saMkhyayA karmabhedasya udAharaNam iti prathamamudAhRtya AhutikriyArUpakarmAnvayinyA saMkhyayA karmabhedasiddheH spaSTatvena atra vyutpAdyanyAyAbhAvAt nedamudAharaNamiti tat parityajya prAjApatyavAkyamudAhRtam | tatra saMkhyayA karmabhedasiddheraspaSTatvaM ca evaM vyutpAditam nAtra kriyAnvayinI saMkhyA; kiMtu dravyAnvayinI; tayA yAgakriyArUpakarmabhedasiddhiH ittham sA na kevalaM pazudravyAnvayinI; yena bahuSvapi pazuSu prajApatidevatAsaMbandhasya ekatvAt tadAkSipto yAgo'pi eko bhavet; kiM tu prajApatidevatAviziSTapazvanvayinI | tathA ca pazUnAmiva prajApatidevatAsaMbandhAnAmapi sAptadazyasiddhau tairAkSepyA yAgA api saptadaza bhaveyuriti | tatrApi evaM zaGkA unmiSati yatra saMkhyA karmAnvayinI tannodAhAryamiti manvAnAnAmidamapi nodAharaNaM bhavitumarhati; 'prAjApatyAnpazUna' iti padadvayasamabhivyAhArapratItadravyadevatAsaMbandhAkSiptayAgakriyAyAmeva aruNAdhikaraNanyAyena sAkSAt saMkhyAnvayopapatteH; na hi paripUrNavAkyArthapratyayAnantaraM yAgAkSepaH; yena saMkhyA yAgakriyAkSepAtprAgeva dravyAnvitA jAtA, na tasyAH punaH kriyAnvayo bhAvIti zaGkyetApi | prAjApatyapazUnAM viniyogAnavagamena vAkyabhedaprasaGgena pazumAtroddeze devatApadavaiyarthyaprasaGgena ca sparzarUpasyAlambhasya saMskArakarmatayA yAgAnvayidevatApadAnanvayaprasaGgena arthakarmatayA vA anvayAsaMbhavena dravyadevatAsaMbandhAkSiptavidhiyogyayAgakriyAdhyAhAraparyantaM paripUrNavAkyArthapratItyasaMbhavAt, tadasaMbhave ca zrutavidhinirvAhArthaM vidhiyogyayAgakriyAdhyAhAre sati, tatrAnvayayogyAyAH saMkhyAyAH 'guNAnAM ca parArthatvAdasaMbandhaH samatvAtsyAt' iti nyAyaviruddhasya guNabhUtadravyAnvayasyAsaMbhavena 'saptadaza' ityasyApi AkhyAtavat dravyAnvayopapatteH, yAgakriyAkSepAtprAgapi yAvacchrutapadasamabhivyAhAralabhyavAkyArthapratyayotpattyabhyupagamena [p.261] 'prAjApatyAnpazUna' ityasyeva 'saptadaza' ityasyApi kArakavibhaktitaH kriyAnvayasyaiva prAptyA dravyAnvayAlAbhAt iti | tatrApyevamuttaraM vAcyam sAmAnAdhikaraNyAt dravyAvacchedakatvena dravyAnvitAyA eva saMkhyAyAH kArakatAmAtreNa kriyAnvayo bhavet| tacca kArakatvaM dravyAvacchedadvArA nirvAhyamiti devatAsaMbandhaviziSTadravyAnvayinyA saMkhyayA tadviziSTadravyabhedavat dravyadevatAsaMbandhabhedasyApi siddhau tadvAraiva saMkhyayA karmabhedasiddhiH ; na sAkSAditi | tasmAt aruNApadam ekahAyanIpadasamAnAdhikaraNaM sadeva kriyAnvayIti madanumAnaM na bAdhitam | tvadanumAnameva tu aprayojakamiti siddham || iti aruNaikahAyanIsAmAnAdhikaraNyavAdaH ||7||

[p.261] krayAruNyAnvayavAdaH || viSNubhAgavatAstu aruNAzabdasya svazaktyA guNaviziSTadravyaparyantatvamaGgIkRtya AruNyasya ekahAyanatvavat dravyaguNatayaivAnvayaH; na svAtantryeNa krayasAdhanatayA - iti manyante | tannirAsArthaM kathA prastUyate - aruNAvAkyasamarpitamAruNyaM somakrayakriyAsAdhanam, aruNAvAkyagatatRtIyArthAnvayitvAt; yadaruNAvAkyagatatRtIyArthAnvayi, tat somakrayakriyAsAdhanam - yathA godravyam | yadyapi uktahetuH sAdhyaparyavasitaH, tathApi rUpabhedena bhedaH iti prathamA kakSyA || atha dvitIyA-- asiddho'yaM hetuH, sAdhyato rUpabhedena bhedasattve'pi pakSe hetoH mama asaMpratipannatvAt ; mayA vizeSaNavibhaktiH 'aruNayA' iti tRtIyA kevalaM sAdhutvArthA, tRtIyAntarapratipannagogatakaraNatvAnuvAdinI vA; na tu aruNimAnvitakaraNatvasarpiNI - ityaGgIkRtatvAt | tasmAt aruNAvAkyapratipannamAruNyaM na somakraya[p.262]kriyAsAdhanam, govizeSaNatvAt, yadgovizeSaNam, na tatsomakrayakriyAsAdhanam - yathA ekahAyanatvam - iti || atha tRtIyA-- 'aruNayA' iti kArakavibhaktisAphalyAya guNe tadarthasaMkrAntiraGgIkaraNIyA; vibhakterarthavattve saMbhavati sAdhutvamAtrArthatvasya anuvAdakatvasya vA abhyupagamAyogAt | na ca vibhaktyarthasya vizeSaNasaMkrAntiradRSTacarI; 'caturo muSTInnirvapati' iti vAkye catuHzabdaparadvitIyArthasya saMkhyeyavizeSaNasaMkhyAnvayitvasya saMpratipannatvAt | tathA hi - 'caturo muSTInnirvapati' iti darzapUrNamAsaprakaraNAmnAtena vAkyena catuH-saMkhyAmuSTidravyobhayasAdhanako nirvApo vidhIyate | katham ? zakaTAnItAdvrIhirAzeH puroDAzArthAnAM vrIhINAM pRthakkaraNamarthataH prApnuvadapi na niyamena prApyate; zakaTena puroDAzopayuktavrIhimAtrasyApi AnayanasaMbhavAt | ataH tatra niyamArtho vidhiH | sa ca vrIhINAM nirvApo muSTibhiH kartavyaH, tasya catuHsaMkhyA ca iti tatrApi niyamaH | yadyapi dvitIyayA muSTInAM saMskAryatA pratIyate, tathApi teSAM prAgaviniyuktatvAt avanejanasaMskRtAdhvaryuhastavat agre viniyokSyamANatvAbhAvAcca saMskAryatA nopapadyate | na ca muSTizabdo muSTiparimitavrIhiparo'stu, tatazca vrIhINAM viniyogasadbhAvAt saMskAryatA upapadyata iti vAcyam; teSAM muSTivaiziSTyena prApterabhAvAt tena rUpeNAnuvAdAnupapatteH, catuH saMkhyAyAH pRthaguddeze tadviziSTamuSTyuddeze vA vAkyabhedApattezca | tasmAt 'saktUJjuhoti' itivat anIpsitakarmaNi dvitIyeti yojyam | atra 'AdazataH saMkhyAH saMkhyeye' iti catuHzabdasya saMkhyeyaparyantatvAvagame'pi kArakavibhaktisAphalyAya vizeSaNe tadarthasaMkramo'GgIkRtaH | evaM zaGkAparihArAvabhipretyaiva 'muSTilopAttu saMkhyAlopastadguNatvAt na nirvApazeSatvAt' iti dazamAdhyAyapUrvapakSasiddhAntAvalambanaM sUtradvayaM pravRttam | atra hi 'bArhaspatyo naivAraH saptadazazarAvazcarurbhavati' iti vAjapeye vihitasya saptadazazarAvaparimitacaroratidezaprAptaizcaturbhirmuSTibhiH parimitena vrIhidravyeNa saMpAdayitumazakyatvAt saMkhyAmuSTyanyataralope kartavye catuHzabdasya saMkhyeyapradhAnatvAt tadvizeSaNatvenApradhAnabhUtAyAH saMkhyAyA lopaH kArya ityabhiprAyeNa pUrvapakSasUtraM catuHzabdasya saMkhyeyaparatve'pi [p.263] tadgatasya kArakatvasya dvitIyAntareNAbhihitatayA catuHzabdaparadvitIyAyAH tadviSaye sAphalyAbhAvAt vizeSaNe tadarthasaMkramo vAcyaH | tathA ca saMkhyAmuSTyorubhayorapi nirvApAGgatvAvizeSAt prathamazrutaprAbalyAnurodhena muSTereva lopaH nyAyya ityabhiprAyeNa siddhAntasUtraM pravRttamiti spaSTameva | tasmAt tatra catuHzabdasya saMkhyeyaparatve'pi tatparadvitIyAyA iva aruNAzabdasya dravyaparatve'pi tatparatRtIyAyAH sAphalyArthamaruNimni saMkrAntirabhyupagantavyA ityevaM prasAdhyo hetuH kRtakatvAdivadupapadyata iti nAtrAsiddhizaGkAvakAzaH | tvadanumAnaM tu aprayojakam, 'ekahAyanyA' ityatra kArakavibhaktervizeSyAnvayena sAphalyAt vizeSaNAnanvaye'pi, atra sAphalyArthaM vizeSaNAnvayaucityAditi || atha caturthI-- caturuttarAyA iva aruNottarAyA vibhakteH vizeSaNAnvayakalpanaM na yuktam; tathA sati 'te vai dazabhiH krINAti' iti dazasAdhanakrayazrutivirodhAt | AruNyasyApi tatsAdhanatve hi saMkhyAtireko bhavet | ato'tra vizeSaNavibhakteH sAdhutvAdyarthataiva yukteti tvadIyo hetuH prasAdhayitumapyazakya iti asiddha eva | madIyastu nAprayojakaH, saMkhyAnatirekArthamAruNyasya ekahAyanatvasamatvena avazyamaGgIkaraNIyatvAditi || atha paJcamI-- dazakrayavacanaM na vidhAyakam, 'hiraNyena krINAti' 'ajayA krINAti' ityAdidazavidhavidhivAkyaiH krayatatsAdhanatatsaMkhyAvizeSANAM siddheH | kiM tu pracayaziSTasaMkhyAnuvAdakam | na ca anuvAdasvArasyAnurodhena vidhivAkyagatakArakavibhaktivaiphalyApAdanaM yuktam | tasmAt yathA 'caturdaza paurNamAsyAmAhutayo hUyante' ityanuvAdagatacaturdazasaMkhyAzravaNaM satISvapi pArvaNahomAdiSu avaSaTkArAhutiSu savaSaTkArAhutiviSayatayA yojyate tathA idamapyanuvAdagatameva dazasaMkhyAzravaNaM svatantrakrayasAdhanadravyaviSayatayA yojayituM yuktam | ato madanumAne heturnAsiddhaH | tvadanumAnaM tu aprayojakameveti || [p.264] atha SaSThI-- dazakrayavacanasya vidhAyakatve saMbhavati na anuvAdakatvakalpanaM yuktam | asti cAtra vibhaktisAphalyalobhena AruNyasyApi krayasAdhanatvaprAptau tadvyAvRttiphalakasya vidheravakAzaH | ataH tatsAphalyAya vibhaktimAtrasya prayogasAdhutvArthatvam anuvAdakatvaM vA kalpayituM yuktam | 'yadi somamapahareyurekAM gAM dakSiNAM dadyAt, abhidagdhe some paJca gA dadyAt' ityatra hi samabhivyAhRtaikAdiprAtipadikavaiyarthyalakSaNAdidoSaparihArAya 'ekAm' 'paJca' ityanayoH kArakavibhaktitvAzrayaNena kriyAsaMbandhaH tyaktaH | tathA hi tatra yadi ekAM gAM dadyAt iti saMbandhaH kriyate, tadA 'gauzcAzvazcAzvatarazca gardabhazcAjAzvayazca vrIhayazca yavAzca tilAzca mASAzca tasya dvAdazazataM dakSiNA' iti jyotiSTomadakSiNAtvena bahuSu gavAdiSu samuccitya deyatvena prApteSu aniyamena yAM kAMcidekAmeva dakSiNAM dadyAt ityarthaH syAt; tadA gozabdasyAniyataprApaNIyagavAdisarvadravyalakSaNA bhavet, pakSaprAptAyAH goH anuvAdaH saMbhavet | atha tu ekAM dadyAt, tAM ca gAm iti vidhIyeta, tadA prAptakriyAnuvAdena anekArthavidhAnAdvAkyabhedaH | yadi tu gAM dadyAt iti gaureva dakSiNAdAne vidhIyeta, tataH tasyA vacanaprAptA dvAdazazatasaMkhyaiva paricchedikA prApnuyAditi ekapaJcazabdau anarthakau syAtAm | tayorapi vidhAne vAkyabhedaH | atha ekavacanabalAt vivakSitaikatvasaMkhyA gaurvidhIyate, tadA ekazabdasya anuvAdatvenAnarthakyam, paJcazabdasya ananvitArthatvaM ca syAt | tasmAduktadoSaparijihIrSayA kArakavibhaktizrutiprAptamapi saMkhyayoH kriyAsaMbandhaM parityajya vAkyalakSaNo gosaMkhyAsaMbandha eva vidheyo'bhyupagamyeta | tatazca ekAM gAm ityetAvatyeva vidhiH samApyate| dakSiNAM dadyAt - ityanuvAdaH | jyotiSTomadakSiNArUpAsveva goSu dvAdazazatasaMkhyAzravaNaliMgAt 'ekAm' 'paJca' iti zabdoktayorapi saMkhyayostAsveva nivezasyArthalabhyatvAt; anuvAdasAmarthyAdeva vA dakSiNArUpagavAzrayatvaniyamaH saMkhyayorlabhyate 'airaM kRtvodgeyam' iti irApadavidhAnasya 'yadgirA gireti brUyAt AtmAnameva tadudgAtA giret' ityanuvAdasAmarthyAt girApadasthAnaniyamasyAGgIkRtatvAditi 'ekAM paJceti dhenuvat' iti dazamAdhyAyAdhikaraNe nirNItam | yadaivam ekAdipadamAtravaiyarthyaparijihIrSayA kArakavibhaktisvArasyaM tyaktam, tadA kimu vaktavyam 'taM vA dazabhiH [p.265] krINAti' iti vAkyavaiyarthyaparihArArthaM tadiha tyaktavyamiti | evaM ca dazakrayavacanarUpavidhivirodhAt AruNyasya tRtIyArthAnvayitvaM na saMbhavatIti tvaddheturasiddha eva, maddhetuzca nAprayojaka iti || atha saptamI-- dazakrayavacanasya dazAdhikasaMkhyAvyAvRttiphalakavidhiparatve parisaMkhyAvidhitvaM bhavet; tathA ca tasya zrutadazasaMkhyArUpasvArthaH tyaktavyaH, azrutadazAdhikasaMkhyAvyAvRttirUpaparArthazca grAhyaH, kArakavibhaktiprAptamAruNyasya krayasAdhanatvaM ca bAdhitavyam - iti traidoSyaM prApnuyAt | ataH tasya pracayaziSTasvatantrakrayasAdhanadravyasaMkhyAnuvAdakatvakalpanameva yuktam; varo hi tridoSaduSTaparisaMkhyAbhyupagamApekSayA tadanuvAdakatvAbhyupagamaH | ata eva 'vaikhAnasaM pUrve'hansAma bhavati' ityaGgirasAM dvirAtre pUrvasyAhno vaikhAnasasAmavidhAnAnantaraM zrutasya 'SoDazyuttare' iti vAkyasya parisaMkhyArthatvaM parityajya anuvAdakatvamabhyupagatam uttarasyAhnaH SoDazitvaM hi SoDazinaM prastutya pravRttena sarvadvirAtrANAmuttarasyAhnaH SoDazividhAyakena 'uttare'handvirAtrasya gRhyate' ityanArabhyAdhItavAkyena sidhyati; tatazca asyApi vAkyasya vidhiparatve aGgirasAM dvirAtrasyaiva uttare'hani SoDazigrahaH grAhyaH ; na tu dvirAtrAntarANAmuttareSvahaHsu iti dvirAtrAntarebhyaH SoDazigrahavyAvRttirUpaparisaMkhyAvidhiparatvameva syAditi aGgirasAM dvirAtre SoDazigrahaprApaNarUpasvArthastyaktavyaH, dvirAtrAntarebhyaH tadvyAvartanarUpaH parArthazca grAhyaH, anArabhyAdhItavAkyena dvirAtrAntareSu prAptaM SoDazimattvaM ca bAdhitavyamiti traidoSyaM prasajyeta; ataH 'SoDazyuttare' ityasya anArabhyAdhItavAkyaprAptaSoDazitvAnuvAdakatvakalpanameva nyAyyamiti dazamAdhyAye vyavasthApitam | tasmAt AruNyasya tRtIyArthAnvayitvasyAvikalatvAt madIyo hetuH nAsiddhaH tvadIyastu aprayojaka eveti || athASTamI-- anuvAdApekSayA jaghanyApi atra krayasAdhanasaMkhyAnatirekaphalA parisaMkhyaiva avazyamabhyupagantavyA | anyathA nirvApavAkye saMkhyAvat ihApyAruNyasya [p.266] svAtantryeNa krayasAdhanatve saMkhyAmuSTyorubhayoH asaMbhavasthale muSTityAgavadiha aruNimaikahAyanyorubhayorasaMbhavasthale goreva ekahAyanIzabdopAttAyAH tyAgaprasaGgAt | na ca tAdRzasthaladaurlabhyam; yatra aruNA piGgAkSI meSI labdhA, atathAbhUtA gaurlabdhA; aruNA piGgAkSI ekahAyanI gaustu na labdhA; tatra Arabdhasya kratoravazyasamApanIyatvena anyataraparityAgena anuSThAnanirvRttyavazyaMbhAvAt | na ca vAcyaM yatrAruNA piGgAkSI ekahAyanAvasthApannA meSI labdhA, atathAbhUtA gaurlabdhA, tatra bhUyo'nugrahanyAyena gaustyaktavyA syAdityevaM tavApi tulyo doSa iti; manmate bhUyasAmapyAruNyAdInAM dravyazeSatvena zeSivirodhe, bhUyastve'pi zeSANAmeva tyaktavyatvAt | ata eva 'dravyasaMskAravirodhe dravyaM tadarthatvAt' iti SaSThAdhyAyAdhikaraNe yatra khadirastanutvena takSaNAdyayogyaH pazuniyojanamAtrayogyo labdhaH, tatpratinidhiH kadarastu mahattvena takSaNAdInAmapi yogyo labdhaH, tatra bahUnAM saMskArANAmanugrahAya yUpArthaM kadaro grAhya iti pUrvapakSaM kRtvA, bahUnAmapi saMskArANAM dravyazeSatvAt zeSyanugrahArthaM khadira eva grAhya iti siddhAntitam | tasmAdasiddha eva tvaddhetuH, maddheturnAprayojaka iti || atha navamI-- parisaMkhyAnaGgIkAre aruNAvAkyasyApi saMkhyAmuSTyadhikaraNanyAyaviSayatvApattiriti yaduktaM tadayuktam; yatra aruNA meSI labdhA, piGgAkSyekahAyanI gaurlabdhA, tatra svAtantryeNa krayasAdhanayoH piGgAkSirUpAvayavaikahAyanIdravyayoH dvayoranugrahAya ekasyAruNyasyaiva tyAgaH 'vipratiSiddhadharmANAM samavAye bhUyasAM syAtsvadharmatvam' iti nyAyataH prApnotIti na tatra saMkhyAmuSTyadhikaraNanyAyaH prasajyate | yatra aruNA piGgAkSI meSI, ekahAyanI gauzca labdhA, tatra uktanyAyaprasaktau iSTApattiriti aruNAdhikaraNanyAyenaiva hi catuSTvasya nirvApazeSatvamAzritya muSTityAgaH tatrAdhikaraNe vyutpAditaH | tathA catuSTvavat aruNimApi tasyAdhikaraNasya kathaM viSayo na syAt | aruNimaikahAyanIvirodhaH kvAcitkatvAt aspaSTaH | saMkhyAmuSTivirodhaH sArvatrikatvAt spaSTa iti vA, aruNAzabdasya niSkRSTaguNe'pi zaktisadbhAvAt 'aruNayA' iti nirdezasya dravyazeSatvapakSe'pi vizeSaNavibhaktitayA prayogasAdhutvamAtrArthatvasya [p.267] avazyavaktavyatvAt tathaiva kriyAzeSatvapakSe'pi strIliGgasya tanmAtrArthatvopapatteH AruNyasya kriyAzeSatvamastu; catuSTvaM tu na kriyAzeSaH; 'AdazataH saMkhyAH saMkhyeye' ityanuzAsanavirodhena catuHzabdasya niSkRSTaguNaparatAyA vaktumazakyatvAt | ato muSTivizeSaNameva catuSTvamityadhikAzaGkA nirAkaraNIyeti tasminnadhikaraNe saMkhyAmuSTyudAharaNaM kRtam | nyAyasAmyenApatat AruNyasyApi tadadhikaraNanyAyaviSayatvaM na daNDena vArayituM zakyam | yatra tu vAkyabhedAdidoSaprasaGgAt 'abhidagdhe some paJca gA dadyAt' ityatreva guNavAcizabdasya guNazaktibalAt abhedopacArAdvA guNasya dravyavizeSaNataiva kevalam yathA 'azvaH zyAvo dakSiNA' 'pizaGgI paSThauhI dakSiNA' 'zvetAyai zvetavatsAyai dugdhaM mathitamAjyaM bhavati' ityAdau, tatra vizeSaNasya guNasyAprAdhAnyAt dravyaguNavirodhe guNasya tyAga iti saMkhyAmuSTyadhikaraNapUrvapakSanyAyaH pravartate | tadviSayameva ca 'vizeSaNaM vizeSyeNa bahulam' iti sUtre 'yatra hi anyataradravyam anyataro vA guNaH, tatra yadravyaM tatpradhAnam, tadyathA 'zuklamAlabheta' 'kRSNamAlabheta' iti na piSTapiNDamAlabhya kRtArtho bhavati iti mahAbhASyavacanam | tasmAt dazakrayavacanasya parisaMkhyAvidhitvakalpakAbhAvAt vaikhAnasAdhikaraNanyAyena tasya yathAprApyanuvAdakataiva uciteti AruNyasya krayAnvaye bAdhakAbhAvAt kArakavibhakteH saMbhavat sAphalyaM nopekSaNIyamiti madIyo heturnAsiddhaH, tvadIyastu aprayojaka iti siddhamAruNyasya krayasAdhanatvam || iti krayAruNyAnvayavAdaH ||8||

zaktirekAnekA cetyetadapyatra cintitam || [p.268] kecidAhuH krayeNaiva dravyeNaivAnvayaM pare | ubhAbhyAmanvayo'smAbhirAruNyasya samarthitaH || zaktimeva draDhIkartumubhayorupajIvyatAm | tAvatparyantaM sA pradhAvetkramAditi || tatra yadi gauro'ham ityAdivyavahAraH matvarthIyapratyayotpattiprayuktena yogena lakSaNayA vA nirvahet, tadA gaurAdizabdAnAM jIva eva zaktirna sidhyet; dUre tadvArA antaryAmiNi zaktiriti zaGkAnirAsAya teSAM jIve zaktiM prasAdhayituM kathA prastUyate-- gauradehaviziSTo jIvaH gaurazabdavAcyaH, gauratvajAtyAzrayatvAt, yo gauratvajAtyAzrayaH, sa gaurazabdavAcyaH yathA gauravarNaH iti prathamA kakSyA || asiddho'yaM hetuH guNagatajAtiM prati Atmana Azrayatve pramANAbhAvAt | tasmAt gauradehaviziSTo jIvaH na gaurazabdavAcyaH, gauratvajAtyanAzrayatvAt, yo gauratvajAtyanAzrayaH sa na gaurazabdavAcyaH yathA zuklavarNaH - iti dvitIyA || maddheturnAsiddhaH, 'gauro varNaH' iti pratItyaviziSTAyAH 'gauro'ham' iti pratIterAtmanaH gauratvajAtyAzrayatve pramANatvAt | ata eva tvaddheturasiddhaH iti tRtIyA || [p.269] 'gauro'ham' iti pratIteH aklRptamAtmanaH gauratvajAtyAzrayatvaM dharmaM parikalpya tadviSayatvakalpanAt klRptagauradehavaiziSTyaviSayatvakalpanaM jyAyaH | atastvaddheturasiddhaH, maddhetuzca nAsiddhaH iti caturthI || tathA sati 'gauro'ham' iti Atmani gaurazabdaprayogo na ghaTeta; gaurazabdasya gauratvajAtiviziSTa iva gauradravyaviziSTe zaktyabhAvAt | tasmAt avigItaprayogAnusAreNa Atmano dehaguNagatajAtyAzrayatvamaGgIkaraNIyam iti maddheturnAsiddhaH | tvaddheturevAsiddhaH iti paJcamI || varNavAcigaurazabdAt arzaAdyajantAt matublope sati gaurazabdasya gauradravyaviziSTaviSayatvaM yogena labhyate | matvarthIyapratyayAntAt tatsarUpasyaiva matvarthIyapratyayAntarasyAniSTatvena tadvirUpapratyayasyAniSedhAt | yadyapi virUpAvapi matvarthIyapratyayau samAnavRttau neSyete, tathApi gauravarNo'sminnasti iti saptamyarthavRttAt arzaAdyajantAt gauro deho'sya astIti SaSThI || evaM yogena lakSaNayA vA nirvAhaH sArvatriko na bhavati, 'pANDuraham' 'babhruraham' 'zveto'ham' ityAdiprayogAvyApanAt | atastatra AtmanaH paramparayA guNagatajAtyAzrayatvamaGgIkRtya ityAdivyavahArasya pANDvAdivarNaviziSTadehAbhedopacAreNa upapatteH na tannirvAhArthaM guNagatajAtyAzrayatvam AtmanaH kalpanIyam, aklRptapravRttinimittakalpanena zaktinirvAhAt klRptena zakyasaMbandhena padadvayasamabhivyAhArasya gauratvajAtiviziSTAbhedaviSayatvaM ca tAvat tavApi saMpratipannam | tadeva tadviziSTavaiziSTyabubodhayiSayA prayukte'pi vAkye aGgIkartuM yuktam; klRptatvAt laghutvAcca | viziSTavaiziSTye tu arthAt sidhyati ityapi tAtparyamupapadyate - dhUmo'stIti vAkyasya vahnAviva | sidhyati cArthAt guNagatajAtiparamparAsaMbandhaghaTakatattadviziSTavaiziSTyam | yadyapi zabdata eva antaryAmiNo vA paramparayA dehaguNagatajAtyAzrayatvakalpanAdapi tatkalpanAgauravaM na doSaH | klRptatvena laghutvena ca gauro'ham ityupacAratAtparyarahitasyApi vAkyasya
