klRptatvalaghuzarIratvAnusaMdhAnarUpalAghavatarkAnugRhItaM gauro'hamiti vyavahAramUlaM pratyakSameva AtmanaH paramparayA guNagatajAtyAzrayatAyAM pramANaM paryavasyati | evameva guNAzrayadravyasya paramparayA guNagatajAtyAzrayatAyAm 'nIlo ghaTaH' ityAdipratItivyavahArau pramANamiti guNazakteH guNiparyantatA | tatrApi vivadamAnaH uktarItyaiva pratibodhanIyaH | avyatirekavArtikapradarzanAcca- 'avyatirekAtsiddhamiti cenna dRSTo vyatirekaH' iti zabdavArtikaM hi guNavacanebhyo matublopAnuzAsanasya vaiyarthyazaGkAmudbhAvya tatparihArArtham; guNaguNinorabhedabhrAntiratra zaGkAbIjam | abhedabhrAntezca 'nIlo ghaTaH' ityAdivyavahAra eva mUlam | sa ca tasyAH tadA mUlaM bhavet; yadi tatra nIlazabdo nIlatvajAtiviziSTaviSayaH syAt | nIlaguNaviziSTaviSayatve tu abhedabhrAntinivartakatAmevAzruvIta; na tanmUlatAm | nanu udAhRtavArtikagatam avyatirekapadaM nAbhedaparam; kiMtu avyabhicAraparam, 'na guNA guNinaM vyabhicaranti' iti tadvivaraNabhASyadarzanAt iti cet; na; bhinnayoH satyapi avyabhicAre bhedaprayuktamatupprasaktisattvena avyabhicArasya zaGkAbIjatvAbhAvAt | 'dRSTo vyatirekaH' iti parihAravArtikavivaraNabhASye 'dRzyate vyatirekaH | tadyathA vastrasya zuklaH' iti bhedavyavahArasya udAhRtatvAcca | tasmAt 'na guNA guNinaM vyabhicaranti' iti bhASyam vyabhicAre hi bhedaH pratIyeta iti abhedo na sidhyet; na tu vyabhicAro'stIti abhedabAdhakazaGkAnirAsena abhedopapAdanaparaM pIlupAkaprakriyAvalambanena pravRttam iti vyAkhyeyam | evaM ca bhedavyavahAradarzanAt satyapi guNaguNinorbhede, guNavat guNino'pi guNagatajAtyAzrayatayaiva guNavacanairbodhyamAnatayA tanmUlA tayorabhedabhrAntiH zaGkAvAdinaH iti bhASyakArAzayaH sthitaH | tanmUlAbhedabhrAntikRta eva keSAMcit guNaguNyabhedavAdo'pi | yattu parihAravArtikavivaraNabhASyAzayapradarzanapravRttena pradIpakRtA upAyApAyavanto guNA guNinaM vyabhicarantItyuktam, tat bhASyakAraiH pIlupAkaprakriyAvalambanena pravRttaH zaGkAvAdI tadavalambanenaiva bodhanIyaH ityabhipretya bhedavyavahArapradarzanenaiva parihAre kRte'pi pakSAntare'pi parihArA bhavanti iti [p.272] nyAyAt piTharapAkaprakriyAmAzritya parihArAntarapradarzanaparam na tu tasya bhASyasya vyAkhyAnarUpam; tadananuguNatvAt | tasmAt 'mama deho gauraH' iti dehasyeva 'ahaM gauraH' iti jIvasyApi gauratvAzrayatvena bhAsamAnatvAt tathAbhUtabhAnamUlakAbhedabhrAntinivartanArthaM zabdAbhiyuktakRtayatnadarzanAt tathAbhUtabhAnamevAvalambya adyApi kaizcit guNaguNyabhedasya prasAdhyamAnatvAcca paramparayA gauratvAzrayatvaM dehasyeva jIvasya tadantaryAmiNazca avazyamaGgIkartavyamiti maddheturnAsiddhaH | tvaddheturevAsiddhaH | ato jIve'ntaryAmiNi ca gaurAdizabdAnAM na yogena lakSaNayA vA vRttiH | kiMtu zaktireveti siddham || iti yogalakSaNAzaGkAnirAsArthajIvAntaryAmizaktivAdaH ||9||

[p.272] bhramamUlatvazaGkAnirAsArthajIvAntaryAmizaktivAdaH || gaurAdizabdAnAM jIve yogena lakSaNayA rUDhizaktyA vA vRttirnAGgIkaraNIyA; dUre'ntaryAmiNi; 'gauro'ham' ityAdivyavahArANAM zuktirajatavyavahAravat bhramamUlatvAt - iti zaGkAnirAsena tatra teSAM zaktiM vyavasthApayituM kathAntaraM prastUyate | atra AditaH kakSyAtrayaM pUrvavat || atha caturthI-- 'gauro'ham' ityAdipratItiH nAtmani gauratvAdivaiziSTyaviSayA; kiMtu gaurAdiguNaviziSTe zarIra eva AtmatvAroparUpA; sthUlo'ham, gauro'ham, brAhmaNo'ham-ityAdiprakAreNa svazarIraM pazyanneva hi puruSaH tadeva ahamarthamAtmAnaM manyate| uktapratIteH AtmavizeSyakatve'pi raktasphaTikarItyA tasminsannihitazarIragatagauraguNAdyAroparUpaiva sA | ataH tvaddheturasiddhaH, maddhetuzca nAsiddhaH iti || atha paJcamI-- udAhRtapratIteH autsargike pramAtve maduktarItyA nirvoDhuM zakye bhramatvaM na kalpayitumucitam | ato viparIte hetvoH siddhyasiddhI iti || atha SaSThI-- [p.273] bhramatvamasaMpratipannaM nAsyAH kalpyam; dehAtmabhramasya lokAyatikAtiriktasakalavAdisaMpratipannatvAt | ataH hetvasiddhisiddhI tvaduktaviparIte-iti || atha saptamI-- dehAtiriktamAtmAnamavagacchatAm iyaM pratItiH pramANatvena upapAdanIyA; na bhramatvaM netavyA; bAdhakAbhAvAt| ataH maduktarItyaiva hetvoH siddhyasiddhI iti || athASTamI-- asiddho bAdhakAbhAvaH zarIrasya AtmAnAzritatvena zarIragatasya gauraguNAdeH zarIradvAreNa AtmAzritatvAnupapatteH | zarIraM hi svAvayavAnevAzritya tiSThati| tatpraviSTaH puruSaH gRhaM praviSTa iva tatra sukhaduHkhAdi anubhavan Aste | tasmAt zAstrato dehAhamarthavivekavatAmapi sAkSAtkArarUpaH dehAtmabhramaH raktasphaTikadarpaNamukhAdibhramanyAyena anuvartate ityevamuktapratIteH bhramatvamevAzrayaNIyam | ataH tvaddheturasiddhaH, maddheturnAsiddhaH iti || atha navamI-- zarIramAtmAzritaM tadapRthaksiddhameva | tadetat AtmotkramaNAnantarameva zarIrasya zoSavizaraNAvasthAprAptyanubhavena avagamyate | ataH vivekinAM vyutthAnasamayeSu kadAcit bhramAnuvRttisaMbhave'pi dehAtmabhedAnusaMdhAnasamayeSu bhavantI 'gauro'ham' ityAdipratItiH pramAtvenaiva upapAdanIyA | ato maddheturnAsiddhaH, tvaddhetuzca asiddha iti || [p.274] atha dazamI-- jIvotkramaNAnantaraM zarIrasya yat zoSavizaraNaM dRzyate, tat jIvavizeSaprayuktam ityatra gamakaM nAsti; jIvotkramaNasamaye tadantarapratipattyadhikaraNoktanyAyena tadIyopAdAnasArAMzabhUtasUkSmANAmapi sahotkrAntyA tadvizleSaNe mUlavizleSaNe zAkhAnAmiva zoSaNavizaraNopapatteH | ataH tvaddheturasiddhaH maddheturnAsiddhaH iti || atha ekAdazI-- 'asya somya mahato vRkSasya yo mUle'bhyAhanyAjjIvansravedyo madhye' bhyAhanyAjjIvansravedyo' gre'bhyAhanyAjjIvansravetsa eSa jIvenAtmanA anuprabhUtaH pepIyamAno modamAnastiSThati | asya yadekAM zAkhAM jIvo jahAti atha sA zuSyati dvitIyAM jahAtyatha sA zuSyati tRtIyAM jahAtyatha sA zuSyati sarvaM jahAti sarvaM zuSyati' iti zrutyA zAkhAzoSaNasya jIvotkramaNaprayuktatvaM pratipAditam | tanyAyena zarIrAntarazoSaNamapi tatprayuktam ityevAvagamyate | tasmAt ubhayathApi upapadyamAnaH jIvotkramaNAnantaraM zarIrazoSaNAdyanubhavaH jIvavizleSaprayuktazoSAdiviSayaH ityeva AzrayaNIyamiti, tataH zarIrasya jIvAzrayatvasiddheH maddheturnAsiddhaH, tvaddheturevAsiddha iti || atha dvAdazI-- na udAhRtazrutiH zAkhAzoSaNasya jIvotkramaNaprayuktatvaM pratipAdayati; kiMtu tadAnantaryamAtram | tattu zarIropAdAnabhUtasUkSmANAM jIvena saha utkrAntyavazyaMbhAvAt pAralaukikadehArambhakAdRSTakRtamapi upapadyate | ato dehasya jIvAzritatve pramANAbhAvAt tvaddheturasiddhaH, maddheturnAsiddha iti || atha trayodazI-- 'sa eSa jIvenAnuprabhUtaH' ityasya 'yadekAM zAkhAm' iti ca dehapoSaNazoSaNayoH jIvAnvayatadvyatirekau pradarzayantI udAhRtazrutiH dehazoSasya [p.275] jIvotkramaNaprayuktatvaM pratipAdayatyeva | na tu ArthasamAjavazaprAptatadAnantaryamAtraM pratipAdayati; anvayavyatirekapradarzanasya tatprayuktatvapratipattyarthatvAt | tasmAt udAhRtazrutiH jIvotkramaNasamaye bhUtasUkSmotkramaNazrutiM ca anurudhya dehopAdAnabhUtasUkSmANAM tadvArA dehasya ca jIvAzritatvamabhyupagamya 'gauro'ham' ityAdivivekivyavahArANAM pramAtvopapAdanameva yuktam, autsargikapramAtvanirvAhasaMbhave bhramatvakalpanAyogAt | na ca evaM vivekivyavahAra eva nAstIti vivadituM zakyam, 'puruSaM kRSNapiGgalam' ityAdivaidikavyavahArANAm, 'ardhapuMlakSaNaM vande puruSaM kRSNapiGgalam' ityAditanmUlapaurANikavyavahArANAM ca vedeSu purANeSu ca gaurakRSNalohitAdInAM prayogANAM ca bahulamupalambhAt, vedasya yathAvat vedArthanibandhRRNAmRSIzvarANAM ca paramezvarAdidehatAdAtmyaikyabhramamUlo'yaM vyavahAra iti vA tadIyasyaiva dehAtmaikatvabhramasya anuvAdarUpo'yamiti vaktumazakyatvAt tannyAyena vivekinAmapi 'gauro'ham' ityAdivyavahArANAM tanmUlapratItInAM ca saMbhAvitAnAM pratiSeddhamazakyatvAt | tasmAt dehopAdAnabhUtasUkSmatadAzritadehavat gaurAdiguNatadIyagauratvAdijAtInAmapi jIvAzritatvopapatteH madIyahetusiddhyarthapramANe tvaduktabAdhakAnavatArAt madIyo heturnAsiddhaH tvadIya evAsiddha iti siddhaM jIvasya gaurAdizabdavAcyatvam | tata eva tadvArA antaryAmiNo'pi tadvAcyatvamiti || iti bhramamUlatvazaGkAnirAsArthajIvAntaryAmizaktivAdaH ||10||

[p.275] gauro'hamityAdipratItyaviSayatvazaGkAnirAsArthAntaryAmizaktivAdaH || asmAdidehAdiguNavAcinAM gaurAdizabdAnAM jIve zaktisattve'pi na tadantaryAmiNi zaktirasti; pramANAbhAvAt iti zaGkAnirAsAya kathAntaraM prastUyate--tatra AditaH kakSyAdvayaM pUrvavat | gauradehaviziSTajIvAntaryAmI iti pakSanirdezaH iti vizeSaH || 'gauro'ham' ityAdibrahmavidanusaMdhAnasya antaryAmiparyantatvAt maddhetau pramANAbhAvo'siddhaH | ata eva tvaddheturasiddhaH iti tRtIyA ||
[p.276] atha caturthI-- daharAdyupAsakAH khalu brahmavidaH | tAzcAhaMgrahopAsanAH jIvabrahmAbhedaviSayAH; 'tvaM vA ahamasmi bhagavo devate ahaM vai tvamasi tadyohaM so'sau yo'sau so'ham atha yo'nyAM devatAmupAste anyosAvanyo'hamasmIti na sa veda yathA pazurevam' ityAdizrutisvArasyAt | tatazca daharAdyupAsanAbhyAsadazApannAnAM svabhAvanApratipAdakavAkye yathA zivAdizabdAH parabrahmaparAH evamahaMzabdaH pratyagAtmaparaH | upAsanAviSayastayorabhedastu mukhacandrAdyabhedavat vAkyArthavidhayA bhAsate | evaM ca tatra ahaMzabdasyaiva brahmaparyantatvAbhAve, kutaH tadvizeSaNatvena tvayA ucyamAnasya gaurAdizabdasya tatparyantatA | ye tu daharAdyupAsakAH ciraM nirantarAbhyAsadArDhyena pratyagAtmanaH zivAt bhedaM vismRtya ziva ityeva svAtmAnaM bhAvayanti, teSAm 'zivo'hamasmi' iti vAkye zuktirajatabhramamUlavAkye rajatazabdasya zuktAviva ahaMpadasya zive paryavasAnasattve'pi rajatavAcyatvaM zukteriva ahaMzabdavAcyatvameva antaryAmiNastena na sidhyati | natarAM tasya vizeSaNagaurAdizabdavAcyatvopayogitatpravRttinimittAzrayatvam | ataH pramANAbhAvAt tvaddheturasiddhaH, maddhetuzca nAsiddha iti ||
atha paJcamI-- paramazivaM paraM brahma daharAdividyAbhiH svAbhedenopAsInA iva svasya anyeSAM ca sarveSAmantaryAmitayA nityaniyAmakatvena svAmI paramazivaH taddAsabhUtA vayaM sarve ityantaryAmibrAhmaNAdizAstrato'vagamya laukikagarbhadAsAnAM tattatsvAminIva asmAkaM sahajasiddhe svAmini sarvaizvaryasaMpanne mahAkAruNike sarvAtmanA asmadrakSaNajAgarUke sthite, na anyasya yasya kasyacit AzrayaNaM yuktamiti 'tvaM deveSu brAhmaNo'syahaM manuSyeSu brAhmaNo vai brAhmaNamupadhAvati upa tvA dhAvAmi tvAM prapadye tvayA prasUta idaM karma kariSyAmi yo vai svAM devatAmatiyajate prasvAyai devatAyai cyavate na parAM prApnoti pApIyAnbhavati' ityAdizAstrataH taddAsabhUtaiH sarvairasmAbhiH ananyazaraNatayA tameva pratipadya tadanujJayA tadArAdhanarUpeNaiva sarvANi karmANi kartavyAnIti [p.277] avabudhya tathaiva taM prapadya tadArAdhanarUpANyeva satkarmANi anutiSThanti, te'pi brahmavido bhavantyeva | teSAmantaryAmidRSTyA ahamityanusaMdhAneSu ahamarthavizeSaNatayA bhAsamAnAnAM gauratvAdInAmapi tatparyavasAnAvazyaMbhAvaH | atastathAbhUtAnusaMdhAnarUpapramANasadbhAvAt maddheturnAsiddhaH, tvaddheturevAsiddhaH - iti ||
atha SaSThI-- zivasya parabrahmaNaH prapatteH muktiphalakatvAt tadantargate zivasvAtmanoH svAmyadAsyAnusaMdhAne svAtmanyantaryAmiNi zive vA heyaprAkRtatadAtanagauratvAdivizeSaNaM na pravizati, 'yathAkraturasmi~lloke puruSo bhavati tathetaH pretya bhavati' iti zrutyA brahmavit yathAbhUtayAvadguNakalApaviziSTaM brahma anusaMdhatte tathAbhUtatAvadviziSTaM tatprApnoti ityupadiSTatvena pratipattimataH svaviziSTabrahmAnusaMdhAne'smin antaryAmiNi vA prAkRtadehatadguNAdipraveze tathAbhUtasvaviziSTabrahmaprAptiprasaktyA muktyabhAvApatteH; 'eka AtmanaH zarIre bhAvAt' ityadhikaraNoktanyAyena brahmaNi tattadvidyopasaMhAryaguNakalApAdiviziSTe prajApatividyopadiSTamuktiprApyApahatapApmatvAdimAtraviziSTasyaiva pratyagAtmanaH tanniyamyatayA anusaMdhAneSu nivezanIyatvAt | ataH pramANAbhAvAt tvaddheturasiddha eva, maddhetuzca nAsiddha iti ||
atha saptamI-- zaraNAgatirUpAyAH prapatteH muktireva phalaM na bhavati, 'bhItAH surA dudruvuraGga sezvarA arakSyamANAH zaraNaM sadA zivam' ityAdivacanaiH kAlakUTAdyApannivAraNArthAyA api tasyAH pratipAdanAt | muktiphalakatvamapi tasyAH daharAdyupAsanAjanyasAkSAtkAradvArA nirantaracintAnuvRtteH sAkSAtkArajanakatvasya loke darzanAt | 'tatastu taM pazyate niSkalaM dhyAyamAnaH' iti zAstradarzanAcca | AphalodayamAvartanIyAnAM daharAdyupAsanAnAmiva zivameva vizvasya tadAdhArarUpatayA sakalasatkarmAnuSThAnArambhAt prAganuSTheyAyAH tasyAH sAkSAtkArajanakatvAsaMbhavAt sAkSAtkArAbhAve 'draSTavyaH zrotavyo mantavyaH' 'tasmindRSTe parAvare' ityAdizrutibhiH sAkSAtkAraphalatvenAvagatAyA [p.278] mukteH tayA sAdhayitumazakyatvAt | na ca 'mumukSurvai zaraNamahaM prapadye' 'ajAta ityevaM kazcidbhIruH prapadyate' 'rudra yatte dakSiNaM mukhaM tena mAM pAhi nityam' ityAdizAstraprAmANyAt sAkSAtkAradvArAnapekSAyA eva tasyA muktiphalakatvaM kalpanIyam; udAhRtazrutyoH prapattyanuSThAnakaraNamantratvena vidhimantrAdhikaraNanyAyena svayaM mokSakAmaM prati pratipattyavidhAyakatvAt | sUktavAkamantragatAyurAdiphalakIrtanaiH prastarapraharaNasya AyurAdiphalakatvabodhakavidheriva atra phalavidheH kalpane ca, zrutyantarasiddhasAkSAtkAraniyamAvirodhAya tasyAH kramamuktyupayogizivakaiMkaryaikAntyAbhiratidvArA paramparayA mokSaphalakatvenaiva tasyA vidheH kalpanIyatvAt | anyathA sakRtkRtAyA api tasyAH sAkSAnmokSasAdhanatve tathaiva dhyAnopAstyAdizabdarahitAnAM vidimAtrazravaNavatInAmupakosalAdividyAnAmapi sakRdanuSThitAnAmeva sAkSAnmuktisAdhanatvasaMbhavena tAsAmAvRttyakalpanApatteH | etena 'khAdyAni cAdanIyAni peyAnyanyAni yAni ca | deyAni tAni vai zaMbhoraznIyAddAsabhAvataH | dAsamArgaprapannA ye zaivapAzupate sthitAH | taireva peyaM bhojyaM ca ghrAtavyaM ca mumukSubhiH' iti zivAgame dAsamArgaprapanneSu mumukSutvavizeSaNAt prapattermokSasAdhanatvamurarIkaraNIyam ityapi zaGkA nirastA, uktarItyA dAsamArgaprapadanamAtrasya tathA prapannenAnuSThIyamAnasya niveditAnAmeva svIkaraNaniyamasya dAsabuddhyA tatsvIkaraNasya vA antaHkaraNazuddhyAdidvAramanapekSya mokSasAdhanatvAyogAt | tasmAt prapadanasya mokSaphale yajJAditulyayogakSematvAt yajJAdyanuSThAturiva prapannasyApi svAtmani 'gauro'ham' ityAdyanusaMdhAnaM na virudhyate | tatazca tasya ahaMpratyayavyavahArau antaryAmiparyantam api dhAvataH iti tasminnapi tat na virudhyate | aviruddhaM ca tatsvAtmanIva svAntaryAmiNyapi kadAcit bhavat anivAryameva | tasmAt maddheturnAsiddhaH, tvaddheturevAsiddha iti siddhamantaryAmiNo'pi gaurAdizabdavAcyatvam || iti gauro'hamityAdipratItyaviSayatvazaGkAnirAsArthAntaryAmizaktivAdaH ||11||

[p.278] ahamAdipadaviSayAntaryAmiparyantatvAbhAvazaGkAnirAsArthAntaryAmizaktivAdaH || [p.279] ahaMpadasvAntaryAmiparyantatve khalu tadvizeSaNAnAM gaurAdipadAnAmapi tatparyantatvaM bhavet, tadevAsiddhamiti vipratipattinirAsArthaM kathAntaraM prastUyate-- tatrAditaH kakSyAtrayam avyavahitapUrvavAdavat ||
atha caturthI-- ahaMpadasya antaryAmiparyantatva eva na pramANam, lokavedayoH tasya tadviSayaprayogAdarzanAt | prapannAnAM svAtmazivayoH svasvAmibhAvAnusaMdhAnajanyaH tatra tatprayogo'stIti cet na, 'mamAntaryAmI matsvAmI zivaH tasyAhaM dAsaH' ityeva tatprayogopapatteH | tathaiva loke tatprayogadarzanAcca | tasmAttvaddheturasiddhaH, maddheturnAsiddha iti ||
atha paJcamI-- 'tadvaitatpazyannRSirvAmadevaH pratipede ahaM manurabhavaM sUryazca ahaM kakSIvAnRSirasmi vipraH' ityAdivaidikaprayogeSu ahaMpadasya antaryAmiparyantatA dRzyate | tatra vyutpannAnAM prapannAnAmapi tathaiva laukikaprayogo bhavet; vaidikAnAmanyatra vedaprayogavAsanAnuvRttidarzanAt | tasmAt maddheturnAsiddhaH, tvaddheturevAsiddha iti ||
atha SaSThI-- 'ahaM manurabhavam' ityatra tAvat ahaMpadaprayogo na niyamyaniyAmakatayA svAtmAntaryAmiNoH svasvAmibhAvaviSayaH, ciranirantarAbhyastAbhedopAsanAlabdhAbhedasAkSAtkAravato vAmadevasya manvAdisarvAtmakaH zivo'hameveti zuktau rajatapadasyeva [p.280] atra zive ahaMpadasya prayoga ityupapatteH | na ca tasya utpannasAkSAtkAratve pramANAbhAvaH, 'tadvaitatpazyan' iti tatraiva taduktidarzanAt; tathA 'mAmeva vijAnIhi etadevAhaM manuSyAya hitatamaM manye yanmAM vijAnIyAH' ityAdiSu pratardanaM prati indravAkyeSu 'mAm' iti prayogo'pi abhedaviSaya eva | tatra indrasya brahmasAkSAtkAravattvapratipAdakazabdAbhAve'pi jJAnasya hitatamatvaliGgena 'sa yo mAM vijAnIyAnnAsya kena ca karmaNA loko mIyate na mAtRvadhena na pitRvadhena na steyena na bhrUNahatyayA' iti tatphalakIrtanena liGgena ca sAkSAtkAraphalakazivAbhedopAsanArUpatAvagamAt | tathA 'tvAmeva vijAnIyAm' iti indraM prati kRtapraznaM vizvAmitraM prati 'prANo vA ahamasmi RSe prANastvaM prANaH sarvANi bhUtAni prANo hyeSa ya eSa tapati sa etena rUpeNa sarvA dizo viSTo'smi' ityuttaravAkye zrutamahaMpadamapi indreNa svAbhinnatayA avagata eva zive paryavasyati; na tvantaryAmitayAvagate, tatra indrasya sAkSAtkAravattvapratipAdakazabdAbhAve'pi tadupadiSTasya jJAnasya muktiphalatvAvagamakaliGgAbhAve'pi 'indrasya priyaM dhAmopeyAya tamindra uvAca RSe priyaM vai me dhAmopAgA varaM te dadAmi' ityAdinA pratardanAkhyAyikAzrutisAmAnyena tattulyArthatvAvagamAt; 'vayaH suparNA upasedurindraM priyamedhA RSayo nAdhamAnAH apa dhvAntamUrNuhi pUrdhi cakSurmumugdhyasmAnnidhayeva baddhAn' iti mantreNa muktiphalakAbhedopAsanAlAbhArthameva RSINAmindropasadanapratipAdanAcca | asya hi mantrasya ayamarthaH priyamedhAH yajJapriyAH vidyArthayajJAdikarmAnuSThAnena zuddhAntaHkaraNAH, ata eva suparNA vayaH zobhanapakSAH pakSiNaH- yathA pakSabalavantaH pakSiNaH satatamutpatanotsAhavanto bhavanti evamarcirAdimArgasAdhyordhvagamanAbhilASavantaH mumukSava iti yAvat evaMbhUtA RSayaH nAdhamAnAH arthayamAnAH santaH indramupaseduH, kiM nAdhamAnA ityAkAGkSitam uttarArdhena ucyate - dhvAntamavidyAvaraNam aporNuhi apAvRNu, cakSuH 'mano'sya daivaM cakSuH' iti zrutiprasiddhaM cicchaktirUpaM cakSuH idAnIM saMsAradazAyAM saMkucitaM pUrdhi pUraya vikasitaM kuru, nidhayeva pAzeneva viSayajAlena baddhAnasmAn mumugdhi muJca iti | nidhAzabdaH pAzavAcitvena aitareyabrAhmaNe eva vyAkhyAtaH 'mumugdhyasmAnnidhayeva baddhAniti pAzA vai nidhAH mumugdhyasmAn pAzAdiva baddhAn ityeva tadAha' iti | na ca 'prANo vA ahamasmi RSe' ityAdeH svAbhinnabrahmaparatvAGgIkAre 'tvAmeva vijAnIyAm' iti vAkyavairUpyApattirdoSaH, indrasya tejovizeSeNa dRzyamAnena indraH svAbhinnabrahmopAsakaH [p.281] iti nizcitya tadabhiprAyAnusAreNa brahmaviSayatayaiva 'tvAm' iti prayogopapatteH | 'brahmavida iva vai somya te mukhaM bhAti' ityAdizrutyantareSu brahmavidi tejovizeSasya prasiddhatvAt | evam 'ahaM brahmAsmi' iti bRhadAraNyakavAkyamapi svAbhinnabrahmAnusaMdhAnArtham; na tu svAntaryAmitadanusaMdhAnArtham; 'yadbrahmavidyayA sarvaM bhaviSyanto manuSyA manyante' iti upakrame svAbhinnabrahmopAsanAjanyasAkSAtkAraphalasya sarvAtmabhAvasya kIrtanAt | sa hi tasyaiva phalam, 'tadapazyattadabhavatprajAsu' iti taittirIyAraNyake zravaNAt; 'tadyo'haM so'sau yo'sau so'haM tvaM vA ahamasmi bhagavo devate ahaM vai tvamasi' iti vAkyayoH tvamahaMzabdasAmAnAdhikaraNyamabhedArthakamiti spaSTameva | anyathA vyatihArAnupapatteH | nahi jIvaparasya ahaMzabdasya tvaMzabdanirdiSTe'ntaryAmiNi vA antaryAmiparasya tvaMzabdasya ahaMzabdanirdiSTe jIve vRttistvayA abhyupagamyate | etena 'tattvamasi' iti vAkyamapi abhedArthakatvena vyAkhyAtam; asizabdayogAt uddezyatvenAvagate jIve tatpadasya jagatkAraNabrahmaparasya vRttyayogAt | ataH pramANAbhAvAt tvaddheturasiddhaH maddhetuzca nAsiddha iti ||
atha saptamI-- 'ahaM manurabhavam' iti zrutau manvAdizabdAstAvat manvAdyantaryAmitvAbhiprAyAH, na tu tadabhedAbhiprAyAH, brahmaNi svAbhedasyeva manvAdyabhedasya prAganucintanAbhAvena tadvAsanAdArDhyasya dUranirastatvAt; tatsamabhivyAhArAt 'aham' iti prayogo'pi svAntaryAmitvAbhiprAyaH ityeva yuktam | na ca abhedavAsanAdArDhyAt vismRtabhedasya vAmadevasya prayoge tathA abhiprAyakalpanaM na yuktamiti vAcyam; svAtmazivayoH tAttviko bhedo'stIti vAsanAyAH sarvAtmanA luptatve'pi aupAdhikabhedapratItitadvAsanAvRttyoH sattvAt | anyathA utpannasAkSAtkArasya bhagavataH kRSNasya gItAsu 'tameva cAdyaM puruSaM prapadye' iti karmakartRbhAvanirdezasya harivaMze tatkRtabilvodakezvarastutau 'ahaM brahmA kapilo'thApyanantaH putrAH sarve brahmaNazcAtivIrAH | tvattaH sarve devadeva prasUtA evaM sarvezaH kAraNAtmA tvamIDyaH' iti kAryakAraNabhAvanirdezasya anyeSAM ca zrutipurANAdiSu bahulamupalabhyamAnAnAmevaMvidhanirdezAnAm anupapattiprasaGgAt | tathA 'mAmeva vijAnIhi' iti indravAkye 'mAm' iti [p.282] prayogo'pi evameva vyAkhyeyaH | 'zAstradRSTyA tUpadezo vAmadevavat' iti sUtrakRtA tasya vAmadevavAkyasamAnArthatvasya sUtritatvAt; tathaiva bhASyakRtA tasya sUtrasya vyAkhyAtatvAcca | yattu 'athavA' ityAdibhASyeNa dRSTAntadArSTAntikayorabhedadRSTiparatayA vyAkhyAnAntaraM kRtam, tat muktiphalakadaharAdyupAsanam abhedenaiva, na tu svasvAmibhAveneti 'Atmeti tUpagacchanti grAhayanti ca' ityAdyadhikaraNeSu vyavasthApayiSyamANasyArthasya sUtraNamAtram, na tu zAstradRSTisUtravivakSitatvAbhiprAyam; 'jIvamukhyaprANaliGgAnneti cennopAsAtraividhyAt' iti tadanantarasUtratadbhASyayoH brahmaNaH svarUpeNa indrazarIrakatvena prANazarIrakatvena ca trividhamupAsanamiha vivakSitamiti sphuTaM pratipAditatvAt | anyathA tadvirodhApatteH | muktiphalakamupAsanamabhedena kAryamiti pakSamAsthitavataH kathamihAntaryAmitvAbhiprAyavarNanaM saMgacchate iti cet- bhrAnto'si, na hi indrAntaryAmi paraM brahma ahamevAsmi iti indrAntaryAmitvena anusaMdhIyamAnasya svAbhedena anyapratardanAdikartRkopAsane kazcidvirodho'sti, indrakartRke'pi svAntaryAmitayA svAbhedena ca upAsane virodho nAsti, aupAdhikasvAbhAvikabhedAbhedAnusaMdhAnena ca tadubhayopapatteH | ata eva muktiphalAsu madhvAdividyAsu vasvAdInAmapi adhikAro devatAdhikaraNe vyavasthApitaH | evameva vizvAmitraM prati indravacanamapi antaryAmiviSayaM draSTavyam | evaM ca ahamAdipadAnAmantaryAmiparyantatAyA vedeSu dRSTatvAt teSu vyutpannAH prapannAH zivasvAtmanoH svAmyadAsyAnusaMdhAnasamayeSu antaryAmiparyantamahaMpadaM prayuJjAnA virodhAbhAvAdanusaMhitaM gauratvAdyapi ahamarthavizeSaNatvena vyavahAre nivezayeyuH | tasmAnmaddheturnAsiddhaH, tvaddheturevAsiddha iti siddhamantaryAmiNo'pi gaurAdizabdavAcyatvam || ityahamAdipadaviSayAntaryAmiparyantatvAbhAvazaGkAnirAsArthAntaryAmizaktivAdaH ||12||

[p.282] ahamAdipadaviSayAntaryAmitvaparyantazaktyabhAvaprayuktazaGkAnirAsArthAntaryAmizaktivAdaH || atha ahamAdizabdAnAM prapannaprayuktAnAmantaryAmiNi zaktisattve'pi antaryAmitvena zaktirnAstItyetatprayuktazaGkAnirAsArthaM kathAntaraM prastUyate-- atra AditaH kakSyApaJcakamavyavahitapUrvavAdavat ||
[p.283] atha SaSThI-- ahamAdipadAnAmantaryAmiNi zaktirnAstIti nocyate, kiMtu antaryAmitvena zaktirnAsti iti; ekazaktipakSe paramasiddhAntatvena tvayA abhyupagate svAtmanyahaMpadasya vRttau yadAtmatvaM pravRttinimittaM yazca svAtmamAtravRttirdharmaH prayogopAdhiH, tAbhyAmeva paramparAsaMbandhAbhyAM tasyAntaryAmiparyantatAyAH samarthanIyatvAt anekazaktipakSe antaryAmiNyantaryAmitvena zaktyantarAbhyupagamasaMbhave'pi tasya paramasiddhAntatvena tvayA anabhyupagatatvAt ityucyate | antaryAmitvena zaktireva ca tvatpakSopapAdanArthamapekSitA | atastadabhAvAt prapannA na gauro'hamityAdi antaryAmyabhiprAyeNa prayuJjate; kiMtu gauratvAdiviziSTasya mamAntaryAmI zivaH svAmI zivaH ityAdyeva prayuJjate iti vaktavyam | atastvaddheturasiddhaH maddheturnAsiddha iti ||
atha saptamI-- antaryAmitvena zaktiH matpakSopapAdanArthaM nApekSyate, prapannAnAM svaprayuktasya ahaMpadasya antaryAmiNi zaktisadbhAvAvagatimAtrAt antaryAmyabhiprAyeNa gauro'ham ityAdiprayogasyApyupapatteH | ataH maddheturnAsiddhaH tvaddheturevAsiddha iti ||
athASTamI-- prapannAH svAtmazivayoH svasvAmibhAvarUpaM niyamyaniyAmakabhAvaM svAnusaMhitaM prakAzayituM khalu zabdAnprayuJjate | ahaMpadasyAntaryAmitvena zaktyabhAvAt sa tatprakAzane na samarthaH | ataH te mamAntarAtmA ityAtmanyeva svAnusaMhitArthaprakAzanArthaM prayuJjata iti tvaddheturasiddha eva, maddhetuzca nAsiddha iti ||
[p.284] atha navamI-- ahaMpadasyAntaryAmiNi svAtmavRttibhyAmeva pravRttinimittaprayogopAdhibhyAM vRttAvapi tayostasmin paramparAsaMbandhaghaTakasvAtmazivasaMbandharUpatayA svAtmazivayorniyamyaniyAmakabhAvasyApi antaryAmiparyantAdahaMpadAt pratItirbhavatyeva | ataH prapannAnAM svAnusaMhitasvAmyadAsyaprakAzanArthamantaryAmiparyanto gauro'ham ityAdivyavahAraH saMbhavatyeveti maddheturnAsiddhaH, tvaddheturevAsiddha iti ||
atha dazamI-- svAtmazivayorAzrayAzrayibhAvAdisaMbandhAntarasyApi sattvAt tenApi paramparAsaMbandhaghaTanopapatteH antaryAmiparyantenAhaMpadena antaryAmitvapratItiniyamaH samarthayituM na zakyate | ataH tatprakAzanaikaparAyaNaM prapannAnAmuktavyavahAro na saMbhavatIti tvaddheturevAsiddhaH, maddhetuzca nAsiddha iti ||
athaikAdazI-- prapannAnAM zivakaiMkaryAbhiratisthirIkaraNArthaM sahakAryantarAdhikaraNoktanyAyasAmyena zivasvAtmanoH svAmyadAsyAnusaMdhAnAbhyAsavazAt svAmyadAsyarUpa eva tayoH saMbandhaH svAtmavRttyahaMpadapravRttinimittaprayogopAdhidharmaparamparAsaMbandhaghaTakatayA sphuret | sphuritasaMbandhatAtparyeNaiva ca teSAmantaryAmiparyanto gauro'ham iti vyavahAro vaktRtAtparyAnusAreNaiva ca zrotRRNAM tato'rthapratItizca bhavet | tasmAdahamAdizabdAnAmantaryAmitvena zaktyabhAve'pi tebhyo'ntaryAmitvapratIterupapannatayA prapannAnAmantaryAmiparyante gauro'ham ityAdivyavahAre anupapattyabhAvAt maddheturnAsiddhaH, tvaddheturevAsiddha iti siddhaM madanumAnena antaryAmiNo gaurAdizabdavAcyatvam || iti ahamAdipadaviSayAntaryAmitvaparyantazaktyabhAvaprayuktazaGkAnirAsArthAntaryAmizaktivAdaH ||13||

[p.284] brAhmaNAdizabdadRSTAntaprasaktagaurAdizabdavAcyatvazaGkAnirAsArthAntaryAmizaktivAdaH || [p.285] atha gauro'ham ityAdiprapannAnusaMdhAnamUlakavyavahAreSu ahaMpadasya antaryAmiparyantatve'pi tadvizeSaNagaurAdipadAnAM na tatparyantatvam, tathAtve bAdhakasadbhAvAt iti zaGkAnirAsArthaM kathAntaraM prastUyate-- tatra AditaH kakSyAtrayaM pUrvavat ||
atha caturthI-- gauratvAdivizeSaNAnAm antaryAmiparyantatve tattulyanyAyatayA 'brAhmaNo'ham' 'kSatriyo'ham' ityAdivyavahAreSu brAhmaNatvAdivizeSaNAnAmapi tatparyantatA syAditi antaryAmiNaH parabrahmaNaH zivasyApi brAhmaNAdyadhikArikarmavidhiniyojyatA prasajyate | na ca brAhmaNakSatriyAdizarIrAvacchinnAvasthatayA brAhmaNAdizabdavAcyatApannasyAntaryAmiNaH tattaccharIrAvacchedena tattadadhikArikarmaniyojyatAyAmiSTApattiH; tathA sati pUrvapUrvazarIrAvacchinnAntaryAmikRtakarmajanyAnyuttarottarazarIrANi sa parigRhNAtIti tasya karmajanyazarIraparigraheNa jIvAviziSTatayA jIvavattattaccharIraprayuktasukhaduHkhabhoktRtvApatteH | na ca zivoddezyakairaGgapradhAnabhUtairyAgahomaiH karambiteSu karmasu tasya niyojyatA na saMbhavati, AtmAnamuddizya tyajyamAnasya hUyamAnasya vA dravyasya svatvatyAgAsaMbhavAt, ato na devatA devatAntarAbhAvAt iti tiryagadhikaraNoktadevatAnadhikAranyAyasAmyAttasya karmasu niyojyatA na prasajyata iti vAcyam; tathApi devatoddezarahiteSu varNAzramAdyupAdhinA vihiteSu karmasu tasya niyojyatvaprasaktyanivAraNAt | na ca teSAmapi karmaNAM ziva eva phaladAteti 'phalamata upapatteH' ityadhikaraNe nirNItatvAt svAtmane svayaM phaladAtRtvAsaMbhavAcca na uktadoSaH prasajyata iti vAcyam; sthite hi tasya varNAzramAdidharmabhUtakarmaniyojyatvAbhAve teSAM sa phaladAtA sidhyet, svayamapi tattatkarmaniyojyazcet kathaM teSAM sa phaladAtA iti nyAyasahasreNApi sidhyet; na ca 'na karmaNA vardhate no kanIyAn' iti zrutibalAttasya [p.287] vihitaniSiddhakarmAnuSThAneSvapi na taM puNyapApe spRzataH iti tasya puNyapApAdhInazarIrAprasaktyA na tatprayuktasukhaduHkhabhoktRtvamApadyata iti vAcyam; na hi sA zrutiH tAtparyavadvidhiniSedhavAkyamUlakarmAdhInazarIravattvenApatat taccharIraprayuktasukhaduHkhabhoktRtvamarthavAdo niroddhamISTe | sa eva kevalaM prazaMsArthaH syAt | tasmAttasya jIvatulyatayA sAMsArikabhogaprasaktiM nivArayitumicchatA tasya vidhiniSedhavAkyagatabrAhmaNAdizabdavAcyatvApAdakatatpravRttinimittabrAhmaNatvAdyanAzrayatvamabhyupagantavyam ityanicchatApi brAhmaNo'ham ityAdyanusaMdhAneSu brAhmaNatvAdInAmahaMpadapravRttinimittaparamparAsaMbandhaghaTakatayA pratIyamAne jIva evAnvayo'GgIkAryaH, tathaiva gauratvAdInAmapi syAt | na ca sarvAdhArasya brahmaNo gauratvabrAhmaNatvAdyanAzrayatvaM saMghaTata iti vAcyam; dezakAlavat sarvAdhAratAprayojakasaMbandhena tadAzrayatve'pi tatpravRttinimittakazabdavAcyatAprayojakasaMbandhena tadanAzrayatvopapatteH | ataH tvaddheturasiddhaH, maddhetuzca nAsiddha iti || atha paJcamI-- lokavedasAdhAraNaprasiddhibAhulyAt vidhiniSedhavAkyagataiH brAhmaNAdizabdaiH prathamopasthitAnAM jIvAnAmeva vAkyArthAnvayaH syAt, na tu vaidikavyutpattigrahApekSayA paramparAsaMbandhaghaTakajIvAtmagatabrAhmaNatvagrahaNApekSayA ca vilambitopasthitikasya zivasyApi | ataH tasya brAhmaNatvAdyAzrayatve'pi tatpravRttinimittakabrAhmaNAdizabdavAcyatve'pi karmasu niyojyatA na prasajyata iti brAhmaNatvAdivat gauratvAdInAmapi pradhAnAnvayopapatteH na apradhAnAnvayo'bhyupagantavyaH | tasmAnmaddheturnAsiddhaH, tvaddheturevAsiddha iti || atha SaSThI-- pravRttinimittAzrayatvena zabdavAcyatvAvizeSe kasyacidvAkyArthAnvayaH kasyacinna ityardhajaratIyAzrayaNena saMkoco na yuktaH | na ca laukikavaidikagavAdizabdaprayogeSu prasiddhibAhulyAt sAsnAvatprabhRtInAmeva vAkyArthAnvayaH, netareSAmiti [p.288] dRSTamiti vAcyam; pravRttinimittabhedena zaktibhede hi vRttidvayavirodhaparihArArthaM kasyacideva zakyasya grahaNaM kAryam, nAnyasya iti sthitau, kasya grahaNaM kAryamityAkAGkSAyAM tatra vinigamakatvena prasiddhibAhulyamAdaraNIyam | pravRttinimittAbhedena zaktyabhede tu vRttidvayavirodhAprasakteH sarvasyApi zakyasya grahaNaM yuktam | kasyacidvaidikavyutpattividhurasya tadapekSArthAnupasthitau tadvyutpattyanusArAt tatprasiddhimAtrAnvayena vAkyArthapratyayaH tasya jAyate cedapi, puruSAparAdhaprayukto bhramarUpa ityeva kalpayitumucitam | anyathA svIyAyAmeva gavi gozabdaprasiddhibAhulyavataH 'gAmAlabheta' iti vAkye gozabdAttanmAtraviSaya iva bhavantI pratItirapi bhramo na syAt | tathA antaryAmigatabrAhmaNatvagrahaNasya jIvagatatadgrahaNApekSatve'pi antaryAmiNo'pi vidhiniSedhavAkyagataiH brAhmaNazabdairgrahaNaM nivArayituM na zakyam; anyathA putragatabrAhmaNyagrahaNasya pitRgatabrAhmaNyagrahaNApekSatvApatteH pitureva grahaNaM syAt na putrasya | tasmAdantaryAmiNaH zivasya brAhmaNatvAdyAzrayatve pUrvoktadoSo na nivArayituM zakyate iti tadanAzrayatvam, tattulyanyAyatayA gauratvAdyanAzrayatvamapi svIkartavyam | ataH tvaddheturasiddhaH, maddheturnAsiddha iti || atha saptamI-- sarvapuruSasAdhAraNyena niyataM yasya yacchabdArthatayA prasiddhibAhulyAdinA jhaTiti pratItiH, tasyaiva vaidikavAkyagatena zabdena grahaNamiti niyamo'styeva | ata eva 'darzapUrNamAsAbhyAM svargakAmo yajeta' ityadhikAravAkyagatena pUrNamAsazabdena 'yadAgneyo'STAkapAlo'mAvAsyAyAM ca paurNamAsyAM cAcyuto bhavati' 'tAbhyAmetamagnISomIyamekAdazakapAlaM pUrNamAse prAyacchat' iti pratyakSazrutapaurNamAsIkAlasaMbandhatayA zIghrapratItikayorAgneyAgnISomIyayAgayoreva grahaNena tayoreva phalasaMbandhAt prAdhAnyaM syAt, na tu 'upAMzuyAjamantarA yajati' iti tadubhayAntarAlakAlavidhisAmarthyalabhyapaurNamAsIsaMbandhasya upAMzuyAjasyApi | ataH tasyApi tatsiddhaye zrutapaurNamAsIkAlatvena tadubhayAvizeSasiddhyartham 'tAvabrUtAmagnISomAvAjyasyaiva nAvupAMzu paurNamAsyAM yajan' iti pratyakSaH tasya paurNamAsIkAlavidhiriti mImAMsakairaGgIkRtam | gozabdena tattadgovizeSapratItistu na sarvasAdhAraNI | tathA niyamena yadgatayacchabda[p.289]pravRttinimittAvagatisApekSA anyatra tadavagatiH, tatra vaidikena tena zabdena tasyaiva grahaNamityapi niyatameva | ata eva 'na prathamayajJe pravRJjyAt' iti vAkyagatena prathamazabdena sAkSAtprAthamyAzrayatayA prathamaM pramitasya prayogasyaiva grahaNam; na tu tadgataprAthamyAnusaMdhAnasApekSAvagatikaparamparAsaMbandhiprAthamyAzrayasya jyotiSTomasyeti prathamaprayogAGgamayaM pravargyaniSedhaH; na tu prathamaprayogaviSayajyotiSTomakratvaGgam iti 'saMkhyAyuktaM kratoH prakaraNAtsyAt' iti tRtIyAdhyAyAdhikaraNe nirNItam | putragatabrAhmaNyagrahaNaM tu na niyamena pitRbrAhmaNyagrahaNasApekSam, pratyakSeNa tadgrahaNe tadapekSAsattve'pi AptavAkyena tadgrahaNe tadapekSAbhAvAt | iha tu jIvagataM brAhmaNyaM laukikavaidikaprasiddhibAhulyena antaryAmigatabrAhmaNyAvagatyapekSitAnusaMdhAnatvena ca jhaTiti prathamaM pratIyata iti tadgrahaNena caritArthaiH vidhiniSedhavAkyagatabrAhmaNAdizabdaiH jIvAnAmeva grahaNaM bhavati; na tu antaryAmiNo'pi; vilambitapratItikatvAt | zabdAnAM viramyavyApArAbhAvena pazcAt tadgrahaNAsaMbhavAt | tasmAt pravRttinimittAzrayatve'pi vidhiniSedhavAkyagatairbrAhmaNazabdairantaryAmiNo na grahaNaM prasajyata iti maddheturnAsiddhaH, tvaddheturevAsiddha iti || athASTamI-- prasiddhibAhulyena apekSitatvena vA niyatazIghrapratItirapi aniyatazIghrapratItivadeva na vaidikazabdArthagrahaNe prayojikA, ubhayatrApi vRttyabhedasya arthadvayagrahaNaprayojakasya aviziSTatvena niyamataH prathamapratItArthamAtragrahaNe prayojakatvakalpanAbhAvAt | na caivaM sati udAhRtapaurNamAsIvidhivaiyarthyam ; 'tAvabrUtAm' ityAdivAkyAmnAnAntaram 'upAMzuyAjamantarA yajati' iti vAkyAmnAnena tasminnutpattivAkye zrutasya antarAzabdasya paurNamAsyAmeva paryavasAnena 'tAvabrUtAm' ityAdivAkyasya utpattivAkyazrutapaurNamAsIkAlatvena upAMzuyAjasya AgneyAgnISomIyAvizeSopAdAnArthatvAt | evaM ca utpattivAkyazrutapaurNamAsyavirodhAdeva upAMzuyAjasya amAvAsyAyAM na prasaktiriti na tadvyAvRttiphalakaparisaMkhyArUpatvaM jaghanyam 'tAvabrUtAm' ityAdivAkyasya kalpanIyam; pravargyAdhikaraNanyAyasiddhaM ca; anyathA hi pravargyaniSedhaH prAthamyasaMyuktasya jyotiSTomasya kratorevAGgaM bhavet; prakaraNAnugrahalAbhAt | na ca prAthamyasaMyuktAvasthApannasyaiva grahaNaM syAditi prakaraNAnugrahalAbhaH; [p.292] antaryAmiviSayapRthivyAdizabdavAcyatvasamarthanavAdaH || atha zarIravAcinAM guNavacanabrAhmaNAdizabdAnAmantaryAmiparyantatvasiddhAvapi pRthivyudakAditaTasthadravyavAcinAM tatparyantatvaM suSuptimUrchAdizarIrANAM tattadabhimAninaM pratyaniyamyatvena kalpAdau paramezvareNa sRSTAnAM kalpAntaparyantasthAyinAM prANendriyAdInAM pratimanvantarabhinnAn tattadabhimAnidevAn prati kAlAntareSu sarvamuktyabhAvapakSe tat kvacit kvacit nAsti | bhRtyAdInAmAjJAdinA niyamanamAtraM ca na zarIratvaprayojakam; kiM tu vyAptyA niyamanameveti vyaJjayituM tAvaddUraM lakSaNamuktam| na caivamapi ityAdyanusaMdhAnaiH teSAM pRthivItvAdyAzrayatvasiddhau teSAmapi antaryAmibrAhmaNoktarItyA antaryAmizarIratvAt brahmavidAM teSAM pRthivyaham ityAdyanusaMdhAnam antaryAmiparyantaM bhavediti tato'ntaryAmiNaH paramezvarasya pRthivItvAdyAzrayatvalAbhe sati tasya tatpravRttinimittakapRthivyAdizabdavAcyatvamapi sidhyatIti sarvaM samaJjasam || ityantaryAmiviSayapRthivyAdizabdavAcyatvasamarthanavAdaH ||15||

[p.298] antaryAmiviSayasakalazabdavAcyatvasamarthanavAdaH || nanvevamapi paramezvarasya zarIrAdizabdavAcyatvAsiddheH sarvazabdavAcyatvaM tasya na sidhyatIti zaGkAnirAsAya kathAntaraM prastUyate-- tatra paramezvaraH zarIrAdizabdavAcyaH, zarIrazabdapravRttinimittAdhikaraNatvAt, yat zarIrazabdapravRttinimittAdhikaraNam, tat zarIrazabdavAcyam - yathA zarIram - iti prathamA kakSyA || atha dvitIyA-- asiddho'yaM hetuH, zarIrazabdapravRttinimittaM hi zarIratvam; tadadhikaraNatvaM jIvasyaiva tAvat nAsti, gauro'ham, brAhmaNo'ham ityAdyanubhavavat zarIramaham, kalebaramaham, hasto'ham, pAdo'ham ityAdyanubhavasya kadApyadarzanAt; kutaH tadvArA antaryAmiparyantena ahamanubhavena prApaNIyam antaryAmiNaH zarIratvAdhikaraNatvaM sidhyet | na ca ahamanubhavamanapekSya tatprApakaM pramANAntaraM vaktuM zakyam; tathAbhUtasya kasyacit pramANasya adarzanAt; na ca sarvAdhAratvAt brahmaNaH zarIratvAdhikaraNatvamapi tasya apratikSepyamiti vAcyam, kAlAdisAdhAraNena sarvAdhAratAprayojakasaMbandhena pravRttinimittAdhikaraNatvasya zabdavAcyatvAprayojakatvAt; tatprayojakasaMbandhavizeSeNa zarIratvAdhikaraNatva eva pramANasya mRgyatvAt| tasmAt paramezvaraH zarIrazabdavAcyo na bhavati, zarIratvAnadhikaraNatvAt, yat zarIratvAnadhikaraNaM na tat zarIrazabdavAcyam yathA jIvasvarUpam - iti ||
atha tRtIyA kakSyA-- paramezvarasya zarIratvAdhikaraNatve pramANAbhAvo'siddhaH, 'sarvaM khalvidaM brahma' 'sarvo vai rudra' 'anena jIvenAtmanAnupravizya nAmarUpe vyAkaravANi' 'taddhedaM tarhAvyAkRtamAsIt tannAmarUpAbhyAM vyAkriyata' 'sarvANi rUpANi vicitya dhIro nAmAni [p.299] kRtvAbhivadanyadAste' iti zrutirUpapramANasadbhAvAt | ato na maddheturasiddhaH, tvaddheturevAsiddha iti || atha caturthI-- 'sarvaM khalvidaM brahma' 'sarvo vai rudra' iti zrutidvayamaupacArikAbhedAbhiprAyeNa tAttvikAbhedAbhiprAyeNa vA upapannaM na sarvapravRttinimittAzrayatvasamarpaNakSamam | 'anena jIvenAtmanA' iti zrutivacanaM prapaJcagatanAmarUpavyAkaraNArthatvenApyupapadyamAnaM sarveSAM nAmarUpANAM paramezvaraparyantavyAkaraNArthatvaM nApekSate | 'tannAmarUpAbhyAM vyAkriyata' iti vacanaM parabrahmAsAdhAraNanAmarUpavyAkaraNArthatvenApyupapadyamAnaM na sarvanAmarUpavyAkaraNasya parabrahmaparyantatvArthaM spRzati | 'sarvANi rUpANi' iti mantre sarvanAmarUpagrahaNe satyapi sa mantraH prapaJcagatanAmarUpasaMpAdanatatpravRttinimittakatattadvastunAmakaraNArthatvenApyupapadyate | ataH pramANAbhAvAt tvaddheturasiddhaH, maddhetuzca nAsiddha iti || atha paJcamI-- 'nAmarUpe vyAkaravANi' 'tannAmarUpAbhyAM vyAkriyata' 'sarvANi rUpANi' iti zrutitrayasyApi nAmarUpavyAkaraNazrutitvAvizeSAt saMbhavadaikArthyaM na parityaktuM yuktam| ataH zrutitrayArthopasaMhAre tejo'bannAtmakasakalaprapaJcagatanAmarUpavyAkaraNasyApi parabrahmaparyantatvaM labhyate | ata eva 'sarvANi rUpANi' iti mantrAnantaram 'dhAtA purastAdyamudAjahAra zakraH pravidvAnpradizazcatasraH' iti paramezvareNa svaviSayatayA kRtairnAmabhiH paramezvarasya prathamaputro hiraNyagarbhaH prathamaM vyavajahAra; tataH prakarSeNa vidvAn zakro'pi vyavajahAra; tataH krameNa sarvAsu dikSu sthitAH prajA api vyavajahuH-- ityartho jJAyate| yadyapi asminmantre paramezvaraviSayavyavaharaNamAtraM zrUyate na tu pUrvamantroktaiH sarvairnAmabhirityapi zrUyate; tathApi pUrvamantraikavAkyatAsAmarthyAt so'rtho labhyate | ataH pramANasattvAt na maddheturasiddhaH, tvaddheturevAsiddhaH iti || atha SaSThI-- [p.300] zarIrAdinAmavyAkaraNasya paramezvaraparyantatvAGgIkAro na yuktaH, tatparamparAsaMbandhaghaTakatvAbhimatajIvaparyantatvasyaivAbhAvAt tadabhAve tadvArA paramezvaraparyantatvAsaMbhavAt | tasmAt bAdhite'rthe zrutInAM parasparArthopasaMhAreNa tAtparyakalpanamayuktam, zarIratvAdInAM jIvaparyantatvAbhAvazca zarIramaham, hasto'ham, pAdo'ham - ityanubhavAbhAvAdeva spaSTaH | evaM zarIratadavayavagatazarIratvAdidharmANAM svato jIvaparyantatvAbhAvAdeva - kANo'ham, khaJjo'ham ityAdyanubhavavyavahAranirvAhArtham arzaAdigaNe 'svAGgAddhInAt' iti pAThena svAGgavAcinaH kANakhaJjAdizabdAt matvarthIyo'cpratyayo vihitaH; tataH pramANAbhAvAt tvaddheturasiddhaH, maddhetuzca nAsiddhaH iti || atha saptamI-- zarIrasya antaryAmiNi paramezvare zarIrazabdavAcyatvasaMbandhaghaTanArthadvArabhUte jIve tasya saMbandhamAtramapekSyate | na tu tathAbhUta eva saMbandhaH; saMyuktavizeSaNavizeSyabhAvAdiSu ghaTakaghaTanIyasaMbandhayorekarUpatvaniyamAbhAvadarzanAt | tathA ca udAhRtazrutitrayasya nyAyalabhyArthaikyopapAdanAya zarIrasya jIve'pi kazcit zarIradvArakaH paramparAsaMbandho'sti iti kalpyate; zrutopapAdanArthamazrutakalpanaucityAt | athavA zrutopapAdanArthameva jIvadvAramanapekSya paramezvare zarIrazabdavAcyatAprayojakaH sAkSAdeva zarIratvasya saMbandhavizeSo'stIti kalpyate | evaM ca kANatvakhaJjatvAdInAM jIve tattacchabdavAcyatAprayojakasaMbandhavizeSAbhAvAt jIve tattacchabdaprayoganirvAhArthaM matvarthIyapratyayavidhAnamapi yujyate | na cAyamasti niyamaH, hInasvAGgavAcinAM matvarthIyapratyayenaiva jIvaparyantatvanirvAha iti, kuNiraham-ityAdAvadarzanAt| evaM ca kuNitvAdivat khaJjatvAdInAM zabdavAcyatAprayojakasaMbandhasaMbhave'pi svaravizeSAdisiddhyarthaM kANakhaJjAdizabdebhyo matvarthIyapratyayavidhAnam ityupapAdanIyam | tathaiva ca upapAditaM padamaJjaryAm | tataH pramANavattvAt maddheturnAsiddhaH, [p.301] tvaddheturevAsiddha iti madanumAnAt siddhamantaryAmiNaH zarIrazabdavAcyatvam | zarIrazabdenaiva ahaMpadasAmAnAdhikaraNyaviSayAH sarve'pi zabdA vyAkhyAtAH || iti antaryAmiviSayasakalazabdavAcyatvasamarthanavAdaH ||16||

[p.301] vyAkaraNazrutigatatRtIyaikavacanasamarthanavAdaH || udAhRtAyAM nAmarUpavyAkaraNazrutau 'jIvena' iti tRtIyA kartRtvArthA iti viSNubhAgavatAH; sahArthA iti siddhAntaH; prasaGgAt tadvyavasthApanArthaM kathAntaraM prastUyate nAmarUpavyAkaraNazrutigatA 'jIvena' iti tRtIyA kartRtvArthA, kartratiriktakArakArthatvarahitakArakavibhaktitvAt; mayA zivaH sevyate ityatra tRtIyAvat | 'jIvena' iti tRtIyAyAH kArakAntarArthatvarAhityasya vAdidvayasaMpratipannatvAt na hetorvizeSaNAsiddhiH, nApi vizeSyAsiddhiH, upapadavibhaktitaH kArakavibhakterbalIyastvena saMbhavataH kArakavibhaktitvasya tyAgAyogAt iti pUrvapakSiNaH prathamA kakSyA || atha dvitIyA-- bAdhitamidamanumAnam, kartuH 'vyAkaravANi' ityAkhyAtAbhihitatvena tRtIyAyAstAdarthyAyogAt | ataH kArakArthatvAsaMbhavAt upapadavibhaktitvamevAzrayaNIyam iti heturapyasiddhaH | tasmAdudAhRtA 'jIvena' iti tRtIyA kartrarthA na bhavati, tadabhidhAyipadAntarasamabhivyAhRtatvAt; putreNa saha ahaM bhokSye - ityatra tRtIyAvat iti || atha tRtIyA-- vyAkaraNakriyAkartRzakteH AkhyAtenAbhihitatve'pi pravezakriyAkartRzakteH kenApyanabhihitatvena tadarthatvaM tRtIyAyAH saMbhavatIti madanumAnasya na bAdhAsiddhidoSaH ; tvadanumAnasyaiva tu bAdhAsiddhidoSaH iti || [p.302] atha caturthI-- tathA sati ahaM bhuktvA vrajAmi ityAdiprayogaviSaye'pi mayA bhuktvA ityAdiprayogaH syAt; bhojanAdikriyAkartuH pratyayAntareNAnabhidhAnAt ; tadihApi kartRzaktyadhiSThAne prathamayaiva bhAvyamiti tRtIyAyAH kartrarthatvakArakavibhaktitvayorasaMbhavAt tvaddhetoreva prAguktadoSaH na maddhetoriti || atha paJcamI-- vyAkaraNamaryAdayA prasajyamAno'yaM doSaH tavApi tulyaH, ahaM bhuktvA vrajAmi iti prayoge hi yathA ahamartho'bhihitavrajanakriyAkartRzaktyAzrayaH evamanabhihitabhojanakriyAkartRzaktyAzrayo'pi, ktvApratyayasya zabdAntarAbhihitakartRzaktidvayAbhedamAtrArthakatvena kartRzaktyanabhidhAyakatvAt | anabhihitasUtreNa ca anabhihitakArakazaktyAzraye yathAyathaM vibhaktayo vidhIyante; na tvabhihitAzraye pratiSidhyante ; paryudAsapakSAzrayaNAt | ata eva anabhihitasUtre dvayoH kriyayoH kArake'nyatareNAbhihite vibhaktyabhAvaprasaGga iti vArtikena prAsAda Aste - ityatra prasIdantyasminniti adhikaraNArthe niSpannena saderghaJpratyayena prAsAdagatAdhikaraNakArakazakterabhidhAnAt saptamI na syAt iti pratiSedhapakSAzrayaNena zaGkAmudbhAvya, 'na vAnyatareNAnabhidhAnAt' iti vArtikena AsikriyAdhikaraNazakteH AseruparitanAkhyAtapratyayenAnabhidhAnAt nAyaM doSaH prasajyate iti paryudAsapakSAzrayaNena saptamyupapAditA | tasmAdvyAkaraNamaryAdayA prasajyamAno'yaM doSaH tanmaryAdayaiva yatnAntaramAsthAya ahaM bhuktvA ityAdau kathaM cit prathamAsamarthanena samAdheya iti, iha tRtIyAyAH kartrarthatvakArakavibhaktitvayoranupapattyabhAvAt na maddhetoH prAguktadoSaH, tvaddhetoreva sa doSa iti || atha SaSThI-- vaiyAkaraNamaryAdayA naiSa doSaH prasajyate | evaM hi tanmaryAdA - ahaM bhuktvA vrajAmi - ityatra ahamarthasya bhojanavrajanakriyAbhyAmubhAbhyAmapi na zAbdo'nvayaH ; [p.303] kiMtu vrajanakriyayaiva, prAdhAnyAt; Arthastu bhojanakriyayA anvayaH | ataH zabdavyApArApekSAyAmabhidhAnameva, na tvanabhidhAnamapi iti na ahamarthe tRtIyA prasajyate | ata eva paktvA bhujyate odanaH ityatra pAkakarmatvAnabhidhAnamAzritya odanamiti dvitIyA na pravartate; pAke karmAnvayasya ArthatvAt | prAsAda Aste ityatra tu prAsAdasya prasadanAsanakriyAbhyAM zAbda evAnvayaH padArthadazAyAM ghaJantaprAtipadikena prasadanenAdhikaraNatayA anvitasyaiva hi prAsAdadravyasya vAkyArthadazAyAm AsanakriyAdhikaraNatvenApi anvayAt | atastatra zAbdavyApArApekSayaiva anabhihitAsanakriyAdhikaraNakArakazaktyAzrayatvamapi prAsAdasyAstIti tatra saptamyupapadyate| evamasaGkIrNA prAksiddhA vaiyAkaraNamaryAdeti tadAzrayaNenaiva sarvopapAdane saMbhavati laukikaprayoge kartari prathamAmupapAdayituM na yatnAntaramAstheyamiti, iha tRtIyAyAH kartrarthatvakaraNArthatvayorasaMbhavAt tvaddhetoreva pUrvoktadoSaH, na maddhetoriti || atha saptamI-- 'jIvena' iti tRtIyAyAH sahArthatvena upapadavibhaktitve yena jIvena paramezvarasya sAhityaM tasya AtmatvAvyabhicArAt 'AtmanA' ityetat vyarthaM bhavet, kArakavibhaktitve tu jIvazarIrakeNa mayA ityetadarthalAbhArthatvena tat sArthakaM bhavati | tasmAdabhyarhitasya kArakavibhaktitvasya 'AtmanA' iti padasArthakatvasya ca lAbhArthaM yatnAntaramAsthAyaiva prathamopapAdanaM yuktam | yadi tu prAksiddhamaryAdayaiva tadupapAdanaM kAryam, tadA jIvazarIrakeNa mayA ityasya pravezakriyAmAtrAnvayo'stu; tadA hi tasyopasarjanakriyAnvayaH zAbdavyApAraviSayo bhavati | tasya pradhAnakriyAnvayAbhAvena arthAt siddhyasaMbhavAt | tatazca tatra zAbdavyApAraviSayAnAzrayam anabhihitasUtraM pravartata iti bhAvyaM tatra kArakavibhaktyA | dRzyate ca tathaiva prayogaH 'iSTakAH paktvA bhuGkte' iti| atra khalu iSTakAnAM pAkakriyayaivAnvaya iti tadanvayasya zAbdavyApAraviSayatvAt anabhihitAdhikAravihitA karmaNi dvitIyA dRzyate | evamihApi kartari tRtIyA upapadyate | tasmAnna maddhetoH pUrvoktadoSaH, tvaddhetoreva sa doSa iti || athASTamI-- 'jIvenAtmanA' ityasya jIvazarIrakeNa mayA ityarthavarNanAyAm 'anena' iti padaM vyarthaM syAt | na hi mayA ityasya arthaH svAtirikto'pi kazcidasti | yena tadvyAvartanAya tatsArthakaM bhavet | jIvarUpeNa kenacidAtmanA saha ityarthavarNanAyAM tu trivRtkaraNIyabhUtAbhimAnitvena saMnihitaM parAmRzataH 'anena' ityasyAsaMnihitajIvAntaravyAvartanayA sArthakatvaM labhyate | ataH prathamazrutapadasArthakatvalambhanArthaM caramazrutapadasya anatiprayojanatvAzrayaNaM na doSAya | tasmAt 'AtmanA' ityasya sArthakatvalAbhamanurudhya na yatnAntaramAstheyam | yattu pravezakriyAmAtrAnvayamaGgIkRtya prAksiddhavaiyAkaraNamaryAdayaiva kartari tRtIyAsamarthanam tattuccham, ktvAprayoge kArakAntarasya ekakriyAnvaye'pi samAnakartRkatvanirvAhArthaM kartuH kriyAdvayAnvayAvazyaMbhAvAt, karturvyAkaraNAsaMbandhe ahamiti prathamAntAnanvayaprasaGgAcca | vyAkaraNakartuH AkhyAtenAbhidhAnamapekSyaiva tatra prathamAyAH samarthanIyatvAt | saMjJAmUrtiklRptyadhikaraNe paramezvarasyAnupraveza iva vyAkaraNe'pi kartRtvavyavasthApanena tadvirodhApattezca | tasmAttvaddhetoreva prAguktadoSaH, na maddhetoriti || atha navamI-- 'anena jIvena' ityasya saMnihitajIvaparatve trivRtkaraNIyatejo'bannAbhimAnidevatAtrayaparatvaM vaktavyam, saMnidhAne vizeSAbhAvAt; tathA ca 'jIvena' iti ekavacanamanupapannam; jIvapadaM ca jalAbhimAnini nArAyaNe'nupapannam; tasya brahmakoTitvAGgIkArAt; tatazca 'jIvena' ityatra prakRtipratyayoH asAmaJjasyam, 'AtmanA' ityasya vaiyarthyam iti bahudoSAbhyupagamAt varamekasyaiva 'anena' iti idaMzabdasya - ayamahamAgacchAmi - ityAdAviva na atiprayojanatvAbhyupagamaH ataH tannirvAhAya yatnAntarAzrayaNameva yuktamiti na maddhetoruktadoSaH, kiM tu tvaddhetoreva iti || [p.304] atha dazamI-- 'jIvena' ityatra ekavacanaM jIvAjIvasamudAye jIvapadaM ca kAkAddadhi rakSyatAm ityatra ekavacanavat, kAkatadanyasamudAye kAkapadavacca upapadyate ; trimUrtyantargato nArAyaNaH paranArAyaNAdanyo jIva iti pakSe tu tatra prakRtyaMzasya na kApyanupapattiH; tasmAt 'AtmanA' ityetAvanmAtrasya matpakSe nAtiprayojanatvam 'anena' iti prathamazrutapadasArthakatvAnusAreNa soDhuM yuktamiti tadvyAkaraNamaryAdAmatilaGghya - ahaM bhuktvA ityAdi prayogasamarthanasyAkartavyatvAt vyAkaraNamaryAdayA ca atra kartari tRtIyAnupapatteH tvaddhetoreva bAdhAsiddhidoSaH, na maddhetoriti sahArthaivAtra tRtIyA iti yuktam || iti vyAkaraNazrutigatatRtIyaikavacanasamarthanavAdaH ||17||

[p.304] sarvopAdAnatvahetukasarvazabdavAcyatvasamarthanavAdaH || vyAkaraNazrutibalAdiva sarvopAdAnatvazrutibalAdapi brahmaNaH sarvazabdavAcyatvaM sidhyatIti nirNetuM kathAntaraM prastUyate-- brahma vRkSatvajAtipravRttinimittakazaktyA vRkSazabdavAcyam, vRkSopAdAnatvAt, yat vRkSopAdAnam tat vRkSatvajAtipravRttinimittakazaktyA vRkSazabdavAcyam - yathA vRkSopAdAnapArthivadravyam - iti prathamA kakSyA || atha dvitIyA-- vRkSAkArapariNatasya vRkSAbhinnasya pArthivadravyasyeva nirvikAratayA vRkSAkArapariNatirahitasya tato bhinnasya vRkSo'yaM jIvati ityAdivRkSazabdasAmAnAdhikaraNyavyavahAraviSayAt vRkSAbhimAnijIvato'pi bhinnasya brahmaNaH tvaduktazaktyA vRkSazabdavAcyatvaM na saMbhavatIti bAdhitamidamanumAnam; hetuzca asiddhaH, [p.305] kAryAkArapariNatatadabhinnadravyasya upAdAnatvena tathAbhUtasya brahmaNaH kvacidapyupAdAnatvAsaMbhavAt; tasmAt brahma vRkSatvajAtipravRttinimittakazaktyA vRkSazabdavAcyaM na bhavati, ubhayasiddhatvaduktasAdhyavadbhinnatvAt, yat ubhayasiddhatvaduktasAdhyavadbhinnam, na tat vRkSatvajAtipravRttinimittakazaktyA vRkSazabdavAcyam - yathA kUTam iti || atha tRtIyA-- vRkSatvapravRttinimittakazaktyA vRkSazabdavAcyatve prayojakaM vRkSazabdapravRttinimittavRkSatvAzrayatvam, tat pArthivadravyasya brahmaNazca aviziSTam; ataH pArthivadravyavat brahmaNo'pi maduktasAdhyavattvaM yujyate iti madanumAnasya na bAdhaH; nApi hetvasiddhiH, kAryAvasthAzrayakAraNatvasya upAdAnalakSaNatayA tasyApi brahmapArthivadravyayoraviziSTatvAt; tvadanumAnaM tu aprayojakam, ubhayasiddhamaduktasAdhyavadbhinnasyApi brahmaNo vRkSatvAzrayatayA tatpravRttinimittakazabdavAcyatvopapatteH iti || atha caturthI-- vRkSatvajAtyAzrayatvamAtrasya tatpravRttinimittakazabdavAcyatAprayojakatve kAryAvasthAzrayakAraNatvamAtrasya upAdAnalakSaNatve ca kAlasyApi vRkSatvapravRttinimittakazabdavAcyatvaM vRkSopAdAnatvaM ca prasajyeta; ataH kAlavat brahmaNaH sarvAdhAratayA vRkSatvAzrayatve'pi tatpravRttinimittakazabdavAcyatvaM vRkSopAdAnatvaM ca na saMbhavatIti tvadanumAnasya bAdhAsiddhI tadavasthe; madanumAnaM ca nAprayojakam, brahmaNaH vRkSatvAzrayatvamAtreNa tatpravRttinimittakazabdavAcyatve kAlasyApi tadvAcyatvaM syAditi vipakSabAdhakAnukUlatarkasadbhAvAt iti || atha paJcamI-- tvayApi vRkSatvAzrayatvaM tatpravRttinimittakazabdavAcyatAyAM prayojakameSTavyam, na tu vRkSAbhinnatvam, vRkSAbhimAnijIvAvyApakatvAt; tatra AzrayatvaM tvayApi [p.306] kAlAtiprasaGgavAraNAya sarvAdhAratAprayojakasaMbandhAtiriktasaMbandhavizeSagarbhameSTavyam; mayApi tathaiveSyata iti nAtiprasaGgaH ato madanumAnasya na bAdhAsiddhI, tvadanumAnaM ca aprayojakameveti || atha SaSThI-- vRkSAbhimAnijIvasya vRkSatvAzrayatvaM saMbandhavizeSagarbhamityatra asti pramANam-vRkSo jIvati - ityAdisAmAnAdhikaraNyavyavahAraH; brahmaNastu tat saMbandhavizeSagarbhamityatra nAsti pramANam 'akSaramambarAntadhRteH' ityadhikaraNasiddhakAlasAdhAraNasarvAdhAratAprayojakasaMbandhamAtreNa tasya vRkSatvAzrayatvopapatteH ; atastvayA kAlAtiprasaGgo nirasituM na zakyate iti tvadanumAnasya bAdhAsiddhI tadavasthe, madanumAnaM ca nAprayojakamiti || atha saptamI-- brahmaNaH saMbandhavizeSagarbhaM vRkSatvAzrayatvamastItyatra pramANAbhAvo'siddhaH ; tasya zrutisiddhena sarvopAdAnabhAvena prAptasya vRkSopAdAnatvasya nirvAhArthaM kAlaguNajAtasya kAla iva vRkSatvasya brahmaNi sarvAdhAratAprayojakasaMbandhabhinnenApi saMbandhavizeSeNa vRtterabhyupagantavyatve sati nIlatvAderghaTAdAviva vRkSatvasyApi brahmaNi tadvArakavaizeSikasaMbandhasyApratyAkhyeyatvAt; tasmAt madanumAnasya na bAdhAsiddhI, tvadanumAnaM tu aprayojakameveti || athASTamI-- brahmaNaH sarvopAdAnabhAve tava nAsti pramANam; 'Atmana AkAzaH saMbhUtaH' 'yato vA imAni bhUtAni jAyante' 'sarvANi ha vA imAni bhUtAnyAkAzAdeva samutpadyante' ityAdyupAdAnatvasamarpakazrutInAM tvayA cicchaktiviSayatvasyAbhyupagatatvAt; nIlatvAdidRSTAntavarNanamapi ayuktam, rUpatvAdipratidRSTAntasadbhAvAt; [p.307] tasmAt tava mate brahmaNaH saMbandhavizeSagarbhavRkSatvAzrayatve pramANAbhAvena kAlAdAvatiprasaGgatAdavasthyAt tvadanumAnasya bAdhAsiddhI tadavasthe, madanumAnaM ca nAprayojakamiti || atha navamI-- brahmaNaH sarvopAdAnabhAve pramANAbhAvo'siddhaH, chAndogye brahmavijJAnena sarvavijJAnaM pratijJAya 'yathA somyaikena mRtpiNDena sarvaM mRNmayaM vijJAtaM syAt' ityAdinA tatropAdAnopAdeyabhAvApannamRtpiNDaghaTAdidRSTAntopAdAnasAmarthyAt brahmaprapaJcayorapi upAdAnopAdeyabhAvo vivakSita ityavagamAt; na ca tasya aupacArikatve mukhyasarvavijJAnadRSTAntIkaraNAyogaH, candramaNDalamiva jagadvisRmarakAntimanoharamasya mukham ityAdivyavahAreSu mukhyasyApi stutyarthaupacArikadRSTAntIkaraNasya bahulamupalambhAt; mRtpiNDasya ghaTazarAvAdInAM ca bhinnabhinnamRtkAryatayA tasya tadupAdAnatvAbhAvena dRSTAntopanyAsasyApi mArtikatvarUpa[p.308]prakAraikyaviSayatvena aupacArikatvAcca; dRSTo hi devadatta evaM bhojayitavyaH iti devadattabhojanavyAkhyAnAnantaram, etena yajJadattabhojanaM vyAkhyAtamiti avyAkhyAte'pi vyAkhyAtasamAnaprakArakatvena vyAkhyAtatvavyavahAraH ; evaMrUpamaupacArikatvam 'ekena mRtpiNDena' iti ekagrahaNamapi prasAdhayati; na hi ekamRtpiNDajJAnena dezAntarakAlAntaravartisakalamRtpiNDajJAnaM kathamapi samarthayituM zakyam 'sarvaM khalvidaM brahma' ityAdizrutistu jIvaviSayA, tasyaiva paryAyeNa devamanuSyapazupakSisarIsRpAdisarvAtmabhAvasya lokavedayoH prasiddhataratvAt; tatra hi 'sadeva somyedamagra AsIdekamevAdvitIyaM brahma' ityatra satpadaM sUkSmacidacidrUpApannacicchaktiviziSTazivaparam; anyathA tadatiriktajIvavargAdisattvena avadhAraNavirodhAt; tajjJAne ca tatkAryaM sarvamapi viSayo bhavati, kAryadravyasya kAraNAnanyatvAt; tadgatasthaulyAvasthAparaM na viSayaH; ataH tenAkAreNa azrutatvAdizravaNamupapadyate; dRSTAnto'pi upAdAnajJAnena upAdeyAnAM jJAtatvaviSaya eva; na ca mRdeva ghaTAdInAmupAdAnam, na tu mRtpiNDaH, ghaTAdiSu mRdAkArasyeva piNDAkArasyAnuvRttyabhAvAditi vAcyam, mRtpiNDa eva ghaTaH kRtaH iti vyavahArAnusAreNa tasya ghaTAdyupAdAnatvAt; evaM dRSTAntasya mukhyatvavyavasthitau tadgauNatvamukhena Apatat dArSTAntikasya gauNatvaM pratyAkhyAtaM bhavati; dArSTAntike sarvavijJAnaM prAdhAnyaviSayatayA gauNam, dRSTAnte tu tatprakAraikyaviSayatayA iti tayorvairUpyaM ca parihRtaM bhavati; 'sarvaM khalvidaM [p.309] brahma' ityAdizrutirapi na jIvaviSayA, 'tajjalAn' iti sarvasya prapaJcasya brahmAdhInajanmasthitilayatvokterjIve'nanvayAt; ato madanumAnasya na bAdhAsiddhI, tvadanumAnaM ca aprayojakameveti || atha dvAdazI-- sadvijJAnena sarvavijJAnaM mukhyamupapAdayituM na zakyam, sacchabdasya sUkSmacidacidviziSTavastuparatvakalpane pramANAbhAvAt; 'sadeva' ityavadhAraNasya asatkAraNatvaniSedhadRDhIkaraNArthatvena 'ekamevAdvitIyam' iti padAnAmadhiSThAtrantaraniSedhadRDhIkaraNArthatvena ca anvayasaMbhavAt; ataH satpadaM brahmasvarUpamAtraparaM vAcyamiti tadvijJAnena mukhyaM sarvaprapaJcavijJAnaM na saMbhavati; ato dRSTAntasya mukhyasarvavijJAnaviSayatvasaMbhave'pi dArzantikaM mukhyaprAdhAnyApekSasarvavijJAnAvalambanameveti vAcyam; na ca dArSTAntike sarvavijJAnasya gauNatve tatra mukhyasarvavijJAnadRSTAntIkaraNAyogaH, dArSTAntikAnuguNyArthaM dRSTAnte'pi sarvavijJAnasya 'sarvaM mRNmayam' ityatra sarvazabdasyAsaMkocamAzritya gauNasyaiva grahaNopapatteH mukhyagauNatvavairUpyApekSayA satyeva gauNatve tat kvacit prAdhAnyApekSam anyatra prakAraikyApekSam ityavAntaravairUpyasya sahyatvAt, sarvazabdAsaMkocalAbhena svArasyAcca; 'sarvaM khalvidaM brahma' ityetadapi jIvasArvAtmyaviSayamityeva vaktuM yuktam, tatsArvAtmyasya prasiddhataratvAt; prapaJcajanmasthitilayeSu ca adRSTadvArA jIvAdhInatvasyApi sattvAcca; ato madanumAnasya na bAdhAsiddhI, tvadanumAnaM ca aprayojakamiti || atha trayodazI-- sacchabdasya sUkSmacidacidAkArApannazaktiviziSTazivaparatve pramANAbhAvo'siddhaH, 'sadeva' ityavadhAraNasyAsatkAraNatvanirAkaraNadRDhIkaraNArthatve'pi adhiSThAtrantaraniSedhArthasya 'ekam' ityasya taddRDhIkaraNArthasya evakArasya upAdAnAntaraniSedhArthasya 'advitIyam' ityasya ca tatra pramANatvAt cidacidvargayoH sacchabdArthabahirbhAve hi dvikartRkeSu nAmarUpavyAkaraNaprabhRtisRjyavastuSu kartrantarANAM [p.310] hiraNyagarbhAdInAm acidvargopAdAnabhUtazuddhamAyAyAzca sattvena 'ekamevAdvitIyam' ityavadhAraNAni 'sadeva somyedamagra AsIt' ityetasyAnantaramasadvAdanirAkaraNottarasya 'sattveva somyedamagra AsIt' ityetasya cAnantaramabhyastAni 'ekamevAdvitIyam' ityavadhAraNAni na saMgaccheran; atastadanusAreNa sacchabdasya sUkSmacidacidAkArApannazaktiviziSTazivaparatvamabhyupagantavyam; tathA sati uktaprakAreNa sUkSmadazApannasya sthUlacidacidAkArApannazaktiviziSTatayA sthUladazApattyarthaM 'bahu syAm' iti saMkalpo'pi upapadyate; tatra hi bahuzabdo vaipulyavAcI, na tu saMkhyAvizeSavAcI; cidacidvargabhedavivakSAyAM bahutvasaMkhyAyAH pUrvamapi sattvena, tayorbrahmavizeSaNatayA viziSTarUpaikyAt tadbhedAvivakSAyAM pazcAdapi bahutvasaMkhyAbhAvena ca ubhayathApi 'bahu syAm' iti saMkalpAyogAt, tasmAt uktarItyA sUkSmadazApannaM sat sthUladazApannaM syAm - iti saMkalpamakarot ityeva tadartho vaktavyaH iti tadanusAreNApi viziSTaparatvamaGgIkartavyam; evaM ca dRSTAntadArSTAntikayorubhayorapi ekavijJAnena sarvavijJAnaM mukhyameva labhyate, sarvazabdasya mRNmayazabdAnusAreNeva ekazabdAnusAreNApi saMkocaucityAt; ato bAdhakAbhAvena dRSTAntasAmyAnurodhAt, prapaJcasya sthUlasUkSmAvasthAdvaye'pi brahmavizeSaNatoktyA tadapRthaksiddhatvalAbhAcca, brahmaprapaJcayorupAdAnopAdeyabhAve sadvidyAgataM sarvavijJAnapratijJAnaM pramANaM bhavatyeva; tathA cicchakteH prapaJcopAdAnabhAve pramANatvenAbhyupagatAni 'sarvANi ha vA imAni bhUtAnyAkAzAdeva samutpadyante' ityAdInyapi brahmaNaH sarvopAdAnabhAvamAkSipanti iti tatra pramANabhAvaM bhajante; cicchakteH brahmApRthaksiddhatayA cicchaktyapRthaksiddhasya prapaJcasyApi brahmApRthaksiddhilAbhAt; tasmAt sarvopAdAnatvahetoH nAsiddhiH; nApi sAdhyasya bAdhaH, brahmaprapaJcayorbhede'pi sarve brahma iti sAmAnAdhikaraNyena tasya vRkSatvAdyAzrayatvopapatteH; taddhi 'yato vA imAni bhUtAni jAyante' ityAdizrutiprasiddhaM tajjatvAdikamanuvadatIti yuktam, sArUpyeNa tasya zIghraM buddhisthirIkaraNAt, na tu adRSTadvArakaM jIvajanyatvAdikam, tasya puruSayatnena vilambitaM buddhisthirIkartavyatvAt; ata eva hi 'yato vA' ityAdikaM cicchaktiparam, tadarthasya brahmaNi kathamanvayaH ityAzaGkAvAraNArtham 'sarvaM khalvidaM brahma tajjalAniti zAnta upAsIta' ityupakSiptabrahmopAsanApekSitaguNavidhAnArthe 'manomayaH prANazarIraH' ityAdisaMdarbhe 'AkAzAtmA' ityupAsyasya brahmaNo vizeSaNamuktam; 'yadeSa AkAza Anando na [p.311] syAt' ityAdizrutiSu AkAzazabdoktAyAH cicchakterapi ayamAtmA caitrazarIrasya caitra iva; ato yathA zarIrakRtaM janmasthitilayaM zarIriNi bhavati evaM cicchaktikRtaM prapaJcajanmasthitilayaM brahmaNo bhavatIti 'tajjalAn' ityetat yuktamiti tadabhiprAyaH ; yattu jIvasya sarvAtmabhAvaH prasiddhataraH ityuktam, tat viparItam, 'idaM sarvaM yadayamAtmA' 'AtmaivedaM sarvam' 'sarvo vai rudraH' 'puruSa evedaM sarvam' ityAdizrutiSu 'rudro hyevaitatsarvam' ityAdismRtiSu ca brahmaNa eva hi tat prasiddhataram jIvasya tu 'yonimanye prapadyante zarIratvAya dehinaH | sthANumanye'nusaMyanti yathAkarma yathAzrutam' ityAdizrutimavalambya paryAyataH sarvazarIragrahaNarUpasarvAtmabhAvaH kalpanIya iti na tasya vedataH suprasiddhirasti; nApi lokataH; yadyapi 'yadbrahmavidyayA sarvaM bhaviSyanto manuSyA manyante kiM tadbrahmAvedyasmAttatsarvamabhavat' ityAdizrutiSu jIvasyApi muktau sArvAtmyaM prasiddhataram, tathApi siddhasAdhyayoH sArvAtmyayoH siddhasyaivAtra grahaNaM yuktam, sApekSanirapekSanyAyAt; tasmAt madanumAnasya na bAdhAsiddhI, tvadanumAnaM tu aprayojakamiti anayaiva bhaGgayA ghaTopAdAnatvAdinA ghaTAdizabdavAcyatvasyApi siddhau brahmaNaH svopAdeyasakalavastuvAcakazabdavAcyatvasiddhiH || iti sarvopAdAnatvahetukasarvazabdavAcyatvasamarthanavAdaH ||18||

[p.311] aikazAstryanirAkaraNavAdaH || evaM sakalazabdavAcyatvena siddhaM brahma alaukikazreyaH sAdhanatvapravRttinimittakakarmavAcidharmazabdavAcyamapi bhavati; sAkSAdapi uktapravRttinimittasaMbandhena tadvAcyaM bhavitumarhati, 'zreyaH sAdhanatA hyeSAM nityaM vedAt pratIyate | tAdrUpyeNa ca dharmatvaM tasmAnnendriyagocaraH' iti vedAvagamyazreyaH sAdhanatAkatvapravRttinimittakatayA abhyupagatasya dharmazabdasya karmavat jAtiguNadravyANyapi artha iti mImAMsakairuktatvAt; tena 'athAto dharmajijJAsA' iti sUtragatadharmazabdagRhItatayA brahmaNo'pi jaiminIyadharmavicArapratijJAviSayatvamasti na vA iti vicArArthaM kathAntaraM prastUyate-- [p.312] tadastitatvavAdinAM pareSAmidamanumAnam brahma svarUpapramANasAdhanaphalaiH saha jaiminIyavicArapratijJAviSayaH, alaukikazreyaH sAdhanatvAt, karmavat; brahmaNo'pi nakSatreSTyAdiSu devatAtvenAnvayAt jaiminIye yAgeSu devatAprAdhAnyatadabhAvavicAre devatAsAmAnyAtmanA viSayatvamasti, devatAnAM karmAdhikArAnadhikAravicAre ca tadAtmanA viSayatvamastIti siddhasAdhanavAraNAya svarUpapramANasAdhanaphalaiH saha iti vizeSaNam - iti prathamA kakSyA || atha dvitIyA-- brahmaNo jaiminIyadharmavicArapratijJAviSayatAyAm agre tadvicAreNa bhAvyam, na tvasau tatra dRzyate; na ca tamapi cikIrSato maharSeH vighnaH kazcit utprekSituM zakyate; nApi vismaraNam, yo hi dvAdazalakSaNyAM svarUpapramANasAdhanaphalaiH saha karmajAtaM nirNIya tatrAsUtritAn kAMzcittadupayuktAn nyAyAnAlakSya tatsaMgrahArthaM dvAdazalakSaNIzeSaM saMkarSaNakANDamakArSIt, tasya maharSeH brahmavicAre vighnaM vismaraNaM vA kathamutprekSAmahe; na ca karmavicAropayuktanyAyaireva brahmaNo'pi svarUpapramANasAdhanaphalanirNayaH sidhyatItyabhipretya pRthaktadvicAro na kRta iti zakyaM vaktum, karmaparavAkyArthanirNAyakanyAyebhyo vilakSaNAnAmeva brahmaparavAkyArthanirNAyakanyAyAnAM caturlakSaNyAM grathanadarzanAt; na ca dvAdazalakSaNIcaturlakSaNyoH aikazAstryAbhyupagamena brahmavicArapratijJAnirvAhaH kalpayituM zakyaH, bhinnavaktRkayoH dharmabrahmavicArapratijJopakramayoH parasparavilakSaNasAdhyAsAdhyArthanirNAyakanyAyagrathanapravRttayoH tayoraikazAstryakalpane pramANAbhAvAt; ato bAdhitamidamanumAnam iti brahma noktasAdhyavat, karmAnyatvAt ghaTavat - iti ||
atha tRtIyA kakSyA-- na bAdhitaM madanumAnam, aikazAstryakalpanena pratijJAnirvAhopapatteH; na ca tatkalpakAbhAvaH, vedarUpaikagranthavyAkhyAtvasya tatkalpakatvAt; na ca vaktRbhedaH tatkalpanAbAdhakaH, vaktRbhede'pi vyAkaraNarUpaikaprabandhavyAkhyAnatvena vAmana[p.313]jayAdityavRttyoH, aikazAstryadarzanAt; nApi dharmabrahmavicArapratijJopakramau tadbAdhakau, dharmazabdena karmabrahmasAdhAraNena dvAdazalakSaNIcaturlakSaNIsAdhAraNapratijJAyAM vRttAyAM karmavicArAnantaram 'athAto brahmajijJAsA' ityasya 'athAtaH zeSalakSaNam' itivat avAntarapratijJAparatvopapatteH na ca parasparavilakSaNanyAyagrathanaM tadbAdhakam, nirNeyArthAnusAreNa nyAyavailakSaNyopapatteH ; dvAdazalakSaNyAmapi karmabhedazeSazeSibhAvAdinirNeyArthAnusAreNa vilakSaNanyAyagrathanadarzanAt; ato na bAdhitaM madanumAnam, tvadanumAnaM tu aprayojakam - iti ||
atha caturthI kakSyA-- nyAyAnAmiha vailakSaNyaM bhedamAtraM na vivakSitam, kiMtu virodhaH; prasiddho hi virodhaH, caturlakSaNyAM jaiminimatasya prAyaH pUrvapakSIkaraNadarzanAt; na hi parasparaviruddhamatadvayAvalambanena dvAbhyAM pravartitaM zAstradvayaM tAbhyAmaikamatyena ekazAstratayA kRtamiti kalpayituM zakyam; dharmazabdazca brahmapratikoTitayA yAgAdikarmaparatvena 'phalamata upapatteH' ityadhikaraNe 'dharmaM jaiminirata eva' iti sUtre prayuktaH; Apastambena ca maharSiNA 'dharmAdharmau carata AvAM svaH iti na devagandharvA na pitara ityAcakSate ayaM dharmo'yaM dharma iti yattvAryAH kriyamANaM prazaMsanti sa dharmaH yadgarhante so'dharmaH' iti anuSTheyayAgAdAveva dharmazabdaH prayuktaH atastadanusAreNa dharmajijJAsAsUtragatasyApi tasya yAgAdyanuSTheyaparatvaM vAcyamiti dharmabrahmajijJAsAsUtrayoH sAdhAraNAvAntarapratijJAparatvavibhAgakalpanamapi na yuktam tasmAt bAdhitaM tvadanumAnam, madanumAnam ca nAprayojakam, yat yena rUpeNa nAnuSTheyam tasya tena rUpeNa anuSTheyaparadharmazabdagrAhyatvAyogAt iti || atha paJcamI-- asiddho'yaM jaiminIyavaiyAsikamatayoH virodhaH; caturlakSaNyAM kvacidapi jaiminIyamatasya pUrvapakSIkaraNAbhAvAt; yattu puruSArthAdhikaraNe 'zeSatvAt puruSArthavAdo yathAnyeSviti jaiminiH' iti brahmApalApaparaM pUrvapakSasUtram, na tat jaiminyabhi[p.314]matasya pakSasya anuvAdakam, kiM tu tadIyasyAbhyuccayavAdasya; ata eva 'paraM jaiminirmukhyatvAt' ityAdisUtreSu brahmAbhyupagamenaiva pravRttasya pakSasya jaiminIyatvenAnuvAdaH; evaM 'parAmarza jaiminiracodanA cApavadati hi' iti sUtraM saMnyAsApalApasya jaimininA abhyuccayavAdatvena upanyastasyaivAnuvAdakam, na tu tadIyasiddhAntasya; ata eva 'tadbhUtasya tu nAtadbhAvo jaiminerapi niyamAtadrUpAbhAvebhyaH' iti sUtre saMnyastavataH saMnyAsAt pracyutiH jaiminerapi na saMmatA ityuktam; evamanyatrApi tatra tatra virodhaparihAra unneyaH; dharmazabdasya anuSTheyAsAdhAraNyamapi asiddham, 'ye ca vedavido viprA ye cAdhyAtmavido janAH | te vadanti mahAtmAnaM kRSNaM dharmaM sanAtanam' iti mahAbhArate bhagavatsAdhAraNyokteH; ato virodhAbhAvAt kathaMcit virodhasadbhAve'pi brahmAnabhyupagamAdeH dvAdazalakSaNyAmasUtritatvAt dharmazabdasAdhAraNyAcca kartRbhede'pi aikazAstryAGgIkAre na kAcidanupapattiH; tvadanumAnaM tu aprayojakameva, ananuSTheyasyApi dharmazabdagRhItatvena jaiminIyavicArapratijJAviSayatvopapatteH iti ||
atha SaSThI kakSyA-- yaduktaM virodho nAstIti, tadasiddham, brahmasaMnyAsAbhyupagamAnabhyupagamAbhyAM virodhasyApratyAkhyeyatvAt, yattu jaimineH tadubhayAbhyupagamapradarzakaM sUtram, tat tadabhyupagama eva tasya siddhAnta ityasya jJApakamiti na avadhArayituM zakyam, 'vipratiSedhAccAsamaJjasam' iti sAMkhyAdhikaraNoktanyAyena jaiminimatasya parasparaviruddhatayA anupAdeyatvodghATanaparatvena anyathAsiddheH; yaccoktaM brahmAnabhyupagamatadabhyupagamaikAntyAdiSu jaiminibAdarAyaNayoH virodhasadbhAve'pi brahmAnabhyupagamAdeH dvAdazalakSaNyAm asUtritatvAt aviruddhAyAstasyAH caturlakSaNyA saha aikazAstryamupapadyata iti, tadapyayuktam, aviruddhArthatvAsiddheH; tathA hi 'tasya nimittaparISTiH' iti dharmapramANanirUpaNaM pratijJAya prathamAdhyAyena yat tatra pramANanirUpaNaM kRtam, tattAvadviruddham; tatra hi 'AmnAyasya kriyArthatvAdAnarthakyamatadarthAnAM tasmAdanityamucyate' iti sUtreNa AmnAyasya kRtsnasyApi prayojanaparyavasAnAya kriyArthatvAvazyaMbhAvAt atadarthAnAm 'vAyavyaM zvetamAlabheta bhUtikAmaH' ityAdikriyAvAkyAnapekSitatayA kriyArthatvarahitAnAm AnarthakyaM niSprayojanatvam, tasmAt [p.315] akriyArthavAkyajAtamapramANamucyate ityetadarthakena vedeSvarthavAdAnAmaprAmANyamAzaGkya, 'vidhinA tvekavAkyatvAt stutyarthena vidhInAM syuH' iti sUtreNa siddhArthavAdAnAM vidhivAkyena ekavAkyatvAt vidhInAM stutirUpeNArthena saprayojanAH syuH ityetadarthakena teSAM vidhyekavAkyatayA prAmANyamupapAditamarthavAdAdhikaraNe; audumbarAdhikaraNe'pi 'uktaM tu vAkyazeSatvam' iti arthavAdAdhikaraNoktArthasmAraNena taddRDhIkRtam; dharmavicArapratijJAyA brahmavicArasAdhAraNye hi 'tasya nimittaparISTiH' iti dharmapramANanirUpaNapratijJApi brahmapramANanirUpaNasAdhAraNI syAt; samarthitam, tadapi 'phalamata upapatteH' iti vaiyAsikAdhikaraNena viruddham; tatra karmopAsanaprasAditasya paramezvarasya phalapradatvasamarthanAt; tasmAt zAstrayoreva virodho'sti; teSAmupAsanAvidhizeSatvamuktaM bhavati; tadupAsanAzca kratUpayuktakartRrUpadravyasaMskAratvAt prokSaNAdinyAyena kratuzeSatAM bhajante; kartRmAtrasya laukikakriyAsAdhAraNye'pi dehAtmabhAvenApi jIvAdadhikatvasiddheH tadupAsanAnAM na kratuzeSatvaprasaktiriti [p.316] vAcyam, akriyArthAnAM vidhizeSatvaniyamasya jaiminyabhimatasya samanvayAdhikaraNena bhaGgaM kRtvA vedAntAnAM svAtantryeNa karmAnadhikAriNAmandhapaGgvAdInAM kathaMcit gatyupanyAsaH saMnyAsa iti mImAMsakapravAdaH; tasmAt zAstrayorvirodho nAstIti ayuktam; dharmazabdasya brahmasAdhAraNyavacanamapi ayuktam, 'te vadanti mahAtmAnaM kRSNaM dharmaM sanAtanam' iti bhAratavacanasya 'dharmo vigrahavAnrAmaH' itivat stutyarthatvenApi upapatteH; tasmAt madanumAnaM na bAdhitam, tvadanumAnaM tu aprayojakamiti || atha saptamI-- na tAvat bAdhitaM madanumAnam, pUrvatantre prAmANyapratijJAyA akriyArthAnAmAnarthakyenAprAmANyazaGkAyAzca brahmaparavAkyasAdhAraNye'pi, kriyAvizeSArthavAdeSu vidhizeSatvena sArthakatayA prathamAdhyAyodghATitavirodhaparihArasaMbhavAt; 'vipratipattau haviSA niyamyeta' ityASTamikAdhikaraNasiddhAnte devatAyA guNatvaM na yuktiH, dravyasyApi guNatvAvizeSAt; kiM tu 'karmaNastadupAkhyatvAt' lokeSu kAmacAro bhavati' iti kevalaM sarvalokAvAptyarthatvena zrutA daharopAsanA ca vyarthA syAt; yadi uktopAsanAbhiH sarvalokakAmAvAptisahitA muktiH, tarhi vasurudrAdityamarutsAdhyatattulyabhogamAtraprAptisahitamuktiphalA madhuvidyA gArhapatyAdilokatrayaprAptipUrvakamuktiphalopakosalavidyA lokavizeSaprAptitatratyabhogarahitakevalamuktiphalAkSarAdividyA tatparihAraH siddhAnta[p.319]bhAge na dRzyate; na hi tatra karmabhUyastvAt phalabhUyastvanyAyaH pravartate; 'tathA phalAbhAvAt' iti sUtroktam ucyamAnaphalAbhAvarUpamaprAmANyakAraNam 'zobhate'sya na tu haviSA prItA devatA; tasyAstu dravyoddezyatayA yAgopasarjanatvenaiva zrutiriti siddhAntamabhidhAya, 'atithau tatpradhAnatvamabhAvaH karmaNi syAt tasya prItipradhAnatvAt' iti sUtreNa AtithyatraiSamyaM yajane darzitam Atithyam atithiprItividhAnam yathA atithiH prIyate tathA kartavyatayA vihitamiti tadatithipradhAnaM yujyate, karmaNi tu nAsti prItividhAnam yathA devatA prIyate tathA alaukikazreyaH sAdhanamAtravAcitAyAM na nirvahati tadA hi anuSThAtRtvena alaukikazreyaH sAdhanAnAM jIvAtmanAM nimittatvena tathAbhUtAnAmanyeSAM ca bahUnAM dharmazabdaH sAdhAraNa iti tadvAcyatvoktyA yAgAdiphalasAdhakavilakSaNasAdhakAlAbhaH yAgAdiphalasAdhakasyApi nityatvena bhUtabhaviSyadvartamAnAnyatvarUpasaMgrAhakakroDIkRtatvAt, apahatapApmatvAdyAvirbhAvaviziSTatvena muktasyeva svargAdiviziSTatvena prApyatvAcca; tasmAt anupapannA parakIyA yojaneti, tAmupekSya asmAbhiH anyA RjvI yojanA darzitA, prathamapAdena yat balavadaniSTAnanubandhikSayiSNusAtizayaphalam, yacca tadanubandhi tathAbhUtaphalam, tadubhayavilakSaNaM sAdhanamupapanyastam, dvitIyapAdena 'plavA hyete'dRDhA yajJarUpA aSTAdazoktamavaraM yeSu karma | etacchreyo ye'bhinandanti mUDhA jarAmRtyU te punarevApiyanti' ityAdimantrAntaraninditayAgAdisAdhakavilakSaNaH sAdhaka upanyastaH; tatra kRtAkRtAt ityasya kRtamapyakRtam akRtaprAyaM yasya tasmAditi vyAkhyA, tRtIyapAdena svargAdisAdhyavilakSaNaM sAdhyamupanyastam, tatra cakArAbhyAM bhUtabhavyAnvitAbhyAM bhUtabhavyobhayAkArasya vartamAnasya saMgrahaH, evam-- anyatradharmamanunA prathitetareSu praznatraye karaNasAdhakatatphaleSu | pAdatrayeNa suvace kramazo nibaddhe kliznanti taM kimiti tatparayojanArtham || iti matadvaye'pi tatra dharmazabdaH kSayiSNusAtizayaphalakarmamAtrapara iti nirvivAdam; kiMca tathAvidhaprayogadarzane'pi agrimavyavahArAnusAreNa tAvanmAtraparatvaM jaiminIyaprayogasya tAvAneva dharmo nAnyo'stIti bhrAntikRtaM kalpayituM zakyam; dRSTaM hi [p.323] 'athAto dharmaM vyAkhyAsyAmaH' iti kaNAdasUtre vicAryaparasya dharmazabdasya agrimasUtrAnusAreNa kiMciddharmaparatvam; agre hi yAgAdyanuSThAnaprakAro na cintitaH, kiMtu SaSThAdhyAye prathamAhnike dAnapratigrahadharmazcintitaH, dvitIyAhnikena cAturAzramyadharmaH kiyAnapi cintitaH saMgrahataH; tasmAt yuktaM dharmavicArapratijJAnasya na brahmavicArasAdhAraNyamiti siddham | atrAyaM matadvayasaMkSepaH-- jAtidravyaguNAnvayi dharmatvaM brahmaNo'pi samavetam | tadupAsanakriyAyA niHsaMdehaM ca dharmatvam ||1|| tatsAkSAdgocarayeddharmavicArapratijJAnam | svargAdivadvicAryaM kriyAphalatvena vA brahma ||2|| ityaikazAstryamubhayormImAMsAzAstrayoryadAsthiSata | tatrAzAmapanetuM teSAM pratibodhanaM kriyate ||3|| bhavadabhimatarUpaM brahma tattadvaco hi spRzati yadi vicArya pUrvatantrapratijJA | kimiti na bhavitavyaM tadvicAraistadAstAM nanu pariharaNIyaM sarvathA tadviruddham ||4|| lakSasUtragatacodanApadaM prerakaM vidhipadaM hi nojjhati | akriyArthavacasAmanarthatAmarthavAdanayapUrvapakSatAm ||5|| arthavAdavacasAmanarthatA paryahAri vidhizeSabhAvataH | tatkimAgamavacAMsi na spRzeccodanaM tava samaM hi teSvapi ||6|| na spRzedyadi tato hi jaiminiH sa svayaMphalaparANi sUtrayet | atra gUDhamabhisaMdhimalpadhIrasya kalpayati kena hetunA ||7|| [p.324] itthaM gUDhAbhisaMdhiH kiyati tu viSaye kalpanIyo maharSeranyAnarthakyayuktyAdyapi hi viphalatoktyAdisAdhAraNaM te | kRtsnaM dharmapramANaM kalayitumanasaM lakSaNe tanyamAne smRtyAcArAdi tadvatsmRtipathamagamatkiM nu vedAntabhAgaH ||8|| atra nirbharamazakyasaMvare prasphuTe praNava eva visvare | zraddadhIta ka iveha tAvakImaikamatyakRtikatthanAM tayoH ||9|| aSTame ca navame ca devatAmadhvareSu guNamAha jaiminiH | svapradhAnamatha bAdarAyaNastatra vA parihRtiH krameta kA ||10|| dvAraM yAgAdInAM yadapUrva devatAprasAda iti | vaiSamyaM tatrApi pravartate naiva parihRtiH kApi ||11|| tasmAtpUrvatra tantre kSayaniyataphalaH kAmikAryo vicAryo dharmastatraiva vedaM sakalamapi munijaiminirmAnamUce | etatkarmaikaniSThairgurukRtamahitaM tanmataM bodhayiSyanbrahmajJAnAya sUtrANyakuruta bhagavAnpuNDarIkAkSa eva ||12|| iti tantrayorvirodhe pradhAnabhUtArthaphalasamAlambe | jAgrati na dhISu viduSAM labhate padamaikazAstryavArtApi || iti aikazAstryanirAkaraNavAdaH ||19||

[p.324] adhyayanavidherarthajJAnaphalakatvanirAkaraNavAdaH || evam 'athAto dharmajijJAsA' iti sUtre vicAryaparasya dharmazabdasya brahmasAdhAraNyAbhAvena brahmavicArArthaM pRthak 'athAto brahmajijJAsA' iti zAstrAntare [p.325] samArabdhavye samarthite, yadi adhyayanavidhiH arthajJAnaphalakaH, tadA brahmavicAre'pi dharmavicAra iva sa prayojako bhavet; sa yadi akSarAvAptimAtravizrAntaH tadA tatra prayojakAntaramanveSaNIyam; pakSadvayamapi bhASyakAraiH darzitam tatra kataraH pakSaH zreyAniti nirNayArthaM kathAntaraM prastUyate tatra 'svAdhyAyo'dhyetavyaH' iti vidhivihitam arthavadvedabhAgAdhyayanam arthajJAnaphalakam, arthavadviSayavihitAdhyayanatvAt, aGgAdhyayanavat iti prathamA kakSyA; 'vedAGgAni samastAni kRSNapakSe tu saMpaThet' iti vidhivihitaM vedAGgAnAmadhyayanam arthajJAnaphalakam, anyathA teSAM vaidikasvaravarNAdinirNAyakatvAsaMbhavAt; tannirNayArthatvenaiva ca teSAM vedAGgatvAt; ato na dRSTAntasya sAdhyavaikalyam; stobhAkSarAdhyayane kautukAdipravRttalaukikAdhyayane ca vyabhicAravAraNArthaM hetau vizeSaNadvayam; tayoreva aMzato bAdhavAraNArthaM pakSe vizeSaNadvayam; evamapi vinApyarthajJAnaM japamAtreNa mahAphalajanakasya zrIpaJcAkSarAdimantrasya gurUpadezAnUccAraNarUpe grahaNe vyabhicAravAraNAya hetau vihitavizeSaNasya sthAne nityavidhivihitatvaM kAryam iti ||
atha dvitIyA kakSyA-- aprayojakamidam, vipakSe aniSTaprasaGgAbhAvAt; na ca arthajJAnaphalakatvAbhAve adhyayanavidheH niSphalatvaprasaGgaH; akSarAvAptyA tasya sAphalyAt, akSarAvAptirhi prApyakarmArthakatavyapratyayena phalatayA upanItA stobhabhAgAdhyayane svAdhyAyAdhyayanaphalatvena klRptA arthajJAnAtprAgbhAvinI adhyayanasya arthajJAnaphalakatvapakSe'pi tannirvAhakatayA prathamamapekSitA ca, adhyayanasya kAlAntarabhAvicArasAdhyArthajJAnakAraNatvasya akSarAvAptizabditasvAdhInoccAraNahetudRDhasaMskAralAbhamUlakavicAraNIyavedavAkyasmRtidvArA nirvAhyatvAt; kiM ca arthajJAne dRSTaphale niyamavidhirAstheyaH, na ca tasyAvakAzo'sti; yadA tAvat adhyayanajanyavidhivAkyArthajJAnavatAmeva vihitAnuSThAnamabhyudayakaram - itivat adhyayanajanyaniSedhavAkyArthajJAnavatAmeva niSiddhAnuSThAnaM pratyavAyakaramiti niyamaphalamAzriyate, tadA [p.326] tadadhyayanarahitAnAM tacchAkhinAM hiMsAnRtAdibhiH pratyavAyo na syAt, vedAdhyayane sati etAni niSiddhAni pratyavAyakarANi iti viziSya tajjJAnAvazyaMbhAvAt tadanuSThAne pratyavAyo bhavediti aniSTaphalatvapratisaMdhAnena tatra pravRttirapi na syAt; athocyeta niSedhavAkyeSu pratyavAyaphalakaM niSiddhAnuSThAnaM na zAstrArthaH, kiMtu pratyavAyaparihAraphalakaM tadananuSThAnamiti, tadviSayo niyamaH adhyayanapUrvakaniSedhavAkyArthajJAnavata eva niSiddhAnanuSThAnaM pratyavAyaparihArAya bhavatIti; tathA ca niSiddhAnuSThAnena pratyavAyajanane niSedhavAkyArthajJAnaM nApekSitamiti, tadadhyayanarahitAnAM tadanuSThAne pratyavAyAbhAvo na prasajyate; pratyavAyaparihAropayogijJAnaphalakamadhyayanaM nAniSTaphalakamiti teSAmadhyayanapravRttyabhAvo'pi na prasajyate iti evamapi anadhItavedAnAM niSiddhAnanuSThAne'pi pratyavAyAparihArAt avazyaMbhAvI pratyavAyaH tadanuSThAnenaivAstu - iti pratisaMdhAnena teSAM niSiddhAnuSThAnaprasaGgo durvAraH adhyayanaM vinA upAyAntareNa niSedhavAkyArthaM jAnan pratyaveyAt iti niyamaphalamAzriyata iti cet, evaM sati adhyayanarahitAnAM niSiddhavarjanArthamupAyAntareNa jJAne tatkRtaH pratyavAyaH, tadajJAne niSiddhAnuSThAnakRtaH ityubhayathApyavarjanIyaH pratyavAyo bhavet; evam adhyayanasaMdhyAvandanAdividhyarthAnAmadhyayanAtprAgupAyAntareNa jJAne adhyayananiyamavidhyatikrameNa, tadajJAne adhyayanAdiSu apravRttyA ca pratyavAyaprasaGgo draSTavyaH | nanu adhyayanagRhItavedArthajJAnavatAmeva anuSThAnamabhyudayakAri ityevamAdiphalam adhyayananiyamasya na kalpyate, kiM tu karmasu zUdrAnadhikArasiddhiH tatphalamabhyupagamyate karmavidhayo hi anuSTheyArthajJAnavadadhikArisApekSAH svAdhyAyAdhyayanavidheH arthajJAnaphalakatve tadvidhiprApitAnuSTheyArthajJAneSu traivarNikeSvevAdhikAriSu labdhAnuSThAnAH na zUdrANAM tajjJAnamAkSipanti, tasya arthajJAnaphalakatvAbhAve tu karmavidhibhirevAnuSThAnArtham arthajJAnamAkSepyamiti avizeSAt zUdrANAmapi arthajJAnamAkSipya tAnapi adhikAritvena gRhNIyuriti cet; maivam, adhyayananiyamasadbhAve'pi zUdrAdhikArApatteranivAryatvAt, traivarNikAnAmapi 'svAdhyAyo'dhyetavya-' iti pitRpitAmahAdiparamparAgatasvazAkhAmAtra eva adhyayanasya vihitatvena na zAkhAntaragatAGgamantropasaMhAreNa karmAnuSThAnArthaM zAkhAntaragatatattadvAkyArthagrahaNAkSepAvazyaMbhAvena, tathaiva zUdrasyApi karmatadaGgaviSayavAkyArthagrahaNAkSepopapatteH na ca vAcyam zUdrasya karmAnuSThAnApekSitajJAnaM kArtsnyenApekSaNIyam, traivarNikAnAM tu zAkhAntarAmnAta[p.327]mAtragocaramiti AkSepalAghavAt vidhayaH traivarNikAnevAdhikuryuH na tu zUdramiti, evaM tarhi yasya karmaNo yasyAM zAkhAyAM bhUyasAmaGgAnAM vidhAnam, 'bhUyastvenobhayazruti' iti nyAyena pradhAnasyApi tatra vidhAnam, zAkhAntare tu svalpAGgavidhAnam - tatra karmaNi tacchAkhAdhyAyinAmevAdhikAraH syAt tasmAt traivarNikAnAmapi arthajJAnAkSepAvazyaMbhAvAt zUdrAnadhikAre tasya vedatadarthagrahaNayajJAnuSThAnaniSedha eva heturiti, nAsau arthajJAnaphalakasya adhyayananiyamavidheH phalamiti; tasmAt vihitamarthavadvedabhAgAdhyayanam arthajJAnaphalakaM na bhavati, vihitavedAdhyayanatvAt, stobhabhAgAdhyayanavat; arthajJAnaphalamuddizya kautukena kriyamANe laukikAdhyayane aMze bAdhasya vyabhicArasya ca vAraNArthaM pakSahetvoH vihitavizeSaNamiti ||
atha tRtIyA kakSyA-- madanumAnaM nAprayojakam, adhyayanasyArthajJAnaphalakatvAbhAve vedasya arthaparatvAbhAvaprasaGgasya vipakSe bAdhakasya sattvAt viziSTArthapratItyuddezyakoccAraNAdhInaM hi zabdAnAmarthaparatvam; ata eva gaurazvaH puruSo hastI iti padasamabhivyAhArasya nAstyarthaparatvam, taduccAraNasya viziSTArthapratItyuddezyakatvAbhAvAt; yatra tu AptakRtasya taduccAraNasya viziSTArthapratItyuddezyakatvamastItyavasitam, tatra ayaM gauH puMgavaH, azvo vegavAn, puruSo niyataceSTaH, hastI mahAbalaH iti gavAzvAdipadAnAM puMgavAdau gauNavRttikalpanayA arthaparatvamupapAdyata eva tatazca vede'pi arthaparatvasiddhyarthamarthAvabodhoddezyakoccAraNApekSaNe loke iva rAgaprayuktasya tasyAsaMbhavAt vidhiprayuktaM tadeSTavyam; vidhizca adhyayanavidheranyaH kazcit arthajJAnaphalakatvakalpanAyogyaH svAdhyAyoccAraNaviSayo na dRzyata iti adhyayanavidherapi arthAvabodhoddezyakatvAbhAve vedasya arthaparatvaM na sidhyedeva; na ca vAcyam vyutpannasya laukikAdiva vaidikAdapi vAkyAdarthapratIteranivAryatayA bAdhakAbhAvAdautsargikaM prAmANyaM na bhajyate, tata eva vedasyArthaparatvaM sidhyet iti, evamapi yeSAM yajamAnaprastarAdivAkyAnAM yathAzrutArthe bAdhakamasti, teSAM gauNIlakSaNAvyavadhAraNakalpanAdyAzrayaNena arthaparatvasya asamarthanIyatayA teSAM nirarthakatvaprasaGgAt; na ca teSAmapi vedAntargatatvahetunA sAmAnyato'rthavattvasiddhau tannirvAhAyArthavizeSaparyavasAnopa[p.328]yogilakSaNAdikalpanayA sArthakatvaM sidhyediti vAcyam, vedAntargatatve'pi stobhabhAgavat anarthakatvopapattyA tatraiva vyabhicAreNa ca anumAnasyAsAdhakatvAt; evaM tarhi stobhabhAgAdhyayanAvyApakaM niSedhAdhyayanasaMdhyAvandanAdividhiSu azakyaniyamanaM cAdhyayanam arthajJAnaphalaM na syAditi cet, na, stobhabhAgAMze'dhyayanavidheH akSarAvAptiphalakatvAbhyupagamAt; na caivaM sati vidhivairUpyaM doSaH, yadeva hi kiMcana vidhyantaropayogi svAdhyAyAdhyayanena zakyaM bhAvayitum, tatsarvaM tena bhAvayet - iti ekayaiva vacanabhaGgayA ubhayArthatvalAbhAt; evaM ca stobhabhAgavyApitvena arthajJAnopajIvyatvena ca akSarAvApteradhyayanaphalatvAvazyaMbhAve'pi, arthajJAnasyApi tatphalatvasyAvazyavAcyatayA tannirvAhAya svAdhyAyasyopAdeyAdhyayanaguNatvena anvaye'bhyupagantavye svAdhyAyaprAdhAnyasamarpakatavyapratyayasvArasyabhaGgo na doSa iti tasyArthasiddhasvAdhyAyagatAdhyayanasaMskAryatAnuvAdakatvameva kalpayitumucitam asti hi zAbdabodhe adhyayanaguNasyApi svAdhyAyasya ArthikamadhyayanasaMskAryatvam, adhyayanasya tatsaMskRtaM svAdhyAyadvAramanapekSya tadarthajJAnAdyutpAdakatvAsaMbhavAt; tasmAt nAprayojakaM madanumAnam, tvadanumAnaM tu adhyayanasya arthajJAnaphalakatvAbhAve vedasya nirarthakatvaprasaGga iti pratikUlatarkaparAhatam iti ||
atha caturthI kakSyA-- zabdAnAmarthaparatve arthapratItyuddezyakamuccAraNaM na prayojakam, mauninA likhitvA datte pareNApyetenaiva likhitaM dRSTvA manasAnusaMhite zlokAdau tadabhAvAt ; atastatra prayojakApekSaNe anyadeva kiMcit prayojakamanveSaNIyamiti tvadanumAnamaprayojakameva, madanumAnaM ca na pratikUlatarkaparAhatamiti ||
atha paJcamI kakSyA-- zabdAnAmarthatAtparye saMbhavadanugataM prayojakaM nopekSaNIyamiti arthapratItyuddezyakamuccAraNaM tatra prayojakaM bhavatyeva, kiMtu noccAraNatvena prayojakam, kathaM tarhi? arthapratItyuddezyakavyApAratvena, ato maunizloke'pi likhitvA dAnarUpasya [p.329] taduddezyakavyApArasya sattvAt noktadoSaH; evaM ca vede tadanukUlavyApAro adhyayanarUpAduccAraNAdanyo na saMbhavatIti tasyArthavattvanirvAhArthamadhyayanavidherarthajJAnaphalakatvamavazyamAstheyamiti nAprayojakaM madanumAnam, tvadanumAnaM ca pratikUlatarkaparAhatamiti ||
atha SaSThI kakSyA-- yadi arthapratItyuddezyakavyApAratvena tAtparyaprayojakatA, tarhi adhyayanAdanya eva kRtsnasvAdhyAyArthapratItyuddezena adhyayanAnantaraM kartavyo vicAraH; na hi vicAramantareNa kasyApi kratUpAsanopayuktaH vedArthanirNayaH saMbhavati; atastasyaiva vedAnAmarthaparatve prayojakatvopapattau nAdhyayanasya tatprayojakatvanirvAhArthaM tavyapratyayasvArasyAdiprAptamakSarAvAptimatilaGghya arthajJAnasyApi tatphalatvaM kalpanIyamiti tvadanumAnamaprayojakameva, madanumAnaM ca na pratikUlatarkaparAhatamiti ||
