atha saptamI kakSyA-- adhyayanasyArthajJAnaphalakatvAbhAve kRtsnasvAdhyAyArthapratIteruddezena vicAra eva na prApnuyAt; tasyArthAvabodhaphalakatve hi tadIyaphalopakAryaGgatayA tadvidhyAkSiptaH sa prApnuyAt; na hi vidhyantaramUlA rAgamUlA vA tatprAptiH saMbhAvayituM zakyate; evaM ca adhyayanAnantaraM kRtsnavedArthavicArakartavyatAmicchatA tvayApi tannirvAhArthamadhyayanavidherarthajJAnaphalakatvasyAvazyAbhyupagantavyatayA sAkSAdarthapratItyuddezyakavyApAro'dhyayanameva, vicArastu tadaGgamiti vedatAtparyaprayojakavyApAratvamapi adhyayanasyaiva paryavasyati, ato madanumAnaM nAprayojakam, tvadanumAnameva pratikUlatarkaparAhatamiti || athASTamI kakSyA-- adhyayanasya tavyapratyayopAttAkSarAvAptyarthatvena arthajJAnArthatvAbhAve'pi adhyayanasaMskRtasya svAdhyAyasya viniyogAkAGkSAyAmarthajJAne tasya viniyogo bhavati [p.330] yathA 'agnInAdadhIta' iti vihitasyAdhAnasya dvitIyAzrutyapanItAgnisiddhyarthatvena kratvarthatvAbhAve'pi AdhAnasaMskRtAnAmagnInAM viniyogAkAGkSAyAM teSAM kratuSu viniyogo bhavati; evaM ca svAdhyAyasyArthajJAnakaraNatvaM vicAramitikartavyatAmapekSata iti taditikartavyatArUpeNa vicAraH prApnuyAdeva; ato vedAnAmarthaparatvasya tadarthapratItyuddezyakavicAravyApAreNa siddheH na tatsiddhyartham adhyayanasyArthajJAnaphalakatvaM kalpanIyamiti tvadanumAnamevAprayojakam, madanumAnaM tu na pratikUlatarkaparAhatamiti ||

atha navamI kakSyA-- saMskRtasvAdhyAyetikartavyatArUpeNa vicArasya prAptau hi sa eva tAtparyaprayojakavyApAro bhavet; tatprAptireva na saMbhavati, tanmUlabhUtasyArthajJAne svAdhyAyaviniyojakasya vacanasyAbhAvAt| nanu vacanAbhAve'pi viniyogAkAGkSasya svAdhyAyasya arthajJAnajananasAmarthyarUpaliGgAdarthajJAne tasya viniyogo labhyata iti cet, na, 'dhanyaM yazasyamAyuSyaM puNyaM svargApavargadam | dhAraNaM dharmazAstrasya vedAnAM dhAraNaM tathA' 'tribhiranaznanpArAyaNaiH pUto bhavati' ityAdivacanaprAptena dhAraNapArAyaNAdiviniyogena nirAkAGkSIkRtasya svAdhyAyasya tato'nyatra laiGgikaviniyogakalpanAyA apravRtteH ; na hi AdhAnasaMskRtAnAmagnInAm 'AhavanIye juhoti' 'gArhapatye havIMSi zrapayati' 'dakSiNAgnAvanvAhAryaM pacati' ityAdivAcanikaviniyogaiH nirAkAGkSIkRtAnAmanyatrApi yatra yatra laukike vaidike vA kArye sAmarthyamasti, tatra sarvatrApi vastusAmarthyena viniyogakalpanA pravartate; na vA sAdanaprokSaNAdisaMskRtAnAM sphyAdInAM yajJAyudhAnAm 'sphyenoddhanti' ityAdivAcanikaviniyoganirAkAGkSIkRtAnAm anyatrApi yatra yatra yajJopayuktakArye sAmarthyamasti, tatra sarvatrApi tena viniyogakalpanA pravartate; tasmAt vicAraprAptiradhyayanavidherarthajJAnaphalakatvamabhyupagamya tatprasAdAyattaiva sA samarthanIyA; tatazca tAtparyaprayojakavyApAro'pi prathamopasthitaM sAkSAdarthapratItyuddezyakam adhyayanameveti paryavasyati iti nAprayojakaM madanumAnam, tvadanumAnaM ca pratikUlatarkaparAhatamiti ||

atha dazamI kakSyA-- [p.331] svAdhyAyasya dhAraNapArAyaNAdiSu viniyogena AkAGkSAzAntAvapi sAGgAdhyayanavazAt phalavattvenApAtapratItAnAM karmaNAmanuSThAnArthamarthanirNayAkAGkSANAm asya vAkyasya ayamevArtha iti nirNayarUpe'rthajJAne'pi viniyogo bhavatyeva; kratvapekSitArthanirNaye kAraNAkAGkSAyAM tatkAraNIbhAvayogyasya adhyayanasaMskRtasvAdhyAyasyApi tatra viniyogAkAGkSosthApanasaMbhavAt; asti hi utthApitAkAGkSayApi viniyogaH yathA jyotiSTome sAmavedagatastotraprakaraNAmnAtAyAH 'agna AyAhi vItaye' ityAdikAyA RcaH stotraviniyogena nirAkAGkSAyA api 'AgneyyA agnIdhramupatiSThate' iti yajurvedavihitasyopasthAnasya karaNabhUtAgnidevatyakarmAkAGkSotthApitayA AkAGkSayA tatrApi karaNatvena viniyogaH | evaM ca arthanirNayakaraNatayA viniyuktasya adhItasvAdhyAyasya itikartavyatAkAGkSayA vicAraH prApnuvan arthapratItyuddezyakavyApAro bhavet iti na tatsiddhyartham adhyayanasya kliSTamarthajJAnaphalakatvaM kalpanIyam ataH tvadanumAnamaprayojakam; madanumAnaM ca na pratikUlatarkaparAhatam iti ||

atha ekAdazI kakSyA-- kratvapekSitArthanirNayaH kalpasUtratadabhijJopadraSTRvacanAdinApi saMbhavan na niyamena vicAramitikartavyatAmapekSate; na ca tatrApi kalpasUtrAbhyAsAdikam arthapratItyuddezyakavyApAraH syAditi vAcyam, tathA sati siddhAntAbhimatavicAraniyamAsiddhiprasaGgAt; na ca adhyayanasya arthajJAnaphalakatvapakSe'pyeSa doSaH, tasminpakSe yAvadadhItArthavatsvAdhyAyArthajJAnasaMpAdanArthaM vicArasyAvazyApekSitatvena tadanAkSepazaGkAnavakAzAt; na hi kratvanuSThAnApekSitatattatkratUpayuktavedabhAgArthajJAnamiva yAvadadhItasvAdhyAyArthajJAnamapi vicAramantareNa saMbhavatIti saMbhAvayitumapi zakyate, ekarUpANAM vedavAkyAnAM nyAyato bhinnabhinnArthAnAm asamIkaraNena tattadarthAvadhAraNasya tadupapAdakanyAyAnusaMdhAnaM vinA saMpAdayitumazakyatvAt tasmAt vede tAtparyaniyAmakam arthapratItyuddezyakavyApAraM vicAramabhyupagacchatApi vicAraniyamasiddhyarthadhyayanavidherarthajJAnaphalakatvam abhyupagantavyamiti || [p.332]
atha dvAdazI kakSyA-- yathA adhyayanavidherarthajJAnaphalakatvapakSe phalApavargiNI adhyayanabhAvanA yAvadadhItasvAdhyAyArthajJAnaparyantaM vyApriyata iti snAnAt prAgeva yugapat sakalavedArthajJAnasaMpAdanArthaM vicAramapekSate, tathA tasya akSarAvAptiphalakatvapakSe'pi adhyayanAvAptAnAM vedAkSarANAmarthajJAne karmAnuSThAnAkAGkSotthApitAkAGkSayA viniyoge sati, dAraparigrahamArabhya yAvajjIvaM nirantaramanuSTheyeSu nityanaimittikAvazyApekSitakAmyakarmasu vighnaparihArArthaM tAvadapekSitavidhimantrArthavAdavibhaktasakalasvAdhyAyArthajJAnaM snAnAtprAgyugapadeva saMpAdanIyam; anyathA kadAcit kalpasUtrArthaparAmarzopadraSTRvacanAdyamelane tatkAlAvazyAnuSTheyakarmAnuSThAnavighnaprasaGgAt - iti yugapat saMpAdanArtham astyeva vicArApekSA; tato vicAraniyamAsiddhyabhAvAt vicAra evArthapratItyuddezyakavyApAra iti ||
atha trayodazI kakSyA-- evamapi kRtsnasvAdhyAyArthaviSayo vicAraniyamo na sidhyati, karmakANDa iva brahmakANDe vicArAkSepakAbhAvAt; na ca brahmopAsanarUpakratvanuSThAnaM tadarthanirNayApekSaM brahmabhAgavicArAkSepakamastIti vAcyam, 'vikalpo viziSTaphalatvAt' iti nyAyAt ekAmeva brahmopAsanAM yAvajjIvaM cikIrSamANasya mumukSoH tAvanmAtropayogivedAntavAkyArthanirNayasya Aptopadezato'pi labdhuM zakyatvena tadarthagurUpAyarUpavicArAkSepAsaMbhavAt; na ca siddhAnte zravaNavidhirasti, yena tato vedAntavicArakartavyatA labhyeta; 'tadvijJAnArthaM sa gurumevAbhigacchet' iti gurUpasadanavidhistu svAnuSTheyopAsanApekSitakatipayavedAntavAkyArthopadeSTRgurUpasadanaviSayatayApi caritArthaH; tasmAt vicAraniyamasiddhyartham adhyayanavidheH arthajJAnaphalakatvamevAGgIkaraNIyamiti ||
atha caturdazI kakSyA-- [p.333] adhyayanavidherarthajJAnaphalakatvAbhAve'pi kRtsnasvAdhyAyavicAraH sidhyatyeva, ekAmevopAsanAM cikIrSamANenApi puruSeNa mantrArthavAdAdiprasiddhamahAmahimazAlinAM devatAvizeSANAM madhye viziSya idameva brahmeti nirdhArya yAvajjIvam aharaharAvartanIyA brahmopAsanA ArabdhavyA; tannirdhAraNaM tu yadyapi ayamitivat idaM brahmeti zRGgagrAhikayA AptatamopadezenApi kathaMcit labdhuM zakyam, tathApi tallakSaNamukhenaiva saMpAdanIyam; brahmasadbhAvamAtraM saMpratipadya tat kiM brahma iti idaMtayA jJAnArtham 'adhIhi bhagavo brahma' iti upasannAya bhRgave 'yato vA' ityAdilakSaNamukhena tadupadezasAmarthyataH 'tadvijijJAsasva tadbrahma' iti vAkyazeSatazca lakSaNamukhenaiva brahma nirdhArya upAsanIyamiti niyamonnayanAt, na hi zRGgagrAhikayA upadezasyApi upAsanopayogibrahmanirdhAraNopAyatve gurUpAye lakSaNamukhena brahmanirdhAraNe guruH ziSyaM pravartayet; lakSaNamukhena brahmanirdhAraNaM lipsuH asaMbhavAtivyAptizaGkAnirAsena lakSaNadRDhIkaraNArthaM sarvavedAntatAtparyanirNAyakavicAramapekSate; ata eva zAstre 'janmAdyasya yataH' iti lakSaNamukhenaiva brahma nirUpya lakSaNaviziSTabrahmaNi pramANapradarzanapUrvakaM tadasaMbhavAtivyAptizaGkAnirAsArthaH kRtsnavedAntavicAraH kRto dRzyate; tasmAt adhyayanavidheH akSarAvAptiphalakatve'pi vicAraniyamasiddheH sa evArthapratItyuddezyakavyApAraH ityupapadyata eveti |
atha paJcadazI kakSyA-- evamapi sarveSAM brahmopAsanAnAM vicAraniyamo na sidhyati, AnandamayabrahmopAsanAprakaraNe lakSaNAmnAnasAmarthyAt tadupAsanArthina eva lakSaNamukhena brahma nirdhAraNaniyamasiddheH; na hi upAsanAsvapi prakaraNavizeSAmnAtA dharmAH sarvAsUpAsanAsu pAriplavA bhavanti, tathA sati 'pUrvo'tithibhyo'znIyAt' ityAdividhivihitAnAmapi vaizvAnaravidyAdiprakaraNAmnAtAnAM dharmANAM sarvopAsanAsAdhAraNyaprasaGgAt; ato vicAraniyamamicchatA vicArameva arthapratItyuddezyakavyApAramabhyupagacchatA ca tvayApi tannirvAhArtham arthajJAnaphalakatvamabhyupagantavyamiti || [p.334] [p.335]
atha SoDazI kakSyA-- zravaNavidhimanaGgIkurvato bhASyakArasya mate sarvamumukSusAdhAraNyena vicAraniyamamanabhyupagamyApi upAsanAnuSThAnaM samarthayituM zakyata eva; ye svayaM vicArasamarthAH vicAramantareNa sarvavidhasaMzayanivRttimalabhamAnatayA svayaM vicAramiha kurvanti, teSAM zravaNamananapraNADyA upAsanArambhaH, atathAbhUtAnAM bahukarmAnuSThAnalabdhacittazuddhyatizayatayA brahmAparokSyavati gurau ativizvAsavatAM vinApi zravaNamanane gurUpadiSTaprakAreNa upAsanArambha iti; na hi karmasu upAsaneSu vA sarvAdhikArisAdhAraNyena vicAraniyamaH kartuM zakyate, strINAM tadabhAvAt; evaM ca prAcInazAlAdInAM vaizvAnaravidyAdiSu saMbhAvitasaMzayanivRttyarthaM vidyAnuSThAnaprakAramAtropadeSTRgurUpasarpaNAdiliGgamapi upapadyate; evaM ca vicArahInAnAm arthajJAnArthagurUpasarpaNAdivyApAra eva arthapratItyuddezyakavyApAro bhaviSyati; yadyucyeta zravaNavidhiM pratyAkhyAtavatApi zravaNavidhyarthamanyathAsiddhaM samarthitavatA bhASyakAreNa vedAdhyayanavatAM mumukSUNAM vicAraniyamaH svIkRta eva; prAcInazAlAdInAM tu kRtazravaNAnAmeva zravaNakAle 'zravaNAyApi bahubhiryo na labhyaH zRNvanto'pi bahavo yaM na vidyuH | Azcaryo vaktA kuzalo'sya labdhAzcaryo jJAtA kuzalAnuziSTaH' iti mantroktarItyA viparyAyasenArthaM nirNIya upAsanAsu pravRttAnAM parasparavaimatyadarzanena saMjAtasaMdehAnAM punargurUpasarpaNamiti liGgamapi anyathA upapadyate; ata eva tvayA sarveSAM vedAdhikAriNAM vicAraniyamamabhyupagamya vicAra evArthapratItyuddezyakavyApAratvena pratijJAta iti tathApi na doSaH, prakaraNavizeSAmnAtasyApi lakSaNasya brahmodantanirdhAraNarUpadRSTArthatAbalAt sarvopAsanAsAdhAraNyasyopapatteH; ata eva 'vividiSanti yajJena dAnena tapasAnAzakena' 'vidyAM cAvidyAM ca yastadvedobhayaM saha avidyayA mRtyuM tIrtvA vidyayAmRtamaznute' 'agnirityAdinA bhasma gRhItvA vimRjyAGgAni saMspRzet vratametatpAzupataM pazupAzavimokSAya' 'zAnto dAnta uparatastitikSuH samAhito bhUtvAtmanyevAtmAnaM pazyet' ityAdibhiH upAsanavizeSaprakaraNavihitAnAmapi cittaprasAdacittaikAgryarUpadRSTaprayojanArthAnAM vidyAsAdhanasahakAribhasmoddhUlanazamadamAdInAM sarvopAsanAsAdhAraNyamabhyupagamyate; evamadhyayanasya akSarAvAptiphalakatve'pi adhyayanavatAM mumukSUNAM vedAntavicAraniyamaH samarthayituM zakyata eva; pratyuta tasyArthajJAnaphalakatvapakSa eva vedAntavicAro mumukSUNAmeveti niyamaH samarthayituM na zakyate, phalApavargiNyA adhyayanabhAvanayA snAnAtprAgeva karmakANDa iva brahmakANDe'pi arthajJAnaphalopakArivicArAkSepasyAnivAryatvAt; etena sAkSAt paramparayA vA vedArthajJAnArthasyAGgAdhyayanasya vedAdhyayanakAla eva kRSNapakSeSu kartavyasya anuSThAnasAdezyAvagatAdhyayanAGgatvAnyathAnupapattyA adhyayanasyArthajJAnaphalakatvamabhyupagantavyamityapi zaGkA nirastA, 'SaDaGgo vedo'dhyeyaH' 'vedAGgAni samastAni kRSNapakSeSu saMpaThet' ityAdivacanaiH teSAM vedAGgatvasyaiva pratipAdyamAnatayA teSAmadhyayanAvAptasvAdhyAyaphalArthajJAnArthatvasyaivAvagamAt; tathAtve'pi vedAdhyayanakAla eva aGgAdhyayanakartavyatAvidheH dAraparigrahaprabhRti nirantarasaMtanyamAnakarmAnuSThAnopayogyarthajJAnecchAsaMpAdakApAtajJAnajananArthatvena anyathAsiddheH aGgAdhyayanasya vedAdhyayanasya ca phalArthatvAbhyupagame'pi sAkSAtparamparAsAdhAraNyena tatphalArthatvopapatteH ; anyathA kratvanuSThAnakAlanirNAyakasya jyotiSTomazAstrasya kratvanuSThAnArthasya kalpasUtrasya ca tadaGgatvAbhAvaprasaGgAt; etenaiva aGgAdhyayanasya arthajJAnaparyantatAyAH klRptatvanyAyena vedAdhyayanamapi arthajJAnaparyantaM syAdityapi zaGkA nirastA, 'vedAGgAni samastAni' ityAdivacaneSu aGgaprAtipadikAnusAreNa dvitIyAvibhaktyavagataM prAdhAnyaM parityajya - vedAGgairarthajJAnaM bhAvayet iti teSAmarthajJAne guNabhAvo'GgIkRtaH, anyathA adhyayanAvAptavedajanyArthajJAnopayogitvAbhAvena vedAGgatvAnirvAhAt; na caivaM svAdhyAyavidhivAkye tavyapratyayAvagatasvAdhyAyaprAdhAnyaM parityajya arthajJAnaguNabhAvAGgIkAre kiMcitkAraNamasti; na ca svAdhyAyasya guNatvAnaGgIkAre svazabdArthasya kulakramAgatatvarUpasya svIyatvasya uddezyavizeSaNatvApattyA grahaikatvavat avivakSAprasaGgena svAdhyAyAdhyayanavidheH sarvavedAdhyayanaviSayatvApattiH, sarvavedAnAmadhyetumazakyatayA saMkocApekSAsattvena uddezyasyApi vizeSaNasahiSNutvAt, 'yasyobhayaM havirArtimArcchedaindraM paJcazarAvamodanaM nirvapet' ityatra ArtimAtrasya nimittatayA uddezyatve pratikSaNaM yasya kasyacit nAzasaMbhavena naimittikasyAzakyAnuSThAnatA prasajyata iti tatparihArArtham ArtimAtraM na nimittam, kiMtu havirArtiH nimittamiti havirvizeSaNavivakSAGgIkArAt; tasmAdarthapratItyuddezyakavyApArApekSaNamAtram adhyayanasya arthajJAnaguNatvAGgIkAre heturvAcyaH; sa ca vicAreNAnyathAsiddhisamarthanena pratyAkhyAta eva; vastutastu zabdAnAmarthaparatvam arthapratItyuddezyakavyApAraM nApekSate, [p.336] yataH lokavyutpattisiddhaM tadautsargikam; kvacitkvacit laukikavAkye vaktRdoSAdapavAde'pi apauruSeye vede tadabhAvAt nirapavAdamavatiSThate; tadautsargikamarthaparatvasAmAnyaM vaktRvivakSite vizeSe loke paryavasyati; vede vakturabhAvAt nyAyalabhyavizeSe paryavasyati; tadanusAreNaiva ca vede lakSaNA gauNI vyavadhAraNam iti kalpyate; ata eva zabdAnAmarthaparatvam arthapratItyuddezyakoccAraNAdhInamiti manvAnena bhavadevenApi svAbhimatoktanyAyabalenaiva svAdhyAyAvAptimAtraphalatApratyAyakatavyapratyayasvArasyamullaGghya svAdhyAyAdhyayanena yat bhAvayituM zakyaM tatsarvaM tena bhAvayet iti vyavadhAraNAkalpanA aGgIkRtA; na hi adhyayanavidheH uktArthanizcayo'yam arthAvabodhoddezyakataduccAraNavidhyadhInaH, tathAbhUtavidhyantarAbhAvAt, asyaiva vidherapekSAyAm AtmAzrayApatteH; kiMtu zabdAnAmarthaparatvamautsargikamityetadAzritya tasya svoktanyAyAdhIne vizeSe paryavasAnAt tannirvAhArtham idaM vyavadhAraNakalpanamityeva vaktavyam; evaM ca tathaiva vedavAkyAntarANAmapi arthaparatvasAmAnyatadvizeSaparyavasAnayoH nirvAhe saMbhavati, kiM tannirvAhArthanyAyAbhAsAvalambanayA vyavadhAraNakalpanayA; ito'pyayaM nyAyAbhAsaH, yadi vedavAkyAnAmarthapratItyuddezyakamuccAraNaM na syAt, tadA teSAmarthaparatvaM na syAt iti tarkarUpaH khalu ayaM nyAyaH; ayaM ca svapravRtteH prAgeva ApAdyaviparyayasya pramANataH siddhimapekSate, anyathA tarkasya aniSTaprasaGgatvAbhAvaprasaGgAt, tacca pramANamanviSyamANam - zabdAnAmarthaparatvamautsargikam - ityetadevAvatiSThate; tatazca tenaivAnyeSAmapi vedavAkyAnAmarthaparatvasiddheH na tatra niyAmakAntaramapekSaNIyam, natarAM tadapekSAdhInamadhyayanavidhervyavadhAraNaM kalpanIyam; tatkalpane ca adhyayanena bhAvayituM zakyaM cArvAkApAditaM khyAtilAbhAdikamapi tatphalaM syAt; vidhyantarApekSitaM yadbhAvayituM zakyamiti vizeSaNe'pi atiprasaGgastadavasthaH, dhanalAbhasya kratuvidhibhirapekSitatvAt| khyAterapi dhanalAbhadvArA kratUpayogAt; tasmAt arthapratItyuddezyakavyApArApekSaNanyAyaH nAzrayaNIyaH iti tvadanumAnamaprayojakam, madanumAnaM na pratikUlatarkaparAhatam iti akSarAvAptiphalakameva adhyayanamiti siddham || iti adhyayanavidherarthajJAnaphalakatvanirAkaraNavAdaH ||20||

[p.336] adhyayanasya svavidhiprayuktatvasamarthanavAdaH || [p.337] evam akSarAvAptiphalakatvena nirNItamadhyayanaM na svavidhiprayuktAnuSThAnam, kiM tu adhyApanavidhiprayuktAnuSThAnam iti kecana manyante; teSAmitthaM matam - adhyayanavidhiH na svaviSayAnuSThApakaH, nirniyojyatvAt, prayAjAdividhivat; na ca vizvajinyAyena niyojyaH kalpanIyaH, svargakAmAdiniyojyakairdarzapUrNamAsAdividhibhiH prayAjAdInAmiva AcAryatvakAminiyojyena adhyApanavidhinA adhyayanasya prayuktisaMbhave tatkalpanAnapekSaNAt; tathApi upanayanapUrvakAdhyApanAt AcAryatvaM niSpadyate, 'upanIya tu yaH ziSyaM vedamadhyApayedvijaH | sakalpaM sarahasyaM ca tamAcAryaM pracakSate' iti smRtyA AcAryatvakAmaH ziSyamupanIya vedamadhyApayet iti zrutyunnayanAt, tadanusAreNa 'aSTavarSaM brAhmaNamupanayIta tamadhyApayIta' iti zrutau 'upanayIta' ityatra saMmAnanAdisUtravihitAtmanepadabalAt upanayane pratIyamAnasya AcAryatvahetutvasya tannirvAhakAdhyApanadvArakatvakalpanaucityAcca; tadAcAryatvaM yadyapi AdhAnaniSpAdyamAhavanIyatvAdikamiva alaukikAtizayarUpam, tacca na svataH puruSArthaH, tathApi 'AcAryAya varo deyaH' ityAdismaraNAt dakSiNAdAnAdihetutvena gauNapuruSArtho bhavati; atastatkAmo'yamadhyApanavidhau niyojyaH iti saniyojyakena tena vidhinA adhyayanaM prayujyate - iti; tatra kariSyamANapUrvapakSopayoginamapekSitAMzaM parigRhya adhyayanasya svavidhiprayuktatvasamarthanArthaM kathAntaraM prastUyate -- 'svAdhyAyo'dhyetavyaH' iti vidhivihitaM niyamAdhyayanam adhyayanavidhiprayojyam, vidhyantarAprayojyatve sati vidhiprayojyatvAt iti saiddhAntikI prathamA kakSyA || tvadIyo heturvizeSaNAsiddhaH, adhyayanAnuSThAnasya adhyApanavidhiprayuktatvAt; tasmAt uktAdhyayanAnuSThAnaM na adhyayanavidhiprayuktam, adhyApanavidhiprayuktatvAt, adhyApanavat - iti dvitIyA ||
[p.338]
atha tRtIyA kakSyA-- na madIyo heturvizeSaNAsiddhaH adhyayanasya adhyApanavidhiprayuktatvAyogAt; na ca adhyayanaM vinA adhyApanaM na nirvartate iti tasya tatprayuktatvam, laukikAdhyayanenApi tannirvRttyA vihitAdhyayanasya tatprayuktatvAsiddheH; ata eva tvadIyo heturasiddha iti ||
atha caturthI kakSyA-- upanayanasya adhyApanAGgatayA tasya kiMcitkArAkAGkSAyAm upaneyAsattirUpo dRSTa eva kiMcitkAra iti siddhau, adhyApanaM prati anupayogina upaneyasyAsattiH adhyApanAnupakAriNI na tadaGgakiMcitkAro bhavatIti, upaneyasyAdhyApanApekSitavyApArAkAGkSAyAm upanayanaM prakramya 'svAdhyAyo'dhyetavyaH' iti vidhivihitasya niyamAdhyayanasyaiva tadvyApAratayA anvayena tasya tatprayuktatvasiddheH tvadIyo heturvizeSaNAsiddhaH, madIyazca nAsiddhaH iti || atha paJcamI-- upanayanasya adhyApanAGgatave syAdetadevam, na tvetadasti, tatra pramANAbhAvAt iti || atha SaSThI-- upanayanasya adhyApanAGgatve pramANAbhAvo'siddhaH, tathA hi 'upanIya tu yaH ziSyam' iti smRtyanumitazrutivAkyagatopanayanAdhyApanayoH samAnakartRkatvavAdinI ktvAzrutistAvat tatra pramANam, samAnakartRkatvaM hi prayogaikyaM vinA nopapadyate; yadyapi prayogaikyaM tayoH samaprAdhAnye vaiparItyenAGgAGgibhAve ca saMbhavati, tathApi upanayanamupaneyAsattimasaMpAdya tatkartRkeNAdhyayanena adhyApanaM nirvartayituM [p.339] kSamamiti dRSTadvArasattvAt upanayanasya adhyApanAGgatva eva ktvAzrutiH paryavasyatIti; tathA 'tamadhyApayIta' iti tacchabdaparAmRSTopanItasya adhyApanasaMbandhabodhanAt tacchabdazrutirapi upanayanasya adhyApanAGgatve pramANam, 'tapte payasi dadhyAnayati sA vaizvadevyAmikSA vAjibhyo vAjinam' ityatra 'sA' iti tacchabdena dadhyAnayanasaMskRtapayaH parAmarzinA dadhisaMsargaprAptaghanIbhAvapayovAcyAmikSApadasAmAnAdhikaraNyalabdhastrIliGgena dadhyAnayanasaMskRtasya payaso vaizvadevayAgasaMbandhabodhanAt payaH saMskArasya dadhyAnayanasyApi vaizvadevayAgAGgatvasiddhirabhyupagamyate; tathA 'upanayIta' iti Atmanepadazrutirapi tatra pramANam, sA hi adhyApanaphalasya AcAryatvasya upanayanAnvayaM bodhayantI pUrvoktarItyA upanayanasya adhyApanAGgatve paryavasyati; tasmAt adhyApanavidhinA svAGgamupanayanaM prayuJjAnena upanItavyApArAdhyayanasyApi prayuktirupapadyata iti tvaddheturvizeSaNAsiddhaH, maddhetuzca nAsiddha iti ||
atha saptamI kakSyA-- ktvAzrutistAvat upanayanAdhyApanayoH aGgAGgibhAve na pramANam, 'darzapUrNamAsAbhyAmiSTvA somena yajeta' ityatreva tasya kartRtvAdhiSThAnapuruSaikyamAtrAvalambanatvopapatteH ; 'tamadhyApayIta' ityatra upanItaparAmarzitacchabdazrutirapi na pramANam; tathAhi dadhyAnayanarItyA upanayanasya samabhivyAhRtakriyAnvayamAtraM sidhyati; samabhivyAhRtA ca kriyA tatra vaizvadevayAgavat iha adhyApanakriyaiva na bhavati, kiMtu adhyayanakriyApi, NijantadhAtunA prayojyaprayojakavyApAradvayopasthiteH ; ataH upanayanasya prayojyavyApArabhUtAdhyayanasaMbandhabodhanenApi tacchabdazrutiH caritArthA bhavediti; tathA Atmanepadazrutirapi tatra na pramANam, tayA hi Ahatya upanayanasya na adhyApanAGgatvaM bodhyate, 'saMmAnanotsarjanAcAryakaraNajJAnabhRtivigaNanavyayeSu niyaH' iti sUtreNa upanayanAdhyApanayoraGgAGgibhAve nayateH AtmanepadavidhAnAbhAvAt; upanayane adhyApanaphalAcAryatvasaMbandhabodhanaM tasyAdhyApanAGgatvaM kalpayediti cet, na, adhyayanadvAreNa upanayanopakAryasya adhyApanasya phalam upakArake vyapadizyate iti upapatteH, AdhAnadvArA agnihotradarzapUrNamAsAdisarvakarmopakAriNyAm AdhAnAGgapUrNAhutau 'pUrNAhutyA sarvAnkAmAnavApnoti' iti [p.340] tadupakAryasarvakarmaphalavyapadezadarzanAt; evamanyathAsiddhAtmanepadazrutirapi tatra na pramANamiti || athASTamI kakSyA-- ktvAzrutistAvat ihAGgAGgibhAve pramANaM bhavatyeva, tadabhAve kartRkArakabhedena samAnakartRkatvAnirvAhAt; darzapUrNamAsayoH somayAgasya ca svAtantryeNa pRthakphalasAdhanatayA vAkyAntarAgavatatvena gatyantarAbhAvAt udAhRtavAkye ktvAzrutiH kartRkArakAdhiSThAnaikyamAtrAvalambanA kathaMcidupapadyate; iha tu tathA anupapattyabhAvAt 'vAjapeyenASTvA bRhaspatisavena yajeta' ityatreva ktvAzruteH aGgAGgibhAvaparyavasAnaM yuktam; tathA 'tamadhyApayIta' ityatra tacchabdazrutirapi tatra pramANaM bhavatyeva, kriyAdvayasamabhivyAhAre'pi pradhAnakriyAnvayasya abhyarhitatvAt; tathA Atmanepadazrutirapi, aGgapradhAnabhAvena sAkSAtsaMbandhino'dhyApanasya phalam upanayane nirdizyate iti vaktuM zakyate; paramparAsaMbandhakalpanAyogAt, na hi AdhAnasyeva upanayanasya anyAGgatvamavadhRtamiti ||
atha navamI kakSyA-- upanayanasya anyAGgatvaM nAvadhRtamiti asiddham, tasyAnyAGgatvAvadhAraNAt; tathA hi 'vasante brAhmaNamupanayIta grISme rAjanyaM zaradi vaizyam' iti dvitIyAzrutibhiH upanayanasya upaneyabrAhmaNAdyarthatvamavagamyate; tAdarthye tasya tadIyakAryAdhyayanAGgatve nirvahati, na tu anyadIyakAryAdhyApanAGgatve; tathA upanayanajanyAcAryAsattirUpadRSTasaMskArasya viniyogAkAGkSAyAmadhyayanasya upaneyagatatvena antaraGgatvAcca tAdarthameva yuktam, na tu adhyApanAGgatvam; tasya anyagatatvena bahiraGgatvAt; tathA upanayanajanyAdRSTasaMskArasya upaneyakAryArthatvAdapi tajjanyAcAryAsattirUpadRSTasaMskArasya tadIyakAryAdhyayanArthatvaM yuktam, asti hi adRSTasaMskAro'pi upanayanajanyaH upaneyasya sarvakarmAdhikArArthaH tadapekSameva smaraNam - upanayanAdirniyamaH iti; tadapekSameva ca 'tasmiMstu ziSyamANAni jananena pravarteran' iti SaSThA[p.341]dhyAyAdhikaraNam; tatra hi smRtyAcAraprAptAni karmANi janmaprabhRti kartavyAni santi sAmarthyAnusAreNa prAgapyupanayanAt kartavyAnIti pUrvapakSaM kRtvA, upanayanaprabhRtyeva kartavyAni iti siddhAntitam, tathA bIjagarbhasamudbhavaduritanirharaNArthazcAyamupanayanajanyaH saMskAraH, 'gArbhairhomairjAtakarmacaulamauJjInibandhanaiH | baijikaM gArbhikaM caino dvijAnAmapamRjyate | evamenaH zamaM yAti bIjagarbhasamudbhavam ityAdismaraNAt; itthamenonirharaNArthatvena karmAnadhikRtayorapi mUkonmattayoH upanayanAvazyaMbhAvamabhipretyaiva tayoranupanayanapakSo manunA keSAMcitpakSatvena upanyastaH 'mUkonmattau na saMskAryAviti kecitpracakSate | karmasvanadhikArAcca pAtityaM nAsti caitayoH' iti; evamupanayanasya adRSTasaMskAradvArA upaneyArthatvasya klRptatvAt dRSTasaMskAradvArApi tAdarthamevAbhyupantuM nyAyyam tathA 'saptame brahmavarcasakAma aSTame AyuSkAmam' ityAdyupanayanAzritavayovizeSalakSaNaguNAnAmupaneyArthatvAt tadAzrayopanayanasyApi tAdarthyaM vaktavyam; 'tathA kAmo'rthasaMyogAt' ityadhikaraNe 'yadi kAmayeti varSakaH parjanyaH syAnnIcaiH sado minuyAt' iti vAkye sadomAturadhvaryoH kAmanA na vivakSitA, kiM tu pradhAnaphalAzrayasya yajamAnasyaiva, pradhAnaguNaphalayoH ekArthatvasyautsargikatvAt iti siddhAntitatvAt; kiM ca upanayanavidhinA kimarthamahamAcAryasamIpaM prApitaH iti upaneyasya kAryAkAGkSayA upanayanavidhyanantaraM kartRrahitatayA vihitasyAdhyayanasya kartrAkAGkSayA ca upanayanAdhyayanavidhyorekavAkyatA bhavatIti vAkyapramANamapi upanayanasyAdhyayanAGgatve pramANam; tasmAdupanayanasyAdhyayanAGgatvena bahupramANAvadhRtasya adhyApanAGgatvAbhAvAt adhyayanasya vidhyantarAprayojyatvamupapadyata iti na maddheturvizeSaNAsiddhaH, tvaddhetuzcAsiddha iti ||
atha dazamI kakSyA-- astu upanayanasya tvadAhRtapramANaiH adhyayanAGgatvam, tathApi adhyayanasyaivAGgamiti avadhAraNaM na saMbhavati, madudAhRtapramANaiH adhyApanAGgatvasyApyupapatteH, na hi alpamapi pramANam avirodhe bahupramANabAdhyaM bhavati; na ca kiMcidaGgabhAvasya anyAGgAbhAvena virodho'sti, prayAjAdInAmAgneyAdyanekAGkSatvadarzanAt; na ca yasya yatpradhAnam, tasya tadeva pradhAnaM dvArIkRtya tadanyAGgatvamayuktamiti vAcyam, gIti[p.342]kriyArUpasAmasaMskAryatvena tatpradhAnabhUtAmRcameva dvArIkRtya sAmno guNiniSThaguNAbhidhAnarUpastutisAdhanatvasya 'sAmnA stuvIta' iti zrutibodhitasya saMpratipannatvena tathaiva upanayanasyAdhyayanAGgasya adhyayananirvartanadvArA adhyApanAGgatvasyApi upapatteH; tasmAt adhyayanasya adhyApanavidhinA svAGgabhUtamupanayanaM prayuJjAnena prayuktisaMbhavAt tvaddheturvizeSaNAsiddhaH, maddhetuzca nAsiddhaH iti || athaikAdazI kakSyA-- adhyayanAGgasya sataH upanayanasya adhyApane'pi aGgatvamApAdayat ananyathAsiddhamiha nAsti pramANam tathA hi 'upanIya tu yaH ziSyam' iti smRtiH na adhyApanamAtreNAcAryo bhavati, kiMtu upanayanenApi ityetAvanmAtraparA yathA 'na vidyayA kevalayA tapasA vApi pAtratA | yatra vRttamime cobhe taddhi pAtraM pracakSate' iti smRtiH kevalaM vidyayA tapasA vRttena vA pAtraM na bhavati, kiMtu tribhirityetatparam prAyapAThAnurodhAt; 'niSekAdIni karmANi yaH karoti yathAvidhi | saMbhAvayati cAnnena sa vipro gururiSyate' ityayam 'tamAcAryaM pracakSate' ityetadanantarazloko hi niSekAdyadhyApanAntakarmakaraNena putrANAM vRttikalpanena ca gururbhavati, na tadekadezena ityetadartho dRzyate; 'sa gururyaH kriyAH kRtvA vedamasmai prayacchati' iti tadarthasaMgrAhakasmRtyantare ca ktvApratyayo'pi dRzyate; na caitAvatA niSekAdicaulAntakarmakaraNamapi tatrAdhyApanAGgamiSyate; tasmAt neha kartRkArakaikye'bhiniveSTavyam; tadapi cAtra upapAdayituM zakyam adhyApanopakArakAdhyayanAnvayamAtreNa, dRzyate hi AgneyayAgena saha samuccitya phalasAdhanatayA tadupakArakasya agnISomIyayAgasyAGgAnAm AgneyayAgasya ca kartraikyam ekaprayogAntaHpAtitvAt; 'tamadhyApayIta' ityatra tacchabdaparAmRSTasya upanayanasya prayojyavyApArabhUtAdhyayanAGgatvameva, atikuzalena takSNA rathaM nirmApayati ityatra prayojyavizeSaNasya tadvyApAre rathanirmANa eva upayogadarzanAt; 'upanayIta' ityAtmanepadazrutistu upanayanasya adhyApanakArakatvamAtreNApi upapAdayituM zakyeti prasAdhitaM saptamakakSyAyAm; na ca sAkSAtsaMbandhasaMbhave adhyayanAGgabhAvaddvArakaparamparAsaMbandhagrahaNaM na yuktamiti vAcyam, upanayanamadhyayanAGgamiti bahupramANasiddhatvena klRptasaMbandhaviSayatve saMbhavati smRteH [p.343] kalpyasaMbandhAntarAlambanatvAbhyupagamAyogAt; upanayanasaMskArasya anyatra viniyogAdarzanena kaimarthyAkAGkSAyAM hi aklRpto'pyadhyApanena sAkSAtsaMbandhaH tadAlambanaM kalpyeta; kiMca AtmanepadazrutiH upanayanasya adhyayanAGgatva eva pramANam, saMmAnanAdisUtreNAtmanepadavidhAnasya akartrabhiprAyArthatvAt; kartrabhiprAye kriyAphale nayateH Atmanepadasya 'svaritaJitaH' ityAdisUtreNa siddhatvAt; nanu upanayanaM pratyaGgitvena uddezyasya adhyApanasya tatphalasyAcAryatvasya ca kartRgatatve'pi tasyAdhyApanAGgatAyAM dvArabhUtasya upanayanajanyasaMskArasyAkartRgatatvAt saMmAnanAdisUtreNa Atmanepadam ityetadupapadyata iti cet, na, upanayanajanyAcAryAsattirUpasaMskArasyApi tvanmate upanayanakartRgatatvAt; na hi tadgatatvaM tatsamavetatvam, kiMtu tAdarthyam, anyathA pazuputrahiraNyAdInAM yajamAnasamavetatvAbhAvena 'citrayA yajeta pazukAmaH' ityAdau AtmanepadaM na syAt; upanayanakartrarthatvaM ca mANavakasaMskArasya adhyayananirvartanadvArA tadIyAdhyApanArthatvAt astyeva; kiM ca kartrabhiprAyasyApi kriyAphalasya sattve svaritAdisUtreNaiva AtmanepadasiddheH saMmAnanAdisUtrApravRttizaGkA tadavasthaiva; api ca akartRgatamapi kiMcit kriyAphalam astItyetAvadavalambenApi saMmAnanAdisUtrapravRttiriha azakyazaGkA, zAbdikamaryAdAvirodhAt zAbdikairhi yatpradhAnaM phalamuddizya kriyArambhaH, tasya kartRgatatvAkartRgatatvayoreva padavyavasthAprakaraNaviSayatvamaGgIkRtam; uktaM ca hariNA yajati yAjakaH pacati pAcakaH ityatrAtmanepadaM syAt, tatrApi dakSiNAdeH kartRgAmitvAt ityAzaGkAM nirAkurvatA 'yasyArthasya prasiddhyarthamArabhyante pacAdayaH | tatpradhAnaM phalaM teSAM na lAbhAdi prayojanam' iti; na ca RtvigAdivyApArAH dakSiNAlAbhAdyarthamArabhyante iti zaGkA tadavastheti vAcyam, upAttayAgapAkAdikriyAsaMtatyavacchedakaprayogaprathamapravRtterArambhazabdArthatvAt; sA ca prathamapravRttiH svAmigatA kratupAkasaMkalpavaraNapAcakapravartanAdirUpaiveti nAtiprasaGgaH; tasmAt; 'upanayIta' ityAtmanepadazrutiH upanayanasya adhyayanAGgatva eva pramANam || kaizcidimAM kaumAriloktAM yuktiM vilikhya, tatra upanayanamadhyApanAGgam iti vadatAmAkSepaH tatsamAdhAnaM ceti ubhayaM svabuddhikalpitaM kiMcillikhitam, tat prasaGgAdiha nirAkartuM vilikhyate nanu kriyAphalasya kartrekagatatvaM kartrabhiprAyatvam, tathA ca upanayanaphalasya kartrakartrubhayagatatvena tatra svaritAdisUtrApravRtteH tatra [p.344] AtmanepadArthaM saMmAnanAdisUtramiti cet, na, tathA vivakSAyAm 'agnInAdadhIta' ityatra kartrabhiprAyasUtreNa AtmanepadAbhAvaprasaGgAt, AdhAnaphalasya agniyajamAnobhayagatatvAt kartrekagatatvoktyA kartratiriktacetanagatatvavyAvRttirvivakSiteti cet, na, tathApi 'Rtvijo vRNIte' ityatra svaritAdisUtreNAtmanepadAbhAvaprasaGgAt; atha avyavahitakriyAphalasya kartRgatatvaM kartrabhiprAyatvaM vivakSitam, tatazca upanayanAvyavahitamANavakasaMskArasyAkartRgatatvAt svaritAdisUtrApravRtteH saMmAnanAdisUtramiti cet, na, evamapi 'agnInAdadhIta' ityatra kartrabhiprAyasUtreNa AtmanepadAbhAvaprasaGgAt, AdhAnAvyavahitaphalasya agnisaMskArasya kartRgatatvAbhAvAt kartRgatasyAhitAgnitvasya vyavahitaphalatvAt| nanu AdhAnasaMskRtAgniH kartuH phalamiti AdhAturavyavahitaphalAnvayo'stIti cet, na, evamupanayanasyApi tatsaMskRto mANavakaH phalamiti upaneturavyavahitaphalasattvAt kartrabhiprAyasUtreNaivAtmanepadasiddheH saMmAnanAdisUtravaiyarthyatAdavasthyApAtAt; tasmAt asAdhAraNasya kriyAphalasya kartRgatatvaM kartrabhiprAyaH ityasyArthaH ; evaM cAkartrabhiprAyaphalajanakakriyAyAmasAdhAraNasya phalasyAkartRgatatve'pi sAdhAraNaphalAntaralipsayA kartuH pravRttirupapadyate; asAdhAraNakriyAphalasya AcAryatvasya kartRgatatvAt tAdarthye svaritAdisUtreNaiva AtmanepadasiddheH saMmAnanAdisUtram akartrabhiprAyaphalArthamiti upanayanasya adhyayanAGgatvamapi tataH sidhyatIti - sarvamidam asthAnavijRmbhitam tathA hi yattAvat zaGkitam - kartrekagatatvaM kartrabhiprAyatvaM vivakSitamiti, tatra kiM zAbdikamaryAdayA yat pradhAnaphalamuddizya kriyArambhaH, tadiha kriyAphalaM gRhItvA iyamadhikavivakSAzaGkA, uta yathA kathaMcit kriyAjanyaphalamAtraM kriyAphalaM gRhItvA, yadi AdyaH pakSaH, tadA na anayA adhikavivakSayA prAguktadoSoddhAro labhyata iti vyarthaiva eSA zaGkA bhavet; upanayanasya adhyApanAGgatvapakSe tasya pradhAnaM phalamAcAryatvamiti tatphalasya kartrekagatatvAt | yadi zAbdikamaryAdAmatilaGghaya kriyAphalasya prAdhAnyamiha na vivakSitam, kiMtu kartrekagatatvameva, tathA vivakSAyAmapi yajanti yAjakAH pacanti pAcakAH ityAtmanepadaM na bhaviSyati, svAmigatasvargabhojanAderapi yAgapAkaphalatvena kriyAphalasya kartrekagatatvAbhAvAt iti kalpayitvA dvitIyaH pakSaH samAzrIyate, tadA yajate iti svAmiviSaye'pi AtmanepadaM na syAt, AtreyadAnatvigdakSiNAsadasyadakSiNAdividhimUlakAnAm AtreyAdigatahiraNyalAbhAdInAmapi yathAkathaMcit yAgakriyAphala[p.345]bhUtAnAM sattvena tatrApi kriyAphalasya kartrekagatatvAbhAvAt; krINAtidadAtyozca AtmanepadaM na syAt; krayaphalayoH kreyamUlyadravyalAbhayoH dAnaphalayoH sukRtadeyadravyalAbhayozca krayadAnakartrubhayagatatvAt, vikrayapratigrahaphalayoH AdeyamUlyadravyalAbhayoH krayadAnasApekSatvena tatphalatvasyApi sattvAt; tasmAt prAdhAnyavivakSA avazyamAzrayaNIyA iti na anayA adhikavivakSayA prAguktadoSoddhAro labhyate; yattu kartrekagatatvavivakSAyAM dUSaNamuktam tathA sati 'AdadhIta' ityatra kartrabhiprAyasUtreNa AtmanepadaM na syAditi, tadapi prAdhAnyavivakSAvazyaMbhAvasamarthanena nirastam; kAmazrutiprayuktasyAdhAnasya AdhAnArambhe prayojakasya 'agnihotraM juhuyAtsvargakAmaH' ityAdivAkyeSu kAmanAviSayatvena zrutasya phalasya kartrekagatatvAt, AdhAnasya svavidhi prayuktyaGgIkAre'pi agnisaMskArasya AdhAnakartRgatatvAcca; na hi tadgatatvaM tatsamavetatvam, kiMtu tAdarthyamityuktam; agnisaMskArasya ca yajamAnagatatvaM nirvivAdam; na ca tasya 'agnInAdadhIta' iti dvitIyAzrutyavagatamagnayarthatvamapi astIti vAcyam, dvitIyAzrutyA hi saMbhAreSu mathitAgninidhAnarUpAyA AdhAnakriyAyA nidheyAgnayarthatvamavagatam; na tu tajjanyasaMskArasya, tajjanyasaMskAraviziSTatvena AhavanIyAdizabdavAcyasya 'AhavanIye juhoti' ityAdivAkyAntarAvagatabhAvyupayogasyAgneH tadviziSTarUpeNa AdhAnaM pratyutpAdyakarmatvAGgIkAreNa tasyaiva tadarthatvAyogAt; tasmAt AdhAnakriyAjanyasaMskArasyApi tadviziSTAgneriva AhavanIyAdipadArthAnatirekAt AdhAtRmAtrArthataiva, na tu AhavanIyAdyarthatApi iti neyaM kartrekagatatvapakSe dUSaNabhaGgI yuktA; evaM tu tatra dUSaNaM vaktavyam tathA vivakSAyAM citrAdivAkyeSu AtmanepadaM na syAt, citreSTyAdiphalAnAM pazuhiraNyAdInAM krayapratigrahAdibhiH yajamAnena labhyAnAM vikrayadAnAdibhiH tena anyasAtkartuM zakyAnAM yajamAnArthatvasamayAtprAkpazcAcca anyArthatvasyApi sattvena tasminnapi samaye janmaprabhRti pitRdhanasvAmibhUtaputrAdyarthatvasyApi sattvena teSAM kartrekagatatvAbhAvAt; pacidhAtoH svAmiviSaye'pi AtmanepadaM na syAt, pacikriyAphalasya annasya antato bhikSumakSikAdyakartRsAdhAraNasyAvarjanIyatvAt iti; yattu 'AdadhIta' ityatra svaritAdisUtrApravRttiprasaGgadUSaNaparihArArthaM kartrekagatatvam ityanena kartratiriktacetanagatatvaniSedho vivakSita ityuktam, tadapi ajJAnavilasitam, AdhAnajanyasaMskArANAm AhavanIyAdyabhimAnidevatAgatatvAt; anyathA AdhAnakAle Ahitasya [p.346] AhavanIyAderacetanasyAgneH AdhAnaprayogasamAptyanantaraM parityAgena tadgatasya saMskArasya tannAzAnnAzo'vazyaMbhAvIti AhavanIyAdyanuvRttyabhAvaprasaGgAt; ajasrapakSe'pi AhavanIyAdizabdAnAmanekArthatvApatteH; pazau praNayanavizeSasaMskRtasyAhavanIyasya auttaravedikapraNayanAnantaraM gArhapatyatApattyA tasyAhavanIyazabdapravRttinimittatvAyogAcca; tasmAt AhavanIyAdyabhimAnidevatA eva tattadAyatanavizeSanihiteSu saMbhAreSu mathitAgninidhAnarUpeNa AdhAnena saMskriyante; devatAzca saMskRtA ajasrapakSe yajamAnazarIre agnidAnaparyantaM madhye agnisaMskAravicchedanimittopajanane tatparyantaM ca teSvagniSu saMnidadhate; agnInAmajasradhAraNAbhAve tattatkarmAvasAneSu AhavanIyAdidevatAH gArhapatyamanupravizanti; pazau ca auttaravedike praNayane kriyamANe tamAhavanIyo'nupravizati, tena prAgadhiSThitamagniM gArhapatyaH ityAdikamabhyupagantuM yuktam evaM satyeva 'nAntarAgni saMcarati yadi pUrvo'nugataH saMcarya pazcAhistarhi gataH' iti 'gArhapatyAjjvalantamAhavanIyamuddharati' iti ca kalpasUtrakAravacanaM sAmaJjasyamazrute, na hi saMskAramAtrasya pazcAdgamanaM saMbhavati, nApi AhavanIyasya gArhapatyAnupravezAbhAve gArhapatyAt tatra asataH AhavanIyasyoddharaNaM saMbhavati; yattu kartrekacetanagatatvapakSe 'Rtvijo vRNIte' ityatra AtmanepadaM na syAt iti dUSaNamuktam, tat atisthavIyaH, 'vRG saMbhaktau' iti znAvikaraNapaThitasya GitaH khalu vRNIte iti rUpam, na tu znuvikaraNapaThitasya jitaH 'vRJ varaNe' iti dhAtoH ; ataH atra svaritAdisUtrApravRttiprasaJjanam iSTaprasaJjanameva, 'anudAttaJita Atmanepadam' iti sUtraviSayatvAt; tasmAt ihApi pUrvavat yajate pacate iti svAmiviSaye'pi AtmanepadaM na syAditi dUSaNaM vAcyam; yattu avyavahitakriyAphalasya kartRgatatvaM vivakSitamiti pakSAntaramAzaGkitam, tat kathamAzaGkanIyatvena parahRdayamArUDhamiti na jAnImaH yo hi prAgAzaGkitaM pakSadvayam 'AdadhIta' ityatra AtmanepadaM na syAt, 'vRNIte' ityatrAtmanepadaM na syAditi ekaikena dUSaNenAdUduSat, tasya kathaM taddUSaNadvayamapi atra jAgarti ityabhimantumarhasya 'AdadhIta' ityatrAtmanepadaM na syAdityeva amuM pakSaM dUSayiSyatazca ayaM pakSaH zaGkanIyatvena hRdayamArohet; kiMca svaritAdisUtre kartrabhiprAyavizeSaNasya yajanti yAjakAH pacanti pAcakAH iti prasiddhaM pratyudAharaNam yatra yajanti ityatra kartRgAmi phalaM dakSiNAlAbharUpaM kratuprayogamadhya eva bhavat na kadApi vyavahitaphalaM [p.347] bhavati, kiMtu akartRgAmisvargAdikameva vyavahitaphalam, pacanti ityatra kartRgAmi phalaM bhUtilAbharUpam avyavasthayA prAk madhye pazcAdvA bhavat niyamena vyavahitaM na bhavati; ataH sautravizeSaNasya vyAvartanIyatvAbhimatavyAvartanAkSamatApAdakaM vizeSaNAntaraM vivakSitamiti azaGkanIyatvena kathaM mandasyApi hRdayamArohet; tasmAt neyaM pakSAntarAzaGkodbhAvanazailI paNDitAnAM hRdayaMgamA; yattu asminpakSe 'AdadhIta' ityatra AtmanepadaM na syAt tadavyavahitaphalasya agnisaMskArasya akartRgatatvAt iti dUSaNamuktam, tadapi tuccham, tasyApi pazuhiraNyAdirItyA kartRgatatvasamarthanAt; kiM ca agnisaMskArasya kartRgAmiphalApekSayA pUrvabhAvitvamapi asiddham, kartRgataM hi phalam AhitAgnitvamiti parAbhimatam, tat yadyapi sarvakratvadhikArArthatvAt dAnavizeSeSu pAtratApAdakatvAcca karturiSyamANaM phalaM bhavitumarhati, tathApi na tatpUrvabhAvyagnisaMskAraH, sa khalu sAGgasya kRtsnasyAdhAnaprayogasya samAptyanantaraM jAyate, na tu saMbhAreSu agninidhAnAnantarameva, udIcyAGgavaiyarthyApatteH AhitAgnitvamapi sAGgAdhAnaprayogasamAptyanantarameva jAyate, na tatra kazcidvizeSo'sti; api ca yasmin AdhAnaprayogasamAptyanantarakSaNe agnisaMskAro jAyate, tasminneva kartRsamavetAdhAnakriyAphalarUpamapUrvamapi jAyate; na ca AdhAnasya agnisaMskAramAtrArthatvAt apUrvajanakatvaM nAstIti zaGkanIyam, anAhitAgnitvasya upapAtakeSu parigaNanena agnayAdheyasya catvAriMzatpuruSasaMskAramadhye parigaNanena ca tasya puruSArthatAyA api sattvena tajjanyApUrvasya avazyAbhyupagantavyatvAt; guruNApi vivaraNaTIkAyAm agnayarthasyAdhAnasya niyogaviSayatvamapyasti ityuktatvAcca; na ca AdhAnasaMskArasya sAGgapradhAnAnantarabhAvitve'pi saMbhAreSvagninidhAnarUpapradhAnAnantarameva agnau svAyatanaprAptirUpAtizayo jAyata iti vAcyam, tadAnImeva kartari apUrvajananenAvizeSAt; yattu agnisaMskArasya vyavahitaphalasya kartRgatatvAbhAve'pi AdhAnasaMskRto'gniH avyavahitaM kartRgAmi phalamastIti zaGkitam, tat saMskArAt saMskRtasya kena vizeSeNa kartRgAmitvamastIti zaGkitam iti na vidmaH kartRgatatvaM kartRsamavetatvaM cet saMskRtasyApi asiddham, kartrarthatvaM cet saMskArasyApi aviziSTam; yattu dUSaNamuktam evamupanayanasyApi tatsaMskRto mANavakaH phalaM syAt ityAdi, tadapi uktarItyaiva nirastam, kartRgatatvaM kartRsamavetatvam ityabhyupagame mANavakasya phalatvAyogAt, kartrarthatvam ityabhyupagame upanayanasya phalatvAsaMbhave'pi [p.348] upanItasyAvyavahitaphalatvaM saMbhavatItyuktyayogAt; yattu svayaM kartrabhiprAyasUtrArthanirdhAraNaM kRtam asAdhAraNakriyAphalasya kartRgatatvaM kartrabhiprAyasUtrArthaH iti; tadapi tuccham, yadi tasyA eva kriyAyA yatphalaM na kriyAntarApekSam, tadasAdhAraNamiti vivakSitam, tadA 'navasaptadazenAtirAtreNa prajAkAmo yajeta' iti AtmanepadaM na syAt, prajotpAdanArthaM laukikakriyAyA api apekSaNAt; yadi yadekamAtragatam tadasAdhAraNam iti vivakSitam, tadA citrAdivAkyeSu AtmanepadaM na syAt, pazvAdiphalAnAM putrAdisAdhAraNyAt tasmAt zAbdikamaryAdAnusAreNa pradhAnasya kriyAphalasya kartrarthatvameva kartrabhiprAyasUtrArthaH ityasmadunnIta evAtra AkSepasamAdhAnaprakAro draSTavyaH; tasmAt upanayanasya adhyApanAGgatvAbhAvAt tatprayuktimUlaM vihitAdhyayanasya adhyApanavidhiprayuktatvaM na bhavati iti na maddheturvizeSaNAsiddhaH, tvaddhetuzca asiddha iti ||
atha dvAdazI kakSyA-- upanayanasya adhyApanAGgatve madupanyastAni pramANAni adhyayanAGgatvaviSayatayA anyathAsiddhAnyupapAdayatA tvayA vihitAdhyayanasya adhyApanavidhiprayuktatvameva upapAditamiti tvayaiva tvaddhetorvizeSaNAsiddhiH maddhetorasiddhyuddhArazca ityubhayamapi kRtam; tathA hi adhyayanAGgasyopanayanasya adhyApanasya ca kartRtvAdhiSThAtRpuruSaikyaM katraikyaM vA vAkyArtha iti yatpakSadvayamuktam, tatra adhyayananirvartyasyAdhyApanasya adhyApayitRpuruSakartRkopanayanasAdhyatvena upasthitaM yadvihitAdhyayanaM tannirvartakatvenAnvayaM na bhajate, vRtteH prasakteH 'tamadhyApayIta' ityatra takSakauzalanyAyena upanayanasya prayojyAnvayaM vadatA tvayA upanayanAGgakaM vihitamadhyayanam adhyApanavidhiprayojyam iti spaSTamevoktam; tathA adhyayanAGge upanayane zrutasya AcAryatvasya tadaGgakavihitAdhyayanadvArA adhyApanasaMbandhena AtmanepadazrutimupapAdayatApi tvayA vihitAdhyayanasya adhyApanavidhiprayuktatvaM spaSTamevAGgIkRtam ata upanayanasya adhyApanAGgatvanirAkaraNena mama na kAcitkSatiH, tatprasAdhanIyasya pradhAnArthasya adhyayanAGgatvaM vyavasthApayatA tvayaiva prasAdhitatvAt - iti || atha trayodazI-- [p.349] upanayanasyAdhyApanAGgatve tvadupanyastAni pramANAni mayA tasya vihitAdhyayanAGgatvaviSayatayA na nItAni, kiMtu adhyayanasAmAnyadvArakatvamAtraviSayatayA; adhyayanasAmAnyadvArakatvaM ca laukikAdhyayananivezanenApyupapadyate; vihitAdhyayanasyaiva upanayanena saMbandhaH klRpta iti cet, na, laukikAdhyayanasyApi tatsaMbandhasya klRptatvAt; na hi laukikamadhyayanam AcAryasamIpaprAptiM vinA kartuM zakyam; na vA ktvAzrutyAdikaM vihitAdhyayananivezanamantareNa nopapadyate; athApi kathaMcit vihitAdhyayanaM dvAratvena nivezyata iti pratyAzA kriyeta, yadi virodho na syAt, asti tu nityAnityasaMyogavirodhaH adhyayanaM hi nityam, tadaGgasya upanayanasya nityatvAt, 'ata UrdhvaM trayo'pyete yathAkAlamasaMskRtAH | sAvitrIpatitA vrAtyA bhavantyAryavigarhitAH' iti smaraNAt 'yo'nadhItya dvijo vedAnanyatra kurute zramam | sa jIvanneva zUdratvamAzu gacchati sAnvayaH' iti adhyayanAbhAve doSasmaraNAcca | adhyApanavidhistu kAmya ityaGgIkriyate; evaM ca yatra grAme janapade vA dhanavattvAdihetubhiH AcAryatvakAmo na syAt, san vA kazcit upanIya adhyApayituM pravRttaH pravrajanAdihetubhiH nivRttAdhyApanasaMbandhaH syAt, tadA tadgrAmajanapadavAsinAmapi adhyayanasya tenAcAryeNa upanItAnAm ekadezamadhyApitAnAm ekadezAntarAdhyayanasya ca kathaM nivRttiH syAt ? api ca adhyayanaM yAvadvedasamApti AcAryAdhInena smRtyuktazuzrUSAdiniyamaiH tadupAsanApareNa ca satA yathA taduccAraNaM svaravarNAdibhraMzarAhityena kartavyamatiklezasAdhyam; asminkathamanyapuruSagAmyAcAryatvasiddhyarthamanyaH pravarteta, kathaM ca tasmai dakSiNAM dadyAt; yadyucyeta adhyApayitaiva svasyAcAryatvasiddhyartham adhyayanena mANavakasya arthajJAnAdiphalaM pradarzya taM pravartayediti, tathA sati tasya svAdhikAra eva pravartakaH syAt, na adhyApanavidhiH ; na ca adhyApakena svasyAdhyApanaphalasiddhyarthaM mANavakaH tadadhikArapradarzanena adhyayane pravartata iti etAvatA tasya adhyApanavidhiprayuktatvasiddhiH, dravyArjanapravRttairyAjakaiH niSAdaH svAdhikAraM pradarzya sthapatISTau pravartata iti sthapatISTerapi dravyArjanavidhiprayuktatvaprasaGgAt; api ca dravyArjanopAyatvena pratigrahAdivat prApte adhyApane vidhyasaMbhavAdeva adhyApanavidhiprayuktamadhyayanamiti nirarthakaM vacaH; na [p.350] ca AcAryatvArthatvena vidhiH zaGkanIyaH, tasya apuruSArthatvAt; na ca pUjAdakSiNAdAnAdihetutvena tasya puruSArthatA, mAtRpitRmAtulapitRvyAdInAM yaunasaMbandheneva AcAryasya maukhasaMbandhena pUjyatvopapattau tadarthamAcAryatvanAmakAlaukikAtizayakalpanAyogAt; na hi tatra anyatpramANamasti, na ca 'tamAcAryaM pracakSate' iti smRtiH tatra pramANam, tasya 'AcAryAdhIno bhava' ityAdizrutismRtidRSTAcAryazabdArthakathanamAtraparatvopapatteH; dRzyate hi 'brAhmaNaM pAtre na ca parIkSeta' ityAdizrutismRtidRSTapAtrazabdArthanirNayArthA 'na vidyayA kevalayA' ityAdismRtiH; na ca AcAryakaraNaviziSTe nayatyarthe AtmanepadavidhAyinI vyAkaraNasmRtiH tatra pramANam, AcAryakaraNaM hi AcAryasya kriyA upanayanAGgabhUtAcAryAnuSTheyavaidikakarmarUpA, tadviziSTe samIpaprApaNe Atmanepadam, na tu samIpaprApaNarUpadhAtvarthamAtre ityetadarthatvAt tasyAH smRteH; tasmAt adhyayanasya vidhyantaraprayojyatvAyogAt na maddheturvizeSaNAsiddhaH, tvaddhetuzcAsiddhaH iti svavidhiprayuktamevAdhyayanamiti siddham || iti adhyayanasya svavidhiprayuktatvasamarthanavAdaH ||21||

[p.350] bhasmoddhUlanatripuNDrayoH sakalabrahmavidyAGgatvasamarthanavAdaH || yathA upanayanam adhyayanavidhyekavAkyatApannavidhivihitatvena adhyayanAGgaM sat adhikArApAdakatvena sarvakarmArthamapi, evaM bhasmoddhUlanaM pAzupatavidyAprakaraNAmnAnena tadaGgaM sat cittazuddhyApAdakatvena sakalabrahmavidyAGgamapi - iti samarthanAya kathAntaraM prastUyate - viSNubhAgavatA manyante 'agnirityAdinA bhasma gRhItvA vimRjyAGgAni saMspRzet | vratametat pAzupataM pazupAzavimokSAya' iti atharvazirasi pAzupatavidyAprakaraNe samAmnAtaM bhasmoddhUlanaM prakaraNavazAt tanmAtrAGgam, na tu sakalabrahmavidyAGgam, na hi 'pUrvo'tithibhyo'znIyAt' iti vaizvAnaravidyAprakaraNe samAmnAtam atithiprAgbhojanaM sakalabrahmavidyAGgam iti | saiddhAntikI maryAdA tu sakalabrahmavidyApekSitacittazuddhirUpapApakSayopakArajanakaM bhasmoddhUlanaM sakalabrahmavidyAGgam, [p.351] tasya hi jAbAlazrutau - 'pApaM nAzayate kRtsnamapi janmazatArjitam' ityAdinA pApakSayopakArahetutvamavadhRtamiti || tatreyaM saiddhAntikI prathamA kakSyA 'agniH' ityAdimantravidheyaM bhasmoddhUlanaM sakalabrahmavidyAGgam, vidyAGgatve sati sakalabrahmavidyApekSitopakArajanakatvAt, zamadamAdivat | 'madyaM pItvA gurudArAMzca gatvA steyaM kRtvA brahmahatyAM ca kRtvA | bhasmacchanno bhasmazayyAzayAno rudrAdhyAyI mucyate sarvapApaiH' iti zAtAtapasmRtau prAyazcittaprakaraNe svatantraprAyazcittatayA vihite rudrajape tadaGge bhasmoddhUlanAdau ca vidyApekSitopakArajanake vyabhicAravAraNArthaM vidyAGgatve satIti vizeSaNam ; atithiprAgbhojanAdau vyabhicAravAraNArthaM vizeSyam; na ca udAhRtajAbAlazruteH svatantrabhasmoddhUlanaviSayatvasaMbhavAt vizeSaNAsiddhiriti zaGkanIyam; 'agniriti bhasma vAyuriti bhasma' ityAdibhasmoddhUlanamantrANAmatharvazirasyAmnAtAnAM jAbAlazrutau bhasmoddhUlanArthatayA samAmnAnena pAzupatavidyAGgasyaiva bhasmoddhUlanasya pApakSayadvArakatvaM jAbAlazrutAvucyata iti mantrapratyabhijJayA avagamyamAnatvAt iti ||
atha dvitIyA kakSyA prakaraNaniyantritaM bhasmoddhUlanaM prakRtivikRtibhAvAdikamantareNa apekSAmAtrAt na pAriplavaM bhavitumarhati; na hi sA upadezAtidezapramANeSu antarbhavati; na ca pAzupatavidyA sakalavidyAprakRtiriti vaktuM zakyam, tasyAM vidyAGgavidyAsahakArikarmagaticintanAdInAmanAmnAnAt; kRtsnavidhAnena hi prakRtirbhavati; na ceha pramANAntaraM vAsti; tasmAt aprayojakaM tvadanumAnamiti bhasmoddhUlanaM na sakalabrahmavidyAGgam, vidyAvizeSaprakaraNaniyantritatvAt, atithiprAgbhojanavat iti || atha tRtIyA-- [p.352] tayaiva khalu avahananasya yavAdisAdhAraNyam; anyathA vrIhyuddezena vihitasya tasya kuto yavasAdhAraNyaM syAt; tathaiva ca vidyAvizeSaprakaraNAmnAtAnAM yajJadAnatapaHprabhRtInAM zamadamAdInAmarcirAdigatiparvaNAm anyeSAM caitAdRzAnAM sakalabrahmavidyAsAdhAraNyam | tasmAt apekSAsAmarthyaM tAvat ihaikaM pramANaM bhavatyeva | tathA 'vratametatpAzupataM pazupAzavimokSAya' iti zrutirapi tatra pramANam tatra hi vimokSazabdena vimuktisAdhanaM vidyAmAtramucyate; yathA 'etAvadare khalvamRtatvam' ityatra amRtatvazabdena amRtatvasAdhanamucyate; na hi vidyaikasAdhyAyAM vimuktau bhasmoddhUlanasya sAkSAtsAdhanatvaM saMbhavati | tathA 'teSAmevaitAM brahmavidyAM vadeta zirovrataM vidhivadyaistu cIrNam' iti muNDakavAkyamapi tatra pramANam - tatra hi zirovratazabdena atharvazirasyuktaM pAzupatavrataM pratyabhijJApyate | tasmAt nAprayojakaM madanumAnam | tvadanumAne prakaraNaniyantritatvam kiM prakaraNAmnAnamAtram, uta prakaraNividyAvizeSamAtravazIkRtatvam ? Adye heturaprayojakaH, AdhAnaprakaraNAmnAtAnAM yajJapAtrANAm 'vAraNo yajJAvacaraH vaikaMkato yajJAvacaraH' iti vAkyavazena yajJAGgatvavat zrutiliGgavAkyaiH bhasmoddhUlanasya sakalavidyAGgatvopapatteH, vidyAGgavidyAsahakArikarmazamadamAdiSu vyabhicArI ca; dvitIye heturasiddhaH, prakaraNAt balavadbhiH zrutiliGgavAkyaiH sarvavidyAGgatvApAdanAt - iti ||
atha caturthI kakSyA-- ekatra nibandhanam anyatrApi apekSAmAtreNa pAriplavaM bhavati cet - codakapramANavicAra eva vyarthaH syAt; ataH na tanmAtraM pramANam, na ca avahananasya apekSAmAtrAt yavasAdhAraNyam; kiMtu dvitIyAzrutyavagatam vrIhINAmavahananasAdhyatvaM prAksiddhavrIhitvAkAreNa na saMbhavatIti tatsAdhyatAvacchedakatvena apUrvIyatvaM lakSaNIyam; kiMca avahananavAkye vrIhipadasya laukikApUrvIyasAdhAraNyena vrIhimAtraparatve bhUtabhAviviniyogarahiteSu laukikavrIhiSu avahananasyArthakarmatvam, apUrvIyavrIhiSu viniyuktasaMskAratayA guNakarmatvamiti vidhivairUpyApattiH; laukikavrIhimAtraparatve dvitIyAzrutyavagatadravyaprAdhAnyabhaGgApattiriti, tatparihArArthatvenApi apUrvIyatvaM lakSaNIyam, tatra vrIhitvasyApi vivakSAyAM viziSTAnuvAdadoSaH syAditi pravRtti[p.353]nimittaikyAt yavasAdhAraNyam; yajJAdInAM zamAdInAM ca 'vividiSanti yajJena' ityAdau tRtIyAzrutyA 'samAhito bhUtvA AtmanyevAtmAnaM pazyet' ityatra ktvAzrutyA ca sakalavidyAsAdhAraNyam; tatra hi 'vividiSanti' iti 'pazyet' iti ca vidyAmAtramupAttam; arcirAdigatInAM sakalavidyAsAdhAraNyamapi 'tadya itthaM viduH ye ceme'raNye zraddhA tapa ityupAsate' ityAdivAkyena; zrutivAkye ca prakaraNAt balIyasI; tasmAt apekSAmAtreNa na kvApi viniyogaH; 'pazupAzavimokSAya' ityatra vimokSazabdo na zrutyA sarvasAdhAraNaH, tasya muktivAcitvAt; dvAratvena vidyA nivezyate cet, prakaraNAvirodhena prakRtA pAzupatavidyaiva tathA nivezayitumucitA; 'teSAmevaitAm' iti vAkyaM zirasi aGgAradhAraNarUpamuNDakAdhyayanAGgabhUtavratamAcaSTe; na tu atharvazirasi uktaM pAzupatavratam, tatra AtharvaNikAnAM zirovratazabdaprasiddheH ; tasmAt aprayojakaM tvadanumAnam; maddhetoH dvitIya evArthaH, zrutiliGgavAkyavirodhAbhAvena bhasmoddhUlanasya prakaraNividyAvizeSavazIkRtatvopapatteH - iti || atha paJcamI apekSAsAmarthyaM tAvat pramANaM bhavatyeva, tadvazAdevAkRtsnavidhAnAnAm anyato dharmagrahaNam; tadapekSAmUlameva ca codakavAkyakalpanam; prakRtivizeSavyavasthArthastu ASTamiko vizeSAtidezavicAraH; nirapekSasyApi anyadIyadharmagrahaNe, somasyApi aiSTikadharmagrahaNaM prasajyeta; avahananasya yavasAdhAraNyamapi apekSayaiva, na apUrvIyatvalakSaNayA, lakSaNAhetvoH tvadupanyastayoH ayuktatvAt; tathA hi Adyo hetustAvat ayuktaH, sAdhyatAvacchedakAnupasthitAvapi svargajyotiSTomayoriva vrIhyavahananayoH zabdena sAdhyasAdhanabhAvabodhasaMbhavAt; dvitIyo'pyayuktaH, avahananavAkyajanyazAbdabodhasya vrIhitvaprakArakatve'pi dvitIyAzrutinirdiSTavrIhiguNatayA vidhIyamAnamavahananaM bhUtabhAvyupayogarahitalaukikavrIhiviSayaM bhavituM na yogyamiti vidheyasAmarthyAt zAbdabodhAnantaram apUrvIyavrIhINAM viSayatvAvagatisaMbhavAt; 'yasyobhAvagnI anugatau abhinimrocedabhyudiyAdvA punarAdheyaM tasya prAyazcittiH' ityatra havirArtyadhikaraNanyAyena agnyubhayatvasyAvivakSAyAm agnyanugamamAtrasya zAbdabodhavelAyAM nimittatvAvagatAvapi nimitte vidhIyamAnamAdhAnaM sahitayorevAgnayorutpAdana[p.354]kSamam, na tu ekaikasyAgneriti vidheyasAmarthyAt ubhayAgnyanugamasyaiva nimittatvaparyavasAnAGgIkArAt; 'tapte payasi dadhyAnayati' ityatra zAbdabodhasya payastvamAtraprakArakatve'pi 'sA vaizvadevyAmikSA' iti tadanantaravAkye tasya payaso yAgAnvayavidhAne sati tatsAmarthyAt zAbdabodhAnantaraM tadapUrvasaMbandhinaH payaso viSayatvAvagatiriti avazyavaktavyatvAcca; yajJAdInAM zamAdInAM ca sAdhAraNyaM na zrutyA, 'tametaM vedAnuvacanena brAhmaNA vividiSanti yajJena dAnena tapasAnAzakena' iti zrutau tametaM vividiSanti - iti 'tasmAdevaMvicchAnto dAnta uparatastitikSuH samAhito bhUtvA AtmanyevAtmAnaM pazyet' iti zrutau ca evaMvit pazyet - iti ca prakRtavidyAvizeSasaMbandhabodhanAt; kiM tu apekSayaiva; arcirAdigaterapi apekSayaiva sarvasAdhAraNye siddhe 'ye ceme'raNye' iti vAkyaM paJcAgnividyAyAm arcirAdigatisadbhAve dRSTAntArtham jIvagatAgataviSayA na brahmavidyA iti divyasthAnaprAptiphalAbhAvena tasyA devayAnagatyabhAvaprasaktau tatsadbhAvavidhAnArtham 'tadya itthaM viduH' iti vacane pravartanIye tatra dRSTAntArthaM dIpakAlaMkAreNa brahmavidyA api sahopAttA; 'pazupAzavimokSAya' ityatra vimokSazabdaH zrutyaiva sarvavidyAsAdhAraNaH, tasya bhAvArthapratyayAntatayA muktAviva - karaNArthapratyayAntatayA vidyAyAmapi zaktisadbhAvAt; 'zirovrataM vidhivadyaistu cIrNam' iti vAkyam atharvazirovarNitapAzupatavrataviSayameva, saurapurANAdiSu tathA vyAkhyAtatvAt, upabRMhaNAnusAreNa zrutyarthasya grAhyatvAt; adhyApakaprasiddhitaH purANaprasiddheH balavattvAt; tasmAt nAprayojakaM madanumAnam; tvadanumAne ca hetvasiddhiH - iti || atha SaSThI-- apekSAsAmarthyaM codakakalpanayaiva anyatra anyadharmaprApakam, na tu sAkSAt, iSTipazusomAdiSu tathA darzanAt; na ceha pAzupatavidyA vidyAntarANAM prakRtiriti vaktuM zakyam, kRtsnavidhAnatvAvizeSeNa vaiparItyasyApi vaktuM zakyatvAt; ata evAvahananasya yavasAdhAraNyaM na apekSAmAtreNa, tathA sati codakakalpanAvazyaMbhAvena yavAnAM vrIhivikAratvApattyA 'vrIhINAM medha sumanasyamAnaH' iti mantrasya yavapuroDAzeSu UhaprasaGgAt; ataH avahananasya yavasAdhAraNyamicchatA tadvAkyagatasya [p.355] vrIhipadasya apUrvIyatvalakSaNaiva upAsanIyA; tathA sati kAryatAvacchedakapratItyA kAryakAraNabhAvabodhanam apUrvaviniyuktattvapratItyA viniyuktasaMskArabodhanaM ca nirAkAGkSaM paryavasyati; upapAdakasApekSArthapratipAdakatvakalpanaM hi ananyagatikam; 'teSAmeva' iti zrutau prakRtavAcini 'etAm' iti pade satyapi sakalabrahmavidyAsAdhAraNyamabhyupagacchataH tava mate tathaiva yajJAdInAM, zamAdInAM ca tat saMbhavatIti teSAM zrutyA sarvasAdhAraNyAbhAvoktirapi ayuktA; 'ye ceme'raNye' iti vAkyam arcirAdigateH sarvAsu brahmavidyAsu prAptyarthameva, na tu apekSAvazAt prAptasya anyatra dRSTAntIkaraNArtham, tasyAzcodakakalpanAmantareNa na anyatra anyadIyadharmaprApakatvam ityuktatvAt; ata eva sUtrakAraiH 'aniyamaH sarveSAm' ityadhikaraNe arcirAdigateH sarvavidyAsAdhAraNye 'ye ceme'raNye' iti zabda eva svatantrapramANatayA upanyastaH ; 'pazupAzavimokSAya' ityatra vimokSazabdo vimuktivAcI ityeva yuktam, tAdarthyacaturthyA mukhyapuruSArthatvasya autsargikatvAt; 'zirovrataM vidhivadyaistu cIrNam' ityatra adhyApakaprasiddham adhyayanAGga zirasi aGgAradhAraNameva grAhyam, 'svAdhyAyasya tathAtvena' iti sUtre tathA vyAkhyAtatvena nyAyanirNAyakasUtrAnugRhItatayA arthavAdaprAyapurANaprasiddhito balavattvAt; tasmAt tvadanumAnamaprayojakam; madanumAne ca na hetvasiddhiriti || atha saptamI apekSAsAmarthyasya anyatra anyadIyadharmaprApakatvaM codakakalpanayaiveti na niyamaH, 'AgneyyA AgnIdhramupatiSThate' iti vihitasya AgnIdhropasthAnasya AgneyargapekSAyAH sAkSAdeva 'agna AyAhi vItaye' ityAdistotrAGgabhUtAgneyarvyApakatvAbhyupagamAt; 'triNavenaujaskAmam' trayastriMzena pratiSThAkAmam' ityAdividhivihiteSu vivRddhastomakeSu kratuSu yatra Agamena saMkhyApUraNaM kAryaM tatra anyatra nibaddhAnAmRcAm apekSAsAmarthyAdeva Agamasya abhyupagatatvAcca; ato'vahananasya apekSayaiva yavasAdhAraNyamupapadyate iti tadarthaM na apUrvIyatvalakSaNA vAcyA; nApi kAryatAvacchedakaviniyuktatvapratItyartham, zrutopapattyarthamupapAdakAnveSaNasyaiva sarvatra dRSTatvena tadarthaM zrutapadalakSaNAyAH kvacidapi abhAvAt; apUrvIyatvalakSaNAyAm avahananasya [p.356] juhvAdiSvapi prasaktau teSAmavahananAnapekSAM yavAnAM tadapekSAM ca Alocya taNDuladvArakatvasya zAbdabodhAnantaraM nivezanIyatayA lakSaNApakSe'pi apekSAsAmarthyagaveSaNAvazyaMbhAvAcca; etena vrIhidharmANAmapekSayaiva yaveSu prAptau yavAnAM vrIhivikAratvApattiriti zaGkApi dUranirastA, avahananAdivAkyajanyavrIhitvaprakArazAbdabodhAnantaraM dvitIyAzrutinirdiSTavrIhiprAdhAnyanirvAhAya taNDuladvArA darzapUrNamAsApUrvopayoginAM vrIhINAmavahananAdisaMskAryatvam ityavagatikAle vrIhizAstrasamakAlapravRttikena 'yavairyajeta' iti zAstreNAvagatayAgIyapuroDAzasAdhanabhAvAnAM yavAnAmapi vituSIbhAvavyapekSAmAlocya saMskAravAkyazrutamapi vrIhitvaM prayojakakoTau na niviSTam, kiMtu uktarUpApUrvopayogitvamAtram ityavagatyudayena tadvazAdavahananAdisaMskArANAM yavasAdhAraNyaprAptyA yavAnAM vrIhivikAratvAprasaGgAt, juhvAdivyAvartakasya taNDuladvArakatvasya viziSTAnuvAdadoSApattyA lakSaNIyakoTAvanivezitasya nivezanArthaM zAbdabodhAnantaraM tatpariSkArakabodhAntarodayasya avazyAbhyupagantavyatvAt; yajJAdInAM zamAdInAM ca sarvavidyAsAdhAraNyam apekSayaiva, na zrutyA, tatra vidyAviSayazruteH prakRtavidyAviSayaparAmarzizrutyA niyantritatvAt; 'teSAmevaitAm' iti zrutirapi uktanyAyena zabdapravRttyA prakRtavidyAmAtraviSayA; hetuvazAttu arthataH sakalavidyAsAdhAraNI; tathA hi 'teSAmevaitAm' ityatra vidyAzabdasya 'brahma devAnAM prathamaH saMbabhUva vizvasya kartA bhuvanasya goptA sa brahmavidyAM sarvavidyApratiSThAmatharvAya jyeSThaputrAya prAha' iti upakrAntAkSaravidyArUpabrahmavidyAviSayatvam 'etAm' iti prakRtaparAmarzenaiva sidhyati; atastatra brahmavizeSaNamadhikaM hetusUcanArtham yasmAdeSA vidyA brahmavidyA, tasmAt cIrNazirovratAyaiva deyA; na tu acIrNazirovratAya iti; hetusAdhAraNye ca hetumati sarvatra vidheyamarthAt sidhyati; ata eva AtithyeSTiprakaraNe 'caturupabhRti gRhNAti' iti caturgrahaNAmnAnasya prakaraNyAtithyamAtraviSayatve'pi 'na hyatrAnUyAjAH' iti hetusAdhAraNyAt yatra yatra iSTau anUyAjAnAmabhAvaH, tatra sarvatrApyupabhRti caturgRhItameva, nASTagRhItam iti siddhiriSyate; 'zUrpeNa juhoti tena hyannaM kriyate' ityatra tu annasAdhanatvahetubalAt yadyadannasAdhanam tena sarveNApi hotavyam iti vyAptyAkSepe darvIpiTharAdInAmapi annakaraNatvena homasAdhanatvaprAptau zrutasya zUrpasya azrutairdarvIpiTharAdibhiH viSamaziSTavikalpaH syAditi tatparihArAya vyAptyanAkSepAt hetuvannigadamAtram, na tu heturityabhyupagamyate; 'brahmavidyAm' ityatra [p.357] tu 'na hyatrAnUyAjA ijyante' ityatreva vyAptyAkSepe bAdhakAbhAvAt saddheturiti tena sarvavidyAsAdhAraNyamApatat na nivArayituM zakyam; na ca iha yajJAdivAkye zamAdivAkye ca sarvavidyAsAdhAraNyApAdakaM hetuzravaNamastIti apekSAsAmarthyAdeva sarvavidyAsAdhAraNyamabhyupeyam; tathA 'AnandAdayaH pradhAnasya' ityadhikaraNe nirNItam vidyAvizeSazrutAnAmAnandAdiguNAnAm, 'akSaradhiyAm' ityadhikaraNe nirNItaM sthUlatvAdiniSedhAnAM ca sarvavidyAsAdhAraNyaM apekSAsAmarthyAdeva upapAdanIyam; evaM sarvavidyAnAmupAsanArUpatvamapi, videH upAsinA saha prayogasya saMvargavidyAdau kvacideva darzanena tAvanmAtramavalambya vidyAntareSu kevalavidizravaNasya upAsanArthatvakalpanAyogAt; na ca sAkSAtkArarUpaphalAnusAreNa AvRttisiddhiH, sAkSAtkArarUpaphalazravaNasyApi asArvatrikatvAt, 'tatastu taM pazyate niSkalaM dhyAyamAnaH' ityAdau kvacitkvacideva tasya zravaNAt; evam apekSAsAmarthyasya vidyAntarasAdhAraNyakalpakatvasya vyavasthitau arcirAdigaterapi tata eva sidhyatIti 'ye ceme'raNye' iti vAkyaM dRSTAntArthameva, 'aniyamaH sarveSAm' ityadhikaraNe dRSTAntIkaraNArtham apekSAsAmarthyasiddhArthAnuvAdakameva 'ye ceme'raNye' iti vAkyaM zabdapadamAcaSTe, na tu svatantrapramANabhUtam; yadvA tadadhikaraNam arcirAdigateH sakalabrahmavidyAsAdhAraNyanirNayArthaM na bhavati, apekSAsAmarthyAdeva tatsiddheH; apekSAsAmarthyasya 'AnandAdayaH pradhAnasya' ityAdiSu bahuSvadhikaraNeSu kSuNNatvAt; na ca arcirAdigateH sarvavidyAsAdhAraNye paJcAgnividyAyAmupakosalavidyAyAM dvirdviH pAThaH vyarthaH syAt ityadhikAzaGkAnirAkaraNArthaM tadadhikaraNamiti vAcyam, paJcAgnividyAyA abrahmavidyAtvena tasyAH dhUmAdimArga eveti zaGkAmapanetuM tasyAmacirAdimArgapratipattyartham 'ye ceme'raNye' iti brahmavidyAdRSTAntopanyAsena tatsthirIkaraNArtham 'vettha pathordevayAnasya pitRyANasya ca vyAvartanAH' iti pRSTadevayAnapitRyANavyAvartakapratipAdanArthaM ca arcirAdigativarNanam, upakosalavidyAyAm 'atha yadu caivAsmiJchavyaM kurvanti yadu ca na arciSamevAbhisaMbhavanti' iti upakosalavidyopAsakasya dahanAdizavakarmAbhAve'pi gArhapatyAdInAmagnInAm upakosalavidyAGgavidyAbhiH upAsitAnAmanugrahAt avilambenArcirlokaprAptirasti, na tu vidyAntaropAsakAnAmiva dahanAdizavakarmavaiguNye arcirlokaprAptau kiyAnvilambo'sti iti vizeSapratipAdanArtham 'etena pratipadyamAnA imaM mAnavamAvartaM nAvartante' ityarcirAdimArgeNa paraM brahma prAptAnAm anAvRttipratipAdanArthaM [p.358] ca tatsaMkIrtanamiti dviHpAThAvaiyarthyasya spaSTatvAt, tasmAt tadadhikaraNam arcirAdigateH sakalasaguNavidyAsAdhAraNyapratipAdanArthaM na bhavati, kiM tu kasyAMcidvidyAyAM tadabhAvapratipAdanArtham; nirguNavidyA hi dezAntaraprAptiphalakasaguNavidyAvat gatyapekSA na bhavati, 'na tasya prANA utkrAmanti atraiva samavanIyante' iti zruteH ; bhASyakArairapi etatsUtram 'kecidAhuH' ityAdinA dvitIyavyAkhyAnapradarzanena tathaiva yojitam; tasmAt apekSAsAmarthyAdeva arcirAdigateH sarvavidyAsAdhAraNyam; avazyaM ca tasyAmapi apekSAsAmarthyamupAsanIyam; paryaGkavidyAmnAtendravaruNaprajApatilokAdiparvopasaMhArArtham; 'pazupAzavimokSAya' ityatra vimokSazabdo vimuktikAraNavAcItyeva yuktam, vidyaikasAdhyAyA vimukteH bhasmoddhUlanakriyAphalatvAyogAt, phalatvayogye mukhyArthAntare vidyamAne tadayogyamarthAntaraM gRhItvA kathaMcit prayojyaprayojakabhAvamAtrAvalambaH phalatvavyapadeza iti tannirvahaNAyogAt | 'zirovrataM vidhivadyaistu cIrNam' ityatra zirovratamatharvaziraH prasiddhaM pAzupatavratameva, saurasaMhitAdiSu tathaiva upabRMhaNAt; tathA hi skAnde saurasaMhitAyAM paThyate 'idaM zirovrataM cIrNaM vidhivadyairdvijAtibhiH | teSAmeva parAM vidyAM vadedajJAnabAdhikAm | zirovratamidaM nAma zirasyAtharvaNazruteH | yaduktaM taddhi vAnyattattatpAzupatasaMhitAm | zAkhAbhedeSu nAmAni tasyAsya pravibhedataH | paThyate munizArdUla zAkhAsvekaM vRtaM hi tat | agnirityAdibhirmantraiH SaDbhiH zuddhena bhasmanA | sarvAGgoddhUlanaM kuryAcchirovratasamAhvayam' iti | brahmottarakhaNDe'pi 'ayamityAzramo dharmo yaiH samAcaritastathA | teSAmeva paraM jJAnaM saMsAracchedakAraNam | uddhUlanaM tripuNDraM ca mAyApAzanivRttaye | Amananti munizreSThA atharvazirasi sthitam | ato'pi pApavicchedalAbhAya jJAnasiddhaye | uddhUlanaM tripuNDraM ca mumukSuH sarvadAcaret iti | kathaM tarhi 'svAdhyAyasya tathAtvena samAcAre'dhikArAcca' iti sUtram ? tasya hi ayamarthaH svAdhyAyasya tathAtvena svAdhyAyaM pratyaGgatvena zirasi aGgAradhAraNarUpasya zirovratasya vidhiH, na tu vidyAM pratyaGgatvena, tataH samAcAre adhikArAt AtharvaNikAnAM vedavratopadezArthe samAcArAkhye granthe tathApratipAdanAt, 'naitadacIrNavrato'dhIte' ityagnimavAkye muNDakAdhyayanAGgatvasya spaSTamuktatvAcca iti | evaM ca atra brahmavidyAzabdasya brahmavidyApratipAdakamuNDakopaniSadvAcitvapradarzanAt 'sa brahmavidyAm' iti upaniSadupakramagato'pi brahmavidyAzabdaH tadartha ityuktaM bhavati; yuktaM caitat - 'sa brahmavidyAm' ityupa[p.359]kramAnantaram 'dve vidye veditavye iti ha sma yadbrahmavido vadanti parA caivAparA ca tatrAparA yadRgvedo yajurvedaH sAmavedo'tharvavedaH zikSA kalpo vyAkaraNaM niruktaM chando jyotiSamitihAsapurANanyAyamImAMsAdharmazAstrANIti | atha parA yayA tadakSaramadhigamyate' iti vidyAzabdasya vidyAsthAneSu prayuktatvAt; ucyate udAhRtaM saurasaMhitAdivacanajAtaM vidhAnArthaM pravRttaM nArthavAdatvazaGkAspadam, tat sUtrAdapi prabalam vedopabRhmaNArthatvAt vedArthanirNaye sUtrANAmapi upajIvyatvAcca | ata eva 'smaryate ca' ityAdIni sUtrANi | ataH 'svAdhyAyasya' iti sUtraM kRtvAcintArUpeNa netavyam; yadyatra zirovratapadam AtharvaNikamAtrAnuSTheyaM zirasi aGgAradhAraNameva AcakSIta, tadApi na doSaH, tasminpakSe samAcAragranthAnusAreNaiva tasya muNDakAdhyayanAGgatvasyaiva prApteH, tasya vidyAGgatvAprasakteH iti | santi hi etAdRzAnyapi siddhAntAnabhimatamarthaM kRtvAcintArUpeNa siddhAntatayA pradarzayanti; yathA tAvat jaiminIyasUtram 'api tu vAkyazeSatvAditaraparyudAsaH syAt pratiSedhe vikalpaH syAt' iti | idaM hi sUtram jyotiSTomaprakaraNAmnAtena 'dIkSito na dadAti na juhoti' iti vAkyena kratvarthapuruSArthAnAM sarveSAmapi homAnAM pratiSedhaH kriyate, athavA kratvartheSu pratyakSaziSTAnvihAya atidezaprAptAnAM puruSArthAnAM ca homAnAM pratiSedhaH kriyate iti pUrvapakSanirAkaraNArtham; anena pratiSedhapakSe, svasvavidhibhiH kratumadhye kartavyatvena, dIkSitapratiSedhAt akartavyatvena ca prAptAnAM homAnAM vikalpaH prasajyate; tatparihArAya 'dIkSito na juhoti' iti vAkyasya agnihotrAdivAkyazeSatvamabhyupagamya naJaH paryudAse vRttirAzrayaNIyA, tena adIkSito yAvajjIvamagnihotraM juhoti - ityAdirarthaH saMpadyate iti | atra vastutaH pratiSedha eva yuktaH, na paryudAsaH, vikalpAprasakteH, na khalu puruSArthahomaniSedhe vikalpaH prasajyate, homaH puruSArthaH homaniSedhaH kratvartha iti vidhiniSedhayoH viSayabhedAt; ubhayorapi kratvarthatvena viSayaikye hi SoDazigrahaNAgrahaNarItyA tayoranyatarasya parigrahe'pi kratoH sAdguNyAt vikalpo bhavet; iha homaniSedha eva kratvartha iti homapakSe kratorvaiguNyAt na vikalpaprasaktiH; na kratvarthasyApi homasya niSedhe vikalpaH prasajyate, niSedhasya puruSArthahomaviSayatayA cAritArthena kratvarthatayA prAptasya bAdhanAyogAt; tasmAt yadi pratiSedhAzrayaNe paryudAsAdhikaraNapUrvapakSarItyA vikalpaH prasajyate, tadA tatparihArAya 'dIkSito na juhoti' ityasya agnihotrAdi[p.360]vAkyazeSatvamaGgIkRtyApi vikalpaH pariharaNIya iti kRtvAcintArUpeNa idaM sUtraM pravRttam; kiMcitprakaraNAmnAtasya ekasya zabdasya pradezAntarAmnAtAnekavAkyazeSabhAvAdapi pApIyAn vikalpaH iti nyAyaM vyutpAdayitum iti netavyam tathaiva nItaM bhaTTapAdaiH | evaM vaiyAsikamapi 'prANAdayo vAkyazeSAt' iti sUtraM kRtvAcintAvalambanaM dRzyate 'yasminpaJcapaJcajanA AkAzazca pratiSThitaH' iti mantre paJca paJca iti zabdAbhyAM paJcaviMzatisaMkhyApratIteH 'paJcapaJcajanAH' ityanena paJcaviMzatiH sAMkhyoditAni tattvAnyucyante iti pUrvapakSe prApte 'na saMkhyopasaMgrahAdapi nAnAbhAvAdatirekAcca' iti sUtreNa siddhAntaH kRtaH | paJcaviMzatisaMkhyopasaMgrahAdapi sAMkhyatattvagrahaNapratyAzA na kAryA, nAnAbhAvAt AtmapratiSThitatvarUpasAMkhyatattvavailakSaNyAt AtmAkAzazabdAbhyAM paJcaviMzatisaMkhyAtirekAcca iti tadarthaH; apizabdena paJcaviMzatisaMkhyApratItirapi nAsti, paJcajanazabdasya 'paJcAnAM tvA paJcajanAnAm' ityAdAviva samastasya arthavizeSasaMjJAtvAt ityapi darzitam, tarhi ke te paJcajanasaMjJAsaMjJitA ityAkAGkSAyAm 'prANAdayaH' iti sUtram vAkyazeSe 'prANasya prANamuta cakSuSazcakSuH zrotrasya zrotramannasyAnnaM manaso ye mano viduH' ityasmin SaSThyantaprANAdizabdoditAH prANAdayo manaHparyantAH paJcajanazabdasaMjJinaH iti sUtrArthaH; evaM paJcajanazabdArthagrahaNaM na yuktam, prANAdiSu paJcasu ekasya paJcajanazabdapravRttinimittasyAbhAvAt; tatraivaM sUtramupaskartavyam - prANazabdena vAyuvikAratvAt tvagindriyaM lakSyate, annazabdena pRthivIvikAratvAt ghrANaM lakSyate, tenaiva adyate aneneti vyutpattyA rasanendriyamapi upacaryate, tena SaTsvapi anugatamindriyatvaM pravRttinimittam, annazabdoktatvena ghrANarasanayorekIkaraNAt paJcasaMkhyA yathA hemantazizirayorekIkaraNAt RtuSu 'dvAdaza mAsAH paJcartavaH' iti paJcasaMkhyA iti bhUyAniha klezaH; evaM tu vaktuM yuktam paJcajanAH prasiddharUDhyA manuSyAH, te catvAro varNAH niSAdazveti paJcasaMkhyA, anyeSAM manuSyANAM sattve'pi paJcAnAmeva yAgasaMbandhena abhyarhitatvAt 'janA yadagnimayajanta paJca' iti mantrAntaradarzanAcca teSAmiha grahaNamiti | 'yamAya hArmyamavapanpaJca mAnavAH' 'vaivasvato na tRpyati paJcabhirmAnavairyamaH' iti mantrayoH niSAdapaJcamAzcatvAro varNAH paJca mAnavAH iti bhaTTabhAskarAdibhirvyAkhyAtam; 'yaM tvA hotAraM manasA hi sA viduH trayo bhauvanAH paJca mAnavAH' ityAtharvaNamantre'pi 'paJca mAnavAH' ityetat mAdhavIyabhASye tathA vyAkhyAtam; [p.361] 'yasminpaJca paJcajanAH' ityetasminmantre 'paJcapaJcajanAH' ityetat bRhadAraNyakabhASye vyAkhyAtam - 'paJca paJcajanAH gandharvAH pitaro devAH asurA rakSAMsi, niSAdapaJcamA vA varNAH' iti; evaM prasiddhe'rthe satyapi yat aprasiddhAtikliSTArthagrahaNena 'prANAdayaH' iti sUtram, tat kRtvAcintayA yadi 'paJcapaJcajanAH' ityasya prasiddhamakliSTamarthAntaraM na saMbhAvyate, tathApi vAkyazeSe zruteSu kathaMcana kiMcitpravRttinimittaM paJcasaMkhyAM ca teSu saMpAdya teSu 'paJcapaJcajanA' ityetat vartitavyam, na tu sAMkhyatattveSu, vede kathaMcana vaidikArthagrahaNasyaiva yuktatvAditi nyAyaM vyutpAdayitum | evaM nyAyavyutpAdanArthaM kRtvAcintAyAH pravRttirityetat 'jyotiSaikeSAmasatyanne' iti uttarasUtreNa vyaktam - mAdhyaMdinazAkhAyAmiva kANvazAkhAyAm 'annasyAnnam' ityasya pAThAbhAvAt kathaM paJcasaMkhyAniSpattiriti - asyAM zaGkAyAmidaM sUtram kANvazAkhAyAm 'yasminpaJca' iti mantrAtprAk 'taM devA jyotiSAM jyotirAyurhopAsate'mRtam' ityAmnAyate, tatra SaSThyantopAttAni prakAzakatvAt paJca jJAnendriyANIti tAdarthyam 'paJcapaJcajanA' ityasya grAhyamiti sUtrArthaH; atra mAdhyaMdinazAkhoktAnnopasaMhAreNa annavattvAt 'asatyanne' ityetat kRtvAcintayeti spaSTameva; yadi kANvazAkhAyAmannaM na syAt, yadi ca jyotiHzabdoditeSu manaHSaSThendriyeSu paJcasaMkhyA na syAt, tadApi tAnyeva gRhItvA teSu dvayorekIkaraNena ekaprahANena vA paJcasaMkhyAmapi saMpAdya 'paJcajanAH' ityasya tAdarthyaM grAhyam, na tu vedAspRSTasAMkhyatattvArthatvamiti nyAyavyutpAdanameva tasyApi phalam | evameva kRtvAcintayA nyAyavyutpAdanArtham 'svAdhyAyasya' iti sUtramiti na tatra abhinivezaH kAryaH | yattu 'teSAmevaitAm' ityatra brahmavidyAzabdasya muNDakopaniSadrUpavidyAsthAnaparatvAt 'svAdhyAyasya' iti sUtraM tAttvikArthanirNayaparamevetyuktam, tadayuktam - 'sa brahmavidyAM sarvavidyApratiSThAmatharvAya jyeSThaputrAya prAha' iti muNDakopakramAnantaram atharvaprabhRtyaGgiraH paryantAM guruparamparAmuktvA 'zaunako ha vai mahAzAlo'GgirasaM vidhivadupasannaH papraccha kasminnu bhagavo vijJAte sarvamidaM vijJAtaM bhavati' iti zaunakasya praznamupanyasya 'tasmai sa hovAca' ityArambhAt 'tadetatsatyamRSiraGgirAH purovAca' ityantaM zaunakAGkSiraH saMvAdarUpAkhyAyikA muNDake pravartitA; tasyAmAkhyAyikAyAM yadvijJAne sarvavijJAnaM bhavati, tadadRzyatvAdiguNakaM brahma upadizya 'sa yo ha vai tatparamaM brahma veda brahmaiva bhavati nAsyAbrahmavitkule bhavati' ityAdinA tadupAsanAM tatphalaM ca upadizya, tadanantarameva 'tadetadRcAbhyuktam kriyA[p.362]vantaH zrotriyA brahmaniSThAH svayaM juhvata ekarSi zraddhayantaH | teSAmevaitAM brahmavidyAM vadeta zirovrataM vidhivadyaistu cIrNam' ityuktArthaviSayatvena avatArya aGgirasA zaunakaM prati zrAvitamantre zruto brahmavidyAzabdaH kathamakasmAdupAsanaM parityajya upaniSadaM gRhNIyAt; evaM ca upAsanArthatve'pi zirovratamAtharvaNikaprasiddhaM zirasi aGgAradhAraNameva upAsanAGgatvena vidhIyatAmityapi codyaM garbhasrAveNa gatam, kathaM hi bahRcatvena prasiddhAya zaunakAya aGgirAH svayamimAM vidyAmupadizya AtharvaNikamAtrAnuSTheyavratavata eva etadupadezaH kAryaH iti brUyAt; zaunakAGkSiraH saMvAdarUpAkhyAyikAsamAptyanantaram 'naitadacIrNavrato'dhIte' iti muNDakAdhyayanAGgatvena vidhIyamAnaM vrataM kAmaM samAcAragranthoktamAtharvaNikamAtrAnuSTheyaM vedavrataM bhavatu, naitAvatA idamapi tadbhavet | etena 'dve vidye veditavye' ityatra vidyAzabdasya vidyAsthAnaparatvadarzanAt brahmavidyAzabdasyApi tatparatvamityapi zaGkA nirastA, tatra RgvedAdizabdasamabhivyAhAreNa tathAtve'pi atra tadabhAvAt RgvedAdInAM brahmavidyApratipAdanarahitakarmabhAgarUpANAmaparavidyAtvokteH brahmavidyApratipAdakopaniSadbhAgasya upakramopasaMhAraikarUpyAvadhRtatAtparyaviSayabrahmavidyAprazaMsArthatvasya vaktavyatayA pradhAnArthaparayoH brahmavidyAzabdayoH vidyAsthAnalakSakatvakalpanAyogAt | tasmAt apekSAsAmarthyarUpasya liGgasya 'vimokSAya' iti caturthIzravaNasya 'brahmavidyAM vadeta' iti brahmagrahaNasUcitahetusAmarthyasya ca mUlapramANasya sattvAt nAprayojakaM madanumAnam, tvadanumAnahetuzca asiddha iti siddhaM bhasmoddhUlanasya sakalabrahmavidyAsAdhAraNAGgatvam; etena tripuNDrasyApi tadvyAkhyAtam, bRhajjAbAlazrutau tasya 'agniH' ityAdimantrasAdhyabhasmoddhUlanatulyabalatvena prapaJcitatvAt, kAlAgnirudropaniSadi 'tiryaktisro rekhAH prakurvIta vratametacchAMbhavaM sarvavedeSu vedavAdibhiruktaM tatsamAcarenmumukSurapunarbhavAya' iti tasya muktiphalakatvazravaNAt tatphalakatvasya ca vidyAdvArA samarthanIyatvAt tasya vidyAvizeSasya anukramaNAbhAvAn vinigamanAviraheNa sarvAsAM brahmavidyAnAM dvAratvena nivezanAt || iti bhasmoddhUlanatripuNDrayoH sakalabrahmavidyAGgatvasamarthanavAdaH ||22||

[p.362] ayathArthajJAnasamarthanavAdaH || [p.363] atha asAdhAraNasya jJAnaprAmANyasya sarvajJAnasAdhAraNyaM vadatAM mataM nirAkartuM kathAntaraM prastUyate teSAmitthaM matam - sarvaM jJAnaM yathArtham, zuktirajatAdijJAnamapi grahaNasmaraNAdyAtmakajJAnadvayarUpatayA yathArthameva iti; tAnprati idamanumAnam - rajatArthi pravartakaM zuktAvidamiti jJAnaM rajatatvaprakArakam, rajatArthipravartakajJAnatvAt rajate rajatajJAnavat - iti prathamA kakSyA--
atha dvitIyA kakSyA-- yadi pravartakatvaM pravRttijanakatvam, tadA jJAyamAneSTapurovartibhedAgrahaH pravartaka iti matapakSe hetvasiddhiH; yadi pravRttiprayojakatvam, tadA tvanmate rajatabhramajanakadharmijJAne vyabhicAraH, kAryAbhAvavyApyAbhAvapratiyogitvarUpasya prayojakatvasya kAraNatAvacchedaka iva kAraNakAraNe'pi sattvAt; agRhIteSTabhedapurovartijJAnaM pravartakamiti matpakSe tu anumAnamaprayojakam, tvanmate'pi bhramakAraNatvenAvazyakAt tata eva pravRttyupapattau bhramarUpaviziSTajJAnakalpane gauravAt; tasmAt uktajJAnaM rajatatvaprakArakaM na bhavati, zuktimAtravizeSyakatvAt iyaM zuktiH iti jJAnavat - iti ||
atha tRtIyA kakSyA-- [p.364] anyathAkhyAtibhiyA idamiti na rajatamiti jJAnadvayasya tvayA svIkartavyatvena purovartini iSTabhedAgrahasattvAt; tasmAt jJAnameva pravartakaM vAcyamiti na hetorasiddhiH, nApi aprayojakatvam; iSTapurovartijJAnarUpe kAraNe bhedAgrahasya avacchedakatayA niveze'pi pramAsthale tadasaMbhavena pravRttyabhAvApattyA nedaM rajatamiti bhramasthale tatsattvena pravRttyApattyA ca bhedAgrahaM vihAya iSTapurovartivaiziSTyaviSayaM jJAnaM kAraNamiti jJAnaviSayabhAvena vaiziSTyasya kAraNatAvacchedakakoTau nivezanIyatvAt; na caivaM sati bhramarUpaviziSTajJAnakalpanAgauravaM doSaH, prAmANikatvAt; tasmAt niravadyaM madanumAnam; tvadanumAnaM tu bhramasthale idamiti jJAnasya rajatatvaprakArakatvAbhAve tatra rajatArthipravRttiH na syAt; pravRttimAtre viziSTajJAnasya kAraNatAyAH klRptatvAt - iti pratikUlatarkaparAhatamiti || atha caturthI-- iSTapurovartibhedAgrahaH ityanena purovartini svatantropasthiteSTabhinnatvaprakArakajJAnaviSayatvAbhAva ucyate; tatraiva akhyAtivAdinAM bhedAgrahavyavahArasya nirUDhatvAt, tathA ca pramAsthale tatprasiddheH na apravRttiprasaGgaH; nApi nedaM rajatamiti bhramasthale pravRttiprasaGgaH tatra iSTasya svatantropasthityabhAvAt; ato bhedAgrahasya pravartakatvaM jJAnagatapravartakatAvacchedakatvaM ca upapadyate iti tvaddhetorasiddhiH aprayojakatvaM vA nApaiti, AvazyakAttata eva pravRttyupapatteH viziSTajJAnaM gauravAt na kalpyamiti viziSTajJAnasya pravartakatvaklRptyabhAvAt na madanumAnasya pratikUlatarkaparAhatiH iti || atha paJcamI-- evaM bhedAgrahavivakSAyAmapi nedaM rajatamiti bhramasthale pravRttiprasaGgadoSaH tadavasthaH, purovartigatasya viziSTAbhAvasya iSTabhinnatvaprakArakajJAnaviSayatvarUpavizeSyAbhAvamAtrAdapi sidhyataH svatantropasthitatvarUpavizeSaNasyApi abhAve sutarAM siddheH; tatra svatantropasthitatvaM nAstItyabhipretya khalu idaM vizeSaNaM dattam; ataH tadabhAvAdapi viziSTAbhAvaH kathaM na sidhyet; nedaM rajatamiti pramAsthale'pi [p.365] rajatArthipravRttiH syAditi doSazca atra adhikaH; tatra vizeSaNAbhAvAdviziSTAbhAvasiddheH; ataH tvadvivakSitaviziSTAbhAvasya pravartakatvaM jJAnagatapravartakatAvacchedakatvaM ca na saMbhavatIti viziSTajJAnasyApi ayaM doSa iti madanumAnasya na hetvasiddhiH, na vA aprayojakatvam; tvadanumAnasya pratikUlatarkaparAhatiH iti ||
atha SaSThI kakSyA-- bhedAgrahazabdoktaviziSTAbhAvapratiyogikoTau iSTavizeSaNatvena svAtantryaM na nivezitam, iSTabhinnatvaprakArakajJAnadazAyAm iSTasya bhedapratiyogitvena upasthitiviSayatvaniyamena tadabhAvaviziSTeSTavizeSitasya pratiyogino'prasiddhyA tadabhAvaprasiddhyApatteH; kiM tarhi ? svAtantryamapi pravRttau pRthakkAraNam; ataH tadabhAvAt nedaM rajatamiti pramAbhramasthalayoH pravRttiprasaGgAbhAvAt uktabhedAgrahasya pravartakatvasattvAt tvadanumAnasya asiddhiH aprayojakatvaM vA; madanumAnasya na pratikUlatarkaparAhatiriti ||
atha saptamI kakSyA-- yadi bhedapratiyogitvena anupasthitatvaM tat, tadA idaM rajataM ghaTaniSThabhedapratiyogi - iti jJAnAdapravRttiH; yadi purovartiniSThabhedapratiyogitvenAnupasthitatvam, tadA nedaM rajatamiti bhramasthale doSaH tadavasthaH nanu purovartyagRhItAsaMsargabhedapratiyogitvenAnupasthitatvaM svAtantryam ataH tadabhAvAt nedaM rajatamiti bhrameNa na pravRttiprasaGgaH, tatra purovartini rajatabhedAsaMsargAgrahasattvena tadvizeSitabhedapratiyogitvopasthiteH vidyamAnatayA tadabhAvAbhAvAt; nApi idaM rajataM ghaTaniSThabhedapratiyogi iti jJAnena apravRttiprasaGgaH, tatra purovartini rajatabhedAsaMsargarUparajatAbhedagrahasattvena tadabhAvavizeSitabhedapratiyogitvopasthityabhAvasattvAt iti cet, na; evamapi yatra idaM neti bhedaviziSTapurovartijJAnaM rajatasmaraNaM ca iti jJAnadvayarUpaH nedaM rajatamiti bhramaH, tatra rajatArthipravRttiprasaGgAt; na cedaM bhedajJAnaM na saMbhavatIti vAcyam, idamiti na rajatamiti jJAnadvayapakSe adhikaraNavizeSAnullekhina iva idaM neti [p.366] pratiyogivizeSAnullekhino'pi bhedajJAnasya saMbhavAt tasmAt svAtantryAniruktyA tatsahitabhedAgrahasya pravRttau kAraNatvaM tadavacchedakatvaM ca nopapadyata iti na maddhetorasiddhiH aprayojakatvaM vA, tvaddhetozca pratikUlatarkaparAhatiriti ||
atha aSTamI kakSyA-- svAtantryamiha purovartyagRhItAsaMsargabhedapratiyogitvAsaMsargAgrahAbhAvavattvaM vivakSitam, tathA ca idamiti na rajatamiti ca jJAnadvayAtmakasya nedaM rajatamiti bhramasyodaye purovartini rajatabhedasyAsaMsargAgraho'sti idaM rajatamiti bhramodaye rajatasyeva; tathA ca tatra purovartyagRhItAsaMsargabhedapratiyogitvena rajatasya grahaNAt tatpratiyogitvAsaMsargAgraho'stIti tadabhAvAbhAvAt na tatra rajatArthipravRttiprasaGgaH; nApi idaM neti rajatamiti ca jJAnadvayAtmakasya nedaM rajatamiti bhramasyodaye tatprasaGgaH, tatra purovartini gRhItasaMsargatvena agRhItAsaMsargo yo bhedaH tatpratiyogitvasyAsaMsarga iSTe yadyapi vartate, tathApi tasya nedaM rajatamiti bhramadazAyAm agrahasya sattvena tadabhAvAbhAvAt; idaM rajataM ghaTaniSThabhedapratiyogi iti jJAnodaye ca nApravRttiprasaGgaH, tatra rajate bhedapratiyogitvasaMsargagrahaNena tadasaMsargAgraharUpavizeSyasaMbhave'pi purovartini bhedAsaMsargarUpAbhedagrahaNena vizeSaNAbhAvAt tadviziSTasya bhedapratiyogitvAsaMsargAgrahasyAbhAvasattvAt; yatra tu idaM rajatam ityeva pratyayaH, tatrApi pravRttirupapadyate, tatra bhedapratiyogitveneva bhedApratiyogitvenApi agRhyamANarajate vizeSyasattve'pi purovartini bhedAsaMsargarUpasya rajatAbhedasya graho'stIti vizeSaNAbhAvena viziSTAbhAvasattvAt; tasmAt prAguktarItyA tvaddhetorasiddhiH maddhetozca na pratikUlatarkaparAhatiriti ||
atha navamI kakSyA-- naivamapi svAtantryanirvacanaM yujyate, yatra idaM rajatamiti bhramakAle ghaTo rajatabhinna ityapi smaraNam, tatra apravRttiprasaGgAt, tatra purovartini rajatabhedasyAsaMsargagrahe mithyAjJAnaprasaGgena tadasaMsargAgrahasya vizeSaNasya sattvena rajate [p.367] bhedapratiyogitvagrahakAle tadasaMsargAgrahasya vizeSyasya ca sattvena viziSTAbhAvAbhAvAt; arajatarajatayoH ime rajatArajate iti samUhAlambanabhramAt arajate rajatArthipravRttyabhAvaprasaGgAcca; tatra purovartini arajate rajatabhedAsaMsargagrahe mithyAjJAnaprasaGgena arajatarUpapurovartyagRhItAsaMsargabhedapratiyogitvena rajatasya gRhyamANatayA uktaviziSTAbhAvapratiyogino viziSTasya sattvAt; kiM ca yatra purovartini iSTabhinnatvajJAnaM nAsti, kiM tu tasmin iSTavyAvartakazuktitvAdijJAnam iSTe purovartibhinnatvajJAnam purovartiniSThadharmasAmAnye iSTatAvacchedakabhinnatvajJAnam iSTatAvacchedake purovartiniSThadharmabhinnatvajJAnam anyadvA etAdRzaM pravRttivirodhijJAnamasti tatrApi pravRttiprasaGgaH; na ca yAvanto bhedakagrahAH pravRttivirodhinaH tadabhAvakUTasahitam iSTapurovartijJAnaM pravartakamastviti vAcyam, etadapekSayA iSTapurovartijJAne tadubhayavaiziSTyasya ekasya bhAvarUpasya idaM rajatatvena jAnAmi iti sarvAnubhavasiddhajJAnavittivedyatayA zIghropasthitikasya pravRttikAraNatAvacchedakatvakalpane lAghavAt | tadanusAreNa bhramasthale pravRttyarthaM pazcAt viziSTajJAnakalpane ca phalamukhagauravasyAdoSatvAt; na ca yasya bhramasthale jJAnapravRttyoH kAryakAraNabhAvagrahaH, tasya tanmate vaiziSTyasya jJAnavittivedyatvAbhAvAt uktalAghavaM sArvatrikamiti vAcyam, tanmate'pi rajatatvavaiziSTyaviSayAyA rajatasmaraNaviSayavitteH sattvAt; tenApi hi idaM rajatatvena jAnAmIti lokasiddhA vittirnApahnotuM zakyA; kiMtu idaM rajatamiti jJAnAdagRhItabhedajJAnadvayarUpatayA upapAdanIyatvAt tvadvivakSitabhedAgrahasahitam iSTapurovartijJAnaM mama kAraNatvena saMpratipannamiti, tadapyasiddham, mama mate iSTapurovartibhedAgrahAdikUTo mithyAjJAnakAraNam, tvanmate purovartyagRhItAsaMsargetyAdi; [...] kevaladravyaviSayasya cAkSuSajJAnasyAnudayAt tadIyazuklarUpasya ca tadAnImasphuraNAt; nApi AropitasmaraNam nadIjalAdiSu nIlaM jalamiti niyamena nailyaviziSTatayaiva jAyamAne jJAne niyataM saMbhavati; na cAyamanubhUyamAnAgapaH atidhavalAsu sikatAsu pravahati nadIjale nIladravyasaMnidhAnAbhAvAt; na ca avinAbhUtopaSTambhakakalpanaM yuktam; tasyaiva nadIjalasya vivati vikSiptasya zauklyadarzanAt; tasmAt madanumAnasya nAsiddhiH aprayojakatvaM vA, tvadanumAnasya tu pratikUlatarkaparAhatiriti siddhaM zuktirajatAdijJAnamayathArthamiti || iti ayathArthajJAnasamarthanavAdaH ||23||

[p.368] svapnAyathArthatvavAdaH || zuktirajatajJAnavat svapnajJAnamapi ayathArthamiti samarthanArthaM kathAntaraM prastUyate - svApnaM vahnijalAdijJAnaM vahnitvAdimati vahnitvAdiprakArakam, dAhapipAsopazamAdisamarthaviSayapravRttijanakatve sati vahnitvAdiprakArakatvAt, bhUtajAgradbodhavat - iti prathamA kakSyA ||
atha dvitIyA kakSyA-- asiddho'yaM hetuH, na ca svApnena vahnijalAdinA svapne dAhapipAsopazamAdi dRzyate iti vAcyam, taddarzanasyAroparUpatvAt svapne svazarIrasya dagdhatvadarzane'pi prabodhAnantaraM tasya yathApUrvamavasthAnAt, svApArambhe samyagapAM tRptasya pipAsAyA evAnudayAt, pipAsitasyaiva zayitasya prabodhanAnantaraM pipAsAnuvRttidarzanAcca, argalitApavarakazayitena prabodhakAle'pi tatraivottiSThatA zarIreNa bahirdezadRzyasvApnavahnisparzasvApnajalapAnAdeH atyantAsaMbhAvitatvena tatprayuktadAhapipAsopazamAdipratyayasya pramAtvAyogAcca; tasmAt svApnavahnyAdijJAnam ayathArtham, yathArthavahnyAdijJAnasAmagrIM vinA jAyamAnatvAt, guJjApuJjamarumarIcikAdiSu jalavahnijJAnavat iti ||
