atha tRtIyA kakSyA-- na maddheturasiddhaH, svapne hi dAhaprayuktaduHkhAdyanubhavena dAhAdisadbhAvanizcayAt; na ca agre suptasya zarIradAhAdikAryAdarzanAt svapnasamaye taccharIrasya bahirnirgamanAbhAvAcca dAhAdirna saMbhavatIti zaGkyam, suptazarIraM vinA zarIrAntareNa jIvasya bahirvahnyAdidarzanadezaparyantanirgamanAGgIkArAt tasmindehe dagdhe'pi pUrvadehasya yathApUrvamavasthAnopapatteH, taddehAvacchedenaiva svapnadRzaH pipAsodayatacchAntyoH saMbhavAcca; evaM ca tvadIyaheturevAsiddhaH, na maddhetuH bahiH zarIrAntareNa jIvasya nirgamanAGgIkAreNa tadIyacakSurvahnyAdisaMnikarSarUpatatpramAkaraNasadbhAvAditi ||
atha caturthI kakSyA-- [p.369] tvaddheturasiddha eva, zarIrAntare tena bahirnirgamane ca pramANAbhAvAt, vastuto yatra deze vahnijalAdikaM nAsti, tatra svapnadRk vahnijalAdikaM pazyati, tatprayuktadAhapAnAdeH bhramavijRmbhitatvAvazyaMbhAvAt; ata eva na maddheturasiddha iti || atha paJcamI-- zarIrAntare tena nirgame ca tAvadasti pramANam- 'prANena rakSannavaraM kulAyaM bahiSkulAyAdavarazcaritvA | sa Iyate amRto yatra kAmaM hiraNmayaH puruSa ekahaMsaH' iti zrutiH; yatra deze svapnadRk vahnijalAdikaM pazyati, tatra tadA paramezvaraH tatsRjati, 'na tatra rathA na rathayogA na panthAno bhavanti atha rathAnrathayogAnpathaH sRjate na tatra vezAntAH puSkariNyaH sravantyo bhavanti atha vezAntAn puSkariNyaH sRjate sa hi kartA' iti zruteH; ato nAsti viSayAsaMbhavadoSo'pIti na maddheturasiddhaH tvaddheturevAsiddha iti ||
atha SaSThI kakSyA-- zarIrAntareNa nirgamanaM tAvat argalite'pavarake zayitasya viruddhaH, nirgatasya ca vahnyAdidezaparyantaM gacchato madhye vastuto yadyadyathA vartate tasya tasya tathA darzanAbhAvo viruddhaH; vahnyAdideze sthitvA dAhAdikamanubhavataH tatkAle eva kenacit prabodhitasya apavarake eva utthAnaM ca viruddham; prabodhakAle pratyAgamanAGgIkAre tAvaddUraM gatasya AhvAnamAtreNa jhaTiti utthAnaM viruddham; tathA kAJcyAM rAtrau zayitasya svapne divA dakSiNAdizaM prasthAya ghaTikAmAtreNa kAzIprAptidarzanam, kAJcyAM zayitasya aprasthAyaiva kAzIvRttAntadarzanaM ca viruddham; muhUrtamAtravartini svapne bahukAlaM kAzyAdisaMcAradarzanaM ca viruddham; tasmAt sarvavidhavirodhaparihArArthaM bahiSkulAyazrutiH bahiSkulAyAdiva caritvA iti bhAktatayA vyAkhyeyA; ata eva 'yatraitatsvapnayA carati' ityupakramya 'sve zarIre yathAkAmaM parivartate' iti zrutyantaram; [p.370] evaM ca zarIrAntare'pi nAsti pramANam; dezAntaraM gacchantamAtmAnamanubhavannapi svapnadRk yena zarIreNa zayitaH tenaiva zarIravantamAtmAnaM manyate; zarIrAntaragrahaNe tadaMze bhramatvaM svapnasya tvayApi svIkaraNIyaM syAt; evaM vahnyAdirahite deze vahnyAdisRSTyaGgIkAre prabodhanAnantaraM taddezaM gatvA pazyataH tatra bhasmAdyadarzanaM ca viruddham; yatra deze mahAhradAdikaM tiSThati tatra vahnijalAdidarzanaM ca viruddham, yatra gopuraprAsAdAdikaM tatra kazcit svapnadRk vanaM pazyati, tadaiva kazcit senAM pazyati, anyaH samudram, paraH zailamiti bahUnAM mUrtAnAM yugapat ekatrAvasthAnaM ca viruddham; tasmAt uktasakalavidhavirodhaparihArArthaM rathAdisRSTizrutirapi bhAktatayA vyAkhyeyA; ata eva tatraiva 'na tatra rathA na rathayogA na panthAno bhavanti' ityAdau tadabhAvo'pi zrUyate; tasmAt na maddheturasiddhaH; tvaddheturevAsiddha iti ||
atha saptamI kakSyA-- svapnazarIramargalitAdapyapavarakAt nirgamanayogyaM yojanazataprApyaM dezaM kSaNena gantuM kSaNena tata AgantuM ca zaktaM svapnArthamAtragrahaNayogyacakSurAdiyuktaM ca kalpyate; dezakAlaviparyAsazca svapnadRgekAnubhAvyAdityagatyAdyupAdhivizeSaprayuktAdikkAlavizeSakalpanena upapadyate; agatvA dezAntaradarzanam alpamArgaM gatvA dUradezadarzanaM ca agamanadUramArgagamanayoH ananusaMdhAnena upapadyate; bahukAlAtivAhanaM ca devAdikAlopAdhibhyo manuSyAdikAlopAdhInAmiva jAgratkAlopAdhibhyaH svapnakAlopAdhInAM sUkSmatvatAratamyakalpanena upapadyate; mUrtAnAM viruddhAnAM ca samAvezastu teSvanyAnubhAvyamUrtadravyAntarAvasthitipratighAtakatvAbhAvAnyAnubhAvyaviruddhatvAbhAvakalpanena upapadyate; bhasmAdyadarzanaM paramezvaraH svapnadRgekAnubhAvyamarthajAtaM sRSTvA tadaiva svapnAnte tatsarvaM niravazeSamupasaMharatIti kalpanena upapadyate; ato nAtra yuktivirodhaH kazcidasti; 'sve zarIre' iti zrutiH zarIrAntarasyApi svazarIratvAdupapadyate; tatra zayitazarIrAbhimAnamAtraM bhramo'stu; na hi svapnasatyatvavAdinaH svapne bhrama eva nAsti, svapne'pi zuktirajatAdibhramAGgIkArAt; 'na tatra rathAH' ityAdizrutiH svapnarathAdisRSTeH prAk tatra deze rathAdyabhAvaviSayA; ata eva 'atha rathAn' ityAdau athazabdaH; tasmAt na maddheturasiddhaH, tvaddheturevAsiddha iti ||
atha aSTamI kakSyA-- [p.371] tAvallokadRSTaviruddhaM kimavalambya kalpyate; bahiSkulAyazrutiM rathAdisRSTizrutiM ca iti cet, na, bahiSkulAyazruteH 'sve zarIre' iti zrutyanusAreNa bhAktatayA vyAkhyAtavyatvAt, zarIrAntaraM parigRhNAti cet, tasyApi pUrvazarIravat svIyatvamanubhavasiddhamiti tasyAvaktavyatayA, tasyAH zruteH vikRtazarIrAntaraparigrahadarzane'pi bahiH saMcAradarzane'pi zayite pUrvazarIra eva parivartate, na zarIrAntaraM parigRhNAti, nApi bahiH saMcaratIti tasya sarvasya mithyAtvapradarzanenaiva sAphalyasya upapAdanIyatvAt; rathAdisRSTizruteH 'na tatra rathAH' ityupakramAnurodhena bhAktatayA vyAkhyAtavyatvAcca; tatra laDAkhyAtAbhyAM rathAdyabhAvarathAdisRSTyoH samAnakAlatvapratipAdanena rathAdimithyAtvAvazyaMbhAvAt; 'atha' ityavyayaM tu athApi ityetadarthatvenApi upapannam; kiM ca sRSTizarIrAntarazrutyoH svArthaparatve prakaraNapratipAdyapradhAnArthavirodho'pi syAt; taddhi prakaraNamAtmanaH svayaMjyotiSTvaM pratipAdayituM pravRttam; tatra jAgradavasthAyAm AdityAdijyotirvyatikarAt taddurvivecamiti tadvivecanArthaM svapnAvasthA avatAritA; tatra yadi sRSTiH pratipAdyate, tadA tasyAmapi taddurvivecaM syAt AdityAdisadbhAvAt; ataH pradhAnapratipAdyavirodhAt svargArthajyotiSTomaprakaraNe tadaGgavidhyarthavAdarUpasya 'ko hi tadveda yadyamuSmi~lloke'sti vA na veti' iti vAkyasyeva svapnasRSTazarIrAntaraparigrahavacanasya na svArthe tAtparyam, kiMtu tanmithyAtvapradarzana eva uktarItyA tAtparyamiti yojayituM yuktam; kiM ca svapne mahAsenAyAM gajaturagavRSabhoSTrapadAtirUpAH pAreparArdhaM cetanA dRzyante; kasyacit svapnaM nirvoDhuM kiyantazcetanAH kutaH samAnIya paramezvareNApi melanIyAH; na hi svapnanirvAhArthameva tena kvacitkecana cetanAH pRthaGnivezitAH santi; cetanAMze cet svapnAnAmayathArthatA acetanAMze'pi tathAstu, kimardhajaratIyena; ata eva lokAnAm ArukSamiva zailAgram adrAkSamiva kAnanam ityAdirUpeNa svApnamithyAtvAnuvAdaH; 'uteva strIbhiH saha modamAnaH jakSadutevApi bhayAni pazyan' iti zrutAvapi lokaprasiddhisiddhatanmithyAtvAnuvAdaH ; tasmAt tvaddheturasiddhaH na maddheturiti siddham bahiH saMcAravahnijalatadarthakriyAdidarzanaM sarvamayathArtham || iti svapnAyathArthatvavAdaH ||24||

[p.372] zuktirajatAdyutpattisamarthanavAdaH || atha zuktirajatAdikaM svApnavahnijalAdikaM ca nAsaMnihitamayathArthajJAnaviSayaH ; kiM tu tatratyameveti nirNayArthaM kathAntaraM prastUyate-- etacchuktIdaMtayA gRhyamANaM rajatam etaddezavarti, etadidaMtayA gRhyamANatvAt etacchuktivat; yasyAH zukteradhastAt rajataM nAsti sA zuktiH etacchabdena vivakSitA; ato nAsti siddhasAdhanazaGkA iti prathamA kakSyA || atha dvitIyA-- bAdhitaviSayaM tvadanumAnam, na hi tatra prAksiddhaM rajatamasti, na vA abhinavarajatotpattau anyatra klRptaM rajatakAraNamasti; abhinavarajatotpattau ca pazcAdapi tadupalabdhiprasaGgaH; nanu zuktau rajatAvayavAnupravezo'sti, tatsadRzatvAt, bhUyo 'vayavasAmAnyaM sAdRzyamityaGgIkArAt; mImAMsakairapi pratinidhipeTikAyAm avazyakartavyeSu vrIhidravyakeSu karmasu vrIhyalAbhe tatsadRzA nIvArAH grAhyAH, teSu vrIhitvajAtyabhAve'pi tadavacchedyAnAM vrIhyavayavAnAM sattvena kiMcit vrIhizAstrAnugrahalAbhAt - iti uktatvAt; na ca zuktau rajatAvayavasattve doSAbhAvadazAyAmapi tadupalabdhiprasaGgaH, zuktau zuktyavayavA bhUyAMsa iti tadA teSAM grahaNena pratibandhAt rajatAgrahaNam, doSadazAyAM teSAmagrahaNena pratibandhAbhAvAt rajatagrahaNamityupapatteH; ataH zuktyanupraviSTarajatAvayavaviSayatvena sAdhyaparyavasAnasaMbhavAt na bAdha iti cet; maivam, evaM zuktirajatabhramanirvAhe'pi nIvArAdiSu vrIhyAdibhramAnirvAhAt; na hi yathA rajatAvayavA rajatAni tathA vrIhyavayavA vrIhayaH, rajatAvayaveSu rajatatvasyeva vrIhyavayaveSu vrIhitvAbhAvAt; ataH tAdRzasthalapakSIkaraNe bAdho nApaiti; upalakSaNaM hi sthAlIpulAkanyAyena rajatabhramasthalapakSIkaraNam; evaM nIlaM jalam ityAdibhramasthalapakSIkaraNe'pi bAdho draSTavyaH; vastutastu upanyastAnumAnapakSe'pi bAdhastadavasthaH, zuktau rajatAvayavAnAm abhAvAt; zuktyavayavAnAM zauklyaM cAkacakyaM ca asti, etAvataiva hi tayoH sAdRzyam, zuktau rajatAvayavAnAm api sattve, tasyAm [p.373] atyantAgnisaMyogena zuktyavayaveSu bhasmIbhavatsu, rajatAvayaveSu dravatvotpattiprasaGgAt; tasmAt etacchuktIdaMtayA gRhyamANaM rajatam, etacchuktidezavarti na bhavati, rajatatvAt, dezAntaradvIpAntaragatarajatavat iti ||
atha tRtIyA kakSyA-- na bAdhitaviSayaM madanumAnam, bhramadazAyAm abhinavarajatotpatteH tasya bAdhakAle layasya ca aGgIkArAt; kAraNAbhAvadoSastu asaMnihitarajatasphuraNapakSe'pi tulyaH, asaMnikRSTasya bhAnAyogAt; na ca bhrame zuktirvizeSyaM rajataM vizeSaNamiti na rajatasaMnikarSApekSA, vizeSaNajJAnavizeSyasaMnikarSatadubhayAsaMsargAgrahamAtrAt viziSTapratyakSotpattisaMbhavAditi vAcyam, tathA sati saMnikRSTaparvatapakSakavahnyanumitisthale pratyakSodayaprasaGgAt; anumitsAviharadazAyAM samAne viSaye pratyakSasAmagryA balavattvAt; yadi pramArUpe jJAne klRptasya kAraNasyAbhAve'pi doSavazAt bhramarUpaM jJAnaM jAyate ityucyeta, tadA satyarajate klRptasya kAraNasyAbhAve'pi bhramagrAhyamasatyaM rajataM doSavazAjjAyate ityeva vaktuM yuktam, anubhavAnurodhAt; idaMtayA purodezavarti hi rajatamanubhUyate; na ca doSavazAdasaMnikRSTabhAnasyeva rajatAvayavAnapekSasya rajatasyotpattiH svIkartuM na yuktA, nirupAdAnadravyotpattyayogAditi vAcyam, doSasahitAt mUlakAraNAdeva bhramayiturIzvarasya icchayA tadutpattyupapatteH ; tasmAt madanumAnaM na bAdhitaviSayam, tvadanumAnameva bAdhitaviSayam iti || atha caturthI-- tvadanumAnaM bAdhitaviSayameva, rajatasAmAnye klRptasya kAraNasyAbhAve rajatotpattyayogAt; manmate tu asaMnihite'pi jJAnarUpaH saMnikarSo'stIti na jJAnasAmAnye klRptasya kAraNasyAbhAvaH; evaM ca rajatasya purodezavartitvAsaMbhavenAsaMnihitasyaiva tasya bhAne'vazyaM vaktavye zuktIdamaMzatAdAtmyena sphuraNAt idaM rajatamiti vyavahAraH ityupapAdanIyam; ato'nubhavavirodhaparihArAt madanumAnaM na bAdhitaviSayam iti || [p.374] [p.375]
atha paJcamI kakSyA-- rajate jJAnaM saMnikarSazcet vahnAvapi saMnikarSaH syAditi tatpratyakSodayaH durvAraH ; ataH klRptakAraNAbhAve'pi bhramotpattau doSa eva zaraNIkaraNIyaH iti tadviSayotpattyupapAdanameva yuktam, anubhavasvArasyAnurodhAt, sarvathA tadasaMbhave hi kathaMcit tadupapAdanaM kAryam; tasmAt madanumAnaM na bAdhitaviSayam, kiM tu tvadanumAnameveti ||
atha SaSThI kakSyA-- tvatpakSa eva klRptakAraNAbhAvadoSaH, na tu matpakSe, jJAnasaMnikarSasattvAt; na ca tathA sati vahnyanumitisthale pratyakSodayaprasaGgaH, alaukikapratyakSasAmagryapekSayA samAne'pi viSaye anumitisAmagryA balavattvAbhyupagamAt; ataH prAguktarItyA tvadanumAnameva bAdhitaviSayam iti || atha saptamI-- tathA sati saMzayottarabhramarUpapratyakSasthale anumitiprasaGgaH; ato gatyantarAbhAvAt doSa eva upapAdanIyaH iti maduktarItyA madanumAnaM na bAdhitaviSayam; kiM tu tvadanumAnameveti || athASTamI-- pramArUpAlaukikapratyakSasAmagryapekSayaiva anumitisAmagrI balavatI; saMzayottarapratyakSasthale bhramarUpAlaukikapratyakSasAmagrI tu anumitisAmagryapekSayA balavatIti noktadoSa iti || atha navamI-- tathA sati bhramarUpadhUmaparAmarzasthale vahnyAbhAvavati bhramarUpavahnipratyakSaprasaGgaH ; atastatra jJAnasya saMnikarSatvAyogAt doSa eva zaraNIkaraNIya iti tadavastho maduktadoSaH iti || atha dazamI-- jJAnaM na saMnikarSaH iti tAvadayuktam, surabhi candanam ityAdipratyakSasthale tasya saMnikarSatvaklRpteH, tathaivAtrApi jJAnasya saMnikarSatvasaMbhavAt; na ca tathA sati saMzayottarabhramasthala iva bhramarUpavahnyanumitisthale'pi pratyakSodayaprasaGgaH; tatra pratyakSabhramaprayojakadoSavizeSAbhAvAt; tadabhAvazca tatra vahni pazyAmIti anuvyavasAyAbhAvAt vahnimanuminomi ityevAnuvyavasAyadarzanAcca kalpyate; Arope sati nimittAnusaraNam, na tu nimittamastItyAropaH iti hi nyAyavidaH; evaM ca doSAsahakRtAlaukikapratyakSasAmagryapekSayA samAne viSaye'pi anumitisAmagrI balavatIti phalabalAt kalpyate; tasmAdiha jJAnasya saMnikarSatvAbhyupagatau na kazciddoSa iti tvadanumAnameva bAdhitaviSayam, na tu madanumAnam - iti || athaikAdazI kakSyA-- kvacidapi jJAnaM na saMnikarSaH iti na pratyAkhyAyate, kiMtu vahnyanumitisthala iva rajatabhramasthale'pi jJAnaM saMnikarSo bhavituM nArhati ityetAvaducyate; yatra candanakhaNDe pUrvaM saurabhamavagatam, tatraiva hi smRtyupanItasaurabhaviSayaM surabhicandanamiti pratyakSam; na hi kvaciccandanakhaNDe saurabhamananubhUtavataH candanakhaNDAntaradarzane'pi surabhi candanakhaNDamiti pratyakSamasti; na ca zuktirajatasya pUrvamanubhavo'sti; ato na tatra tajjJAnaM saMnikarSaH iti yujyate; yadi yadavacchedena yatpUrvamavagatam, tadvattvena, jJAte tajjJAnaM saMnikarSaH candanatvAvacchedena prAk saurabhaM gRhItavataH tadvattvena jJAte candanakhaNDAntare'pi surabhi candanamiti pratyakSam iSTamityucyeta, tadApi prakRte [p.376] jJAnasya saMnikarSatvaM na yujyate, rajatatAdAtmyAvacchedakatvena gRhItasya kasyacit zuktAvadarzanAt; tasmAt prAguktarItyA tvadanumAnameva bAdhitaviSayam iti || atha dvAdazI-- rajatatAdAtmyAvacchedakatvena rajate gRhItaM cAkacakyaM zuktau rajatabhramAtpUrvaM gRhyate, sAdRzyajJAnasya taddhetutvasaMpratipatteH, ato'tra jJAnasya saMnikarSatvaM yujyate; ato dezAntarastharajatabhAne klRptakAraNasadbhAvAt rajatAntarotpattau tadabhAvAcca asaMnihitabhAnamevAGgIkRtya tatra zuktIdamaMzatAdAtmyasphuraNAt idaM rajatamiti vyavahAraH ityupapAdanIyam, anyathA caitrAdivyaktivizeSabhramasthale tadevedaM rajatamiti vyaktivizeSapratyabhijJAbhramasthale ca kA gatiH; na hi rajatasAmAnyArthipravRttiH prAtibhAsikarajatajJAnAdiva prasiddhacaitroddezyakapravRttiH prAtibhAsikacaitrajJAnAt naSTAnviSyamANasvakIyarajatoddezyakapravRttiH prAtibhAsikarajatajJAnAdvA upapadyate; tasmAt tvadanumAnameva bAdhitaviSayam iti || atha trayodazI-- zuktAvatiprasaktaM cAkacakyasAmAnyaM na rajatatAdAtmyAvacchedakam; zukticAkacakye rajatavRtticAkacakyavizeSatAdAtmyabhrameNa avacchedakatvajJAnopapAdane rajatacAkacakyavizeSasyApi dezAntaragatasya bhAnAnupapattistulyA; tathA nIlaM jalamiti bhramasthale prAk jalasya nIlimAvacchedakavattvena jJAnaM nAsti, rUpaM vinA kevalajalApratIteH; nIlimAvacchedakatvena zaGkanIyasya kasyacidabhAvAcca; prAgadRSToragasya aindrajAlikapradarzitoragabhrame maNDUkavasAJjanadoSajanyavaMzoragabhrame ca uragatAdAtmyAvacchedakavattvena adhiSThAnasya jJAnaM nAsti, tadavacchedakasya prAgavijJAtatvAt; evaM saMnikarSasyeva nirAlokadezAntarasthitavastubhrame viSayAlokasaMyogasyAbhAvAdapi na dezAntarasthasya bhramaviSayatvamupapadyate; api ca pratArakavAkyAt - zazasya zRGgamasti, tadevaMlakSaNamaraNye patitamanviSya labdhuM zakyam; tena ca idaM prayojanaM bhavati ityevaMrUpAt avAptamohasya tathaivAraNyaM gatvA tadanveSa[p.377]mANasya yathoktacihnayuktakASThazRGgAntarAdidarzane zazazRGgamidam ityanubhavo'sti; asti ca svapne manuSyapazupakSivRkSAdyanekarUpasya ekasyaivAvayavino'nubhavaH; na ca tatra asaMnihitaM zazazRGgAdikaM bhramaviSaya iti vaktuM zakyamiti agatyA doSAt prAtibhAsikasyaiva tasyotpattiraGgIkaraNIyA; evaM tatra doSasya prAtibhAsikaviSayotpattikAraNatvaklRptau tenaiva rajatabhrame'pi viSayotpatteraGgIkaraNaM yuktam; evaM ca bhramaviSayANAm idaMtayA anubhavaH svArasyaM prApito bhavati; caitrAdivyaktivizeSabhrameNa aprasiddhacaitrAdyuddezyakapravRttistu tanniSThacaitratvAdeH prAtibhAsikaviSaye'pi sphuraNAt upapadyate; asti hi caitrazarIre caitratvaM tadAkRtivizeSavyaGgyam; tatprAtibhAsikacaitrazarIre'pi vyaJjakasattvena abhivyaktaM sphurati; tatsphuraNamAtraM prasiddhacaitroddezyakapravRttau tantram; iSTatAvacchedakajJAnAdeva pravRttyupapatteH; rajatavyaktivizeSaviSayapratyabhijJAbhrameNa tadarthapravRttirapi prAtibhAsikarajatasyaiva zuktau vizeSavaiziSTyena sphuraNAdupapadyate; satyarajatAntaraviSaye tadidaM rajatam iti pratyabhijJAbhrame hi purovartinyeva rajate tattAvaiziSTyasphuraNamaGgIkartavyam; tat prAtibhAsikarajate'pi astu; sA tattA tavAsaMnihitA, mama rajatena saha tadvizeSaNabhAvena utpanneti vizeSaH; tat purodeze pratyagrarajatotpattau kAraNasattvAt asaMnihitarajatabhAne kAraNAbhAvAcca madanumAnaM na bAdhitaviSayam, tvadanumAnameva bAdhitaviSayamiti siddhaM zuktirajatAdikamidaM purodezavarti prasiddharajatAdibhinnamiti; etena svapnacittabhramamanorAjyaviSayA api vyAkhyAtAH || iti zuktirajatAdyutpattisamarthanavAdaH ||25||

[p.377] prapaJcasattAnirAkaraNavAdaH || yathA zuktirajatAdikam agre nedaM rajatamityAdiprakAreNa bAdhyamapi bhramakAle zuktIdaMtayA idamitivat zuktisattayA saditi pratIyate, evaM jagadapi brahmasattayA saditi pratIyate, na tvadharmarUpayA sattayeti nirNayArthaM kathAntaraM prastUyate - san ghaTaH iti pratItau bhAsamAnasattA ghaTadharmaH, san ghaTaH iti pratItiprakAratvAt, ghaTatvavat iti pUrvapakSiNaH prathamA kakSyA || [p.378] atha dvitIyA-- aprayojakamidam - zuktirajatasya adhiSThAnasattayeva prapaJcasya sarvopAdAnabhUtayA brahmasattayA satpratItiviSayatvopapatteH; tasmAt san ghaTa ityatra bhAsamAnasattA ghaTadharmo na bhavati, sanmAtravRttitvAt, Anantyavat iti || atha tRtIyA-- nAprayojakaM madanumAnaM, ghaTaH sanniti ghaTatvataulyena ghaTavizeSaNatayA ghaTadharmatvena bhAsamAnAyAH sattAyAH bAdhakAbhAvena tathAtvasyaiva svIkaraNIyatvAt tvadanumAnamevAprayojakam, sattAyAH sanmAtrapravRttitve'pi ghaTAdivRttitvopapatteH teSAmapi sattve bAdhakAbhAvAt iti || atha caturthI-- ghaTadharmaH sattA svarUpasattA vA syAt, jAtisattA vA nAdyaH, svarUpasya taddharmatvAnupapatteH; nApi dvitIyaH, sAmAnyAdiSu sattAjAtirahiteSu tAddharmyeNa sadvyavahArAnirvAhAt; ghaTAdiSu tAddharmyeNa sAmAnyAdiSu tadekArthasamavAyAdisaMbandhAntareNa ityabhyupagame ananugamAt; tasmAt sarvAnugatayA brahmasattayaiva prapaJcAnugatasadvyavahAro nirvoDhavyaH, svarUpasattAyA iva brahmasattAyAM prapaJcAnugatasadvyavahAranirvAhakatve bAdhakAbhAvAt; evaM brahmasattayaiva sarvatra sadvyavahAranirvAhe tasyAH prapaJcadharmatvaprasAdhanam aprayojakameva; madanumAnaM nAprayojakam, ghaTadharmasattAbhyupagame'pi tayA sadvyavahArAnirvAheNa ghaTaH sanniti pratItau bhAsamAnA sattA brahmasattaiva ityabhyupagantavyatvAt iti ||
atha paJcamI kakSyA-- ghaTaH sanniti vyavahAraviSayasattAyA brahmasattArUpatve bAdhakaM nAsti ityetadasiddham, sarvopAdAnabrahmazaktirUpA sA dravyamiti tvayAGgIkRtatvena nIrUpadravyasya [p.379] cAkSuSatvAyogAt; ataH saMbandhAnanugame'pi jAtisattayaiva prapaJce sadvyavahAro nirvoDhavyaH, tavApi brahmaNi sadvyavahAraH tAddharmyeNa jagati upAdAnAnuvedhena sattAyA bhedeneti saMbandhAnanugamasya aGgIkartavyatvAt; tasmAt madanumAnaM nAprayojakam; kiMtu tvadanumAnameveti || atha SaSThI-- nIrUpApi brahmasattA prapaJcasattAvat cAkSuSI bhavitumarhati; nIrUpadravyaM na cAkSuSamityetat aprayojakam, ghaTaH sphuratIti sphuraNarUpAyAstasyAH cAkSuSatvadRSTezca; na ca sattAyA nIrUpatvamapi, upAdAnopAdeyayoH abhinnatayA ghaTarUpeNApi tasyA rUpavattvAt; ata eva jAtisattayeva brahmasattayA sadvyavahAranirvAhe saMbandhAnanugamo'pi nAsti, brahmaNA dharmiNA upAdeyena prapaJcena cAbhinnAyA brahmasattAyAH sarvatrAbhedasaMbandha eva sadvyavahAranirvAhaka ityupapatteH; tasmAt tvadanumAnameva aprayojakam; madanumAnaM nAprayojakamiti ||
atha saptamI kakSyA-- nIrUpadravyaM na cAkSuSam ityetannAprayojakam, dravyacAkSuSatAyAM mahattvasamAnAdhikaraNodbhUtarUpasya prayojakatvena klRptatvAt; ghaTaH sphuratItyetat ghaTavRttyanubhavaviSayArthakam, na tu brahmazaktirUpasphuraNavattvArthakam ; ghaTAdirUpeNa brahmasattA rUpiNItyetadapi ayuktam, tathA ca sati indriyeSvapi sattAyAzcAkSuSatvaprasaGgAt; abhedasaMbandhAnugamoktirapi ayuktA, tena hi suvarNaM kuNDalamitivat ghaTaH sattA ityeva anubhavaH syAt, mama tu sattAyAmapi sattaikArthasamavAyo'sti; ata eva abhede'pi sAmAnAdhikaraNyarUpA vyAptirabhyupeyate; tasmAt ghaTAdisattayaiva teSu sadvyavahAranirvAhaH kAryaH, na brahmasattayeti yathA ghaTagandhenaiva ghaTo gandhavAnityAdivyavahAranirvAhaH kAryaH, na tu 'sarvakarmA sarvakAmaH sarvagandhaH sarvarasaH' iti zrutipratipannabrahmagandheneti nAprayojakaM madanumAnam, tvadanumAnamevAprayojakamiti || [p.380] athASTamI-- dravyacAkSuSatAyAmudbhUtarUpaM na prayojakam, iha pakSI iha netyAdipratyakSe ihapratyayavedyadezatvena nIrUpasyApi AkAzasya cAkSuSatvAt; nanvastu AlokaH ihapratyayavedyadezaH, AkAzasya tathAtvAGgIkAre'pi tasya pakSyAdhAratvAnAdhAratvavyavasthArtham Aloka upAsanIyaH, niravayavasya AkAzasya svAvayavabhedenAvacchedAsaMbhavena AlokAvayavabhedairevAvacchedabhedasya vaktavyatvAt; na ca tattadadhaHkSitibhAgabhedo'vacchedakaH syAditi vAcyam, UrdhvAdharadezayoH iha pakSI iha neti pratItAvAdhAratvavyavasthAyAM tadbhedAbhAvAt; nibiDamUrtadravyAbhAvo vA ihapratyayavedyo dezo'stu; AkAzAGgIkAre'pi hi tadabhAvo'pekSitaH, anyathA kuDyAdyAvaraNe'pi apratighAtapakSisaMcArApatteH; na ca yatra kuDyAdyAvaraNam, tatra AkAzo nAstIti zakyaM vaktuM, tasya asarvagatatvaprasaGgAt; AvaraNApAye'pi AkAzAbhAvaprasaGgAcca; na hi AkAzastatra pUrvamasan AvaraNAbhAve samutpadyate iti cet, ucyate - astyAlokAtiriktaH prAgAdidezarUpa AkAzaH, prAgbhAge candrAlokaH samunmiSati pratyagbhAge tamo'nuvartate ityAdipratItiSu AlokAderapi AdhAratvena prAgAdidezarUpasyAkAzasya pratibhAsAt; AkAzasyaiva sUryagatyAdyavacchedabhedena prAgAdidezatvAGgIkArAt; etena AkAze pradezabhedArtham AlokAvayavApekSaNamAvazyakamityapi zaGkA nirastA, prAgAdyupAdhibhiH tadavacchedopapatteH; nibiDamUrtadravyAbhAvarUpo'vakAza ihapratyayavedyaH ityapi na yuktam, iha prAgbhAge'vakAzo'sti, na pratyagbhAge bhuJjAnairnibiDe-ityAdivyavahAradarzanena prAgAdidezasya tato'pi vyatirekAvasAyAt; idAnIM ghaTaH ayaM ghaTaH ityAdipratItiSu niyamena sphurataH kAlasya cAkSuSatvAdapi na rUpaM dravyacAkSuSatve prayojakam; saMpratipannaM ca brahmazakteH sphuraNarUpeNa cAkSuSatvam; ghaTaH sphurati ityAdipratyayo hi savitA sphurati bhAti prakAzate ityAdipratyayavat sphuraNavaiziSTyaviSayaH; tacca sphuraNaM caitanyarUpA brahmazaktiH ityeva yuktam, ata eva zrutiH-- 'tasya bhAsA sarvamidaM vibhAti' iti; brahmasattAyA ghaTAdirUpeNa rUpavattvAbhyupagame'pi nAtiprasaGgaH, ghaTAdyavacchedena hi tasyA rUpavattvam; abhede'pi san ghaTa ityAdivizeSaNavizeSyabhAva upapadyate idAnImabhijinmuhUrtaH ityAdAvabhede'pi tathAtvadarzanAt; athavA prapaJce [p.381] brahmasattAnuvedho nAma sarvopAdAnabhUtasattAdhiSThAnabhUtabrahmatAdAtmyAnubhavaH; ataH san ghaTaH ityAdipratyayaH sadityaMze sattAviziSTabrahmaviSayakaH iti na kAcidanupapattiH ata eva bhASyam - 'saJcidrUpeNa zivena yadi na vyAptaM jagat, tadA sattAsphUrtibhyAM vinAkRtaM kathamasti sphurati iti ca dRzyeta; ato mRdA ghaTAdikamiva kAraNena zivena kAryamidaM vyAptam' iti; anena bhASyeNa vastutaH prapaJcaH sanna bhavati kiMtu bAdhyaH iti darzitam; itthameva hi bhASyakArasya upAsanAmArganirUpaNatAtparyeNa tatra vineyAnAM rucyutpAdanArthaM sarvatra zuddhAdvaitaM pracchAditavato'pi aniyamAdhikaraNAdiSu nirvizeSabrahmavidyAyAmacirAdimArgaM pratiSiddhavataH paramasiddhAntaH; na ca brahmazaktiH satteti paribhASA kRtA, tasyAH prapaJcadharmatvaniSedhamAtreNa prapaJcasya bAdhyatvaM kathaM sidhyet iti zaGkanIyam, nityatvaprAmANyAderanityatvApramANyAdivirodhitvavat brahmasattAyA bAdhyatvavirodhitvamiSyata iti nityatvavyatirekeNAnityatvavat tadvyatirekeNa bAdhyatvasiddherapratyUhatvAt na caivaM sati gandharasAdikamapi ghaTAdiSu prAkRtaM na sidhyet iti zaGkanIyam; brahmaNi hi nirdoSe surabhireva gandhaH hRdya eva rasaH; sa ca tasya divyanityavigrahe; ata eva paryaGkavidyAyAm arcirAdimArgeNa brahmalokaM gatasya divyavigrahasaMnikarSaM prAptasyaiva brahmagandharasasaMkrAntibhAjanatA zrUyate 'taM brahmagandhaH pravizati taM brahmarasaH pravizati' iti; tasmAt prAguktarItyA tvadanumAnamaprayojakam, madanumAnaM nAprayojakamiti brahma sattayaiva prapaJcaH sanniti pratIyate na svasattayeti siddham; evaM ca zuktirajatasvApnapadArthajAtavat viyadAdiprapaJcasyApi bAdhyatvasiddhiH akhaNDAdvitIyabrahmabhAvaparamamuktyupayoginI avyAhatA; 'na vilakSaNatvAt' ityadhikaraNanyAyena satyamithyAvastunorapi upAdAnopAdeyabhAvopapatteH iti || iti prapaJcasattAnirAkaraNavAdaH ||26||

[p.381] brahmAnandavaiSayikAnandAbhedasamarthanavAdaH || yathA prapaJce bhAsamAnA sattA cidAnandarUpA brahmazaktiH, evaM saMsAriSu bhAsamAno vaiSayikAnando'pi saiveti prasAdhanAya kathAntaraM prastUyate [p.382] sAMsArikAnando brahmAnandAt na bhidyate, AnandatvAt, 'sa eko brahmaNa AnandaH' iti zrutAnandavat; abhede'pi avacchedakabhedena pakSadRSTAntabhAvaH iti prathamA kakSyA || atha dvitIyA-- bAdhitamidamanumAnam, mAnuSAdiprAjApatyAntAt sAMsArikamahAnandAt utkRSTo hi niratizayo brahmAnandaH zrUyate; tena kathamatyantApakRSTasya krimikITAdyAnandasyApi abhedo yujyate; tasmAt sAMsArikAnandaH brahmAnandAt bhidyate, apakRSTatvAt, tAradhvanyapakRSTamandradhvanivat iti || atha tRtIyA-- na bAdhitaM madanumAnam, ekatrApi abhivyaktitAratamyena utkarSAkarSapratibhAsopapatteH; tvadanumAnameva zrutibAdhitam, 'etasyaivAnandasyAnyAni bhUtAni mAtrAmupajIvanti' iti hi sAMsArikAnandajAtasya brahmAnandalezatvaM zrutam, tathA AnandavalyAm 'so'znute sarvAn kAmAnsaha brahmaNA vipazcitA' iti brahmavidaH sakalavaiSayikAnandAvAptiM phalamuktvA nirdhUtasakalasaMsArasya kathaM sukhAvAptiH phalamucyate iti zaGkAnirAkaraNArtham agre tadvivaraNaprastAve 'saiSAnandasya mImAMsA bhavati' ityupakramya 'yuvA syAt sAdhu yuvAdhyAyakaH | AziSTho draDhiSTho baliSThaH | tasyeyaM pRthivI sarvA vittasya pUrNA syAt | sa eko mAnuSa AnandaH' iti mAnuSeSu sarvotkRSTatayA saMbhAvitamAnandaM prathamamabhidhAya tato gAndharvAdi prAjApatyAntAnAmAnandAnAm uttarottarazataguNAnuktvA, 'te ye zataM prajApaterAnandAH | sa eko brahmaNa AnandaH | zrotriyasya cAkAmahatasya' iti prAjApatyAnandAt zataguNatvenAbhidhIyamAnasya brahmAnandasya muktabhogyatvAbhidhAnAt tasya sakalavaiSayikAnandAnantajalakaNikAmahAsamudrAyamANatvena tAvadAnandasamaSTirUpatvaM darzitamiti ||
atha caturthI kakSyA-- [p.383] utkarSApakarSayoH bhedakadharmatvena tayoH satoH bhedasyautsargikatvAt srakcandanavanitAdibhojyaviSayavailakSaNyenAnandabhedAnubhavAcca pratyakSAnubhavAnusAreNa mukhya eva bhedaH svIkartuM yuktaH, na tu aupAdhikatayA netuM yuktaH; 'etasyaivAnandasya' iti zrutiH vaiSayikAnandAH brahmAnandasya lezabhAvamarhanti iti 'amba tvadaMzoraNuraMzumAlI tavaiva mandasmitabindurinduH' iti kavitAsaraNirItyA teSAM tucchatvapradarzanaparA; sarvakAmAvApteH brahmAnandAvAptirUpatayA vivaraNam anAdyanantadezakAlapravahadakhilaprANijAtavarti yAvatsukhabRndam asti, tAvatsamAno brahmAnanda ityetadabhiprAyam; tasmAt madanumAnaM na bAdhitam, tvadanumAnameva bAdhitamiti || atha paJcamI kakSyA-- Anandaikyasya tAragakArAt mandramandrataramandratamagakAreSviva laukikasukheSu anubhUyamAnasya prAjApatyAdimAnuSAnteSu AnandeSu zrUyamANasya ca apakarSatAratamyasya abhivyaktivizeSakRtatvamupapadyate; sukhabhedapratItezca tAragakArAt mandragakAro'nyaH iti bhedapratItivat aupAdhikApakarSabhedakRtatvamupapadyate; tatra sa evAyaM gakAra iti abhedapratyabhijJAsiddhaikyabalAt bhedabhedakapratItyoH aupAdhikaviSayatvaM kalpyata iti cet, ihApi zrutau mAnuSAdiparyAyeSu 'sa eko mAnuSa AnandaH' ityAdinA tattadAnandaikyasya ante 'sa yazcAyaM puruSe | yazcAsAvAditye | sa ekaH' iti puruSAdityazabdoktayormanuSyadevajAtyoH jIvavargaparabrahmaNorvA pratipAdanamastIti tadbalAttayoH aupAdhikatvakalpanaM yuktam; varNeSu dhvanivizeSANAmiva AnandeSvapi viSayavizeSANAmabhivyaJjakAnAM tAratamyasattvAt | pratyakSapratyabhijJAbalAdiva zabdabalAt bhedabhedakapratItyoH aupAdhikaviSayatvakalpanamayuktamiti cet, na, prAgviSayavizeSasukhamanubhUtavataH kAlAntare'pi tadviSayalAbhe tadeva sukhamidAnImapi anubhUyate iti pratyakSapratyabhijJAnasyApi atra sattvAt; na hi so'yaM gakAra iti pratyabhijJAnAt asya mAtrayApi vizeSamIkSAmahe; kiM ca sarveSvapi AnandeSu AnandAkArAnugatabuddhirapi Anandaikye pramANam; na ca sA jAtiviSayeti [p.384] kalpanIyam klRpte spaSTe hi vyaktibhede jAtiviSayatvaM kalpanIyam; AnandeSu viSayavizeSopAdhiM vinA svataH bhedApratIteH vyaktyaikyamevopapadyata iti kuto jAtiH kutastarAM tadviSayatA buddheH; api ca saMbhAvitadoSAt pratyakSAt nirdoSA tAtparyavatI zrutireva prabalA; tAtparyavatI ca AnandamImAMsA, sarvakAmAvAptiphalavivaraNArthaM pravRttatvAt; na ca tAtparyavatyAmupacArakalpanamapi yuktam ; 'na vidhau paraH zabdArthaH' iti nyAyAt; na ca vaiSayikasukhAnAM sarveSAM militAnAmapi akhaNDabrahmAnandalezarUpatvAt tadanubhavaH sakalavaiSayikasukhAvApteH atiricyate iti tavApi niratizayabrahmAnandAnubhavokteH sarvakAmAptiphalavivaraNatvamaupacArikaM neyamiti vAcyam, muktaM prati sarvAtmanA abhivyakte brahmAnande'nubhUyamAne vaiSayikAnandAtmanA vidyamAnAstadaMzAH sarve'pyanubhUyanta eveti tasya sarvakAmAvAptisattvena tatra upacArasyAkalpanIyatvAt; kiMtu mantrArthasarvakAmAvAptivacanaM paraM tadanyUnatAparaM kalpanIyam, dRSTaM hi 'AkAzavatsarvagatazca nityaH' iti vacanamanyUnatAparam; ihApi manuSyagandharvAdiparyAyasthAni akAmahatazrotriyavAkyAni muktAnubhAvyasya brahmAnandasya manuSyagandharvAdyAnandAnyUnatAparANi dRSTAni; na khalu brahmAnandamanubhavanmuktaH kazcit manuSyagandharvAnandamAtramanubhavannAste; sarveSAM muktAnAM niratizayabrahmAnandAnubhavasattvAt; tasmAt tvadanumAnaM bAdhitaviSayam; na tu madanumAnamiti sarve vaiSayikAnandA lezAH parabrahmAnandasyeti siddham || iti brahmAnandavaiSayikAnandAbhedasamarthanavAdaH ||27|| samUlazravaNAzleSacitrAbhAdrapadottarA | navyA nakSatramAleyaM prItyai somasya nirmitA || iti zrImadbharadvAjakulajaladhikaustubhazrIvizvajidyAjizrIraGgarAjAdhvarivarasUnorappayyadIkSitasya kRtiSu pUrvottaramImAMsAvAdanakSatramAlA saMpUrNA ||
